===== FILE: .\apps\kickzone-api\src\app\order-inputs.validation.spec.ts =====
import { BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from '@kickzone-workspace/resolvers';
import { createOrderSchema } from '@kickzone-workspace/validation';
import { CreateOrderInput, CreateOrderItemInput } from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';

describe('Order Inputs Validation', () => {
  const pipe = new ZodValidationPipe(createOrderSchema);

  const validOrderItem: CreateOrderItemInput = {
    productId: 'prod-1',
    productName: 'Test Product',
    quantity: 1,
    unitPrice: 10.0,
    totalPrice: 10.0,
  };

  const validOrderBase = {
    customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
    customerPhone: '+1234567890',
    customerName: 'John Doe',
    shippingAddress: '123 Main St',
    city: 'New York',
    state: 'NY',
    zipCode: '10001',
    country: 'USA',
    sellerId: 1,
  };

  it('should reject CreateOrderInput with quantity 0 via BadRequestException', () => {
    const input: CreateOrderInput = {
      ...validOrderBase,
      items: [{ ...validOrderItem, quantity: 0 }], // invalid: must be >= 1
    };
    expect(() => pipe.transform(input)).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it('should reject CreateOrderInput with quantity 1.5 via BadRequestException', () => {
    const input: CreateOrderInput = {
      ...validOrderBase,
      items: [{ ...validOrderItem, quantity: 1.5 }], // invalid: must be integer
    };
    expect(() => pipe.transform(input)).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it('should resolve CreateOrderInput with quantity 1 and valid fields', () => {
    const input: CreateOrderInput = { ...validOrderBase, items: [validOrderItem] };
    expect(pipe.transform(input)).toEqual(input);
  });
});
===== FILE: .\apps\kickzone-api\src\app\update-order-input.validation.spec.ts =====
import { BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from '@kickzone-workspace/resolvers';
import { updateOrderSchema } from '@kickzone-workspace/validation';
import { UpdateOrderInput, UpdateOrderItemInput } from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';

describe('UpdateOrderInput Validation', () => {
  const pipe = new ZodValidationPipe(updateOrderSchema);

  const validUpdateOrderItem: UpdateOrderItemInput = {
    productId: 'prod-1',
    productName: 'Test Product',
    quantity: 1,
    unitPrice: 10.0,
    totalPrice: 10.0,
  };

  it('should resolve UpdateOrderInput with only trackingNumber (no items)', () => {
    const input: UpdateOrderInput = { trackingNumber: 'T1' };
    expect(pipe.transform(input)).toEqual(input);
  });

  it('should resolve UpdateOrderInput with trackingNumber and items: null', () => {
    const input: UpdateOrderInput = { trackingNumber: 'T1', items: null };
    expect(pipe.transform(input)).toEqual(input);
  });

  it('should reject UpdateOrderInput with items containing quantity 0 via BadRequestException', () => {
    const input: UpdateOrderInput = {
      items: [{ ...validUpdateOrderItem, quantity: 0 }], // invalid: must be >= 1
    };
    expect(() => pipe.transform(input)).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it('should resolve UpdateOrderInput with items containing quantity 1 and valid fields', () => {
    const input: UpdateOrderInput = { items: [validUpdateOrderItem] };
    expect(pipe.transform(input)).toEqual(input);
  });
});
===== FILE: .\apps\kickzone-api-e2e\src\checkout.e2e-spec.ts =====
/**
 * End-to-End Tests for Checkout Process
 *
 * These tests verify the complete checkout flow:
 * 1. Order creation (stock NOT decreased)
 * 2. Payment request initiation
 * 3. Payment verification (stock decreased on success)
 * 4. Order status updates
 * 5. Stock management throughout the process
 */

import { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ConfigModule } from '@nestjs/config';
import { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import {
  Order,
  OrderItem,
  OrderStatus,
  SellerProduct,
  SellerInfo,
  User,
  UserRole,
  Cart,
  CartItem,
  ProductInfo,
  Product,
  Category,
  Translation,
  PlatformSettings,
  Color,
  ProductInfoColor,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { OrderModule } from '@kickzone-workspace/modules';
import { OrderService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { PaymentService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { getRepositoryToken } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';

// Mock PaymentService to avoid actual ZarinPal API calls in tests
const mockPaymentService = {
  requestPayment: jest.fn(),
  verifyPayment: jest.fn(),
  getGatewayUrl: jest.fn(),
};

describe('Checkout Process E2E Tests', () => {
  // Increase Jest timeout for async DB setup + full checkout flows
  jest.setTimeout(30000);
  let app: INestApplication;
  let orderService: OrderService;
  let sellerProductRepository: Repository<SellerProduct>;
  let sellerInfoRepository: Repository<SellerInfo>;
  let userRepository: Repository<User>;

  let testUser: User;
  let testSeller: SellerInfo;
  let testProduct: Product;
  let testSellerProduct: SellerProduct;
  let testCategory: Category;
  let testProductInfo: ProductInfo;
  let testColor: Color;

  const createTestData = async () => {
    // Create test user
    const uniqueSuffix = Date.now().toString();
    const email = `test+${uniqueSuffix}@example.com`;
    testUser = userRepository.create({
      email,
      username: `testuser_${uniqueSuffix}`,
      name: 'Test',
      lastName: 'User',
      password: 'hashedpassword', // In real scenario, use bcrypt
      role: UserRole.CUSTOMER,
    });
    testUser = await userRepository.save(testUser);

    // Create test seller
    testSeller = sellerInfoRepository.create({
      storeName: 'Test Store',
      phone: '1234567890',
      address: 'Test Address',
      shippingFee: 10,
      user: testUser,
    });
    testSeller = await sellerInfoRepository.save(testSeller);

    // Create test category
    const categoryRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(Category));
    testCategory = await categoryRepo.save({
      slug: `test-category-${uniqueSuffix}`,
      sortOrder: 0,
      isActive: true,
      name: `Test Category ${uniqueSuffix}`, // Virtual field for GraphQL, but we need slug for DB
    });

    // Create test color
    const colorRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(Color));
    testColor = await colorRepo.save({
      hexCode: `#${uniqueSuffix.slice(-6)}`, // Use unique suffix for hex code to avoid conflicts
      sortOrder: 0,
      isActive: true,
    });

    // Create test product first
    const productRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(Product));
    testProduct = await productRepo.save({
      name: `Test Product ${uniqueSuffix}`,
      description: 'Test Description',
    });

    // Create test product info with size and colors
    const productInfoRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(ProductInfo));
    testProductInfo = await productInfoRepo.save({
      size: 'M',
      weight: 100,
      product: testProduct,
    });
    const productInfoColorRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(ProductInfoColor));
    await productInfoColorRepo.save({
      productInfo: testProductInfo,
      color: testColor,
      position: 0,
    });

    // Create test seller product with stock
    testSellerProduct = sellerProductRepository.create({
      seller: testSeller,
      product: testProduct,
      productInfo: testProductInfo,
      productInfoId: testProductInfo.id,
      price: 50,
      stockQuantity: 100,
      discount: 0,
      costPrice: 30,
    });
    testSellerProduct = await sellerProductRepository.save(testSellerProduct);
  };

  const cleanupTestData = async () => {
    try {
      // Get repositories
      const orderRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(Order));
      const orderItemRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(OrderItem));
      const productInfoRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(ProductInfo));
      const productRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(Product));
      const categoryRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(Category));

      // Delete orders created by test user (by email pattern) or referencing test seller
      if (testUser?.email || testSeller?.id) {
        // Delete order items first (foreign key constraint)
        const ordersToDelete = await orderRepo.find({
          where: testSeller?.id
            ? { sellerId: testSeller.id }
            : testUser?.email
            ? { customerEmail: testUser.email }
            : {},
        });
        for (const order of ordersToDelete) {
          await orderItemRepo.delete({ orderId: order.id });
          await orderRepo.delete({ id: order.id });
        }
      }

      // Delete test seller product
      if (testSellerProduct?.id) {
        await sellerProductRepository.delete({ id: testSellerProduct.id });
      }

      // Delete test product info
      if (testProductInfo?.id) {
        await productInfoRepo.delete({ id: testProductInfo.id });
      }

      // Delete test product
      if (testProduct?.id) {
        await productRepo.delete({ id: testProduct.id });
      }

      // Delete test category
      if (testCategory?.id) {
        await categoryRepo.delete({ id: testCategory.id });
      }

      // Delete test color
      if (testColor?.id) {
        const colorRepo = app.get(getRepositoryToken(Color));
        await colorRepo.delete({ id: testColor.id });
      }

      // Delete test seller
      if (testSeller?.id) {
        await sellerInfoRepository.delete({ id: testSeller.id });
      }

      // Delete test user (last, as it may be referenced by seller)
      if (testUser?.id) {
        await userRepository.delete({ id: testUser.id });
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Error cleaning up test data:', error);
      // Don't throw - cleanup failures shouldn't fail tests
    }
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    // For e2e tests, we'll use a simplified setup that tests service integration
    // In a real scenario, you might use a test database or Docker container
    const moduleFixture: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({
      imports: [
        ConfigModule.forRoot({
          isGlobal: true,
        }),
        // Reuse the same Postgres configuration as the main app so enums & schema match
        TypeOrmModule.forRoot({
          type: 'postgres',
          host: process.env.DB_HOST,
          port: 5432,
          username: process.env.DB_USERNAME,
          password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD,
          database: process.env.DB_NAME,
          entities: [
            Order,
            OrderItem,
            SellerProduct,
            SellerInfo,
            User,
            Cart,
            CartItem,
            Product,
            ProductInfo,
            Category,
            Translation,
            PlatformSettings,
            Color,
            ProductInfoColor,
          ],
          synchronize: true,
          logging: false, // Disable logging for cleaner test output
        }),
        OrderModule,
      ],
    })
      .overrideProvider(PaymentService)
      .useValue(mockPaymentService)
      .compile();

    app = moduleFixture.createNestApplication();
    await app.init();

    // Get repositories and services
    orderService = moduleFixture.get<OrderService>(OrderService);
    sellerProductRepository = moduleFixture.get(
      getRepositoryToken(SellerProduct)
    );
    sellerInfoRepository = moduleFixture.get(getRepositoryToken(SellerInfo));
    userRepository = moduleFixture.get(getRepositoryToken(User));

    // Create test data
    await createTestData();
  });

  afterAll(async () => {
    // Clean up all test data before closing app
    await cleanupTestData();
    if (app) {
      await app.close();
    }
  });

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  describe('Complete Checkout Flow - Successful Payment', () => {
    it('should complete full checkout: create order â†’ request payment â†’ verify payment â†’ confirm order and decrease stock', async () => {
      const productBefore = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      const initialStock = productBefore?.stockQuantity ?? 0;

      // Step 1: Create Order
      const createOrderInput = {
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: 2,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 100,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      };

      const order = await orderService.createOrder(createOrderInput);

      // Verify order created with PENDING status
      expect(order.id).toBeDefined();
      expect(order.status).toBe(OrderStatus.PENDING);
      expect(order.orderNumber).toBeDefined();
      expect(order.trackingNumber).toBeDefined();

      // Verify stock was NOT decreased on order creation
      const productAfterOrderCreation = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      expect(productAfterOrderCreation?.stockQuantity).toBe(initialStock);

      // Step 2: Request Payment
      const authority = 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000';
      mockPaymentService.requestPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Success',
          authority,
          fee_type: 'Merchant',
          fee: 0,
        },
        errors: [],
      });

      // In real scenario, this would be a GraphQL mutation
      // For e2e, we'll test the service directly
      const paymentRequest = await mockPaymentService.requestPayment({
        amount: order.total,
        callback_url: 'https://example.com/payment/callback',
        description: `Order ${order.orderNumber}`,
        currency: 'IRT',
        metadata: {
          order_id: order.id,
        },
      });

      expect(paymentRequest.data.code).toBe(100);
      expect(paymentRequest.data.authority).toBe(authority);

      // Step 3: Verify Payment (Successful)
      mockPaymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Payment verified',
          ref_id: 123456789,
          card_pan: '1234-****-****-5678',
        },
        errors: [],
      });

      const verificationResult = await mockPaymentService.verifyPayment({
        amount: order.total,
        authority,
      });

      expect(verificationResult.data.code).toBe(100);

      // Step 4: Confirm Order Payment (this happens in PaymentResolver, but we'll test directly)
      const confirmedOrder = await orderService.confirmOrderPayment(order.id);

      // Verify order status changed to CONFIRMED
      expect(confirmedOrder.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);

      // Verify stock was decreased AFTER payment confirmation
      const productAfterConfirmation = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      const finalStock = productAfterConfirmation?.stockQuantity ?? 0;
      // Stock must decrease after successful payment
      expect(finalStock).toBeLessThan(initialStock);
    });
  });

  describe('Checkout Flow - Failed Payment', () => {
    it('should handle failed payment: create order â†’ request payment â†’ verify payment fails â†’ cancel order (stock unchanged)', async () => {
      const productBefore = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      const initialStock = productBefore?.stockQuantity ?? 0;

      // Step 1: Create Order
      const createOrderInput = {
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: 3,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 150,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      };

      const order = await orderService.createOrder(createOrderInput);

      // Verify stock was NOT decreased
      const productAfterOrderCreation = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      expect(productAfterOrderCreation?.stockQuantity).toBe(initialStock);

      // Step 2: Request Payment (successful)
      const authority = 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000';
      mockPaymentService.requestPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Success',
          authority,
        },
        errors: [],
      });

      await mockPaymentService.requestPayment({
        amount: order.total,
        callback_url: 'https://example.com/payment/callback',
        description: `Order ${order.orderNumber}`,
        currency: 'IRT',
        metadata: {
          order_id: order.id,
        },
      });

      // Step 3: Verify Payment (FAILS)
      mockPaymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: -11,
          message: 'Payment not found',
        },
        errors: [],
      });

      const verificationResult = await mockPaymentService.verifyPayment({
        amount: order.total,
        authority,
      });

      expect(verificationResult.data.code).toBe(-11); // Payment failed

      // Step 4: Cancel Order (this happens in PaymentResolver when payment fails)
      const cancelledOrder = await orderService.cancelPendingOrder(order.id);

      // Verify order status changed to CANCELLED
      expect(cancelledOrder.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);

      // Verify stock was NOT decreased (order was never confirmed)
      const productAfterCancellation = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      expect(productAfterCancellation?.stockQuantity).toBe(initialStock);
    });
  });

  describe('Stock Management', () => {
    it('should prevent stock decrease on order creation', async () => {
      const productBefore = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      const initialStock = productBefore?.stockQuantity ?? 0;
      const orderQuantity = 5;

      const order = await orderService.createOrder({
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: orderQuantity,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50 * orderQuantity,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      });

      // Verify stock unchanged
      const productAfterOrder = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      expect(productAfterOrder?.stockQuantity).toBe(initialStock);
      expect(order.status).toBe(OrderStatus.PENDING);
    });

    it('should decrease stock only when payment is confirmed', async () => {
      const productBefore = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      const initialStock = productBefore?.stockQuantity ?? 0;
      const orderQuantity = 3;

      // Create order
      const order = await orderService.createOrder({
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: orderQuantity,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50 * orderQuantity,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      });

      // Stock should still be unchanged
      let product = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      expect(product?.stockQuantity).toBe(initialStock);

      // Confirm payment
      await orderService.confirmOrderPayment(order.id);

      // Now stock should be decreased
      product = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      const finalStock = product?.stockQuantity ?? 0;
      // Stock must decrease after payment confirmation
      expect(finalStock).toBeLessThan(initialStock);
    });

    it('should restore stock when confirmed order is cancelled', async () => {
      const productBefore = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      const initialStock = productBefore?.stockQuantity ?? 0;
      const orderQuantity = 4;

      // Create and confirm order
      const order = await orderService.createOrder({
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: orderQuantity,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50 * orderQuantity,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      });

      // Confirm payment (decreases stock)
      await orderService.confirmOrderPayment(order.id);

      let product = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      let currentStock = product?.stockQuantity ?? 0;
      // Stock must decrease after confirmation
      expect(currentStock).toBeLessThan(initialStock);

      // Cancel order (should restore stock)
      await orderService.cancelPendingOrder(order.id);

      product = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      currentStock = product?.stockQuantity ?? 0;
      // After cancellation, stock should be restored back towards initial value
      expect(currentStock).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(initialStock - orderQuantity);
      expect(currentStock).toBeLessThanOrEqual(initialStock);
    });
  });

  describe('Order Status Transitions', () => {
    it('should create order with PENDING status', async () => {
      const order = await orderService.createOrder({
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: 1,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      });

      expect(order.status).toBe(OrderStatus.PENDING);
    });

    it('should transition order from PENDING to CONFIRMED on payment confirmation', async () => {
      const order = await orderService.createOrder({
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: 1,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      });

      expect(order.status).toBe(OrderStatus.PENDING);

      const confirmedOrder = await orderService.confirmOrderPayment(order.id);
      expect(confirmedOrder.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
    });

    it('should transition order from PENDING to CANCELLED on payment failure', async () => {
      const order = await orderService.createOrder({
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: 1,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      });

      expect(order.status).toBe(OrderStatus.PENDING);

      const cancelledOrder = await orderService.cancelPendingOrder(order.id);
      expect(cancelledOrder.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
    });

    it('should prevent confirming an already confirmed order', async () => {
      const order = await orderService.createOrder({
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: 1,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      });

      // Confirm once
      await orderService.confirmOrderPayment(order.id);

      // Try to confirm again - should return same order without error
      const result = await orderService.confirmOrderPayment(order.id);
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
    });

    it('should cancel a pending order and leave stock unchanged', async () => {
      const productBefore = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      const initialStock = productBefore?.stockQuantity ?? 0;

      const order = await orderService.createOrder({
        customerEmail: testUser.email,
        customerPhone: '1234567890',
        customerName: `${testUser.name} ${testUser.lastName}`,
        shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
        city: 'Test City',
        state: 'Test State',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'Test Country',
        sellerId: testSeller.id,
        items: [
          {
            productId: testProduct.id.toString(),
            sellerProductId: testSellerProduct.id,
            productName: 'Test Product',
            quantity: 1,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50,
          },
        ],
        shippingCost: 10,
      });

      expect(order.status).toBe(OrderStatus.PENDING);

      const cancelled = await orderService.cancelPendingOrder(order.id);
      expect(cancelled.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);

      const productAfter = await sellerProductRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: testSellerProduct.id },
      });
      expect(productAfter?.stockQuantity).toBe(initialStock);
    });
  });
});

===== FILE: .\libs\client\customers\hooks\orders\src\lib\customers-orders-hook.tsx =====
import { useQuery } from '@apollo/client/react';
import { GET_MY_ORDERS_QUERY } from '@kickzone-workspace/customers-graphql';

// Define Order type based on GraphQL schema
interface Order {
  id: string;
  orderNumber: string;
  customerEmail: string;
  customerPhone: string;
  customerName: string;
  shippingAddress: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  zipCode: string;
  country: string;
  status: string;
  subtotal: number;
  shippingCost: number;
  total: number;
  notes?: string;
  trackingNumber?: string;
  createdAt: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  shippingMethod?: string;
  shippingPaymentMode?: 'PAY_ON_DELIVERY' | 'PAY_ONLINE';
  items: OrderItem[];
}

interface OrderItem {
  id: string;
  productId: string;
  productName: string;
  productImage?: string;
  quantity: number;
  unitPrice: number;
  totalPrice: number;
  color?: string;
  size?: string;
}

interface GetMyOrdersData {
  myOrders: Order[];
}

interface GetMyOrdersVariables {
  first?: number;
  after?: string;
}

export function useCustomersOrders({
  first = 50,
  after,
}: {
  first?: number;
  after?: string;
} = {}) {
  const { data, loading, error, refetch, fetchMore } = useQuery<
    GetMyOrdersData,
    GetMyOrdersVariables
  >(GET_MY_ORDERS_QUERY, {
    variables: { first, after },
    fetchPolicy: 'cache-and-network',
  });

  const loadMore = () => {
    if (data?.myOrders && data.myOrders.length > 0) {
      const lastOrder = data.myOrders[data.myOrders.length - 1];
      return fetchMore({
        variables: {
          after: lastOrder.id,
        },
      });
    }
  };

  return {
    orders: data?.myOrders || [],
    loading,
    error,
    refetch,
    loadMore,
  };
}

===== FILE: .\libs\client\customers\screens\customer-payment\src\lib\customer-payment-callback.tsx =====
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import { Link as RouterLink, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import Box from '@mui/material/Box';
import Typography from '@mui/material/Typography';
import CircularProgress from '@mui/material/CircularProgress';
import Alert from '@mui/material/Alert';
import Button from '@mui/material/Button';
import CheckCircle from '@mui/icons-material/CheckCircle';
import ErrorOutline from '@mui/icons-material/ErrorOutline';
import { useTheme } from '@mui/material/styles';
import { CustomersLayout } from '@kickzone-workspace/customers-layout';
import { GlassmorphismContainer } from '@kickzone-workspace/glassmorphism-container';
import { CUSTOMER_ROUTES } from '@kickzone-workspace/customers-routes';
import { useZarinPalPayment } from './use-zarinpal-payment';
import { useCustomersCartMutations } from '@kickzone-workspace/customers-cart-hook';
import { useCartStore } from '@kickzone-workspace/customer-cart-store';

/**
 * Payment Callback Screen
 * Handles the return from ZarinPal payment gateway
 * Verifies the payment and redirects accordingly
 *
 * Per ZarinPal docs: only call verify when Status=OK.
 * Status=NOK means the buyer cancelled or the transaction failed.
 */
export function CustomerPaymentCallback() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const [verificationStatus, setVerificationStatus] = useState<
    'pending' | 'success' | 'error' | 'cancelled'
  >('pending');
  const [refId, setRefId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Get payment parameters from URL
  const authority = searchParams.get('Authority');
  const status = searchParams.get('Status');
  const orderIdFromUrl = searchParams.get('orderId');
  const orderIdsFromUrl = searchParams.get('orderIds');

  // Get order data from sessionStorage
  const [orderData, setOrderData] = useState<{
    orderId: string;
    orderIds?: string[];
    orderNumber: string;
    amount: number;
    orderConfirmation?: {
      id: string;
      orderNumber: string;
      trackingNumber: string;
      customerEmail: string;
      customerPhone: string;
      customerName: string;
      shippingAddress: string;
      city: string;
      state: string;
      zipCode: string;
      country: string;
      subtotal: number;
      shippingCost: number;
      total: number;
      items: Array<{
        productId: string;
        productName: string;
        productImage?: string | null;
        quantity: number;
        unitPrice: number;
        totalPrice: number;
        color?: string | null;
        size?: string | null;
      }>;
      notes?: string | null;
      createdAt: string;
      sellerOrders?: Array<{
        id: string;
        orderNumber: string;
        total: number;
        trackingNumber?: string | null;
      }>;
    } | null;
  } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (orderIdFromUrl) {
      const stored = sessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderIdFromUrl}`);
      if (stored) {
        try {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
          setOrderData(parsed);
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('Failed to parse order data:', e);
        }
      } else {
        // Fallback: construct orderData from URL parameters when sessionStorage is unavailable
        // (e.g., mobile banking redirect in fresh tab, or only orderIds/Authority present)
        const parsedOrderIds = orderIdsFromUrl
          ? orderIdsFromUrl.split(',').map((id) => id.trim()).filter(Boolean)
          : orderIdFromUrl
            ? [orderIdFromUrl]
            : [];
        const primaryOrderId = orderIdFromUrl || parsedOrderIds[0] || '';
        setOrderData({
          orderId: primaryOrderId,
          orderIds: parsedOrderIds.length > 0 ? parsedOrderIds : undefined,
          orderNumber: '',
          amount: 0,
        });
      }
    }
  }, [orderIdFromUrl, orderIdsFromUrl, authority]);

  // Track if verification has already been attempted to prevent multiple calls
  const verificationAttemptedRef = useRef(false);
  const cartClearedRef = useRef(false);

  // Get cart mutations to clear cart after successful payment
  const { clearCart } = useCustomersCartMutations();
  const clearCartAction = useCartStore((state) => state.clearCart);

  const { verifyPayment, cancelPendingOrder: cancelPendingOrderHook } = useZarinPalPayment({
    onSuccess: async (refId) => {
      // Note: This callback is invoked when the GraphQL mutation succeeds.
      // The actual success/failure is determined by the `success` field in the response.
      // We set a preliminary refId and status here; the component will verify `result.success`
      // after `verifyPayment` returns and update accordingly.
      setRefId(refId);
      setVerificationStatus('success');
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      setErrorMessage(error);
      setVerificationStatus('error');
      // Cart is NOT cleared on error - user can retry payment with same items
    },
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    // Prevent multiple verification attempts
    if (verificationAttemptedRef.current) {
      return;
    }

    // Check if payment was cancelled â€” do NOT call verify (ZarinPal docs)
    if (status === 'NOK') {
      verificationAttemptedRef.current = true;
      setVerificationStatus('cancelled');
      const orderIdToCancel = orderIdFromUrl || orderData?.orderId;
      const orderIdsToCancel = orderData?.orderIds || (orderIdToCancel ? [orderIdToCancel] : []);
      // Per ZarinPal docs, when Status=NOK, the Authority param is still present
      // Pass authority so guest users without sessionStorage orderId can still cancel
      if (authority || orderIdToCancel) {
        cancelPendingOrderHook({ orderId: orderIdToCancel ?? null, authority: authority! })
          .then((success) => {
            if (!success) {
              console.error('Failed to cancel pending order after NOK - mutation returned false');
            }
            // Clean up sessionStorage for all order IDs after successful cancellation
            for (const oid of orderIdsToCancel) {
              sessionStorage.removeItem(`order_${oid}`);
            }
            if (orderIdFromUrl) {
              sessionStorage.removeItem(`order_${orderIdFromUrl}`);
            }
          })
          .catch((err) => {
            console.error('Failed to cancel pending order after NOK:', err);
            // Don't clear sessionStorage on failure so user can retry
          });
      }
      return;
    }

    // Validate required parameters
    if (!authority || status !== 'OK') {
      setErrorMessage('ظ¾ط§ط±ط§ظ…طھط±ظ‡ط§غŒ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط§ط³طھ');
      setVerificationStatus('error');
      verificationAttemptedRef.current = true;
      return;
    }

    // Wait for order data to be loaded
    if (!orderData) {
      return;
    }

    // Mark verification as attempted immediately to prevent race conditions
    verificationAttemptedRef.current = true;

    // Verify payment
    const verify = async () => {
      try {
        const result = await verifyPayment({
          authority,
          amount: orderData.amount,
          order_id: orderData.orderId || undefined, // Pass order_id to confirm order and decrease stock; avoid empty string
        });

        if (!result) {
          // Error already handled by hook
          return;
        }

        // Normalize result for backward compatibility with any test mocks returning a raw number
        const normalizedResult =
          typeof result === 'number'
            ? { success: true, ref_id: result, message: '' }
            : result;

        // CRITICAL: Check the `success` field from the resolver response.
        // Only clear cart and show success when `success === true`.
        // If `success === false` (e.g., gateway captured payment but stock reservation failed),
        // do NOT clear cart, show error state, and display the refId for support/refund.
        if (normalizedResult.success === true) {
          // Clear cart only once after successful payment verification AND order confirmation
          if (!cartClearedRef.current) {
            cartClearedRef.current = true;
            try {
              // Try to clear server cart (requires auth). If it fails, log but don't block.
              await clearCart();
            } catch (error) {
              // Log error but don't fail - payment was successful
              console.error('Failed to clear server cart after payment:', error);
            }
            // Always clear local cart (guest or logged in) after successful payment
            clearCartAction();
          }

          // Clean up sessionStorage for all order IDs after successful payment
          if (orderData?.orderIds) {
            for (const oid of orderData.orderIds) {
              sessionStorage.removeItem(`order_${oid}`);
            }
          }
          if (orderIdFromUrl) {
            sessionStorage.removeItem(`order_${orderIdFromUrl}`);
          }

          // onSuccess callback already set status to 'success' and refId
        } else {
          // Payment was captured by gateway (refId exists) but order confirmation failed
          // Do NOT clear cart. Show error with refId for customer support.
          const errorMsg =
            normalizedResult.message ||
            `Payment was captured by gateway (Ref: ${normalizedResult.ref_id}), but order confirmation failed. Please contact support with this reference ID.`;
          setErrorMessage(errorMsg);
          setVerificationStatus('error');
          // Ensure refId is set for display
          if (normalizedResult.ref_id) {
            setRefId(normalizedResult.ref_id);
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        // Error already handled by hook's onError callback
        console.error('Payment verification error:', err);
      }
    };

    verify();
  }, [
    authority,
    status,
    orderData,
    orderIdFromUrl,
    verifyPayment,
    cancelPendingOrderHook,
  ]);

  const handleContinue = () => {
    if (verificationStatus === 'success' && orderData) {
      // Check if orderConfirmation data is available
      const orderConfirmation = orderData.orderConfirmation;
      if (orderConfirmation) {
        // Navigate to order confirmation with full order data
        navigate(CUSTOMER_ROUTES.ORDER_CONFIRMATION, {
          state: { orderId: orderData.orderId, refId, orderData: orderConfirmation },
        });
      } else {
        // No orderConfirmation data (fresh tab / URL fallback) - navigate to orders page
        navigate(CUSTOMER_ROUTES.ORDERS);
      }
    } else {
      // Navigate to orders page
      navigate(CUSTOMER_ROUTES.ORDERS);
    }
  };

  const theme = useTheme();
  const isDark = theme.palette.mode === 'dark';

  return (
    <CustomersLayout>
      <Box
        sx={{
          position: 'relative',
          minHeight: 'calc(100vh - 120px)',
          overflow: 'hidden',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          justifyContent: 'center',
          py: { xs: 6, md: 10 },
          px: 2,
        }}
      >
        {/* Ambient liquid lighting glow orbs */}
        <Box
          sx={{
            position: 'absolute',
            top: '10%',
            right: '15%',
            width: { xs: 260, sm: 420 },
            height: { xs: 260, sm: 420 },
            borderRadius: '50%',
            background: isDark
              ? 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(197, 184, 154, 0.15) 0%, rgba(197, 184, 154, 0.02) 65%, transparent 70%)'
              : 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(230, 215, 185, 0.5) 0%, rgba(240, 230, 205, 0.18) 60%, transparent 70%)',
            filter: 'blur(75px)',
            pointerEvents: 'none',
            zIndex: 0,
            animation: 'floatOrb 14s ease-in-out infinite alternate',
            '@keyframes floatOrb': {
              '0%': { transform: 'translate(0, 0) scale(1)' },
              '100%': { transform: 'translate(-30px, 35px) scale(1.08)' },
            },
          }}
        />
        <Box
          sx={{
            position: 'absolute',

            width: { xs: 240, sm: 380 },
            height: { xs: 240, sm: 380 },
            borderRadius: '50%',
            background: isDark
              ? 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(140, 120, 80, 0.12) 0%, rgba(100, 85, 55, 0.02) 65%, transparent 70%)'
              : 'radial-gradient(circle, rgba(245, 230, 215, 0.45) 0%, rgba(240, 220, 200, 0.15) 60%, transparent 70%)',
            filter: 'blur(80px)',
            pointerEvents: 'none',
            zIndex: 0,
            animation: 'floatOrb2 16s ease-in-out infinite alternate',
            '@keyframes floatOrb2': {
              '0%': { transform: 'translate(0, 0) scale(1)' },
              '100%': { transform: 'translate(35px, -25px) scale(1.06)' },
            },
          }}
        />

        <GlassmorphismContainer
          wrapperSx={{
            width: '100%',
            maxWidth: 580,
            mx: 'auto',
            position: 'relative',
            zIndex: 1,
            p: 0,
          }}

import React from 'react';
import { render, screen, waitFor, act, fireEvent } from '@testing-library/react';

// Mock theme for MUI components

  palette: {
    mode: 'light',

};
