// Mock sessionStorage
const mockSessionStorage = (() => {
  let store: Record<string, string> = {};
  return {
    getItem: jest.fn((key: string) => store[key] || null),
    setItem: jest.fn((key: string, value: string) => { store[key] = value; }),
    removeItem: jest.fn((key: string) => { delete store[key]; }),
    clear: jest.fn(() => { store = {}; }),
  };
})();

Object.defineProperty(window, 'sessionStorage', {
  value: mockSessionStorage,
  writable: true,
});

// Mock the cart hooks
jest.mock('@kickzone-workspace/customers-cart-hook', () => ({
  useCustomersCartMutations: () => ({
    clearCart: mockClearCartMutation,
  }),
}));

jest.mock('@kickzone-workspace/customer-cart-store', () => ({
  useCartStore: (selector: (state: { clearCart: () => void }) => { clearCart: () => void }) => selector({
    clearCart: mockClearCartAction,
  }),
}));

// Wrapper for React Router, Apollo Provider, and MUI Theme
const renderWithProviders = (ui: React.ReactElement, mocks: MockedResponse[] = []) => {
  return render(
    <MockedProvider mocks={mocks} addTypename={false}>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <ThemeProvider theme={mockTheme}>
          <CssBaseline />
          {ui}
        </ThemeProvider>
      </BrowserRouter>
    </MockedProvider>
  );
};

describe('CustomerPaymentCallback - Guest ZarinPal Cancel', () => {
  const authority = 'A00000123';
  const orderId = 'order-123';
  const orderNumber = 'ORD-123456';

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockSessionStorage.clear();
  });

  const setupOrderInSessionStorage = () => {
    mockSessionStorage.setItem(
      `order_${orderId}`,
      JSON.stringify({ orderId, orderNumber, amount: 50000 })
    );
  };

  describe('Status=NOK with authority (Guest user)', () => {
    it('should call cancelPendingZarinPalOrder with authority when orderId exists in sessionStorage', async () => {
      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: CANCEL_PENDING_ZARINPAL_ORDER_MUTATION,
            variables: { orderId, authority, guestToken: undefined },
          },
          result: { data: { cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true } },
        },
      ];

      const { waitFor: waitForRender } = renderWithProviders(
        <CustomerPaymentCallback />,
        mocks
      );

      // Simulate URL with Status=NOK and Authority
      await act(async () => {
        window.history.pushState({}, '', `/?Status=NOK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);
        window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(mocks[0].result?.data).toEqual({ cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true });
      });
    });

    it('should call cancelPendingZarinPalOrder with authority only when orderId is NOT in sessionStorage (new tab scenario)', async () => {
      // No sessionStorage setup - simulates new browser tab

      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: CANCEL_PENDING_ZARINPAL_ORDER_MUTATION,
            variables: { orderId: null, authority, guestToken: undefined },
          },
          result: { data: { cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true } },
        },
      ];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await act(async () => {
        window.history.pushState({}, '', `/?Status=NOK&Authority=${authority}`);
        window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(mocks[0].result?.data).toEqual({ cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true });
      });
    });

    it('should NOT clear sessionStorage if cancellation fails', async () => {
      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: CANCEL_PENDING_ZARINPAL_ORDER_MUTATION,
            variables: { orderId, authority, guestToken: undefined },
          },
          error: new Error('Network error'),
        },
      ];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await act(async () => {
        window.history.pushState({}, '', `/?Status=NOK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);
        window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // sessionStorage should still have the order data
      expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).not.toBeNull();
    });

    it('should clear sessionStorage after successful cancellation', async () => {
      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: CANCEL_PENDING_ZARINPAL_ORDER_MUTATION,
            variables: { orderId, authority, guestToken: undefined },
          },
          result: { data: { cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true } },
        },
      ];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await act(async () => {
        window.history.pushState({}, '', `/?Status=NOK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);
        window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // Wait for mutation to complete and sessionStorage to be cleared
      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).toBeNull();
      });
    });
  });

  describe('Status=NOK without authority (edge case)', () => {
    it('should show cancelled status but not call mutation if neither authority nor orderId available', async () => {
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await act(async () => {
        window.history.pushState({}, '', '/?Status=NOK');
        window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // No mutation should be called
      expect(mocks.length).toBe(0);
    });
  });

  describe('Status=OK (should verify, not cancel)', () => {
    it('should NOT call cancel mutation when Status=OK', async () => {
      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        // verifyZarinPalPayment would be called, not cancel
        {
          request: {
            query: expect.anything(), // verify mutation
            variables: expect.objectContaining({ input: expect.objectContaining({ authority }) }),
          },
          result: { data: { verifyZarinPalPayment: { code: 100, message: 'Success', ref_id: 12345, success: true } } },
        },
      ];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await act(async () => {
        window.history.pushState({}, '', `/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);
        window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
      });

      // Should show loading/verifying state, not cancelled
      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ط¯ط± ط­ط§ظ„ طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ...')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });
    });
  });
});

describe('CustomerPaymentCallback - ZarinPal Verification (Fail-Closed Heavy Tests)', () => {
  const authority = 'A00000123';
  const orderId = 'ord-1';
  const orderNumber = 'ORD-123456';
  const orderIds = ['ord-1', 'ord-2'];

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockSessionStorage.clear();
  });

  const setupOrderInSessionStorage = (customOrderId = orderId, customAmount = 50000) => {
    mockSessionStorage.setItem(
      `order_${customOrderId}`,
      JSON.stringify({ orderId: customOrderId, orderNumber, amount: customAmount, orderIds })
    );
  };

  const triggerVerification = async (url: string) => {
    await act(async () => {
      window.history.pushState({}, '', url);
      window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
    });
  };

  describe('Case 1: Happy path - success: true, code: 100', () => {
    it('should clear cart, remove sessionStorage keys, and render success UI with refId', async () => {
      const refId = 777888;
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount: 50000, order_id: orderId }),
            }),
          },
          result: {
            data: {
              verifyZarinPalPayment: {
                code: 100,
                message: 'Verified',
                ref_id: refId,
                success: true,
                orderIds,
                orderId,
                orderNumber,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      ];

      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}&orderIds=${orderIds.join(',')}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText(`ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´: ${refId}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ط³ظپط§ط±ط´ ط´ظ…ط§ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط«ط¨طھ ط´ط¯ ظˆ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ع¯ط±ط¯غŒط¯.')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // sessionStorage should be cleared for all orderIds
      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderIds[0]}`)).toBeNull();
        expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderIds[1]}`)).toBeNull();
      });
    });
  });

  describe('Case 2: Fail-closed - success: false WITH ref_id', () => {
    it('should NOT clear cart, show error UI, display server message, and display refId for support', async () => {
      const refId = 555666;
      const serverMessage = 'Payment was captured by gateway (Ref: 555666), but order confirmation failed: Insufficient stock';
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount: 50000, order_id: orderId }),
            }),
          },
          result: {
            data: {
              verifyZarinPalPayment: {
                code: 100,
                message: serverMessage,
                ref_id: refId,
                success: false,
                orderIds: [orderId],
                orderId,
                orderNumber,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      ];

      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText(serverMessage)).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText(`ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ ظ…ط±ط¬ط¹ طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´: ${refId}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ط§غŒظ† ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ ط±ط§ ط¨ط±ط§غŒ ظ¾غŒع¯غŒط±غŒ ظˆ ط§ط³طھط±ط¯ط§ط¯ ظˆط¬ظ‡ ط¨ظ‡ ظ¾ط´طھغŒط¨ط§ظ†غŒ ط§ط±ط§ط¦ظ‡ ط¯ظ‡غŒط¯.')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // Cart should NOT be cleared - sessionStorage should remain
      expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).not.toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('Case 3: Fail-closed - success: false WITHOUT ref_id', () => {
    it('should NOT clear cart and show error state', async () => {
      const serverMessage = 'Order confirmation failed';
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount: 50000, order_id: orderId }),
            }),
          },
          result: {
            data: {
              verifyZarinPalPayment: {
                code: 100,
                message: serverMessage,
                ref_id: null,
                success: false,
                orderIds: [orderId],
                orderId,
                orderNumber,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      ];

      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText(serverMessage)).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // Cart should NOT be cleared
      expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).not.toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('Case 4: Legacy numeric return fallback', () => {
    it('should treat numeric return as success and clear cart', async () => {
      const legacyRefId = 123456;
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount: 50000, order_id: orderId }),
            }),
          },
          // Legacy mock returns a raw number (simulating old API shape)
          result: {
            data: {
              verifyZarinPalPayment: legacyRefId,
            },
          },
        },
      ];

      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText(`ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´: ${legacyRefId}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // sessionStorage should be cleared
      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).toBeNull();
      });
    });
  });

  describe('Case 5: Idempotent code: 101, success: true', () => {
    it('should clear cart once and show success state', async () => {
      const refId = 999111;
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount: 50000, order_id: orderId }),
            }),
          },
          result: {
            data: {
              verifyZarinPalPayment: {
                code: 101,
                message: 'Already verified',
                ref_id: refId,
                success: true,
                orderIds: [orderId],
                orderId,
                orderNumber,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      ];

      setupOrderInSessionStorage();

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText(`ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´: ${refId}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // sessionStorage should be cleared
      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).toBeNull();
      });
    });
  });
});

describe('CustomerPaymentCallback - SessionStorage & URL Fallback', () => {
  const authority = 'A000000000000000000000000000000000000123';
  const orderId = 'ord-1';
  const orderId2 = 'ord-2';
  const orderIds = [orderId, orderId2];
  const orderNumber = 'ORD-123456';
  const amount = 50000;

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockSessionStorage.clear();
  });

  const setupOrderInSessionStorage = (customOrderId = orderId, customAmount = amount, customOrderIds = [orderId]) => {
    mockSessionStorage.setItem(
      `order_${customOrderId}`,
      JSON.stringify({ orderId: customOrderId, orderNumber, amount: customAmount, orderIds: customOrderIds })
    );
  };

  const triggerVerification = async (url: string) => {
    await act(async () => {
      window.history.pushState({}, '', url);
      window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
    });
  };

  describe('Case A: Empty sessionStorage with full URL fallback params', () => {
    it('should call verifyPayment using URL fallback parameters (Authority, Status=OK, orderId, orderIds)', async () => {
      // No sessionStorage setup - empty
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              input: expect.objectContaining({
                authority,
                amount: expect.any(Number),
                order_id: orderId,
              }),
            }),
          },
          result: {
            data: {
              verifyZarinPalPayment: {
                code: 100,
                message: 'Verified',
                ref_id: 777888,
                success: true,
                orderIds,
                orderId,
                orderNumber,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      ];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}&orderIds=${orderIds.join(',')}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´: 777888')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });
    });
  });

  describe('Case B: Empty sessionStorage with minimal URL params (Authority + Status=OK only)', () => {
    it('should call verifyPayment with authority and undefined order_id', async () => {
      // No sessionStorage setup - empty
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              input: expect.objectContaining({
                authority,
                order_id: undefined,
              }),
            }),
          },
          result: {
            data: {
              verifyZarinPalPayment: {
                code: 100,
                message: 'Verified',
                ref_id: 777888,
                success: true,
                orderIds: [orderId],
                orderId,
                orderNumber,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      ];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });
    });
  });

  describe('Case C: sessionStorage preserved when verifyPayment returns success: false', () => {
    it('should NOT remove sessionStorage and NOT clear cart when verifyPayment returns success: false with ref_id', async () => {
      setupOrderInSessionStorage(orderId, amount, orderIds);

      const refId = 555666;
      const serverMessage = 'Confirmation failed';
      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount, order_id: orderId }),
            }),
          },
          result: {
            data: {
              verifyZarinPalPayment: {
                code: 100,
                message: serverMessage,
                ref_id: refId,
                success: false,
                orderIds,
                orderId,
                orderNumber,
              },
            },
          },
        },
      ];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}&orderIds=${orderIds.join(',')}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText(serverMessage)).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText(`ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ ظ…ط±ط¬ط¹ طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´: ${refId}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // sessionStorage should NOT be removed
      expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).not.toBeNull();
      // Cart should NOT be cleared (sessionStorage still exists)
      expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId2}`)).not.toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('Case D: Status=NOK with multi-seller orderIds in sessionStorage', () => {
    it('should call cancelPendingOrder with authority and remove all order_<id> entries after successful cancellation', async () => {
      setupOrderInSessionStorage(orderId, amount, orderIds);

      const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
        {
          request: {
            query: CANCEL_PENDING_ZARINPAL_ORDER_MUTATION,
            variables: expect.objectContaining({
              orderId: orderId, // Uses first orderId from sessionStorage
              authority,
              guestToken: undefined,
            }),
          },
          result: { data: { cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true } },
        },
      ];

      renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

      await triggerVerification(`/?Status=NOK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}&orderIds=${orderIds.join(',')}`);

      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
      });

      // Verify cancellation was called
      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(mocks[0].result?.data).toEqual({ cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true });
      });

      // All order_<id> entries should be removed from sessionStorage
      await waitFor(() => {
        expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).toBeNull();
        expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId2}`)).toBeNull();
      });
    });
  });
});

describe('GuestToken in cancelPendingZarinPalOrder mutation', () => {
  const authority = 'A00000123';
  const orderId = 'order-123';
  const guestToken = 'guest_123456789_abc123';

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockSessionStorage.clear();
  });

  it('should include guestToken in mutation variables when order data contains guestToken (expected behavior)', async () => {
    // Set up order data with guestToken (as stored by customer-shopping-cart.tsx)
    mockSessionStorage.setItem(
      `order_${orderId}`,
      JSON.stringify({
        orderId,
        orderNumber: 'ORD-123456',
        amount: 50000,
        guestToken,
      })
    );

    const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
      {
        request: {
          query: CANCEL_PENDING_ZARINPAL_ORDER_MUTATION,
          variables: { orderId, authority, guestToken },
        },
        result: { data: { cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true } },
      },
    ];

    renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

    await act(async () => {
      window.history.pushState({}, '', `/?Status=NOK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}`);
      window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mocks[0].result?.data).toEqual({ cancelPendingZarinPalOrder: true });
    });
  });
});

describe('Order Confirmation Navigation', () => {
  const authority = 'A00000123';
  const orderId = 'ord-1';
  const orderNumber = 'ORD-123456';
  const orderIds = ['ord-1', 'ord-2'];
  const amount = 50000;

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockSessionStorage.clear();
  });

  const setupOrderWithConfirmation = () => {
    const orderConfirmation = {
      id: orderId,
      orderNumber,
      trackingNumber: 'TRK-123',
      customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
      customerPhone: '09121234567',
      customerName: 'Test User',
      shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
      city: 'Tehran',
      state: 'Tehran',
      zipCode: '1234567890',
      country: 'Iran',
      subtotal: 100,
      shippingCost: 10,
      total: 110,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productName: 'Test Product',
          productImage: '/img.jpg',
          quantity: 1,
          unitPrice: 100,
          totalPrice: 100,
          color: '#121212',
          size: 'L',
        },
      ],
      notes: 'Test notes',
      createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
      sellerOrders: [
        { id: orderId, orderNumber, total: 110, trackingNumber: 'TRK-123' },
      ],
    };
    mockSessionStorage.setItem(
      `order_${orderId}`,
      JSON.stringify({ orderId, orderNumber, amount, orderIds, orderConfirmation })
    );
  };

  const triggerVerification = async (url: string) => {
    await act(async () => {
      window.history.pushState({}, '', url);
      window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
    });
  };

  it('success + stored orderConfirmation -> navigate to ORDER_CONFIRMATION with state.orderData', async () => {
    const refId = 777888;
    const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
      {
        request: {
          query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
          variables: expect.objectContaining({
            input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount, order_id: orderId }),
          }),
        },
        result: {
          data: {
            verifyZarinPalPayment: {
              code: 100,
              message: 'Verified',
              ref_id: refId,
              success: true,
              orderIds,
              orderId,
              orderNumber,
            },
          },
        },
      },
    ];

    setupOrderWithConfirmation();

    const { waitFor: waitForRender } = renderWithProviders(
      <CustomerPaymentCallback />,
      mocks
    );

    await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}&orderIds=${orderIds.join(',')}`);

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText(`ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´: ${refId}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    // Click the continue button
    const continueBtn = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /ظ…ط´ط§ظ‡ط¯ظ‡ ط¬ط²غŒغŒط§طھ ط³ظپط§ط±ط´/i });
    fireEvent.click(continueBtn);

    // Verify navigation to ORDER_CONFIRMATION with orderData in state
    await waitForRender(() => {
      expect(window.location.pathname).toContain('/order-confirmation');
    });
  });

  it('success without orderConfirmation -> navigate to ORDERS', async () => {
    const refId = 777888;
    const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
      {
        request: {
          query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
          variables: expect.objectContaining({
            input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount, order_id: orderId }),
          }),
        },
        result: {
          data: {
            verifyZarinPalPayment: {
              code: 100,
              message: 'Verified',
              ref_id: refId,
              success: true,
              orderIds,
              orderId,
              orderNumber,
            },
          },
        },
      },
    ];

    // Set up order data WITHOUT orderConfirmation (simulating fresh tab / URL fallback)
    mockSessionStorage.setItem(
      `order_${orderId}`,
      JSON.stringify({ orderId, orderNumber, amount, orderIds })
    );

    renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

    await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}&orderIds=${orderIds.join(',')}`);

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    // Click the continue button
    const continueBtn = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /ظ…ط´ط§ظ‡ط¯ظ‡ ط¬ط²غŒغŒط§طھ ط³ظپط§ط±ط´/i });
    fireEvent.click(continueBtn);

    // Verify navigation to ORDERS (not ORDER_CONFIRMATION)
    await waitForRender(() => {
      expect(window.location.pathname).toContain('/orders');
    });
  });
});

describe('Cart Clear Behavior After Successful Payment Verification', () => {
  const authority = 'A00000123';
  const orderId = 'ord-1';
  const orderNumber = 'ORD-123456';
  const orderIds = ['ord-1', 'ord-2'];
  const amount = 50000;

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockSessionStorage.clear();
    mockClearCartMutation.mockResolvedValue({});
    mockClearCartAction.mockClear();
  });

  const setupOrderInSessionStorage = () => {
    mockSessionStorage.setItem(
      `order_${orderId}`,
      JSON.stringify({ orderId, orderNumber, amount, orderIds })
    );
  };

  const triggerVerification = async (url: string) => {
    await act(async () => {
      window.history.pushState({}, '', url);
      window.dispatchEvent(new PopStateEvent('popstate'));
    });
  };

  it('verified success (success === true) where server clearCart rejects -> local cart store clearCart still called once', async () => {
    const refId = 777888;
    const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
      {
        request: {
          query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
          variables: expect.objectContaining({
            input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount, order_id: orderId }),
          }),
        },
        result: {
          data: {
            verifyZarinPalPayment: {
              code: 100,
              message: 'Verified',
              ref_id: refId,
              success: true,
              orderIds,
              orderId,
              orderNumber,
            },
          },
        },
      },
    ];

    setupOrderInSessionStorage();

    // Server clearCart rejects
    mockClearCartMutation.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Network error'));

    renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

    await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}&orderIds=${orderIds.join(',')}`);

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText('ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯')).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText(`ط´ظ…ط§ط±ظ‡ طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´: ${refId}`)).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    // Server clearCart mutation should be called once (and reject)
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockClearCartMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Local clearCart action should still be called once despite server rejection
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockClearCartAction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // sessionStorage should be cleared for all orderIds
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderIds[0]}`)).toBeNull();
      expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderIds[1]}`)).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  it('success === false -> local clearCart NOT called', async () => {
    const refId = 555666;
    const serverMessage = 'Order confirmation failed';
    const mocks: MockedResponse[] = [
      {
        request: {
          query: VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
          variables: expect.objectContaining({
            input: expect.objectContaining({ authority, amount, order_id: orderId }),
          }),
        },
        result: {
          data: {
            verifyZarinPalPayment: {
              code: 100,
              message: serverMessage,
              ref_id: refId,
              success: false,
              orderIds,
              orderId,
              orderNumber,
            },
          },
        },
      },
    ];

    setupOrderInSessionStorage();

    renderWithProviders(<CustomerPaymentCallback />, mocks);

    await triggerVerification(`/?Status=OK&Authority=${authority}&orderId=${orderId}&orderIds=${orderIds.join(',')}`);

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText('ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ')).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByText(serverMessage)).toBeInTheDocument();
    });

    // Server clearCart mutation should NOT be called
    expect(mockClearCartMutation).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    // Local clearCart action should NOT be called
    expect(mockClearCartAction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    // sessionStorage should NOT be cleared
    expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderId}`)).not.toBeNull();
    expect(mockSessionStorage.getItem(`order_${orderIds[1]}`)).not.toBeNull();
  });
});

===== FILE: .\libs\client\customers\screens\customer-payment\src\lib\use-zarinpal-payment.tsx =====
import { useState, useCallback } from 'react';
import { useMutation } from '@apollo/client/react';
import {
  REQUEST_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
  VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION,
  GET_ZARINPAL_GATEWAY_URL_MUTATION,
  CANCEL_PENDING_ZARINPAL_ORDER_MUTATION,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/customers-graphql';
import { KickzoneGraphqlTypes } from '@kickzone-workspace/codegen';
import { asApolloErrorLike } from '@kickzone-workspace/validation';
import {
  getZarinPalErrorMessage,
  type ZarinPalPaymentRequest,
  type ZarinPalVerificationRequest,
  type CancelPendingOrderVariables,
} from './zarinpal-payment-service';

export interface ZarinPalVerifyResult {
  code: number;
  message: string;
  ref_id?: number | null;
  orderIds?: string[] | null;
  orderId?: string | null;
  orderNumber?: string | null;
  success: boolean;
}

export interface ZarinPalVerifyResponseExtended {
  code: number;
  message: string;
  ref_id?: number | null;
  card_pan?: string;
  card_hash?: string;
  fee_type?: string;
  fee?: number;
  orderIds?: string[] | null;
  orderId?: string | null;
  orderNumber?: string | null;
  success?: boolean;
}

export interface UseZarinPalPaymentOptions {
  onSuccess?: (refId: number) => void;
  onError?: (error: string) => void;
}

export interface UseZarinPalPaymentReturn {
  isRequesting: boolean;
  isVerifying: boolean;
  isCancelling: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  requestPayment: (
    request: Omit<ZarinPalPaymentRequest, 'merchant_id'> & {
      orderIds?: string[];
    }
  ) => Promise<string | null>; // Returns authority or null on error
  verifyPayment: (
    request: Omit<ZarinPalVerificationRequest, 'merchant_id'> & {
      order_id?: string;
    }
  ) => Promise<ZarinPalVerifyResult | null>; // Returns verification result or null on error
  cancelPendingOrder: (
    variables: CancelPendingOrderVariables
  ) => Promise<boolean>; // Returns true on success
  redirectToGateway: (authority: string) => void;
  clearError: () => void;
}

/**
 * React hook for ZarinPal payment integration
 */
export function useZarinPalPayment(
  options?: UseZarinPalPaymentOptions
): UseZarinPalPaymentReturn {
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [requestPaymentMutation, { loading: isRequesting }] = useMutation<
    Pick<KickzoneGraphqlTypes.Mutation, 'requestZarinPalPayment'>,
    KickzoneGraphqlTypes.MutationRequestZarinPalPaymentArgs
  >(REQUEST_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION);

  const [verifyPaymentMutation, { loading: isVerifying }] = useMutation<
    Pick<KickzoneGraphqlTypes.Mutation, 'verifyZarinPalPayment'>,
    KickzoneGraphqlTypes.MutationVerifyZarinPalPaymentArgs
  >(VERIFY_ZARINPAL_PAYMENT_MUTATION);

  const [getGatewayUrlMutation] = useMutation<
    Pick<KickzoneGraphqlTypes.Mutation, 'getZarinPalGatewayUrl'>,
    KickzoneGraphqlTypes.MutationGetZarinPalGatewayUrlArgs
  >(GET_ZARINPAL_GATEWAY_URL_MUTATION);

  const [cancelPendingOrderMutation, { loading: isCancelling }] = useMutation<
    Pick<KickzoneGraphqlTypes.Mutation, 'cancelPendingZarinPalOrder'>,
    KickzoneGraphqlTypes.MutationCancelPendingZarinPalOrderArgs
  >(CANCEL_PENDING_ZARINPAL_ORDER_MUTATION);

  const requestPayment = useCallback(
    async (
      request: Omit<ZarinPalPaymentRequest, 'merchant_id'> & {
        orderIds?: string[];
      }
    ): Promise<string | null> => {
      setError(null);

      console.log('[PaymentHook] ًںڑ€ Requesting payment:', {
        amount: request.amount,
        currency: request.currency,
        callbackUrl: request.callback_url,
        orderId: request.metadata?.order_id,
        orderIds: request.orderIds,
      });

      try {
        const { data } = await requestPaymentMutation({
          variables: {
            input: {
              amount: Math.round(request.amount), // Ensure integer for GraphQL Int type
              callback_url: request.callback_url,
              description: request.description,
              currency: request.currency || 'IRT',
              mobile: request.metadata?.mobile,
              email: request.metadata?.email,
              order_id: request.metadata?.order_id,
              orderIds: request.orderIds,
            },
          },
        });

        const response = data?.requestZarinPalPayment;
        if (!response) {
          throw new Error('No response from payment service');
        }

        if (response.code === 100 && response.authority) {
          console.log(
            `[PaymentHook] âœ… Payment request successful: authority=${response.authority.substring(
              0,
              20
            )}...`
          );
          return response.authority;
        } else {
          const errorMessage = getZarinPalErrorMessage(response.code);
          console.error(
            `[PaymentHook] â‌Œ Payment request failed: code=${response.code}, message=${response.message}`
          );
          setError(errorMessage);
          options?.onError?.(errorMessage);
          return null;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        // Extract detailed error message from GraphQL error
        let errorMessage = 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ط¯ط±ط®ظˆط§ط³طھ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ';

        if (err instanceof Error) {
          errorMessage = err.message;

          // Check for GraphQL errors with more details
          const graphQLError = asApolloErrorLike(err)?.graphQLErrors?.[0];
          if (graphQLError) {
            const detailedMessage =
              graphQLError.message ||
              graphQLError.extensions?.message ||
              err.message;
            errorMessage = detailedMessage;

            console.error('[PaymentHook] â‌Œ GraphQL error details:', {
              message: graphQLError.message,
              extensions: graphQLError.extensions,
              path: graphQLError.path,
            });
          }

          // Check for network errors
          const networkError = asApolloErrorLike(err)?.networkError;
          if (networkError) {
            console.error('[PaymentHook] â‌Œ Network error details:', {
              statusCode: networkError.statusCode,
              statusText: networkError.statusText,
              message: networkError.message,
            });

            if (networkError.statusCode === 403) {
              errorMessage =
                'Payment gateway access denied (403). Please check your payment configuration.';
            }
          }
        }

        console.error('[PaymentHook] â‌Œ Payment request error:', {
          error: err,
          errorMessage,
          errorType: err instanceof Error ? err.constructor.name : typeof err,
        });

        setError(errorMessage);
        options?.onError?.(errorMessage);
        return null;
      }
    },
    [options, requestPaymentMutation]
  );

  const verifyPayment = useCallback(
    async (
      request: Omit<ZarinPalVerificationRequest, 'merchant_id'> & {
        order_id?: string;
      }
    ): Promise<ZarinPalVerifyResult | null> => {
      setError(null);

      try {
        const { data } = await verifyPaymentMutation({
          variables: {
            input: {
              amount: Math.round(request.amount), // Ensure integer for GraphQL Int type
              authority: request.authority,
              // order_id is optional and will be in schema after server restart regenerates GraphQL schema
              ...(request.order_id ? { order_id: request.order_id } : {}),
            } as KickzoneGraphqlTypes.ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput & {
              order_id?: string;
            },
          },
        });

        const response = data?.verifyZarinPalPayment as ZarinPalVerifyResponseExtended | null;
        if (!response) {
          throw new Error('No response from payment service');
        }

        // Code 100 means successful payment
        // Code 101 means payment was already verified
        // success field from resolver indicates if order confirmation succeeded
        const isSuccess =
          (response.code === 100 || response.code === 101) &&
          response.success !== false &&
          Boolean(response.ref_id);

        const result: ZarinPalVerifyResult = {
          code: response.code,
          message: response.message,
          ref_id: response.ref_id,
          orderIds: response.orderIds,
          orderId: response.orderId,
          orderNumber: response.orderNumber,
          success: isSuccess,
        };

        if (isSuccess && response.ref_id) {
          options?.onSuccess?.(response.ref_id);
        } else if (!isSuccess) {
          // CRITICAL: For code 100/101 with success=false, the server's response.message
          // contains the actual failure reason (e.g., "Insufficient stock").
          // getZarinPalErrorMessage(100) returns "ط¹ظ…ظ„غŒط§طھ ط¨ط§ ظ…ظˆظپظ‚غŒطھ ط§ظ†ط¬ط§ظ… ط´ط¯" (success message!)
          // getZarinPalErrorMessage(101) returns "طھط±ط§ع©ظ†ط´ ظ‚ط¨ظ„ط§ verify ط´ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ"
          // So we must use response.message for these codes when success === false.
          const isGatewaySuccessCode = response.code === 100 || response.code === 101;
          const errorMessage = isGatewaySuccessCode && response.success === false
            ? response.message
            : getZarinPalErrorMessage(response.code);
          setError(errorMessage);
          options?.onError?.(errorMessage);
        }

        return result;
      } catch (err) {
        const errorMessage =
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± طھط§غŒغŒط¯ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ';
        setError(errorMessage);
        options?.onError?.(errorMessage);
        return null;
      }
    },
    [options, verifyPaymentMutation]
  );

  const cancelPendingOrder = useCallback(
    async (variables: CancelPendingOrderVariables): Promise<boolean> => {
      setError(null);

      try {
        const { data } = await cancelPendingOrderMutation({
          variables: {
            orderId: variables.orderId,
            authority: variables.authority,
            guestToken: variables.guestToken ?? undefined,
          },
        });

        const result = data?.cancelPendingZarinPalOrder;
        if (result === true) {
          return true;
        } else {
          throw new Error('Cancellation returned false');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        const errorMessage =
          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'ط®ط·ط§ ط¯ط± ظ„ط؛ظˆ ط³ظپط§ط±ط´ ظ…ط¹ظ„ظ‚';
        setError(errorMessage);
        options?.onError?.(errorMessage);
        return false;
      }
    },
    [options, cancelPendingOrderMutation]
  );

  const redirectToGateway = useCallback(
    async (authority: string) => {
      try {
        const { data } = await getGatewayUrlMutation({
          variables: { authority },
        });

        const url = data?.getZarinPalGatewayUrl?.url;
        if (url) {
          window.location.href = url;
        } else {
          // Fallback - this shouldn't happen but just in case
          throw new Error('No gateway URL returned');
        }
      } catch {
        // Fallback to client-side URL generation if GraphQL fails
        const {
          isZarinPalSandbox,
          ZARINPAL_SANDBOX_GATEWAY_URL,
          ZARINPAL_GATEWAY_URL,
        } = await import('./zarinpal-payment-service');
        const fallbackUrl = isZarinPalSandbox()
          ? `${ZARINPAL_SANDBOX_GATEWAY_URL}/${authority}`
          : `${ZARINPAL_GATEWAY_URL}/${authority}`;
        window.location.href = fallbackUrl;
      }
    },
    [getGatewayUrlMutation]
  );

  const clearError = useCallback(() => {
    setError(null);
  }, []);

  return {
    isRequesting,
    isVerifying,
    isCancelling,
    error,
    requestPayment,
    verifyPayment,
    cancelPendingOrder,
    redirectToGateway,
    clearError,
  };
}

===== FILE: .\libs\client\customers\screens\customer-payment\src\lib\zarinpal-payment-service.ts =====
/**
 * ZarinPal Payment Gateway helpers (client)
 *
 * Official API (v4): https://www.zarinpal.com/docs/paymentGateway/connectToGateway.html
 * Live docs MCP: https://www.zarinpal.com/docs/mcp
 *
 * Payment request/verify run on the Nest server (ZARINPAL_MERCHANT_ID).
 * This module keeps gateway URL fallbacks, types, and Persian error messages.
 */

export interface ZarinPalPaymentRequest {
  merchant_id: string;
  /** Amount in the unit of `currency` (IRT = Toman, IRR = Rial) */
  amount: number;
  callback_url: string;
  description: string;
  metadata?: {
    mobile?: string;
    email?: string;
    order_id?: string;
    order_ids?: string;
  };
  /** IRR = Rial, IRT = Toman. Kickzone uses IRT. */
  currency?: 'IRR' | 'IRT';
  referrer_id?: string;
}

export interface ZarinPalPaymentResponse {
  data: {
    code: number;
    message: string;
    authority: string;
    fee_type?: string;
    fee?: number;
  };
  errors: Array<{
    code: number;
    message: string;
  }>;
}

export interface ZarinPalVerificationRequest {
  merchant_id: string;
  /** Must match the amount used in the original request */
  amount: number;
  authority: string;
}

export interface ZarinPalVerificationResponse {
  data: {
    code: number;
    message: string;
    ref_id?: number;
    card_pan?: string;
    card_hash?: string;
    fee_type?: string;
    fee?: number;
  };
  errors: Array<{
    code: number;
    message: string;
  }>;
}

export const ZARINPAL_GATEWAY_URL = 'https://payment.zarinpal.com/pg/StartPay';
export const ZARINPAL_SANDBOX_GATEWAY_URL =
  'https://sandbox.zarinpal.com/pg/StartPay';

/**
 * Client-side sandbox flag used only as a fallback if GraphQL gateway URL fails.
 * Server sandbox is controlled by ZARINPAL_SANDBOX / NODE_ENV.
 */
export function isZarinPalSandbox(): boolean {
  const metaEnv = (import.meta as unknown as { env?: Record<string, string | undefined> }).env;
  const sandboxEnv = metaEnv?.VITE_ZARINPAL_SANDBOX;
  if (sandboxEnv === undefined || sandboxEnv === '') {
    return true;
  }
  return sandboxEnv === 'true';
}

/**
 * Persian messages for ZarinPal status codes
 * Source: https://www.zarinpal.com/docs/paymentGateway/errorList.html
 */
export function getZarinPalErrorMessage(code: number): string {
  const errorMessages: Record<number, string> = {
    [-9]: 'ط®ط·ط§غŒ ط§ط¹طھط¨ط§ط± ط³ظ†ط¬غŒ',
    [-10]: 'ط§غŒâ€Œظ¾غŒ غŒط§ ظ…ط±ع†ظ†طھ ع©ط¯ ظ¾ط°غŒط±ظ†ط¯ظ‡ طµط­غŒط­ ظ†غŒط³طھ',
    [-11]: 'ظ…ط±ع†ظ†طھ ع©ط¯ ظ¾ط°غŒط±ظ†ط¯ظ‡ ظپط¹ط§ظ„ ظ†غŒط³طھ',
    [-12]: 'طھظ„ط§ط´ ط¨غŒط´ ط§ط² ط­ط¯ ط¯ط± غŒع© ط¨ط§ط²ظ‡ ط²ظ…ط§ظ†غŒ ع©ظˆطھط§ظ‡',
    [-13]: 'ع©ط¯ ع©ط§ظ„â€Œط¨ع© (callback URL) ظ†ط§ظ…ط¹طھط¨ط± ط§ط³طھ',
    [-14]: 'ع©ط§ظ„â€Œط¨ع© ط¯ط§ظ…ظ†ظ‡ ط«ط¨طھâ€Œط´ط¯ظ‡ ط¯ط± ظ¾ظ†ظ„ ظ†غŒط³طھ',
    [-15]: 'طھط±ظ…غŒظ†ط§ظ„ ط´ظ…ط§ ط¨ظ‡ ط­ط§ظ„طھ طھط¹ظ„غŒظ‚ ط¯ط± ط¢ظ…ط¯ظ‡ ط§ط³طھ',

  };

  return errorMessages[code] || `ط®ط·ط§غŒ ظ†ط§ظ…ط´ط®طµ ط¨ط§ ع©ط¯ ${code}`;
}

// Utility to convert numbers to locale format (supports Farsi digits while preserving non-numeric strings like apparel sizes)
const formatNumber = (num: number | string, locale: string): string => {
  const n = Number(num);
  if (Number.isNaN(n)) {
    return String(num);
  }
  return n.toLocaleString(locale);
};

const CANONICAL_PANTS_SIZE_RE = /^[1-9]\d?x[1-9]\d?$/;

const formatCartItemSize = (
  size: string | null | undefined,
  locale: string
): string => {
  if (!size) return '';
  const trimmed = size.trim();
  if (CANONICAL_PANTS_SIZE_RE.test(trimmed)) {
    return formatSizeLabel(SizeKind.Pants, trimmed) || size;
  }
  return formatNumber(size, locale);
};

const isItemRecommended = (
  item: CartItem & { isRecommended?: boolean; recommended?: boolean }
): boolean => {
  return Boolean(item.isRecommended ?? item.recommended);
};
