                </Typography>
                <Typography
                  variant="caption"
                  color="text.secondary"
                  fontWeight={600}
                >
                  {formatNumber(activeStep + 1, currentLocale)} /{' '}
                  {formatNumber(steps.length, currentLocale)}
                </Typography>
              </Box>
              <Box sx={{ display: 'flex', gap: 0.75, width: '100%' }}>
                {steps.map((_, idx) => (
                  <Box
                    key={idx}
                    sx={{
                      flex: 1,
                      height: 4,
                      borderRadius: 2,
                      backgroundColor:
                        idx <= activeStep
                          ? 'primary.main'
                          : isDark
                          ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.15)'
                          : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.1)',
                      transition: 'background-color 0.3s ease',
                    }}
                  />
                ))}
              </Box>
            </Box>

            {activeStep === 0 && (
              <Box>
                <Typography
                  variant="subtitle1"
                  sx={{
                    fontWeight: 700,
                    color: 'text.primary',
                    mb: { xs: 1.5, sm: 2 },
                    display: { xs: 'none', sm: 'block' },
                  }}
                >
                  {LL.checkout.reviewCart()}
                </Typography>
                {hasPayOnDeliverySellers && (
                  <Alert
                    severity="info"
                    data-testid="pay-on-delivery-notice"
                    sx={{
                      mb: 2,
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
                      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
                      background: isDark
                        ? 'rgba(197, 184, 154, 0.12)'
                        : 'rgba(143, 132, 104, 0.1)',
                      border: isDark
                        ? '1px solid rgba(197, 184, 154, 0.25)'
                        : '1px solid rgba(143, 132, 104, 0.2)',
                      color: isDark ? '#f4f4f2' : '#181816',
                      '& .MuiAlert-message': {
                        fontWeight: 500,
                      },
                    }}
                  >
                    {currentLocale === 'fa'
                      ? 'آ«ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡ ط§ط±ط³ط§ظ„ ظ‡ظ†ع¯ط§ظ… طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ظ…ط±ط³ظˆظ„ظ‡ ظ¾ط³طھغŒ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯آ»'
                      : 'Shipping is paid on delivery when you receive the parcel by post'}
                  </Alert>
                )}
                <Box sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
                  {cartItems.map((item: CartItem) => (
                    <Box
                      key={item.id}
                      sx={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        py: { xs: 1.25, sm: 1.75 },
                        borderBottom: '1px solid',
                        borderColor: 'divider',
                        gap: 1.5,
                        '&:last-child': {
                          borderBottom: 'none',
                        },
                      }}
                    >
                      <Box
                        sx={{
                          display: 'flex',
                          gap: 1.5,
                          flex: 1,
                          minWidth: 0,
                          alignItems: 'center',
                        }}
                      >
                        {item.productImage && (
                          <Box
                            component="img"
                            src={item.productImage}
                            alt={item.productName}
                            sx={{
                              width: { xs: 48, sm: 56 },
                              height: { xs: 48, sm: 56 },
                              objectFit: 'cover',
                              borderRadius: 1.5,
                              boxShadow: '0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.08)',
                              flexShrink: 0,
                            }}
                          />
                        )}
                        <Box sx={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                          <Typography
                            variant="body2"
                            fontWeight={600}
                            sx={{
                              overflow: 'hidden',
                              textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                              whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                            }}
                          >
                            {item.productName}
                          </Typography>
                          <Box
                            sx={{
                              display: 'flex',
                              alignItems: 'center',
                              gap: 1,
                              flexWrap: 'wrap',
                              mt: 0.25,
                            }}
                          >
                            {item.color && (
                              <Box
                                sx={{
                                  display: 'flex',
                                  alignItems: 'center',
                                  gap: 0.5,
                                  flexShrink: 0,
                                }}
                              >
                                {parseColorHexes(item.color).map((hex) => (
                                  <Box
                                    key={hex}
                                    sx={{
                                      width: 14,
                                      height: 14,
                                      backgroundColor: hex,
                                      borderRadius: '50%',
                                      border: '1.5px solid white',
                                      boxShadow: '0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)',
                                    }}
                                  />
                                ))}
                              </Box>
                            )}
                            {item.size && (
                              <Typography
                                variant="caption"
                                color="text.secondary"
                                sx={{
                                  fontWeight: 500,
                                  px: 0.75,
                                  py: 0.1,
                                  borderRadius: 1,
                                  backgroundColor: isDark
                                    ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)'
                                    : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.04)',
                                }}
                              >
                                {LL.products.size()}:{' '}
                                {formatCartItemSize(item.size, currentLocale)}
                              </Typography>
                            )}
                            <Typography
                              variant="caption"
                              color="text.secondary"
                            >
                              {LL.table.quantity()}:{' '}
                              {formatNumber(item.quantity, currentLocale)}
                            </Typography>
                          </Box>
                        </Box>
                      </Box>
                      <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'right', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                        <Typography variant="body2" fontWeight={700}>
                          <PriceDisplay
                            priceInUSD={item.totalPrice}
                            showSymbol={true}
                            showOriginal={false}
                          />
                        </Typography>
                      </Box>
                    </Box>
                  ))}
                </Box>
                <Divider sx={{ my: 1.5 }} />
                <Box
                  sx={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    mb: 1,
                  }}
                >
                  <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                    {LL.checkout.subtotal()}:
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="body2" fontWeight={600}>
                    <PriceDisplay
                      priceInUSD={cartTotal}
                      showSymbol={true}
                      showOriginal={false}
                    />
                  </Typography>
                </Box>

                {/* Shipping fee */}
                <Box
                  sx={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    mb: 1,
                  }}
                >
                  <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                    {LL.checkout.shipping()} (
                    {shippingMethod === 'vip'
                      ? currentLocale === 'fa'
                        ? 'ظ¾غŒع© VIP'
                        : 'VIP Courier'
                      : shippingMethod === 'express'
                      ? currentLocale === 'fa'
                        ? 'ظ¾غŒط´طھط§ط²'
                        : 'Express'
                      : currentLocale === 'fa'
                      ? 'ط­ط¶ظˆط±غŒ'
                      : 'Pickup'}
                    ):
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="body2" fontWeight={600}>
                    {totalShippingCost === 0 ? (
                      currentLocale === 'fa' ? 'ط±ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ†' : 'Free'
                    ) : (
                      <PriceDisplay
                        priceInUSD={totalShippingCost}
                        showSymbol={true}
                        showOriginal={false}
                      />
                    )}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>

                <Divider sx={{ my: 1.5 }} />

                <Box
                  sx={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                  }}
                >
                  <Typography variant="subtitle1" fontWeight={700}>
                    {LL.checkout.total()}:
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography
                    variant="subtitle1"
                    fontWeight={700}
                    color="primary.main"
                  >
                    <PriceDisplay
                      priceInUSD={grandTotal}
                      showSymbol={true}
                      showOriginal={false}
                    />
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            )}

            {activeStep === 1 && (
              <Box>
                <Typography
                  variant="subtitle1"
                  sx={{
                    fontWeight: 700,
                    color: 'text.primary',
                    mb: { xs: 1.5, sm: 2 },
                    display: { xs: 'none', sm: 'block' },
                  }}
                >
                  {LL.checkout.shippingInformation()}
                </Typography>
                {hasPayOnDeliverySellers && (
                  <Alert
                    severity="info"
                    data-testid="pay-on-delivery-notice"
                    sx={{
                      mb: 2,
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
                      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
                      background: isDark
                        ? 'rgba(197, 184, 154, 0.12)'
                        : 'rgba(143, 132, 104, 0.1)',
                      border: isDark
                        ? '1px solid rgba(197, 184, 154, 0.25)'
                        : '1px solid rgba(143, 132, 104, 0.2)',
                      color: isDark ? '#f4f4f2' : '#181816',
                      '& .MuiAlert-message': {
                        fontWeight: 500,
                      },
                    }}
                  >
                    {currentLocale === 'fa'
                      ? 'آ«ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡ ط§ط±ط³ط§ظ„ ظ‡ظ†ع¯ط§ظ… طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ظ…ط±ط³ظˆظ„ظ‡ ظ¾ط³طھغŒ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯آ»'
                      : 'Shipping is paid on delivery when you receive the parcel by post'}
                  </Alert>
                )}
                <Grid container spacing={{ xs: 1.5, sm: 2 }}>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 12, sm: 6 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      required
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={LL.form.fullName()}
                      value={shippingInfo.customerName || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange('customerName', e.target.value)
                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('customerName')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('customerName')}
                      sx={{
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      }}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 12, sm: 6 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      required={false}
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={
                        currentLocale === 'fa'
                          ? `${LL.form.email()} (ط§ط®طھغŒط§ط±غŒ)`
                          : `${LL.form.email()} (Optional)`
                      }
                      type="email"
                      value={shippingInfo.customerEmail || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange(
                          'customerEmail',
                          e.target.value
                        )
                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('customerEmail')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('customerEmail')}
                      {...getLtrTextFieldProps({
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      })}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 12, sm: 6 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      required
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={LL.form.phone()}
                      value={shippingInfo.customerPhone || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange(
                          'customerPhone',
                          e.target.value
                        )
                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('customerPhone')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('customerPhone')}
                      {...getLtrTextFieldProps({
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      })}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 12, sm: 6 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      required
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={LL.form.country()}
                      value={shippingInfo.country || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange('country', e.target.value)
                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('country')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('country')}
                      sx={{
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      }}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 12 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      required
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={LL.form.address()}
                      value={shippingInfo.shippingAddress || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange(
                          'shippingAddress',
                          e.target.value

                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('shippingAddress')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('shippingAddress')}
                      sx={{
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      }}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 12, sm: 4 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      required
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={LL.form.city()}
                      value={shippingInfo.city || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange('city', e.target.value)
                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('city')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('city')}
                      sx={{
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      }}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 6, sm: 4 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      required
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={LL.form.state()}
                      value={shippingInfo.state || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange('state', e.target.value)
                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('state')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('state')}
                      sx={{
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      }}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 6, sm: 4 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      required
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={LL.form.zipCode()}
                      value={shippingInfo.zipCode || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange('zipCode', toEnglishDigits(e.target.value))
                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('zipCode')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('zipCode')}
                      slotProps={{ htmlInput: { dir: 'ltr' } }}
                      sx={{
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      }}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 12 }}>
                    <TextField
                      fullWidth
                      size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                      label={LL.form.notes()}
                      multiline
                      rows={isMobile ? 2 : 3}
                      value={shippingInfo.notes || ''}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleShippingInfoChange('notes', e.target.value)
                      }
                      error={!!shippingFieldError('notes')}
                      helperText={shippingFieldError('notes')}
                      sx={{
                        '& .MuiOutlinedInput-root': {
                          borderRadius: 2,
                        },
                      }}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                  <Grid size={{ xs: 12 }}>
                    <Divider sx={{ my: 1.5 }} />
                    <Typography
                      variant="caption"
                      sx={{
                        display: 'block',
                        textTransform: 'uppercase',
                        letterSpacing: '0.18em',
                        fontWeight: 700,
                        fontSize: '0.72rem',
                        color: isDark ? '#c5b89a' : '#7c6d48',
                        mb: 1.25,
                      }}
                    >
                      {currentLocale === 'fa' ? 'ط§ظ†طھط®ط§ط¨ ط±ظˆط´ ط§ط±ط³ط§ظ„' : 'SHIPPING METHOD'}
                    </Typography>
                    <ShippingMethodSelector
                      currentMethod={shippingMethod}
                      onSelect={setShippingMethod}
                      vipCost={vipShippingCost}
                      expressCost={expressShippingCost}
                      pickupCost={pickupShippingCost}
                      locale={currentLocale}
                      isDark={isDark}
                    />
                  </Grid>
                </Grid>
              </Box>
            )}

            {activeStep === 2 && (
              <Box>
                <Typography
                  variant="subtitle1"
                  sx={{
                    fontWeight: 700,
                    color: 'text.primary',
                    mb: { xs: 1.5, sm: 2 },
                    display: { xs: 'none', sm: 'block' },
                  }}
                >
                  {LL.checkout.orderSummary()}
                </Typography>

                {hasPayOnDeliverySellers && (
                  <Alert
                    severity="info"
                    data-testid="pay-on-delivery-notice"
                    sx={{
                      mb: 2,
                      borderRadius: '12px',
                      backdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
                      WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(20px)',
                      background: isDark
                        ? 'rgba(197, 184, 154, 0.12)'
                        : 'rgba(143, 132, 104, 0.1)',
                      border: isDark
                        ? '1px solid rgba(197, 184, 154, 0.25)'
                        : '1px solid rgba(143, 132, 104, 0.2)',
                      color: isDark ? '#f4f4f2' : '#181816',
                      '& .MuiAlert-message': {
                        fontWeight: 500,
                      },
                    }}
                  >
                    {currentLocale === 'fa'
                      ? 'آ«ظ‡ط²غŒظ†ظ‡ ط§ط±ط³ط§ظ„ ظ‡ظ†ع¯ط§ظ… طھط­ظˆغŒظ„ ظ…ط±ط³ظˆظ„ظ‡ ظ¾ط³طھغŒ ظ¾ط±ط¯ط§ط®طھ ظ…غŒâ€Œط´ظˆط¯آ»'
                      : 'Shipping is paid on delivery when you receive the parcel by post'}
                  </Alert>
                )}

                {/* Shipping address review card */}
                {shippingInfo.shippingAddress && (
                  <Box
                    sx={{
                      p: { xs: 1.5, sm: 2 },
                      mb: 2,
                      borderRadius: 2,
                      backgroundColor: isDark
                        ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)'
                        : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.02)',
                      border: '1px solid',
                      borderColor: 'divider',
                    }}
                  >
                    <Box
                      sx={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        mb: 0.75,
                      }}
                    >
                      <Typography variant="subtitle2" fontWeight={700}>
                        {LL.checkout.shippingInformation()}
                      </Typography>
                      <Button
                        size="small"
                        startIcon={<Edit sx={{ fontSize: '0.9rem !important' }} />}
                        onClick={() => setActiveStep(1)}
                        sx={{
                          minWidth: 'auto',
                          py: 0.25,
                          px: 1,
                          fontSize: '0.8rem',
                        }}
                      >
                        {LL.button.edit()}
                      </Button>
                    </Box>
                    <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                      {shippingInfo.customerName} - {shippingInfo.customerPhone}
                    </Typography>
                    <Typography
                      variant="body2"
                      color="text.secondary"
                      sx={{ mt: 0.25 }}
                    >
                      {[
                        shippingInfo.shippingAddress,
                        shippingInfo.city,
                        shippingInfo.state,
                        shippingInfo.zipCode,
                        shippingInfo.country,
                      ]
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .join('طŒ ')}
                    </Typography>
                  </Box>
                )}

                {/* Display cart items */}
                <Box sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
                  {cartItems.map((item: CartItem) => (
                    <Box
                      key={item.id}
                      sx={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        justifyContent: 'space-between',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        py: 1,
                        borderBottom: '1px solid',
                        borderColor: 'divider',
                        gap: 1.5,
                        '&:last-child': {
                          borderBottom: 'none',
                        },
                      }}
                    >
                      <Box sx={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0 }}>
                        <Typography
                          variant="body2"
                          fontWeight={500}
                          sx={{
                            overflow: 'hidden',
                            textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                            whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                          }}
                        >
                          {item.productName}
                        </Typography>
                        <Box
                          sx={{
                            display: 'flex',
                            alignItems: 'center',
                            gap: 1.5,
                            mt: 0.25,
                          }}
                        >
                          {item.color && (
                            <Box
                              sx={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: 0.5,
                              }}
                            >
                              <Typography
                                variant="caption"
                                color="text.secondary"
                              >
                                {LL.products.color()}:
                              </Typography>
                              <Box
                                sx={{
                                  display: 'flex',
                                  alignItems: 'center',
                                  gap: 0.5,
                                }}
                              >
                                {parseColorHexes(item.color).map((hex) => (
                                  <Box
                                    key={hex}
                                    sx={{
                                      width: 14,
                                      height: 14,
                                      backgroundColor: hex,
                                      borderRadius: '50%',
                                      border: '1px solid #ccc',
                                    }}
                                  />
                                ))}
                              </Box>
                            </Box>
                          )}
                          {item.size && (
                            <Box
                              sx={{
                                display: 'flex',
                                alignItems: 'center',
                                gap: 0.5,
                              }}
                            >
                              <Typography
                                variant="caption"
                                color="text.secondary"
                              >
                                {LL.products.size()}:
                              </Typography>
                              <Typography
                                variant="caption"
                                color="text.secondary"
                                fontWeight={500}
                              >
                                {formatCartItemSize(item.size, currentLocale)}
                              </Typography>
                            </Box>
                          )}
                          <Typography
                            variant="caption"
                            color="text.secondary"
                          >
                            {LL.table.quantity()}:{' '}
                            {formatNumber(item.quantity, currentLocale)}
                          </Typography>
                        </Box>
                      </Box>
                      <Box sx={{ textAlign: 'right', flexShrink: 0 }}>
                        <Typography
                          variant="body2"
                          sx={{
                            fontWeight: 700,
                            color: isItemRecommended(item)
                              ? theme.palette.primary.main
                              : 'text.primary',
                          }}
                        >
                          <PriceDisplay
                            priceInUSD={item.totalPrice}
                            showSymbol={true}
                            showOriginal={false}
                          />
                        </Typography>
                      </Box>
                    </Box>
                  ))}
                </Box>

                {/* Order totals */}
                <Box
                  sx={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    mb: 1,
                  }}
                >
                  <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                    {LL.checkout.subtotal()}:
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="body2" fontWeight={600}>
                    <PriceDisplay
                      priceInUSD={cartTotal}
                      showSymbol={true}
                      showOriginal={false}
                    />
                  </Typography>
                </Box>

                {/* Shipping fee */}
                <Box
                  sx={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    mb: 1,
                  }}
                >
                  <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                    {LL.checkout.shipping()} (
                    {shippingMethod === 'vip'
                      ? currentLocale === 'fa'
                        ? 'ظ¾غŒع© VIP'
                        : 'VIP Courier'
                      : shippingMethod === 'express'
                      ? currentLocale === 'fa'
                        ? 'ظ¾غŒط´طھط§ط²'
                        : 'Express'
                      : currentLocale === 'fa'
                      ? 'ط­ط¶ظˆط±غŒ'
                      : 'Pickup'}
                    ):
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography variant="body2" fontWeight={600}>
                    {totalShippingCost === 0 ? (
                      currentLocale === 'fa' ? 'ط±ط§غŒع¯ط§ظ†' : 'Free'
                    ) : (
                      <PriceDisplay
                        priceInUSD={totalShippingCost}
                        showSymbol={true}
                        showOriginal={false}
                      />
                    )}
                  </Typography>
                </Box>

                <Divider sx={{ my: 1.5 }} />

                <Box
                  sx={{
                    display: 'flex',
                    justifyContent: 'space-between',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    mb: 1,
                  }}
                >
                  <Typography
                    variant="subtitle1"
                    fontWeight={700}
                  >
                    {LL.checkout.total()}:
                  </Typography>
                  <Typography
                    variant="subtitle1"
                    fontWeight={700}
                    color="primary.main"
                  >
                    <PriceDisplay
                      priceInUSD={grandTotal}
                      showSymbol={true}
                      showOriginal={false}
                    />
                  </Typography>
                </Box>
              </Box>
            )}

            {activeStep === 3 && (
              <Box>
                <Typography
                  variant="subtitle1"
                  sx={{
                    fontWeight: 700,
                    color: 'text.primary',
                    mb: { xs: 1.5, sm: 2 },
                    display: { xs: 'none', sm: 'block' },
                  }}
                >
                  {LL.checkout.payment()}
                </Typography>
                {isCreatingOrder ? (
                  <Box
                    sx={{
                      display: 'flex',
                      flexDirection: 'column',
                      alignItems: 'center',
                      justifyContent: 'center',
                      py: 4,
                    }}
                  >
                    <CircularProgress sx={{ mb: 2 }} />
                    <Typography variant="body1" color="text.secondary">
                      {LL.checkout.processingOrder()}
                    </Typography>
                  </Box>
                ) : !usesZarinPal && createdOrder ? (
                  <Box>
                    <Alert severity="success" sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
                      {LL.checkout.demoCheckout()}
                    </Alert>
                    <Alert severity="info" sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
                      {LL.checkout.orderProcessed()}
                    </Alert>
                    <Typography
                      variant="body2"
                      color="text.secondary"
                      sx={{ mb: 2 }}
                    >
                      {LL.checkout.orderNumberLabel()}:{' '}
                      {createdOrder.orderNumber}
                    </Typography>
                    <Button
                      variant="contained"
                      color="primary"
                      fullWidth={isMobile}
                      size={isMobile ? 'medium' : 'large'}
                      onClick={() => {
                        closeCheckoutDialog();
                        resetCheckout();
                        // Read order confirmation data from sessionStorage
                        let orderDataForNavigation = null;
                        try {
                          const stored = sessionStorage.getItem(`order_${createdOrder.id}`);
                          if (stored) {
                            const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
                            orderDataForNavigation = parsed.orderConfirmation;
                          }
                        } catch (e) {
                          console.warn('Failed to read order confirmation data:', e);
                        }
                        navigate(CUSTOMER_ROUTES.ORDER_CONFIRMATION, {
                          state: { orderId: createdOrder.id, orderData: orderDataForNavigation },
                        });
                      }}
                    >
                      {LL.orderConfirmation.viewOrders()}
                    </Button>
                  </Box>
                ) : paymentAuthority ? (
                  <Box>
                    <Alert severity="success" sx={{ mb: 2 }}>
                      {LL.checkout.orderCreatedRedirecting()}
                    </Alert>
                    <Box
                      sx={{
                        display: 'flex',
                        flexDirection: 'column',
                        alignItems: 'center',
                        gap: 2,
                        py: 2,
                      }}
                    >
                      <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
                        {LL.checkout.orderNumberLabel()}:{' '}
                        {createdOrder?.orderNumber}
                      </Typography>
                      <Typography variant="body1" fontWeight={600}>
                        {LL.checkout.totalAmountLabel()}:{' '}
                        <PriceDisplay
                          priceInUSD={grandTotal}
                          showSymbol={true}
                          showOriginal={false}
                        />
                      </Typography>
                      <Button
                        variant="contained"
                        color="primary"
                        fullWidth={isMobile}
                        size="large"
                        onClick={() => redirectToGateway(paymentAuthority)}
                        sx={{
                          mt: 1,
                          px: 4,
                          py: 1.25,
                          fontSize: '1rem',
                          fontWeight: 600,
                        }}
                      >
                        {LL.checkout.continueToPaymentGateway()}
                      </Button>
                    </Box>
                  </Box>
                ) : (
                  <Alert severity="error">
                    {LL.checkout.paymentInitializationFailed()}
                  </Alert>
                )}
              </Box>
            )}
          </DialogContent>
          <DialogActions
            sx={{
              px: { xs: 2, sm: 3 },
              py: { xs: 1.25, sm: 2 },
              borderTop: '1px solid',
              borderColor: 'divider',
              gap: 1,
            }}
          >
            <Button
              size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
              onClick={closeCheckoutDialog}
            >
              {LL.button.cancel()}
            </Button>
            {activeStep > 0 && activeStep < 3 && (
              <Button
                size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                onClick={handleBack}
              >
                {LL.button.back()}
              </Button>
            )}
            {activeStep < 2 ? (
              <Button
                variant="contained"
                size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                onClick={handleNext}
                sx={{
                  transition: 'all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)',
                  '&:hover': {
                    transform: 'translateY(-1px)',
                    boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                  },
                }}
              >
                {LL.button.next()}
              </Button>
            ) : activeStep === 2 ? (
              <Button
                variant="contained"
                size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
                onClick={handleNext}
                loading={isCreatingOrder}
                sx={{
                  transition: 'all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)',
                  '&:hover': {
                    transform: 'translateY(-1px)',
                    boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                  },
                }}
              >
                {LL.checkout.proceedToPayment()}
              </Button>
            ) : null}
          </DialogActions>
        </Dialog>

        {/* Authentication Required Dialog */}
        <Dialog
          open={authDialogOpen}
          onClose={closeAuthDialog}
          maxWidth="xs"
          fullWidth
          PaperProps={{
            sx: {
              borderRadius: { xs: 3, sm: '24px' },
              m: { xs: 1.5, sm: 2 },
              width: { xs: 'calc(100% - 24px)', sm: '100%' },
              maxWidth: 440,
              backdropFilter: 'blur(28px) saturate(190%)',
              WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(28px) saturate(190%)',
              background: isDark
                ? 'linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(28, 28, 24, 0.94) 0%, rgba(20, 20, 18, 0.9) 100%)'
                : 'linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.95) 0%, rgba(250, 248, 244, 0.92) 100%)',
              border: isDark
                ? '1px solid rgba(240, 239, 235, 0.12)'
                : '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)',
              boxShadow: isDark
                ? '0 24px 60px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7), inset 0 1px 1.5px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)'
                : '0 24px 60px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12), inset 0 1px 2px 0 rgba(255, 255, 255, 1)',
            },
          }}
        >
          <DialogTitle
            sx={{
              px: { xs: 2, sm: 3 },
              py: { xs: 1.5, sm: 2 },
              fontSize: { xs: '1.05rem', sm: '1.25rem' },
              fontWeight: 700,
            }}
          >
            {LL.checkout.authenticationRequired()}
          </DialogTitle>
          <DialogContent sx={{ px: { xs: 2, sm: 3 }, py: 1 }}>
            <Typography variant="body2" color="text.secondary">
              {LL.checkout.authenticationMessage()}
            </Typography>
          </DialogContent>
          <DialogActions sx={{ px: { xs: 2, sm: 3 }, py: { xs: 1.5, sm: 2 } }}>
            <Button
              size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
              onClick={closeAuthDialog}
            >
              {LL.button.cancel()}
            </Button>
            <Button
              variant="contained"
              size={isMobile ? 'small' : 'medium'}
              onClick={() => {
                closeAuthDialog();
                navigate(CUSTOMER_ROUTES.LOGIN, {
                  state: { from: CUSTOMER_ROUTES.CART },
                });
              }}
              sx={{
                transition: 'all 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1)',
                '&:hover': {
                  transform: 'translateY(-1px)',
                  boxShadow: '0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)',
                },
              }}
            >
              {LL.checkout.loginRegister()}
            </Button>
          </DialogActions>
        </Dialog>
      </Box>
    </CustomersLayout>
  );
}

export default CustomerShoppingCart;
