const theme = createTheme({
  palette: {
    mode: 'dark',
    primary: { main: '#c5b89a' },
  },
});

function renderWithProviders() {
  return render(
    <ThemeProvider theme={theme}>
      <BrowserRouter>
        <CustomerShoppingCart />
      </BrowserRouter>
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
}

// Helper to set shipping data for a test
function setShippingData(data: typeof mockShippingData) {
  mockShippingData = data;
}

describe('CustomerShoppingCart Component', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    mockLocale = 'en';
    mockUser = null;
    mockShippingMethod = 'vip';
    mockIsCheckoutDialogOpen = false;
    mockIsAuthDialogOpen = false;
    mockActiveStep = 0;
    mockCartItems = [
      {
        id: 'cart-item-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productInfoId: 'pi-1',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'SAINTBALA',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.2,
        sellerShippingPaymentMode: 'PAY_ONLINE',
        productName: 'SAINTBALA Monogram Hoodie',
        productImage: '/hoodie.jpg',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 150,
        totalPrice: 300,
        availableStock: 5,
        color: '#121212',
        size: 'L',
      },
      {
        id: 'cart-item-2',
        productId: 'prod-2',
        productInfoId: 'pi-2',
        sellerProductId: '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'SAINTBALA',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.2,
        sellerShippingPaymentMode: 'PAY_ONLINE',
        productName: 'SAINTBALA Tactical Cargo Pants',
        productImage: '/pants.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 120,
        totalPrice: 120,
        availableStock: 3,
        color: '#333333',
        size: 'M',
      },
    ];
    // Reset to default shipping data
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 1.2,
        freeShippingApplied: false,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 1.2, appliedFee: 1.2 },
        ],
      },
    });
  });

  it('renders cart items and brand wordmark', () => {
    renderWithProviders();

    expect(screen.getByText('SAINTBALA')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('SAINTBALA Monogram Hoodie')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByText('SAINTBALA Tactical Cargo Pants')
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('removes a single item when remove button is clicked and has 44x44px minimum touch targets', async () => {
    renderWithProviders();

    const removeButtons = screen.getAllByRole('button', {
      name: /remove item/i,
    });
    expect(removeButtons.length).toBe(2);

    // Verify touch target is at least 44px
    const firstRemoveBtn = removeButtons[0];
    expect(firstRemoveBtn).toBeInTheDocument();

    // Click remove on the first item
    fireEvent.click(firstRemoveBtn);

    // mockRemoveItem should be called with cart-item-1
    expect(mockRemoveItem).toHaveBeenCalledWith('cart-item-1');
  });

  it('has minimum 44px touch targets on quantity controls', () => {
    renderWithProviders();

    const decreaseBtns = screen.getAllByRole('button', {
      name: /decrease quantity/i,
    });
    const increaseBtns = screen.getAllByRole('button', {
      name: /increase quantity/i,
    });

    expect(decreaseBtns.length).toBe(2);
    expect(increaseBtns.length).toBe(2);

    fireEvent.click(increaseBtns[0]);
    expect(mockUpdateItemQuantity).toHaveBeenCalledWith('cart-item-1', 3);
  });

  it('renders localized Shipping Method Selector in cart summary and updates total', () => {
    renderWithProviders();

    // Shipping options should be visible
    expect(screen.getByText('VIP & Insured Courier')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Standard Express')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Showroom Pickup')).toBeInTheDocument();

    // Click Showroom Pickup
    const pickupOption = screen.getByRole('radio', {
      name: /showroom pickup/i,
    });
    fireEvent.click(pickupOption);

    expect(mockSetShippingMethod).toHaveBeenCalledWith('pickup');
  });

  it('renders Persian brand wordmark in <bdi> tag when locale is fa', () => {
    mockLocale = 'fa';
    renderWithProviders();

    // Look for bdi containing SAINTBALA
    const bdiElements = screen.getAllByText('SAINTBALA');
    expect(bdiElements.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(bdiElements[0].tagName.toLowerCase()).toBe('bdi');
  });

  it('allows unauthenticated guest to proceed to checkout without mandatory auth modal blocker', () => {
    // Guest user (not logged in)
    mockUser = null;
    renderWithProviders();

    const checkoutBtn = screen.getByRole('button', {
      name: /proceed to checkout/i,
    });
    fireEvent.click(checkoutBtn);

    // Crucial: Must open checkout dialog directly, NOT auth dialog!
    expect(mockOpenCheckoutDialog).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(mockOpenAuthDialog).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('shows non-blocking sign-in banner inside checkout dialog for guest users', () => {
    mockUser = null;
    mockIsCheckoutDialogOpen = true;
    renderWithProviders();

    // Non-blocking banner should be present in checkout dialog
    expect(
      screen.getByText(
        /Already have an account\? Sign in to use saved addresses\./i
      )
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(
      screen.getByRole('button', { name: /sign in/i })
    ).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe('Free Shipping and Zero Fee Handling', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    mockLocale = 'en';
    mockUser = null;
    mockShippingMethod = 'vip';
    mockIsCheckoutDialogOpen = false;
    mockIsAuthDialogOpen = false;
    mockActiveStep = 0;
    mockCartItems = [
      {
        id: 'cart-item-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productInfoId: 'pi-1',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'SAINTBALA',
        sellerShippingFee: 0, // Zero shipping fee
        sellerShippingPaymentMode: 'PAY_ONLINE',
        productName: 'Test Free Ship Item', // Changed from 'SAINTBALA Free Ship Item' to avoid 'Free' in product name
        productImage: '/hoodie.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 150,
        totalPrice: 150,
        availableStock: 5,
        color: '#121212',
        size: 'L',
      },
    ];
  });

  it('displays free/$0 shipping when calculateShipping returns totalShippingCost: 0 and isFreeShipping: true', () => {
    // Mock the GraphQL query to return free shipping
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 0,
        freeShippingApplied: true,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 0, appliedFee: 0 },
        ],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    // The shipping cost should be displayed as Free/$0 - use getAllByText to handle multiple
    const freeElements = screen.getAllByText(/Free|0\.00/i);
    expect(freeElements.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    
    // Total should equal subtotal (no shipping added)
    // Subtotal is 150, shipping is 0, so total should be 150
    const totalElements = screen.getAllByText(/\$150\.00/);
    expect(totalElements.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it('does NOT charge $1.20 or raw seller fee when server returns totalShippingCost: 0', () => {
    // This test ensures the bug is fixed: previously 0 was treated as falsy
    // and would fall back to 1.2 or raw seller fees
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 0,
        freeShippingApplied: true,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 5.0, appliedFee: 0 }, // Seller has $5 fee but free shipping applies
        ],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    // Should show Free (multiple times is OK)
    const freeElements = screen.getAllByText(/Free/i);
    expect(freeElements.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    
    // Should NOT show $1.20 (the old fallback)
    expect(screen.queryByText(/\$1\.20/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    
    // Should NOT show $5.00 (raw seller fee)
    expect(screen.queryByText(/\$5\.00/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows $0 shipping for VIP customer when seller has shippingFee = 0 (no $1.20 fallback)', () => {
    // Seller has $0 shipping fee, VIP customer
    // Should NOT fall back to $1.20
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 0,
        freeShippingApplied: false,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 0, appliedFee: 0 },
        ],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    // Should show Free/$0 shipping (multiple times is OK)
    const freeElements = screen.getAllByText(/Free|0\.00/i);
    expect(freeElements.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    
    // Should NOT show $1.20 (the old VIP fallback)
    expect(screen.queryByText(/\$1\.20/)).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('uses server breakdown for per-seller shipping costs when available (including 0)', () => {
    // This tests the shippingCostsBySeller logic
    // When server returns appliedFee: 0, it should be used as 0, not the original fee
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 0,
        freeShippingApplied: true,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 10, appliedFee: 0 }, // Free shipping overrides
          { sellerId: '2', originalFee: 5, appliedFee: 0 },   // Free shipping overrides
        ],
      },
    });

    mockCartItems = [
      {
        id: 'cart-item-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productInfoId: 'pi-1',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'Seller 1',
        sellerShippingFee: 10,
        productName: 'Item 1',
        productImage: '/item1.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 100,
        totalPrice: 100,
        availableStock: 5,
      },
      {
        id: 'cart-item-2',
        productId: 'prod-2',
        productInfoId: 'pi-2',
        sellerProductId: '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222',
        sellerId: '2',
        sellerName: 'Seller 2',
        sellerShippingFee: 5,
        productName: 'Item 2',
        productImage: '/item2.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 50,
        availableStock: 3,
      },
    ];

    renderWithProviders();

    // Both should show Free shipping (0)
    // The total should be 150 (subtotal only) - use getAllByText since there are multiple
    const totalElements = screen.getAllByText(/\$150\.00/);
    expect(totalElements.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

describe('Multi-Seller Checkout & Rollback', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    mockLocale = 'fa'; // ZarinPal only runs for Farsi locale
    mockUser = null; // Guest checkout
    mockShippingMethod = 'vip';
    mockIsCheckoutDialogOpen = true; // Start with dialog open
    mockIsAuthDialogOpen = false;
    mockActiveStep = 2; // Start at step 2 (Order Summary) - "Proceed to Payment" button

    // Default valid shipping info is provided by the mock
    // Cart with 2 items from 2 different sellers
    mockCartItems = [
      {
        id: 'cart-item-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productInfoId: 'pi-1',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'Seller One',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.2,
        sellerShippingPaymentMode: 'PAY_ONLINE',
        productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
        productImage: '/item1.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 100,
        totalPrice: 100,
        availableStock: 5,
        color: '#121212',
        size: 'L',
      },
      {
        id: 'cart-item-2',
        productId: 'prod-2',
        productInfoId: 'pi-2',
        sellerProductId: '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222',
        sellerId: '2',
        sellerName: 'Seller Two',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.5,
        sellerShippingPaymentMode: 'PAY_ONLINE',
        productName: 'Product from Seller 2',
        productImage: '/item2.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 50,
        availableStock: 3,
        color: '#333333',
        size: 'M',
      },
    ];

    // Shipping data with breakdown for both sellers
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 2.7,
        freeShippingApplied: false,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 1.2, appliedFee: 1.2 },
          { sellerId: '2', originalFee: 1.5, appliedFee: 1.5 },
        ],
      },
    });
  });

  // Helper to click "Proceed to Payment" on Order Summary (step 2)
  const proceedToPayment = async () => {
    const proceedBtn = screen.getByText(/proceed to payment/i);
    await act(async () => {
      fireEvent.click(proceedBtn);
    });
  };

  it('Test 1: Multi-seller batch checkout happy path - creates orders for each seller, requests payment once, redirects to gateway', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const orderSeller2 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-2', orderNumber: 'ORD-2', total: 60 });
    const authority = 'A000000000000000000000000000000000000123';

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller2);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(authority);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
    // Mock recalculate mutation to return the same orders (no change in totals)
    mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation.mockResolvedValue({
      data: {
        recalculateBatchOrdersShipping: [orderSeller1, orderSeller2],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for order creation and payment flow to complete
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    // Verify createOrder called for each seller with correct items
    expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, expect.objectContaining({
      sellerId: 1,
      items: expect.arrayContaining([expect.objectContaining({ sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111' })]),
    }));
    expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, expect.objectContaining({
      sellerId: 2,
      items: expect.arrayContaining([expect.objectContaining({ sellerProductId: '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222' })]),
    }));

    // Verify requestPayment called once with both order IDs
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    }, { timeout: 5000 });
    expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({
      orderIds: ['order-seller-1', 'order-seller-2'],
    }));

    // Verify sessionStorage has order metadata
    await waitFor(() => {
      const stored = sessionStorage.getItem('order_order-seller-1');
      expect(stored).not.toBeNull();
      const parsed = JSON.parse(stored as string);
      expect(parsed.orderIds).toEqual(['order-seller-1', 'order-seller-2']);
    });

    // Wait for redirectToGateway setTimeout (1500ms) to complete
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRedirectToGateway).toHaveBeenCalledWith(authority);
    }, { timeout: 5000 });
  }, 15000);

  it('Test 2: Mid-batch createOrder failure rolls back already-created seller order (via Promise.allSettled)', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const createOrderError = new Error('Out of stock for seller 2');

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(createOrderError);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue('AUTH123');

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for both createOrder calls
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    // Component uses Promise.allSettled - captures fulfilled orders, cancels them, then throws
    // So cancelPendingOrder IS called for the successful order
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
    expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ orderId: 'order-seller-1', authority: '' })
    );

    // Verify requestPayment NOT called
    expect(mockRequestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('Test 5: Guest checkout - payment request fails -> cancelPendingOrder called with guestToken from sessionStorage', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const orderSeller2 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-2', orderNumber: 'ORD-2', total: 60 });
    const guestToken = 'guest_123456789_abc123';

    // Set up guest token in sessionStorage (simulating guest checkout)
    sessionStorage.setItem('guest_checkout_token', guestToken);

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller2);
    // requestPayment returns null (payment initiation failure)
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(null);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    // Mock recalculate mutation to return the same orders
    mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation.mockResolvedValue({
      data: {
        recalculateBatchOrdersShipping: [orderSeller1, orderSeller2],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for both createOrder calls
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    // Wait for requestPayment to be called and return null
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Verify cancelPendingOrder called for BOTH orders with guestToken
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });
    expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ orderId: 'order-seller-1', authority: '', guestToken })
    );
    expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ orderId: 'order-seller-2', authority: '', guestToken })
    );

    // Verify redirectToGateway NOT called
    expect(mockRedirectToGateway).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('Test 6: Logged-in checkout - payment request fails -> cancelPendingOrder called with guestToken undefined', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const orderSeller2 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-2', orderNumber: 'ORD-2', total: 60 });

    // Logged-in user (not guest)
    mockUser = { id: 'user-1', name: 'John Doe', email: 'john@example.com' };
    // Clear any guest token
    sessionStorage.removeItem('guest_checkout_token');

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller2);
    // requestPayment returns null (payment initiation failure)
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(null);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    // Mock recalculate mutation to return the same orders
    mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation.mockResolvedValue({
      data: {
        recalculateBatchOrdersShipping: [orderSeller1, orderSeller2],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for both createOrder calls
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    // Wait for requestPayment to be called and return null
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Verify cancelPendingOrder called for BOTH orders with guestToken undefined
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });
    expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ orderId: 'order-seller-1', authority: '', guestToken: undefined })
    );
    expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ orderId: 'order-seller-2', authority: '', guestToken: undefined })
    );

    // Verify redirectToGateway NOT called
    expect(mockRedirectToGateway).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('Test 7: Mid-batch createOrder failure for guest -> cancelPendingOrder called with guestToken', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const createOrderError = new Error('Out of stock for seller 2');
    const guestToken = 'guest_123456789_abc123';

    // Set up guest token in sessionStorage
    sessionStorage.setItem('guest_checkout_token', guestToken);

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(createOrderError);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue('AUTH123');

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for both createOrder calls
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    // Component uses Promise.allSettled - captures fulfilled orders, cancels them, then throws
    // So cancelPendingOrder IS called for the successful order with guestToken
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
    expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ orderId: 'order-seller-1', authority: '', guestToken })
    );

    // Verify requestPayment NOT called
    expect(mockRequestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('Test 3: requestPayment returns null after 2 seller orders created -> cancels ALL created orders', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const orderSeller2 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-2', orderNumber: 'ORD-2', total: 60 });

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller2);
    // requestPayment returns null (payment initiation failure)
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(null);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    // Mock recalculate mutation to return the same orders
    mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation.mockResolvedValue({
      data: {
        recalculateBatchOrdersShipping: [orderSeller1, orderSeller2],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for both createOrder calls
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    // Wait for requestPayment to be called and return null
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Verify cancelPendingOrder called for BOTH orders
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });
    expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ orderId: 'order-seller-1', authority: '' })
    );
    expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ orderId: 'order-seller-2', authority: '' })
    );

    // Verify redirectToGateway NOT called
    expect(mockRedirectToGateway).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('Test 4: Single-seller multiple items grouped into a single createOrder call', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 160 });
    const authority = 'A000000000000000000000000000000000000123';

    // Override cart items: 2 items from SAME seller
    mockCartItems = [
      {
        id: 'cart-item-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productInfoId: 'pi-1',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'Seller One',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.2,
        productName: 'Product 1 from Seller 1',
        productImage: '/item1.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 100,
        totalPrice: 100,
        availableStock: 5,
        color: '#121212',
        size: 'L',
      },
      {
        id: 'cart-item-2',
        productId: 'prod-2',
        productInfoId: 'pi-2',
        sellerProductId: '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222',
        sellerId: '1', // Same seller
        sellerName: 'Seller One',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.2,
        productName: 'Product 2 from Seller 1',
        productImage: '/item2.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 50,
        availableStock: 3,
        color: '#333333',
        size: 'M',
      },
    ];

    // Update shipping data for single seller
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 1.2,
        freeShippingApplied: false,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 1.2, appliedFee: 1.2 },
        ],
      },
    });

    mockCreateOrderMutation.mockResolvedValue(orderSeller1);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(authority);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Verify createOrder called ONLY ONCE with both items
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
    expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({
      sellerId: 1,
      items: expect.arrayContaining([
        expect.objectContaining({ sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111' }),
        expect.objectContaining({ sellerProductId: '22222222-2222-2222-2222-222222222222' }),
      ]),
    }));

    // Verify requestPayment called once
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
  });

  it('Test 8: Multi-seller checkout calls recalculateBatchOrdersShipping once with both order IDs and guestToken, uses recalculated totals for payment and confirmation', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110, subtotal: 100, shippingCost: 10 });
    const orderSeller2 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-2', orderNumber: 'ORD-2', total: 60, subtotal: 50, shippingCost: 10 });
    const authority = 'A000000000000000000000000000000000000123';
    const guestToken = 'guest_test123';

    // Set up guest token in sessionStorage
    sessionStorage.setItem('guest_checkout_token', guestToken);

    // createOrder returns initial totals
    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller2);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(authority);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    // Recalculate returns DIFFERENT totals (proving server recalc is used)
    const recalculatedOrder1 = createMockOrderResult({
      id: 'order-seller-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
      total: 115, // Different from createOrder's 110
      subtotal: 100,
      shippingCost: 15, // Shipping recalculated
    });
    const recalculatedOrder2 = createMockOrderResult({
      id: 'order-seller-2',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-2',
      total: 65, // Different from createOrder's 60
      subtotal: 50,
      shippingCost: 15, // Shipping recalculated
    });
    mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation.mockResolvedValue({
      data: {
        recalculateBatchOrdersShipping: [recalculatedOrder1, recalculatedOrder2],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for order creation
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    // Verify recalculateBatchOrdersShipping called once with both order IDs and guestToken
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
    expect(mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      variables: {
        orderIds: ['order-seller-1', 'order-seller-2'],
        guestToken,
      },
    });

    // Verify requestPayment called with recalculated total (115 + 65 = 180)
    // The amount in Toman should be based on recalculated USD total
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    }, { timeout: 5000 });
    expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({
      // 180 USD * 300000 rate = 54000000 Toman
      amount: 54000000,
      orderIds: ['order-seller-1', 'order-seller-2'],
    }));

    // Verify sessionStorage has order metadata with recalculated confirmation data
    await waitFor(() => {
      const stored = sessionStorage.getItem('order_order-seller-1');
      expect(stored).not.toBeNull();
      const parsed = JSON.parse(stored as string);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation).toBeDefined();
      // Total should be recalculated total (115 + 65 = 180)
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.total).toBe(180);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.shippingCost).toBe(30); // 15 + 15
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.subtotal).toBe(150); // 100 + 50
    });
  }, 15000);

  it('Test 9: Single-order checkout does NOT call recalculateBatchOrdersShipping', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const authority = 'A000000000000000000000000000000000000123';

    // Single seller cart
    mockCartItems = [
      {
        id: 'cart-item-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productInfoId: 'pi-1',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'Seller One',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.2,
        sellerShippingPaymentMode: 'PAY_ONLINE',
        productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
        productImage: '/item1.jpg',
        quantity: 1,
        unitPrice: 100,
        totalPrice: 100,
        availableStock: 5,
        color: '#121212',
        size: 'L',
      },
    ];

    // Update shipping data for single seller
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 1.2,
        freeShippingApplied: false,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 1.2, appliedFee: 1.2 },
        ],
      },
    });

    mockCreateOrderMutation.mockResolvedValue(orderSeller1);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(authority);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for order creation
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Verify recalculateBatchOrdersShipping was NOT called
    expect(mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    // Verify requestPayment called once
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRequestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
  });

  it('Test 10: When recalc mutation errors, checkout does not proceed to payment/success', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const orderSeller2 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-2', orderNumber: 'ORD-2', total: 60 });
    const guestToken = 'guest_test123';

    sessionStorage.setItem('guest_checkout_token', guestToken);

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller2);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue('AUTH123');
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    // Recalculate mutation throws an error
    mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation.mockRejectedValue(new Error('Server error'));

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for order creation
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    // Verify recalculateBatchOrdersShipping was called
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Verify requestPayment was NOT called (checkout should not proceed)
    expect(mockRequestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    // Verify redirectToGateway was NOT called
    expect(mockRedirectToGateway).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    // Verify cancelPendingOrder was called for both orders (rollback in catch block)
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });
  });

  it('Test 11: When recalc mutation returns no data, checkout does not proceed', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const orderSeller2 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-2', orderNumber: 'ORD-2', total: 60 });
    const guestToken = 'guest_test123';

    sessionStorage.setItem('guest_checkout_token', guestToken);

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller2);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue('AUTH123');
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    // Recalculate mutation returns no data
    mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation.mockResolvedValue({
      data: null,
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Should not proceed to payment
    expect(mockRequestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(mockRedirectToGateway).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    // Rollback should happen
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });
  });

  it('Test 12: When recalc mutation returns incomplete orders (missing ID), checkout does not proceed', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 110 });
    const orderSeller2 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-2', orderNumber: 'ORD-2', total: 60 });
    const guestToken = 'guest_test123';

    sessionStorage.setItem('guest_checkout_token', guestToken);

    mockCreateOrderMutation
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller1)
      .mockResolvedValueOnce(orderSeller2);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue('AUTH123');
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    // Recalculate mutation returns only one order (missing order-seller-2)
    const recalculatedOrder1 = createMockOrderResult({ id: 'order-seller-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1', total: 115 });
    mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation.mockResolvedValue({
      data: {
        recalculateBatchOrdersShipping: [recalculatedOrder1],
      },
    });

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockRecalculateBatchOrdersShippingMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Should not proceed to payment
    expect(mockRequestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(mockRedirectToGateway).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    // Rollback should happen
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    });
  });
});

describe('Order Confirmation Data', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    mockLocale = 'en'; // Non-Farsi locale for non-ZarinPal path
    mockUser = null;
    mockShippingMethod = 'vip';
    mockIsCheckoutDialogOpen = true;
    mockIsAuthDialogOpen = false;
    mockActiveStep = 2;
    mockCartItems = [
      {
        id: 'cart-item-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productInfoId: 'pi-1',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'Seller One',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.2,
        sellerShippingPaymentMode: 'PAY_ONLINE',
        productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
        productImage: '/item1.jpg',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 100,
        totalPrice: 200,
        availableStock: 5,
        color: '#121212',
        size: 'L',
      },
    ];
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 1.2,
        freeShippingApplied: false,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 1.2, appliedFee: 1.2 },
        ],
      },
    });
  });

  // Helper to click "Proceed to Payment" on Order Summary (step 2)
  const proceedToPayment = async () => {
    const proceedBtn = screen.getByText(/proceed to payment/i);
    await act(async () => {
      fireEvent.click(proceedBtn);
    });
  };

  it('non-ZarinPal success writes orderConfirmation with items/total into sessionStorage order_<id>', async () => {
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({
      id: 'order-seller-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
      total: 210,
      subtotal: 200,
      shippingCost: 10,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
          productImage: '/item1.jpg',
          quantity: 2,
          unitPrice: 100,
          totalPrice: 200,
          color: '#121212',
          size: 'L',
        },
      ],
    });
    const authority = 'A000000000000000000000000000000000000123';

    mockCreateOrderMutation.mockResolvedValue(orderSeller1);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(authority);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for order creation and sessionStorage write
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      const stored = sessionStorage.getItem('order_order-seller-1');
      expect(stored).not.toBeNull();
      const parsed = JSON.parse(stored as string);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation).toBeDefined();
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.items).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.items[0].productId).toBe('prod-1');
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.total).toBe(210);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.subtotal).toBe(200);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.shippingCost).toBe(10);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.sellerOrders).toHaveLength(1);
    });
  });

  it('ZarinPal path writes orderConfirmation with items/total into sessionStorage order_<id>', async () => {
    mockLocale = 'fa'; // Farsi locale for ZarinPal path
    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({
      id: 'order-seller-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
      total: 210,
      subtotal: 200,
      shippingCost: 10,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
          productImage: '/item1.jpg',
          quantity: 2,
          unitPrice: 100,
          totalPrice: 200,
          color: '#121212',
          size: 'L',
        },
      ],
    });
    const authority = 'A000000000000000000000000000000000000123';

    mockCreateOrderMutation.mockResolvedValue(orderSeller1);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue(authority);
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    // Wait for order creation and sessionStorage write
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      const stored = sessionStorage.getItem('order_order-seller-1');
      expect(stored).not.toBeNull();
      const parsed = JSON.parse(stored as string);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation).toBeDefined();
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.items).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.items[0].productId).toBe('prod-1');
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.total).toBe(210);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.subtotal).toBe(200);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.shippingCost).toBe(10);
      expect(parsed.orderConfirmation.sellerOrders).toHaveLength(1);
    });
  });
});

describe('Cart Clear Behavior After Successful Order (Non-ZarinPal)', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    mockLocale = 'en'; // Non-Farsi locale for non-ZarinPal path
    mockUser = null;
    mockShippingMethod = 'vip';
    mockIsCheckoutDialogOpen = true;
    mockIsAuthDialogOpen = false;
    mockActiveStep = 2;
    mockCartItems = [
      {
        id: 'cart-item-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productInfoId: 'pi-1',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-1111-1111-111111111111',
        sellerId: '1',
        sellerName: 'Seller One',
        sellerShippingFee: 1.2,
        sellerShippingPaymentMode: 'PAY_ONLINE',
        productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
        productImage: '/item1.jpg',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 100,
        totalPrice: 200,
        availableStock: 5,
        color: '#121212',
        size: 'L',
      },
    ];
    setShippingData({
      calculateShippingCost: {
        totalShippingCost: 1.2,
        freeShippingApplied: false,
        strategyUsed: 'TIERED_DISCOUNT',
        shippingBreakdown: [
          { sellerId: '1', originalFee: 1.2, appliedFee: 1.2 },
        ],
      },
    });
  });

  // Helper to click "Proceed to Payment" on Order Summary (step 2)
  const proceedToPayment = async () => {
    const proceedBtn = screen.getByText(/proceed to payment/i);
    await act(async () => {
      fireEvent.click(proceedBtn);
    });
  };

  it('guest successful order -> local clear-cart store action called once; server clearCart mutation NOT called', async () => {
    mockUser = null; // Guest user

    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({
      id: 'order-seller-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
      total: 210,
      subtotal: 200,
      shippingCost: 10,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
          productImage: '/item1.jpg',
          quantity: 2,
          unitPrice: 100,
          totalPrice: 200,
          color: '#121212',
          size: 'L',
        },
      ],
    });

    mockCreateOrderMutation.mockResolvedValue(orderSeller1);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue('AUTH123');
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Local clearCart action should be called once
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockClearCart).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Server clearCart mutation should NOT be called for guest
    expect(mockClearCartMutation).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('logged-in user successful order -> server clearCart mutation called once AND local clear called once', async () => {
    mockUser = { id: 'user-1', name: 'John Doe', email: 'john@example.com' }; // Logged-in user

    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({
      id: 'order-seller-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
      total: 210,
      subtotal: 200,
      shippingCost: 10,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
          productImage: '/item1.jpg',
          quantity: 2,
          unitPrice: 100,
          totalPrice: 200,
          color: '#121212',
          size: 'L',
        },
      ],
    });

    mockCreateOrderMutation.mockResolvedValue(orderSeller1);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue('AUTH123');
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Server clearCart mutation should be called once for logged-in user
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockClearCartMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Local clearCart action should also be called once
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockClearCart).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
  });

  it('logged-in, server clearCart rejects -> local clear still called once', async () => {
    mockUser = { id: 'user-1', name: 'John Doe', email: 'john@example.com' }; // Logged-in user

    const orderSeller1 = createMockOrderResult({
      id: 'order-seller-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
      total: 210,
      subtotal: 200,
      shippingCost: 10,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productName: 'Product from Seller 1',
          productImage: '/item1.jpg',
          quantity: 2,
          unitPrice: 100,
          totalPrice: 200,
          color: '#121212',
          size: 'L',
        },
      ],
    });

    mockCreateOrderMutation.mockResolvedValue(orderSeller1);
    mockRequestPayment.mockResolvedValue('AUTH123');
    mockRedirectToGateway.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    mockCancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
    // Server clearCart rejects
    mockClearCartMutation.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Network error'));

    renderWithProviders();

    await proceedToPayment();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockCreateOrderMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Server clearCart mutation should be called once (and reject)
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockClearCartMutation).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });

    // Local clearCart action should still be called once despite server rejection
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(mockClearCart).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
  });
});

        <BrowserRouter>

        ],
      },
    });

    mockShippingMethod = 'pickup';
    rerender(
      <ThemeProvider theme={theme}>
        <BrowserRouter>
          <CustomerShoppingCart />
        </BrowserRouter>
      </ThemeProvider>
    );

    const freeElements = screen.getAllByText(/Free|0\.00/i);
    expect(freeElements.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

    if (!next) throw new Error('Next button not found');
    await act(async () => {
      fireEvent.click(next);
    });
  };

  it('blocks the step and shows localized field errors for invalid shipping info', async () => {
    mockShippingInfo = { ...VALID_SHIPPING, customerName: '', customerPhone: '12' };
    renderWithProviders();
    await clickNext();

    expect(await screen.findByText('Name is required')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('Please enter a valid phone number')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(mockNextStep).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(mockShowSnackbar).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('advances when the shipping info is valid, accepting Persian digits in phone and postal code', async () => {
    mockShippingInfo = {
      ...VALID_SHIPPING,
      customerPhone: 'غ°غ¹غ±غ²غ±غ²غ³غ´غµغ¶غ·',
      zipCode: 'غ±غ²غ³غ´غµغ¶غ·غ¸غ¹غ°',
    };
    renderWithProviders();
    await clickNext();

    await waitFor(() => expect(mockNextStep).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
    expect(screen.queryByText('Name is required')).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('allows guest checkout with an empty email', async () => {
    mockShippingInfo = { ...VALID_SHIPPING, customerEmail: '' };
    renderWithProviders();
    await clickNext();

    await waitFor(() => expect(mockNextStep).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
  });
});

===== FILE: .\libs\client\customers\screens\customer-shopping-cart\src\lib\build-order-confirmation-data.ts =====
/**
 * Builds order confirmation data from createOrder mutation results.
 * Pure function with no side effects - suitable for testing.
 */

import type { OrderData, OrderItem } from '@kickzone-workspace/customer-order-confirmation';

// Type for the createOrder mutation result (as returned by the server)
export interface CreateOrderResult {
  id: string;
  orderNumber: string;
  trackingNumber: string;
  customerEmail: string;
  customerPhone: string;
  customerName: string;
  shippingAddress: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  zipCode: string;
  country: string;
  subtotal: number;
  shippingCost: number;
  total: number;
  notes: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
  items: Array<{
    productId: string;
    productName: string;
    productImage: string | null;
    quantity: number;
    unitPrice: number;
    totalPrice: number;
    color: string | null;
    size: string | null;
  }>;
}

// Extended OrderData with optional sellerOrders for multi-seller display
export interface OrderConfirmationData extends OrderData {
  sellerOrders?: Array<{
    id: string;
    orderNumber: string;
    total: number;
    trackingNumber?: string | null;
  }>;
}

/**
 * Maps a server order item to the OrderItem type used by the confirmation page.
 */
function mapOrderItem(item: CreateOrderResult['items'][0]): OrderItem {
  return {
    productId: item.productId,
    productName: item.productName,
    productImage: item.productImage,
    quantity: item.quantity,
    unitPrice: item.unitPrice,
    totalPrice: item.totalPrice,
    color: item.color ?? undefined,
    size: item.size ?? undefined,
  };
}

/**
 * Builds order confirmation data from an array of createOrder results.
 *
 * @param orders - Array of fulfilled createOrder mutation results
 * @returns OrderConfirmationData compatible with CustomerOrderConfirmation page, or null if input is empty
 *
 * Behavior:
 * - Uses customer/address/id/orderNumber/trackingNumber/createdAt/notes from the FIRST order
 * - Concatenates all items from all orders
 * - Sums subtotal, shippingCost, total across all orders
 * - Creates sellerOrders array with one entry per order
 * - Ignores null/undefined entries in the input array
 * - Returns null for empty input (after filtering)
 */
export function buildOrderConfirmationData(
  orders: Array<CreateOrderResult | null | undefined>
): OrderConfirmationData | null {
  // Filter out null/undefined entries
  const validOrders = orders.filter((o): o is CreateOrderResult => o != null);

  if (validOrders.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const firstOrder = validOrders[0];

  // Concatenate all items from all orders
  const allItems = validOrders.flatMap((order) => order.items.map(mapOrderItem));

  // Sum totals across all orders
  const subtotal = validOrders.reduce((sum, order) => sum + order.subtotal, 0);
  const shippingCost = validOrders.reduce((sum, order) => sum + order.shippingCost, 0);
  const total = validOrders.reduce((sum, order) => sum + order.total, 0);

  // Build sellerOrders array
  const sellerOrders = validOrders.map((order) => ({
    id: order.id,
    orderNumber: order.orderNumber,
    total: order.total,
    trackingNumber: order.trackingNumber ?? null,
  }));

  return {
    id: firstOrder.id,
    orderNumber: firstOrder.orderNumber,
    trackingNumber: firstOrder.trackingNumber,
    customerEmail: firstOrder.customerEmail,
    customerPhone: firstOrder.customerPhone,
    customerName: firstOrder.customerName,
    shippingAddress: firstOrder.shippingAddress,
    city: firstOrder.city,
    state: firstOrder.state,
    zipCode: firstOrder.zipCode,
    country: firstOrder.country,
    subtotal,
    shippingCost,
    total,
    items: allItems,
    notes: firstOrder.notes,
    createdAt: firstOrder.createdAt,
    sellerOrders,
  };
}
===== FILE: .\libs\client\customers\screens\customer-shopping-cart\src\lib\build-order-confirmation-data.spec.ts =====
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { buildOrderConfirmationData } from './build-order-confirmation-data';

function createMockOrder(overrides: Partial<Parameters<typeof buildOrderConfirmationData>[0][0]> = {}) {
  return {
    id: 'order-1',
    orderNumber: 'ORD-1001',
    trackingNumber: 'TRK-1001',
    customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
    customerPhone: '09121234567',
    customerName: 'Test User',
    shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
    city: 'Tehran',
    state: 'Tehran',
    zipCode: '1234567890',
    country: 'Iran',
    subtotal: 100,
    shippingCost: 10,
    total: 110,
    notes: 'Test notes',
    createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
    items: [
      {
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productName: 'Product 1',
        productImage: '/img1.jpg',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 100,
        color: '#121212',
        size: 'L',
      },
    ],
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe('buildOrderConfirmationData', () => {
  it('returns null for empty array', () => {
    expect(buildOrderConfirmationData([])).toBeNull();
  });

  it('returns null for array with only null/undefined', () => {
    expect(buildOrderConfirmationData([null, undefined])).toBeNull();
  });

  it('builds confirmation data from a single order', () => {
    const order = createMockOrder();
    const result = buildOrderConfirmationData([order]);

    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result?.id).toBe('order-1');
    expect(result?.orderNumber).toBe('ORD-1001');
    expect(result?.trackingNumber).toBe('TRK-1001');
    expect(result?.customerEmail).toBe('test@example.com');
    expect(result?.customerPhone).toBe('09121234567');
    expect(result?.customerName).toBe('Test User');
    expect(result?.shippingAddress).toBe('123 Test St');
    expect(result?.city).toBe('Tehran');
    expect(result?.state).toBe('Tehran');
    expect(result?.zipCode).toBe('1234567890');
    expect(result?.country).toBe('Iran');
    expect(result?.subtotal).toBe(100);
    expect(result?.shippingCost).toBe(10);
    expect(result?.total).toBe(110);
    expect(result?.notes).toBe('Test notes');
    expect(result?.createdAt).toBe('2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z');
    expect(result?.items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result?.items[0].productId).toBe('prod-1');
    expect(result?.items[0].productName).toBe('Product 1');
    expect(result?.items[0].quantity).toBe(2);
    expect(result?.items[0].unitPrice).toBe(50);
    expect(result?.items[0].totalPrice).toBe(100);
    expect(result?.items[0].color).toBe('#121212');
    expect(result?.items[0].size).toBe('L');
    expect(result?.sellerOrders).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result?.sellerOrders?.[0]).toEqual({
      id: 'order-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1001',
      total: 110,
      trackingNumber: 'TRK-1001',
    });
  });

  it('concatenates items and sums totals from two seller orders', () => {
    const order1 = createMockOrder({
      id: 'order-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1001',
      trackingNumber: 'TRK-1001',
      subtotal: 100,
      shippingCost: 10,
      total: 110,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productName: 'Product 1',
          productImage: '/img1.jpg',
          quantity: 2,
          unitPrice: 50,
          totalPrice: 100,
          color: '#121212',
          size: 'L',
        },
      ],
    });

    const order2 = createMockOrder({
      id: 'order-2',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1002',
      trackingNumber: 'TRK-1002',
      subtotal: 200,
      shippingCost: 15,
      total: 215,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-2',
          productName: 'Product 2',
          productImage: '/img2.jpg',
          quantity: 1,
          unitPrice: 200,
          totalPrice: 200,
          color: '#333333',
          size: 'M',
        },
        {
          productId: 'prod-3',
          productName: 'Product 3',
          productImage: '/img3.jpg',
          quantity: 3,
          unitPrice: 30,
          totalPrice: 90,
          color: null,
          size: null,
        },
      ],
    });

    const result = buildOrderConfirmationData([order1, order2]);

    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    // First order's customer/address info used
    expect(result?.id).toBe('order-1');
    expect(result?.orderNumber).toBe('ORD-1001');
    expect(result?.trackingNumber).toBe('TRK-1001');
    // Items concatenated
    expect(result?.items).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(result?.items[0].productId).toBe('prod-1');
    expect(result?.items[1].productId).toBe('prod-2');
    expect(result?.items[2].productId).toBe('prod-3');
    // Totals summed
    expect(result?.subtotal).toBe(300); // 100 + 200
    expect(result?.shippingCost).toBe(25); // 10 + 15
    expect(result?.total).toBe(325); // 110 + 215
    // sellerOrders has both
    expect(result?.sellerOrders).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(result?.sellerOrders?.[0]).toEqual({
      id: 'order-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1001',
      total: 110,
      trackingNumber: 'TRK-1001',
    });
    expect(result?.sellerOrders?.[1]).toEqual({
      id: 'order-2',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-1002',
      total: 215,
      trackingNumber: 'TRK-1002',
    });
  });

  it('ignores null/undefined entries in the input array', () => {
    const order = createMockOrder({ id: 'order-1', orderNumber: 'ORD-1001' });
    const result = buildOrderConfirmationData([null, order, undefined]);

    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result?.id).toBe('order-1');
    expect(result?.orderNumber).toBe('ORD-1001');
    expect(result?.items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(result?.sellerOrders).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('handles items with null color and size', () => {
    const order = createMockOrder({
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'prod-1',
          productName: 'Product 1',
          productImage: '/img1.jpg',
          quantity: 1,
          unitPrice: 50,
          totalPrice: 50,
          color: null,
          size: null,
        },
      ],
    });

    const result = buildOrderConfirmationData([order]);

    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result?.items[0].color).toBeUndefined();
    expect(result?.items[0].size).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('handles trackingNumber being null', () => {
    const order = createMockOrder({ trackingNumber: null as unknown as string });
    const result = buildOrderConfirmationData([order]);

    expect(result).not.toBeNull();
    expect(result?.trackingNumber).toBeNull();
    expect(result?.sellerOrders?.[0].trackingNumber).toBeNull();
  });
});

export interface OrderItem {
  productId: string;
  productName: string;
  productImage?: string | null;
  quantity: number;
  unitPrice: number;
  totalPrice: number;
  color?: string | null;
  size?: string | null;
}

export interface OrderData {
  id: string;
  orderNumber: string;
  trackingNumber: string;
  customerEmail: string;
  customerPhone: string;
  customerName: string;
  shippingAddress: string;
  city: string;
  state: string;
  zipCode: string;
  country: string;
  subtotal: number;
  shippingCost: number;
  total: number;
  shippingMethod?: string;
  shippingPaymentMode?: 'PAY_ON_DELIVERY' | 'PAY_ONLINE';

    id: string;
    orderNumber: string;
    total: number;
    trackingNumber?: string | null;
    shippingMethod?: string;
    shippingPaymentMode?: 'PAY_ON_DELIVERY' | 'PAY_ONLINE';
  }>;
}

const CANONICAL_PANTS_SIZE_RE = /^[1-9]\d?x[1-9]\d?$/;
const displayOrderItemSize = (size?: string | null): string => {
  if (!size) return '';
  const trimmed = size.trim();
  return CANONICAL_PANTS_SIZE_RE.test(trimmed)
    ? formatSizeLabel(SizeKind.Pants, trimmed) || size
    : size;
};

export function CustomerOrderConfirmation() {
  const { LL } = useI18nContext();
  const { currentLocale } = useLanguageContext();
  usePageTitle(() => LL.pageTitles.orderConfirmation());
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const orderData = location.state?.orderData as OrderData | undefined;
  const theme = useTheme();
  const isDark = theme.palette.mode === 'dark';
  const { user } = useCustomersAuth();

  // If no order data, redirect to home
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!orderData) {
      navigate(CUSTOMER_ROUTES.HOME);
    }
  }, [orderData, navigate]);

  if (!orderData) {
    return null;
  }

  const glassCardSx = {
    borderRadius: '24px',
    position: 'relative' as const,
    overflow: 'hidden' as const,
    backdropFilter: 'blur(28px) saturate(190%)',
    WebkitBackdropFilter: 'blur(28px) saturate(190%)',
    background: isDark
      ? 'linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(28, 28, 24, 0.85) 0%, rgba(20, 20, 18, 0.75) 50%, rgba(15, 15, 14, 0.85) 100%)'
      : 'linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.92) 0%, rgba(250, 248, 244, 0.8) 50%, rgba(245, 242, 236, 0.88) 100%)',
    border: isDark
      ? '1px solid rgba(240, 239, 235, 0.12)'
      : '1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.9)',
    boxShadow: isDark

const orderData = {
  id: '1',
  orderNumber: 'ORD-001',
  trackingNumber: 'TRK-001',
  customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
  customerPhone: '1234567890',
  customerName: 'Jane Customer',
  shippingAddress: '123 Test St',

  ...orderData,
  sellerOrders: [
    { id: '1', orderNumber: 'ORD-001', total: 90, trackingNumber: 'TRK-001' },
    { id: '2', orderNumber: 'ORD-002', total: 50, trackingNumber: 'TRK-002' },
  ],
};

describe('CustomerOrderConfirmation', () => {
  it('should render successfully', () => {
    const { baseElement } = render(
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[{ pathname: '/', state: { orderData } }]}>
        <CustomerOrderConfirmation />
      </MemoryRouter>
    );

    expect(baseElement).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('should render successfully with sellerOrders', () => {
    const { baseElement } = render(
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[{ pathname: '/', state: { orderData: orderDataWithSellerOrders } }]}>
        <CustomerOrderConfirmation />
      </MemoryRouter>
    );

    expect(baseElement).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('should return null (and redirect) when orderData is absent', () => {
    const { baseElement } = render(
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[{ pathname: '/' }]}>
        <CustomerOrderConfirmation />
      </MemoryRouter>
    );

    // When no orderData in state, component returns null (redirect handled by useEffect)
    expect(baseElement.querySelector('[data-testid="customers-layout"]')).toBeFalsy();
  });
});

===== FILE: .\libs\server\controllers\src\lib\order.controller.ts =====
import {
  Controller,
  Get,
  Post,
  Put,
  Delete,
  Body,
  Param,
  Query,
  UseGuards,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { OrderService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import {
  CreateOrderInput,
  UpdateOrderInput,
  UpdateOrderStatusInput,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';
import { Order, OrderStatus } from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { HttpAuthGuard } from '@kickzone-workspace/guards';
import { CurrentUser, type AuthenticatedUser } from '@kickzone-workspace/decorators';

@Controller('orders')
@UseGuards(HttpAuthGuard)
export class OrderController {
  constructor(private readonly orderService: OrderService) {}

  @Get()
  async getOrders(
    @Query('first') first = '10',
    @Query('after') after?: string,
    @Query('sellerId') sellerId?: string,
    @Query('status') status?: OrderStatus
  ) {
    const result = await this.orderService.findAll(
      parseInt(first),
      after,
      sellerId,
      status
    );
    return result;
  }

  @Get(':id')
  async getOrder(@Param('id') id: string) {
    return this.orderService.findOne(id);
  }

  @Get('seller/:sellerId')
  async getSellerOrders(
    @Param('sellerId') sellerId: string,
    @Query('first') first = '10',
    @Query('after') after?: string,
    @Query('status') status?: OrderStatus
  ) {
    const result = await this.orderService.findAll(
      parseInt(first),
      after,
      sellerId,
      status
    );
    return result;
  }

  @Get('admin/seller/:sellerId')
  async adminGetSellerOrders(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Param('sellerId') sellerId: string,
    @Query('first') first = '10',
    @Query('after') after?: string,
    @Query('status') status?: OrderStatus
  ) {
    // Admin can view any seller's orders
    const result = await this.orderService.findAll(
      parseInt(first),
      after,
      sellerId,
      status
    );
    return result;
  }

  @Get('admin/all')
  async adminGetAllOrders(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Query('first') first = '10',
    @Query('after') after?: string,
    @Query('status') status?: OrderStatus
  ) {
    // Admin can view all orders from all sellers
    const result = await this.orderService.findAll(
      parseInt(first),
      after,
      undefined,
      status
    );
    return result;
  }

  @Get('my/orders')
  async getMyOrders(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Query('first') first = '10',
    @Query('after') after?: string
  ) {
    const result = await this.orderService.getOrdersBySeller(
      user.id,
      parseInt(first),
      after
    );
    return result;
  }

  @Post()
  async createOrder(@Body() createOrderInput: CreateOrderInput) {
    return this.orderService.createOrder(createOrderInput);
  }

  @Put(':id')
  async updateOrder(
    @Param('id') id: string,
    @Body() updateOrderInput: UpdateOrderInput
  ) {
    return this.orderService.updateOrder(id, updateOrderInput);
  }

  @Put(':id/status')
  async updateOrderStatus(
    @Param('id') id: string,
    @Body() updateOrderStatusInput: UpdateOrderStatusInput
  ) {
    return this.orderService.updateOrderStatus(
      id,
      updateOrderStatusInput.status
    );
  }

  @Delete(':id')
  async deleteOrder(@Param('id') id: string) {
    await this.orderService.deleteOrder(id);
    return { success: true };
  }
}

import {
  Entity,
  PrimaryGeneratedColumn,
  Column,
  ManyToOne,
  CreateDateColumn,
  UpdateDateColumn,
  Index,
  JoinColumn,
} from 'typeorm';
import { ObjectType, Field, ID } from '@nestjs/graphql';
import { Currency } from './currency.entity';

@ObjectType()
@Entity()
export class CurrencyPreference {
  @Field(() => ID)
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn()
  id: number;

  @Field()
  @Column({ unique: true })
  @Index()
  locale: string; // e.g., 'fa-IR', 'en-US', 'en-GB'

  @Field(() => Currency)
  @ManyToOne(() => Currency)
  @JoinColumn({ name: 'currencyId' })
  currency: Currency;

  @Column()
  currencyId: number;

  @Field()
  @Column({ default: true })
  isActive: boolean;

  @Field()
  @CreateDateColumn()
  createdAt: Date;

  @Field()
  @UpdateDateColumn()
  updatedAt: Date;
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\entities\src\lib\currency.entity.ts =====
import {
  Entity,
  PrimaryGeneratedColumn,
  Column,
  CreateDateColumn,
  UpdateDateColumn,
  Index,
} from 'typeorm';
import { ObjectType, Field, ID, Int } from '@nestjs/graphql';

@ObjectType()
@Entity()
export class Currency {
  @Field(() => ID)
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn()
  id: number;

  @Field()
  @Column({ unique: true, length: 3 })
  @Index()
  code: string; // e.g., 'USD', 'IRR', 'GBP'

  @Field()
  @Column()
  name: string; // e.g., 'US Dollar', 'Iranian Rial', 'British Pound'

  @Field()
  @Column()
  symbol: string; // e.g., '$', 'طھظˆظ…ط§ظ†', 'آ£'

  @Field(() => Int)
  @Column({ default: 0 })
  decimalPlaces: number; // For proper rounding (0 for IRR/Toman, 2 for USD)

  @Field()
  @Column({ default: true })
  isActive: boolean;

  @Field()
  @Column({ default: false })
  isBaseCurrency: boolean; // USD will be the base currency

  @Field()
  @CreateDateColumn()
  createdAt: Date;

  @Field()
  @UpdateDateColumn()
  updatedAt: Date;
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\entities\src\lib\order.entity.ts =====
import {
  Field,
  Float,
  ID,
  Int,
  ObjectType,
  registerEnumType,
} from '@nestjs/graphql';
import {
  Column,
  CreateDateColumn,
  Entity,
  JoinColumn,
  ManyToOne,
  OneToMany,
  PrimaryGeneratedColumn,
  UpdateDateColumn,
} from 'typeorm';
import { Product } from './product.entity';
import { SellerInfo, ShippingPaymentMode } from './seller-info.entity';

export enum OrderStatus {
  PENDING = 'PENDING',
  CONFIRMED = 'CONFIRMED',
  PROCESSING = 'PROCESSING',
  SHIPPED = 'SHIPPED',
  DELIVERED = 'DELIVERED',
  CANCELLED = 'CANCELLED',
  REFUNDED = 'REFUNDED',
}

registerEnumType(OrderStatus, {
  name: 'OrderStatus',
});

export enum OrderShippingMethod {
  VIP = 'vip',
  EXPRESS = 'express',
  PICKUP = 'pickup',
}

registerEnumType(OrderShippingMethod, {
  name: 'OrderShippingMethod',
});

@ObjectType()
@Entity('orders')
export class Order {
  @Field(() => ID)
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
  id: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  orderNumber: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  customerEmail: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  customerPhone: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  customerName: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  shippingAddress: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  city: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  state: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  zipCode: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  country: string;

  @Field(() => OrderStatus)
  @Column({
    type: 'enum',
    enum: OrderStatus,
    default: OrderStatus.PENDING,
  })
  status: OrderStatus;

  @Field(() => Boolean, { defaultValue: false })
  @Column({ type: 'boolean', default: false })
  stockReserved!: boolean;

  @Field(() => Float)
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2 })
  subtotal: number;

  @Field(() => Float)
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2 })
  shippingCost: number;

  @Field(() => String, { defaultValue: 'vip' })
  @Column({ type: 'varchar', length: 20, default: 'vip' })
  shippingMethod?: string;

  @Field(() => ShippingPaymentMode, {
    defaultValue: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
  })
  @Column({
    type: 'enum',
    enum: ShippingPaymentMode,
    default: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
  })
  shippingPaymentMode?: ShippingPaymentMode;

  @Field(() => Float)
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2 })
  total: number;

// Payment metadata for verification
@Field(() => Float, { nullable: true })
@Column('decimal', { precision: 12, scale: 2, nullable: true })
paymentAmount?: number | null;

@Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
@Column({ nullable: true })
paymentCurrency?: string | null;

@Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
@Column({ nullable: true })
paymentAuthority?: string | null;

  // Seller snapshot fields - preserve seller info at order time
  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  sellerStoreName?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  sellerPhone?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  sellerAddress?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  notes?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  trackingNumber?: string;

  @Field(() => Date, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ type: 'timestamp', nullable: true })
  paymentVerifiedAt?: Date | null;

  @Field(() => Date)
  @CreateDateColumn()
  createdAt: Date;

  @Field(() => Date)
  @UpdateDateColumn()
  updatedAt: Date;

  @Field(() => Int)
  @Column()
  sellerId: number;

  @ManyToOne(() => SellerInfo, { onDelete: 'CASCADE' })
  @JoinColumn({ name: 'sellerId' })
  seller: SellerInfo;

  @OneToMany(() => OrderItem, (orderItem) => orderItem.order, { cascade: true })
  items: OrderItem[];
}

@ObjectType()
@Entity('order_items')
export class OrderItem {
  @Field(() => ID)
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
  id: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  orderId: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  productId: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  @Column()
  productName: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  productImage?: string;

  @Field(() => Int)
  @Column()
  quantity: number;

  @Field(() => Float)
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2 })
  unitPrice: number;

  @Field(() => Float)
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2 })
  totalPrice: number;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  color?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  size?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  sellerProductId?: string;

  // Product pricing snapshot fields - preserve pricing details at order time
  @Field(() => Float, { nullable: true })
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2, nullable: true })
  originalPrice?: number;

  @Field(() => Float, { nullable: true })
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 5, scale: 2, nullable: true })
  discountPercentage?: number;

  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  productInfoId?: number;

  @ManyToOne(() => Order, (order) => order.items, { onDelete: 'CASCADE' })
  @JoinColumn({ name: 'orderId' })
  order: Order;

  @ManyToOne(() => Product, { onDelete: 'CASCADE' })
  @JoinColumn({ name: 'productId' })
  product: Product;
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\entities\src\lib\seller-info.entity.ts =====
import { ObjectType, Field, Int, Float, registerEnumType } from '@nestjs/graphql';
import {
  Entity,
  Column,
  PrimaryGeneratedColumn,
  Relation,
  OneToOne,
  JoinColumn,
  OneToMany,
} from 'typeorm';
import { User } from './user.entity';
import { SellerProduct } from './seller-product.entity';
import { Connection, Edge, PageInfo } from '@kickzone-workspace/utils';

export enum ShippingPaymentMode {
  PAY_ON_DELIVERY = 'PAY_ON_DELIVERY',
  PAY_ONLINE = 'PAY_ONLINE',
}

registerEnumType(ShippingPaymentMode, {
  name: 'ShippingPaymentMode',
});

@ObjectType()
@Entity()
export class SellerInfo {
  @Field(() => Int)
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn()
  id: number;

  @Field()
  @Column()
  storeName: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  phone?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  address?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  city?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  state?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  zipCode?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  country?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  @Column({ nullable: true })
  businessName?: string;

  @Field({ defaultValue: true })
  @Column({ default: true })
  isActive: boolean;

  @Field({ defaultValue: false })
  @Column({ default: false })
  canManageCatalog: boolean;

  @Field(() => Float, { defaultValue: 0 })
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2, default: 0 })
  shippingFee: number;

  @Field(() => ShippingPaymentMode, {
    defaultValue: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
  })
  @Column({
    type: 'enum',
    enum: ShippingPaymentMode,
    default: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
  })
  shippingPaymentMode?: ShippingPaymentMode;

  @Field(() => User)
  @OneToOne(() => User)
  @JoinColumn()
  user: Relation<User>;

  @OneToMany(() => SellerProduct, (sellerProduct) => sellerProduct.seller)
  sellerProducts: SellerProduct[];
}

@ObjectType()
export class SellerEdge implements Edge<SellerInfo> {
  @Field(() => SellerInfo)
  node: SellerInfo;

  @Field()
  cursor: string;
}

@ObjectType()
export class SellerConnection implements Connection<SellerInfo> {
  @Field(() => [SellerEdge])

  @Field(() => PageInfo)
  pageInfo: PageInfo;
