===== FILE: .\libs\server\entities\src\lib\seller-order.entity.ts =====
import { ObjectType, Field, ID, Float, Int } from '@nestjs/graphql';
import { Order, OrderItem, OrderStatus } from './order.entity';
import { ShippingPaymentMode } from './seller-info.entity';

const GUEST_TOKEN_TAG_RE = /\s*\[GuestToken:\s*[^\]]+\]/g;

export function stripGuestTokenFromNotes(notes?: string | null): string | undefined {
  if (notes === undefined || notes === null) {
    return undefined;
  }
  const cleaned = notes.replace(GUEST_TOKEN_TAG_RE, '').trim();
  return cleaned;
}

@ObjectType()
export class SellerOrder {
  @Field(() => ID)
  id: string;

  @Field(() => String)
  orderNumber: string;

  @Field(() => OrderStatus)
  status: OrderStatus;

  @Field(() => Float)
  subtotal: number;

  @Field(() => Float)
  shippingCost: number;

  @Field(() => String, { defaultValue: 'vip' })
  shippingMethod?: string;

  @Field(() => ShippingPaymentMode, {
    defaultValue: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
  })
  shippingPaymentMode?: ShippingPaymentMode;

  @Field(() => Float)
  total: number;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  notes?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  trackingNumber?: string;

  @Field(() => Date)
  createdAt: Date;

  @Field(() => Date)
  updatedAt: Date;

  @Field(() => Int)
  sellerId: number;

  // Seller snapshot fields
  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  sellerStoreName?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  sellerPhone?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  sellerAddress?: string;

  @Field(() => [OrderItem])
  items: OrderItem[];
}

// Helper function to convert Order to SellerOrder (removes customer PII)
export function toSellerOrder(order: Order): SellerOrder {
  return {
    id: order.id,
    orderNumber: order.orderNumber,
    status: order.status,
    subtotal: order.subtotal,
    shippingCost: order.shippingCost,
    shippingMethod: order.shippingMethod || 'vip',
    shippingPaymentMode:
      order.shippingPaymentMode || ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
    total: order.total,
    notes: stripGuestTokenFromNotes(order.notes),
    trackingNumber: order.trackingNumber,
    createdAt: order.createdAt,
    updatedAt: order.updatedAt,
    sellerId: order.sellerId,
    sellerStoreName: order.sellerStoreName,
    sellerPhone: order.sellerPhone,
    sellerAddress: order.sellerAddress,
    items: order.items || [],
  };
}


===== FILE: .\libs\server\entities\src\lib\seller-product.entity.ts =====
// src/entities/seller-product.entity.ts
import {
  Entity,
  PrimaryGeneratedColumn,
  ManyToOne,
  Column,
  Relation,
  BeforeInsert,
  BeforeUpdate,
  Index,
} from 'typeorm';
import { SellerInfo } from './seller-info.entity';
import { Product } from './product.entity';
import { ProductInfo } from './product-info.entity';
import { ObjectType, Field, Int, Float } from '@nestjs/graphql';

@ObjectType()
@Entity()
@Index(['seller', 'productInfoId'], { unique: true })
export class SellerProduct {
  @Field(() => String)
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid')
  id: string;

  @Field(() => SellerInfo, { nullable: true })
  @ManyToOne(() => SellerInfo, (seller) => seller.sellerProducts)
  seller: SellerInfo;

  @Field(() => Product)
  @ManyToOne(() => Product, (product) => product.sellerProducts)
  product: Relation<Product>;

  @Field(() => ProductInfo)
  @ManyToOne(() => ProductInfo, { nullable: false })
  productInfo: ProductInfo;

  @Field(() => Int)
  @Column({ nullable: false })
  productInfoId: number;

  @Field(() => Float)
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2 })
  price: number;

  @Field(() => Float, { nullable: true })
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 10, scale: 2 })
  costPrice: number;

  @Field(() => Float)
  @Column('decimal', { precision: 5, scale: 2, default: 0 })
  discount: number;

  @Field(() => Int)
  @Column()
  stockQuantity: number;

  @Field(() => Boolean, { defaultValue: false })
  @Column({ type: 'boolean', default: false })
  recommended: boolean;

  @Field(() => Boolean, { defaultValue: false })
  @Column({ type: 'boolean', default: false })
  boxLess: boolean;

  @BeforeInsert()
  @BeforeUpdate()
  setProductInfoId() {
    // If productInfo relation is set but productInfoId is not, set it from the relation
    if (this.productInfo && !this.productInfoId) {
      this.productInfoId = this.productInfo.id;
      console.log(
        'ًں”§ Setting productInfoId:',
        this.productInfoId,
        'from productInfo:',
        this.productInfo.id
      );
    }
    // If productInfoId is set but productInfo relation is not, this is handled in the service layer
  }
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\currency-translation-validation.spec.ts =====
import { BadRequestException, PipeTransform, Type } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ROUTE_ARGS_METADATA } from '@nestjs/common/constants';
import { CurrencyResolver } from './currency.resolver';
import { TranslationResolver } from './translation.resolver';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from './zod-validation.pipe';

type ParamMeta = { index: number; pipes?: PipeTransform[] };

function zodPipe(cls: Type<unknown>, method: string, argIndex: number): PipeTransform {
  const raw = (Reflect.getMetadata(ROUTE_ARGS_METADATA, cls, method) ?? {}) as Record<string, ParamMeta>;
  const param = Object.values(raw).find((p) => p.index === argIndex);
  const pipe = param?.pipes?.find((p) => p instanceof ZodValidationPipe);
  if (!pipe) throw new Error(`no ZodValidationPipe on ${method} arg ${argIndex}`);
  return pipe;
}

const run = (cls: Type<unknown>, method: string, index: number, value: unknown) =>
  zodPipe(cls, method, index).transform(value, { type: 'body' });

describe('currency and translation argument validation', () => {
  it('validates currency create/update', () => {
    expect(() =>
      run(CurrencyResolver, 'createCurrency', 0, { code: 'USD', name: 'US Dollar', symbol: '$', decimalPlaces: 2 })
    ).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run(CurrencyResolver, 'createCurrency', 0, { code: '$$', name: 'x', symbol: '$', decimalPlaces: 2 })).toThrow(
      BadRequestException
    );
    expect(() => run(CurrencyResolver, 'updateCurrency', 1, { decimalPlaces: -1 })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it('validates exchange rates and currency preferences', () => {
    expect(() =>
      run(CurrencyResolver, 'setExchangeRate', 0, { fromCurrencyCode: 'USD', toCurrencyCode: 'IRR', rate: 600000 })
    ).not.toThrow();
    expect(() =>
      run(CurrencyResolver, 'setExchangeRate', 0, { fromCurrencyCode: 'USD', toCurrencyCode: 'IRR', rate: -1 })
    ).toThrow(BadRequestException);
    expect(() => run(CurrencyResolver, 'setCurrencyPreference', 0, 'fa')).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run(CurrencyResolver, 'setCurrencyPreference', 1, 'IR-R')).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it('validates translations', () => {
    const t = { entityType: 'product', entityId: 1, locale: 'fa', fieldName: 'name', value: 'ع©ظپط´' };
    expect(() => run(TranslationResolver, 'setTranslation', 0, t)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run(TranslationResolver, 'setTranslation', 0, { ...t, fieldName: '' })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
    expect(() =>
      run(TranslationResolver, 'bulkTranslateEntities', 0, {
        entityType: 'product', entityIds: [1], sourceLocale: 'en', targetLocales: ['fa'],
      })
    ).not.toThrow();
  });
});
===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\currency.resolver.ts =====
import {
  Resolver,
  Query,
  Mutation,
  Args,
  Int,
  Float,
  InputType,
  Field,
} from '@nestjs/graphql';
import { UseGuards, SetMetadata } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  Currency,
  ExchangeRate,
  CurrencyPreference,
  UserRole,
  User,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { CurrencyService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { RolesGuard } from '@kickzone-workspace/guards';
import { CurrentUser } from '@kickzone-workspace/decorators';
import {
  currencyCodeSchema,
  createCurrencySchema,
  languageCodeSchema,
  setExchangeRateSchema,
  updateCurrencySchema,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/validation';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from './zod-validation.pipe';

// =============== Input Types ===============

@InputType()
export class CreateCurrencyInput {
  @Field()
  code: string;

  @Field()
  name: string;

  @Field()
  symbol: string;

  @Field(() => Int)
  decimalPlaces: number;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  isActive?: boolean;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  isBaseCurrency?: boolean;
}

@InputType()
export class UpdateCurrencyInput {
  @Field({ nullable: true })
  code?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  name?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  symbol?: string;

  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })
  decimalPlaces?: number;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  isActive?: boolean;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  isBaseCurrency?: boolean;
}

@InputType()
export class SetExchangeRateInput {
  @Field()
  fromCurrencyCode: string;

  @Field()
  toCurrencyCode: string;

  @Field(() => Float)
  rate: number;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  effectiveDate?: Date;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  notes?: string;
}

// =============== Resolver ===============

@Resolver(() => Currency)
export class CurrencyResolver {
  constructor(private readonly currencyService: CurrencyService) {}

  // =============== Queries (Public/Protected) ===============

  @Query(() => [Currency])
  async currencies(): Promise<Currency[]> {
    return this.currencyService.findAllCurrencies();
  }

  @Query(() => Currency, { nullable: true })
  async currency(
    @Args('id', { type: () => Int }) id: number
  ): Promise<Currency | null> {
    return this.currencyService.findCurrencyById(id);
  }

  @Query(() => Currency, { nullable: true })
  async currencyByCode(@Args('code') code: string): Promise<Currency | null> {
    return this.currencyService.findCurrencyByCode(code);
  }

  @Query(() => ExchangeRate, { nullable: true })
  async currentExchangeRate(
    @Args('fromCurrencyCode') fromCurrencyCode: string,
    @Args('toCurrencyCode') toCurrencyCode: string
  ): Promise<ExchangeRate | null> {
    return this.currencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate(
      fromCurrencyCode,
      toCurrencyCode
    );
  }

  @Query(() => [ExchangeRate])
  async exchangeRateHistory(
    @Args('fromCurrencyCode') fromCurrencyCode: string,
    @Args('toCurrencyCode') toCurrencyCode: string,
    @Args('limit', { type: () => Int, nullable: true }) limit?: number
  ): Promise<ExchangeRate[]> {
    return this.currencyService.getExchangeRateHistory(
      fromCurrencyCode,
      toCurrencyCode,
      limit || 10
    );
  }

  @Query(() => [CurrencyPreference])
  async currencyPreferences(): Promise<CurrencyPreference[]> {
    return this.currencyService.findAllCurrencyPreferences();
  }

  @Query(() => CurrencyPreference, { nullable: true })
  async currencyPreferenceByLocale(
    @Args('locale') locale: string
  ): Promise<CurrencyPreference | null> {
    return this.currencyService.findCurrencyPreferenceByLocale(locale);
  }

  // =============== Mutations (Admin Only) ===============

  @Mutation(() => Currency)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  async createCurrency(
    @Args('input', new ZodValidationPipe(createCurrencySchema))
    input: CreateCurrencyInput
  ): Promise<Currency> {
    return this.currencyService.createCurrency(input);
  }

  @Mutation(() => Currency)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  async updateCurrency(
    @Args('id', { type: () => Int }) id: number,
    @Args('input', new ZodValidationPipe(updateCurrencySchema))
    input: UpdateCurrencyInput
  ): Promise<Currency> {
    return this.currencyService.updateCurrency(id, input);
  }

  @Mutation(() => ExchangeRate)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  async setExchangeRate(
    @Args('input', new ZodValidationPipe(setExchangeRateSchema))
    input: SetExchangeRateInput,
    @CurrentUser() user?: User
  ): Promise<ExchangeRate> {
    return this.currencyService.setExchangeRate({
      ...input,
      createdById: user?.id,
    });
  }

  @Mutation(() => CurrencyPreference)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  async setCurrencyPreference(
    @Args('locale', new ZodValidationPipe(languageCodeSchema)) locale: string,
    @Args('currencyCode', new ZodValidationPipe(currencyCodeSchema))
    currencyCode: string
  ): Promise<CurrencyPreference> {
    return this.currencyService.setCurrencyPreference(locale, currencyCode);
  }

  @Mutation(() => Boolean)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  async deleteCurrencyPreference(
    @Args('id', { type: () => Int }) id: number
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.currencyService.deleteCurrencyPreference(id);
  }
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\order-validation.spec.ts =====
import { BadRequestException, PipeTransform } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ROUTE_ARGS_METADATA } from '@nestjs/common/constants';
import { OrderStatus } from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { ORDER_STATUSES } from '@kickzone-workspace/validation';
import { OrderResolver } from './order.resolver';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from './zod-validation.pipe';

type ParamMeta = { index: number; pipes?: PipeTransform[] };

function zodPipe(method: string, argIndex: number): PipeTransform {
  const raw = (Reflect.getMetadata(ROUTE_ARGS_METADATA, OrderResolver, method) ?? {}) as Record<
    string,
    ParamMeta
  >;
  const param = Object.values(raw).find((p) => p.index === argIndex);
  const pipe = param?.pipes?.find((p) => p instanceof ZodValidationPipe);
  if (!pipe) throw new Error(`no ZodValidationPipe on ${method} arg ${argIndex}`);
  return pipe;
}

const run = (pipe: PipeTransform, value: unknown) =>
  pipe.transform(value, { type: 'body' });

const item = {
  productId: '101',
  sellerProductId: '301',
  productName: 'Air Jordan 1',
  productImage: null,
  quantity: 1,
  unitPrice: 100,
  totalPrice: 100,
  color: null,
  size: '42',
};

const order = {
  customerName: 'Ali Rezaei',
  customerEmail: '',
  customerPhone: '09121234567',
  shippingAddress: 'Valiasr St, No 12',
  city: 'Tehran',
  state: 'Tehran',
  zipCode: '1234567890',
  country: 'Iran',
  sellerId: 1,
  items: [item],
  notes: '[GuestToken: abc]',
  shippingCost: 0,
};

describe('OrderResolver argument validation pipes', () => {
  it('shared ORDER_STATUSES match the OrderStatus entity enum', () => {
    expect([...ORDER_STATUSES].sort()).toEqual(Object.values(OrderStatus).sort());
  });

  it('createOrder validates createOrderInput', () => {
    const pipe = zodPipe('createOrder', 0);
    expect(() => run(pipe, order)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run(pipe, { ...order, items: [] })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
    expect(() => run(pipe, { ...order, customerPhone: '1' })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it('updateOrder validates id and updateOrderInput', () => {
    expect(() => run(zodPipe('updateOrder', 1), '')).toThrow(BadRequestException);
    const input = zodPipe('updateOrder', 2);
    expect(() => run(input, { city: 'Shiraz' })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run(input, { zipCode: '1' })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it('updateOrder pipe keeps items so the resolver can reject item rewrites', () => {
    const out = run(zodPipe('updateOrder', 2), { items: [item] }) as { items?: unknown[] };
    expect(out.items).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('updateOrderStatus validates id and status', () => {
    const pipe = zodPipe('updateOrderStatus', 1);
    expect(() => run(pipe, { id: 'o-1', status: 'SHIPPED' })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run(pipe, { id: 'o-1', status: 'LOST' })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it('cancelPendingOrder and deleteOrder reject an empty id', () => {
    expect(() => run(zodPipe('cancelPendingOrder', 1), ' ')).toThrow(BadRequestException);
    expect(() => run(zodPipe('deleteOrder', 1), '')).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });
});

import { OrderResolver } from './order.resolver';
import {

  SellerInfo,

import { ForbiddenException, BadRequestException, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';

import type { AuthenticatedUser } from '@kickzone-workspace/decorators';

describe('OrderResolver', () => {
  let resolver: OrderResolver;

    updateOrder: jest.Mock;

  const createMockOrder = (overrides: Partial<Order> = {}): Order => ({

    lastName: 'Doe',
    username: 'johndoe',
    email: 'john@example.com',

    createMockUser({

      email: 'seller@example.com',
      username: 'seller',
      role: UserRole.SELLER,
      ...overrides,

      id: 'admin-user-1',
      email: 'admin@example.com',
      username: 'admin',
      role: UserRole.ADMIN,
      ...overrides,
    });

  const createMockSellerOrder = (overrides: Partial<SellerOrder> = {}): SellerOrder => ({
    id: 'order-1',
    orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
    status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
    subtotal: 100,
    shippingCost: 10,
    total: 110,
    notes: 'Test order',
    trackingNumber: 'TRACK-123',
    createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    updatedAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    sellerId: 1,
    sellerStoreName: 'Test Store',
    sellerPhone: '09123456789',
    sellerAddress: 'Seller Address',
    items: [
      {
        id: 'item-1',
        orderId: 'order-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productName: 'Product 1',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 100,
        sellerProductId: 'seller-prod-1',
      } as OrderItem,
    ],
    ...overrides,
  });

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockOrderService = {
      findAll: jest.fn(),
      findOne: jest.fn(),
      updateOrder: jest.fn(),
      updateOrderStatus: jest.fn(),
      deleteOrder: jest.fn(),
      cancelPendingOrder: jest.fn(),
      getOrdersBySeller: jest.fn(),
      getOrdersByCustomerEmail: jest.fn(),
      recalculateBatchOrdersShipping: jest.fn(),
    };
    mockSellerService = {
      findOneByUserId: jest.fn(),
    };

    resolver = new OrderResolver(
      mockOrderService as never,
      mockSellerService as never
    );
  });

  // Helper to create mock connection
  const createMockConnection = (orders: Order[] = []): OrdersConnection => ({
    edges: orders.map((order, index) => ({
      node: order,
      cursor: `cursor-${index}`,
    })),
    pageInfo: {
      hasNextPage: false,
      hasPreviousPage: false,
      endCursor: orders.length > 0 ? `cursor-${orders.length - 1}` : undefined,
    },
    totalCount: orders.length,
  });

  const createMockSellerConnection = (orders: Order[] = []): SellerOrdersConnection => ({
    edges: orders.map((order, index) => ({
      node: toSellerOrder(order),
      cursor: `cursor-${index}`,
    })),
    pageInfo: {
      hasNextPage: false,
      hasPreviousPage: false,
      endCursor: orders.length > 0 ? `cursor-${orders.length - 1}` : undefined,
    },
    totalCount: orders.length,
  });

  describe('orders query', () => {
    const mockConnection = createMockConnection([createMockOrder()]);

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findAll.mockResolvedValue(mockConnection);
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      await expect(resolver.orders(user, 10, undefined, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
      expect(mockOrderService.findAll).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects SELLER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser();
      await expect(resolver.orders(user, 10, undefined, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
      expect(mockOrderService.findAll).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('allows ADMIN and calls findAll', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const result = await resolver.orders(user, 10, 'cursor-1', 'seller-1', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);

      expect(mockOrderService.findAll).toHaveBeenCalledWith(10, 'cursor-1', 'seller-1', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
      expect(result).toEqual(mockConnection);
    });
  });

  describe('adminSellerOrders query', () => {
    const mockConnection = createMockConnection([createMockOrder()]);

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findAll.mockResolvedValue(mockConnection);
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      await expect(
        resolver.adminSellerOrders(user, 'seller-1', 10, undefined, undefined)
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('rejects SELLER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser();
      await expect(
        resolver.adminSellerOrders(user, 'seller-1', 10, undefined, undefined)
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('allows ADMIN and calls findAll with sellerId', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const result = await resolver.adminSellerOrders(user, 'seller-1', 10, 'cursor-1', OrderStatus.SHIPPED);

      expect(mockOrderService.findAll).toHaveBeenCalledWith(10, 'cursor-1', 'seller-1', OrderStatus.SHIPPED);
      expect(result).toEqual(mockConnection);
    });
  });

  describe('allSellerOrders query', () => {
    const mockConnection = createMockConnection([createMockOrder()]);

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findAll.mockResolvedValue(mockConnection);
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      await expect(resolver.allSellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
    });

    it('rejects SELLER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser();
      await expect(resolver.allSellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
    });

    it('allows ADMIN and calls findAll without sellerId', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const result = await resolver.allSellerOrders(user, 10, 'cursor-1', OrderStatus.DELIVERED);

      expect(mockOrderService.findAll).toHaveBeenCalledWith(10, 'cursor-1', undefined, OrderStatus.DELIVERED);
      expect(result).toEqual(mockConnection);
    });
  });

  describe('myOrders query', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com' });
    const mockConnection = createMockConnection([mockOrder]);

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.getOrdersByCustomerEmail.mockResolvedValue(mockConnection);
    });

    describe('JWT Shape & Role Matrix', () => {
      it('succeeds for CUSTOMER with { sub, userType: "customer", email } (lowercase userType, sub instead of id)', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'u-1', userType: 'customer', email: 'customer@example.com' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.myOrders(user, 10, undefined);

        expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersByCustomerEmail).toHaveBeenCalledWith('customer@example.com', 10, undefined);
        expect(result).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(result[0]).toEqual(mockOrder);
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException for SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" }', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 's-1', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        await expect(resolver.myOrders(user, 10, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
        await expect(resolver.myOrders(user, 10, undefined)).rejects.toThrow('Sellers should use mySellerOrders query instead');
        expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersByCustomerEmail).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException for ADMIN with { id, role: "ADMIN" }', async () => {
        const user = { id: 'a-1', role: 'ADMIN' } as never;
        await expect(resolver.myOrders(user, 10, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
        await expect(resolver.myOrders(user, 10, undefined)).rejects.toThrow('Sellers should use mySellerOrders query instead');
        expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersByCustomerEmail).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException for UNKNOWN role', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'u-1', userType: 'INVALID' } as never;
        await expect(resolver.myOrders(user, 10, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
        await expect(resolver.myOrders(user, 10, undefined)).rejects.toThrow('Sellers should use mySellerOrders query instead');
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException for null user', async () => {
        await expect(resolver.myOrders(null as never, 10, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException for empty object user', async () => {
        await expect(resolver.myOrders({} as never, 10, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
      });
    });
  });

  describe('mySellerOrders query', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.PENDING });
    const mockOrderDelivered = createMockOrder({ id: 'order-2', status: OrderStatus.DELIVERED });
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });
    const mockSellerConnection = createMockSellerConnection([mockOrder, mockOrderDelivered]);

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerConnection);
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with NotFoundException', async () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        NotFoundException
      );
      await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Only sellers can access seller orders'
      );
      expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects ADMIN with NotFoundException', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        NotFoundException
      );
      expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('allows SELLER and calls getOrdersBySeller without status', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      const result = await resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, 'cursor-1', undefined);

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', 10, 'cursor-1', undefined);
      expect(result).toEqual(mockSellerConnection);
    });

    it('allows SELLER and calls getOrdersBySeller with status filter', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      const result = await resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, 'cursor-1', OrderStatus.DELIVERED);

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller).toHaveBeenCalledWith('1', 10, 'cursor-1', OrderStatus.DELIVERED);
      expect(result).toEqual(mockSellerConnection);
    });

    it('maps nodes with toSellerOrder (redacts customer PII)', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      const result = await resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined);

      // Verify the connection structure and that nodes are SellerOrder (no customer PII)
      expect(result.edges).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(result.edges[0].node).toBeDefined();
      expect((result.edges[0].node as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeUndefined();
      expect((result.edges[0].node as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerPhone']).toBeUndefined();
      expect((result.edges[0].node as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerName']).toBeUndefined();
      expect((result.edges[0].node as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['shippingAddress']).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('throws NotFoundException when SELLER has no seller profile', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        NotFoundException
      );
      await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Seller not found for this user'
      );
    });

    describe('JWT Shape, Status Filter, & PII Redaction Matrix', () => {
      const mockOrderProcessing = createMockOrder({ id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING });
      const mockOrderPending = createMockOrder({ id: 'order-2', status: OrderStatus.PENDING });
      const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 42, user: { id: 'seller-user-42' } as never });
      const mockSellerConnection = createMockSellerConnection([mockOrderProcessing, mockOrderPending]);

      beforeEach(() => {
        mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerConnection);
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
      });

      it('succeeds for SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" } (lowercase userType and sub claim)', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'seller-user-42', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined);

        expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-42');
        expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller).toHaveBeenCalledWith('42', 10, undefined, undefined);
        expect(result).toEqual(mockSellerConnection);
      });

      it('forwards status filter (PROCESSING) to getOrdersBySeller', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'seller-user-42', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        await resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, OrderStatus.PROCESSING);

        expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller).toHaveBeenCalledWith('42', 10, undefined, OrderStatus.PROCESSING);
      });

      it('forwards undefined status to getOrdersBySeller', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'seller-user-42', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        await resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined);

        expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller).toHaveBeenCalledWith('42', 10, undefined, undefined);
      });

      it('strips customer PII via toSellerOrder', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'seller-user-42', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined);

        expect(result.edges).toHaveLength(2);
        result.edges.forEach((edge) => {
          expect((edge.node as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeUndefined();
          expect((edge.node as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerPhone']).toBeUndefined();
          expect((edge.node as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerName']).toBeUndefined();
          expect((edge.node as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['shippingAddress']).toBeUndefined();
        });
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException for CUSTOMER with { sub, userType: "customer" }', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'c-1', userType: 'customer' } as never;
        await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
        await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow('Only sellers can access seller orders');
        expect(mockOrderService.getOrdersBySeller).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException for null user', async () => {
        await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(null as never, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
        await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(null as never, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow('Only sellers can access seller orders');
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException for empty object user', async () => {
        await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders({} as never, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
        await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders({} as never, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow('Only sellers can access seller orders');
      });

      it('throws NotFoundException when sellerService.findOneByUserId returns null', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'seller-user-42', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(null);

        await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
        await expect(resolver.mySellerOrders(user, 10, undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow('Seller not found for this user');
      });
    });
  });

  describe('order query', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com', sellerId: 1 });
    const mockSellerOrder = toSellerOrder(mockOrder);
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    describe('ADMIN role', () => {
      it('allows ADMIN to read any order', async () => {
        const user = createMockAdminUser();
        const result = await resolver.order(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
        expect(result).toEqual(mockOrder);
      });
    });

    describe('SELLER role', () => {
      it('allows SELLER to read own order and returns redacted SellerOrder', async () => {
        const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
        const result = await resolver.order(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
        expect(result).toEqual(mockSellerOrder);
        // Verify customer PII is not present
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeUndefined();
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerPhone']).toBeUndefined();
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerName']).toBeUndefined();
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['shippingAddress']).toBeUndefined();
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException when SELLER tries to read another seller order', async () => {
        const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'other-seller-user' });
        const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-seller-user' } as never });
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);

        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          ForbiddenException
        );
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          'You do not have permission to view this order'
        );
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException when SELLER has no seller profile', async () => {
        const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(null);

        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          ForbiddenException
        );
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          'You do not have permission to view this order'
        );
      });
    });

    describe('CUSTOMER role', () => {
      it('allows CUSTOMER to read own order', async () => {
        const user = createMockUser({ email: 'customer@example.com' });
        const result = await resolver.order(user, 'order-1');

        expect(result).toEqual(mockOrder);
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException when CUSTOMER tries to read another customer order', async () => {
        const user = createMockUser({ email: 'other@example.com' });
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          ForbiddenException
        );
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          'You do not have permission to view this order'
        );
      });
    });

    describe('UNKNOWN role', () => {
      it('throws ForbiddenException for unknown role', async () => {
        const user = { id: 'user-1', userType: 'INVALID' };
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          ForbiddenException
        );
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow('Access denied');
      });
    });

    describe('JWT Shape { sub, userType } Claims', () => {
      const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com', sellerId: 1 });
      const mockSellerOrder = toSellerOrder(mockOrder);
      const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 's-user' } as never });

      beforeEach(() => {
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
      });

      it('allows SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" } owning order to receive redacted SellerOrder', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 's-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.order(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s-user');
        expect(result).toEqual(mockSellerOrder);
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeUndefined();
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerPhone']).toBeUndefined();
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerName']).toBeUndefined();
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['shippingAddress']).toBeUndefined();
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException for non-owner SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" }', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'other-s-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-s-user' } as never });
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);

        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow('You do not have permission to view this order');
      });

      it('allows CUSTOMER with { sub, userType: "customer", email } to read own order', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'customer@example.com' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.order(user, 'order-1');

        expect(result).toEqual(mockOrder);
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException for CUSTOMER with { sub, userType: "customer" } reading another customer order', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'other-c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'other@example.com' } as never;
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
        await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow('You do not have permission to view this order');
      });

      it('allows ADMIN with { sub, userType: "admin" } to read any order', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'a-user', userType: 'admin' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.order(user, 'order-1');

        expect(result).toEqual(mockOrder);
      });
    });
  });

  describe('updateOrderStatus mutation', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ sellerId: 1 });
    const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
    const updatedSellerOrder = toSellerOrder(updatedOrder);
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
      mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
      ).rejects.toThrow('Customers are not allowed to update order status');
    });

    it('rejects SELLER trying to update another seller order', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'other-seller-user' });
      const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-seller-user' } as never });
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
      ).rejects.toThrow('You can only update status for your own seller orders');
    });

    it('rejects SELLER with no seller profile', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('rejects SELLER moving own PENDING order to CONFIRMED (payment flow confirms orders)', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('allows ADMIN to update any order and returns full Order', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const result = await resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, {
        id: 'order-1',
        status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
      });

      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.SHIPPED);
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedOrder);
    });

    it('rejects UNKNOWN role', async () => {
      const user = { id: 'user-1', userType: 'INVALID' };
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    describe('JWT Shape { sub, userType } Claims', () => {
      const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ sellerId: 1 });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      const updatedSellerOrder = toSellerOrder(updatedOrder);
      const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 's-user' } as never });

      beforeEach(() => {
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
        mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
      });

      it('rejects SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" } moving own PENDING order to CONFIRMED', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 's-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        await expect(
          resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
        ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

        expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s-user');
        expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });

      it('allows ADMIN with { sub, userType: "admin" } to update any order and receive full Order', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'a-user', userType: 'admin' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, {
          id: 'order-1',
          status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
        });

        expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.SHIPPED);
        expect(result).toEqual(updatedOrder);
        expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeDefined();
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException for SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" } not owning order', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'other-s-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-s-user' } as never });
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);

        await expect(
          resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
        ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
        await expect(
          resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
        ).rejects.toThrow('You can only update status for your own seller orders');
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException for CUSTOMER with { sub, userType: "customer" }', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer' } as never;
        await expect(
          resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
        ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
        await expect(
          resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED })
        ).rejects.toThrow('Customers are not allowed to update order status');
      });
    });
  });

  describe('cancelPendingOrder mutation', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com', sellerId: 1 });
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
      mockOrderService.cancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    describe('CUSTOMER role', () => {
      it('allows CUSTOMER to cancel own PENDING order', async () => {
        const user = createMockUser({ email: 'customer@example.com' });
        const result = await resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockOrderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
        expect(result).toBe(true);
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException when CUSTOMER tries to cancel another customer order', async () => {
        const user = createMockUser({ email: 'other@example.com' });
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          ForbiddenException
        );
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          'You do not have permission to cancel this order'
        );
      });

      it('throws BadRequestException when CUSTOMER tries to cancel CONFIRMED order', async () => {
        const confirmedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, customerEmail: 'customer@example.com' });
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(confirmedOrder);

        const user = createMockUser({ email: 'customer@example.com' });
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          BadRequestException
        );
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          'Only pending orders can be cancelled'
        );
      });
    });

    describe('SELLER role', () => {
      it('allows SELLER to cancel own PENDING order', async () => {
        const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
        const result = await resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
        expect(mockOrderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
        expect(result).toBe(true);
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException when SELLER tries to cancel another seller order', async () => {
        const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'other-seller-user' });
        const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-seller-user' } as never });
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);

        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          ForbiddenException
        );
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException when SELLER has no seller profile', async () => {
        const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(null);

        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(
          ForbiddenException
        );
      });
    });

    describe('ADMIN role', () => {
      it('allows ADMIN to cancel any PENDING order', async () => {
        const user = createMockAdminUser();
        const result = await resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockOrderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
        expect(result).toBe(true);
      });
    });

    describe('JWT Shape { sub, userType } Claims', () => {
      const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'buyer@example.com', sellerId: 1 });
      const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 's-user' } as never });

      beforeEach(() => {
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
        mockOrderService.cancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
      });

      it('allows SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" } owning PENDING order to cancel', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 's-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s-user');
        expect(mockOrderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
        expect(result).toBe(true);
      });

      it('allows CUSTOMER with { sub, userType: "customer", email } to cancel own PENDING order', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'buyer@example.com' } as never;
        const result = await resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockOrderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
        expect(result).toBe(true);
      });

      it('throws BadRequestException for non-PENDING order (CONFIRMED) for SELLER', async () => {
        const confirmedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, sellerId: 1 });
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(confirmedOrder);

        const user = { sub: 's-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow('Only pending orders can be cancelled');
      });

      it('throws BadRequestException for non-PENDING order (CONFIRMED) for CUSTOMER', async () => {
        const confirmedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, customerEmail: 'buyer@example.com' });
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(confirmedOrder);

        const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'buyer@example.com' } as never;
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow('Only pending orders can be cancelled');
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException for SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" } not owning order', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'other-s-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
        const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-s-user' } as never });
        mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);

        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      });

      it('throws ForbiddenException for CUSTOMER with { sub, userType: "customer" } not owning order', async () => {
        const user = { sub: 'other-c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'other@example.com' } as never;
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
        await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow('You do not have permission to cancel this order');
      });
    });
  });

  describe('updateOrder mutation', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder();
    const updateInput = {
      customerName: 'Jane Doe',
      notes: 'Updated notes',
    };

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
      mockOrderService.updateOrder.mockResolvedValue({ ...mockOrder, ...updateInput });
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrder(user, 'order-1', updateInput as never)
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('rejects SELLER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser();
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrder(user, 'order-1', updateInput as never)
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('allows ADMIN to update order (non-financial fields)', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const result = await resolver.updateOrder(user, 'order-1', updateInput as never);

      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', updateInput);
      expect(result.customerName).toBe('Jane Doe');
    });

    it('throws BadRequestException when ADMIN tries to update items', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const inputWithItems = {
        ...updateInput,
        items: [{ productId: 'prod-1', quantity: 1, unitPrice: 50, totalPrice: 50 }],
      };

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrder(user, 'order-1', inputWithItems as never)
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrder(user, 'order-1', inputWithItems as never)
      ).rejects.toThrow('Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation');
    });

    it('throws BadRequestException when ADMIN tries to update CONFIRMED order', async () => {
      const confirmedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(confirmedOrder);

      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrder(user, 'order-1', updateInput as never)
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrder(user, 'order-1', updateInput as never)
      ).rejects.toThrow('Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation');
    });
  });

  describe('deleteOrder mutation', () => {
    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.deleteOrder.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
      mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      await expect(resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('rejects SELLER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser();
      await expect(resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('allows ADMIN to delete PENDING order directly', async () => {
      const pendingOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PENDING });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(pendingOrder);

      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const result = await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(result).toBe(true);
    });

    it('allows ADMIN to delete CANCELLED order directly', async () => {
      const cancelledOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(cancelledOrder);

      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
    });

    it('cancels CONFIRMED order before deletion to restore stock', async () => {
      const confirmedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(confirmedOrder);

      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
      expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
    });

    it('cancels PROCESSING order before deletion to restore stock', async () => {
      const processingOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(processingOrder);

      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
      expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
    });

    it('cancels SHIPPED order before deletion to restore stock', async () => {
      const shippedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(shippedOrder);

      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
      expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
    });

    it('cancels order with stockReserved flag before deletion', async () => {
      const orderWithReservedStock = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PENDING });
      (orderWithReservedStock as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['stockReserved'] = true;
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(orderWithReservedStock);

      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
      expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
    });

    describe('Stock Restoration Guard - Call Order Verification', () => {
      const verifyCallOrder = async (order: Order) => {
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
        mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
        mockOrderService.deleteOrder.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

        const user = createMockAdminUser();
        await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

        const updateCalls = mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus.mock.invocationCallOrder;
        const deleteCalls = mockOrderService.deleteOrder.mock.invocationCallOrder;

        expect(updateCalls).toBeDefined();
        expect(deleteCalls).toBeDefined();
        expect(updateCalls[0]).toBeLessThan(deleteCalls[0]);
        expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
        expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      };

      it('calls updateOrderStatus BEFORE deleteOrder for CONFIRMED order', async () => {
        await verifyCallOrder(createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED }));
      });

      it('calls updateOrderStatus BEFORE deleteOrder for PROCESSING order', async () => {
        await verifyCallOrder(createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING }));
      });

      it('calls updateOrderStatus BEFORE deleteOrder for SHIPPED order', async () => {
        await verifyCallOrder(createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED }));
      });

      it('calls updateOrderStatus BEFORE deleteOrder for PENDING order with stockReserved=true', async () => {
        const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PENDING });
        (order as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['stockReserved'] = true;
        await verifyCallOrder(order);
      });

      it('does NOT call updateOrderStatus for PENDING order with stockReserved=false', async () => {
        const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PENDING });
        (order as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['stockReserved'] = false;
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
        mockOrderService.deleteOrder.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

        const user = createMockAdminUser();
        await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      });

      it('does NOT call updateOrderStatus for PENDING order with stockReserved=undefined', async () => {
        const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PENDING });
        (order as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['stockReserved'] = undefined;
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
        mockOrderService.deleteOrder.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

        const user = createMockAdminUser();
        await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      });

      it('does NOT call updateOrderStatus for DELIVERED order', async () => {
        const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.DELIVERED });
        (order as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['stockReserved'] = false;
        mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
        mockOrderService.deleteOrder.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

        const user = createMockAdminUser();
        await resolver.deleteOrder(user, 'order-1');

        expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        expect(mockOrderService.deleteOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      });
    });
  });

  describe('getUserId helper', () => {
    it('extracts id from user.id', () => {
      const user = { id: 'user-123' };
      expect(resolver['getUserId'](user as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('user-123');
    });

    it('extracts id from user.sub when id is missing', () => {
      const user = { sub: 'sub-456' };
      expect(resolver['getUserId'](user as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('sub-456');
    });

    it('returns empty string when both id and sub are missing', () => {
      const user = {};
      expect(resolver['getUserId'](user as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('');
    });
  });

  describe('getUserRole helper', () => {
    it('returns ADMIN for role ADMIN', () => {
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ role: UserRole.ADMIN } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('ADMIN');
    });

    it('returns SELLER for role SELLER', () => {
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ role: UserRole.SELLER } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('SELLER');
    });

    it('returns CUSTOMER for role CUSTOMER', () => {
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('CUSTOMER');
    });

    it('normalizes lowercase role', () => {
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ role: 'admin' } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('ADMIN');
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ role: 'seller' } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('SELLER');
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ role: 'customer' } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('CUSTOMER');
    });

    it('uses userType when role is missing', () => {
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ userType: 'ADMIN' } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('ADMIN');
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ userType: 'seller' } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('SELLER');
    });

    it('returns UNKNOWN for invalid role', () => {
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({ role: 'INVALID' } as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('UNKNOWN');
      expect(resolver['getUserRole']({} as AuthenticatedUser)).toBe('UNKNOWN');
    });
  });

  describe('assertAdmin helper', () => {
    it('does not throw for ADMIN', () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      expect(() => resolver['assertAdmin'](user as AuthenticatedUser)).not.toThrow();
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException for SELLER', () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser();
      expect(() => resolver['assertAdmin'](user as AuthenticatedUser)).toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException for CUSTOMER', () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      expect(() => resolver['assertAdmin'](user as AuthenticatedUser)).toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException for UNKNOWN', () => {
      const user = { role: 'INVALID' };
      expect(() => resolver['assertAdmin'](user as AuthenticatedUser)).toThrow(ForbiddenException);
    });
  });

  describe('isCustomerOrderOwner helper', () => {
    it('returns true when both emails match (case-insensitive)', () => {
      const user = { email: 'Customer@Example.COM' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const order = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com' });
      expect(resolver['isCustomerOrderOwner'](user, order)).toBe(true);
    });

    it('returns false when emails differ', () => {
      const user = { email: 'other@example.com' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const order = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com' });
      expect(resolver['isCustomerOrderOwner'](user, order)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('returns false when user email is empty string', () => {
      const user = { email: '' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const order = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com' });
      expect(resolver['isCustomerOrderOwner'](user, order)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('returns false when order customerEmail is empty string', () => {
      const user = { email: 'customer@example.com' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const order = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: '' });
      expect(resolver['isCustomerOrderOwner'](user, order)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('returns false when both emails are empty strings', () => {
      const user = { email: '' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const order = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: '' });
      expect(resolver['isCustomerOrderOwner'](user, order)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('returns false when user email is undefined', () => {
      const user = {} as AuthenticatedUser;
      const order = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com' });
      expect(resolver['isCustomerOrderOwner'](user, order)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('returns false when order customerEmail is undefined', () => {
      const user = { email: 'customer@example.com' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const order = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: undefined as never });
      expect(resolver['isCustomerOrderOwner'](user, order)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('returns false when user is null', () => {
      const user = null as never;
      const order = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: 'customer@example.com' });
      expect(resolver['isCustomerOrderOwner'](user, order)).toBe(false);
    });
  });

  describe('toSellerOrder GuestToken redaction', () => {
    it('strips [GuestToken: ...] from notes while preserving regular text', () => {
      const order = createMockOrder({
        notes: 'Customer note [GuestToken: guest_1234567890abcdef] end note',
      });
      const sellerOrder = toSellerOrder(order);
      expect(sellerOrder.notes).toBe('Customer note end note');
    });

    it('strips [GuestToken: ...] when it is the only content', () => {
      const order = createMockOrder({
        notes: '[GuestToken: guest_1234567890abcdef]',
      });
      const sellerOrder = toSellerOrder(order);
      expect(sellerOrder.notes).toBe('');
    });

    it('handles undefined notes', () => {
      const order = createMockOrder({ notes: undefined });
      const sellerOrder = toSellerOrder(order);
      expect(sellerOrder.notes).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('handles null notes', () => {
      const order = createMockOrder({ notes: null as never });
      const sellerOrder = toSellerOrder(order);
      expect(sellerOrder.notes).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('preserves notes without GuestToken tag', () => {
      const order = createMockOrder({ notes: 'Just a regular note' });
      const sellerOrder = toSellerOrder(order);
      expect(sellerOrder.notes).toBe('Just a regular note');
    });
  });

  describe('order query - empty email edge case', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: '', sellerId: 1 });
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException when CUSTOMER email is empty string and order email is empty string', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: '' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(resolver.order(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow('You do not have permission to view this order');
    });
  });

  describe('cancelPendingOrder mutation - empty email edge case', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ customerEmail: '', sellerId: 1 });
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
      mockOrderService.cancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue(true);
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException when CUSTOMER email is empty string and order email is empty string', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: '' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(resolver.cancelPendingOrder(user, 'order-1')).rejects.toThrow('You do not have permission to cancel this order');
    });
  });

  describe('recalculateBatchOrdersShipping mutation', () => {
    const mockOrder1 = createMockOrder({
      id: 'order-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-001',
      customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
      status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      notes: 'Test order [GuestToken: guest_abc123]',
      total: 110,
      shippingCost: 10,
    });
    const mockOrder2 = createMockOrder({
      id: 'order-2',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-002',
      customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
      sellerId: 2,
      sellerStoreName: 'Store 2',
      status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      notes: 'Test order [GuestToken: guest_abc123]',
      total: 220,
      shippingCost: 15,
    });
    const recalculatedOrders = [
      { ...mockOrder1, shippingCost: 8, total: 108 },
      { ...mockOrder2, shippingCost: 12, total: 217 },
    ];

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return mockOrder1;
        if (id === 'order-2') return mockOrder2;
        return null;
      });
      mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping.mockResolvedValue(recalculatedOrders);
    });

    it('recalculates shipping across multiple PENDING orders for same guest token', async () => {
      const result = await resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(
        ['order-1', 'order-2'],
        undefined, // no user
        'guest_abc123'
      );

      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
      expect(mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).toHaveBeenCalledWith([
        mockOrder1,
        mockOrder2,
      ]);
      expect(result).toEqual(recalculatedOrders);
      expect(result[0].shippingCost).toBe(8);
      expect(result[1].shippingCost).toBe(12);
    });

    it('recalculates shipping for authenticated customer (no guest token)', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'customer@example.com' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const ordersNoGuest = [
        { ...mockOrder1, notes: 'Test order' },
        { ...mockOrder2, notes: 'Test order' },
      ];
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return ordersNoGuest[0];
        if (id === 'order-2') return ordersNoGuest[1];
        return null;
      });

      const result = await resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], user, undefined);

      expect(mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ordersNoGuest);
      expect(result).toEqual(recalculatedOrders);
    });

    it('throws BadRequestException when no orderIds provided', async () => {
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping([], undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping([], undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'At least one orderId is required'
      );
    });

    it('throws BadRequestException when orderIds contain only whitespace', async () => {
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['   ', ''], undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['   ', ''], undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'At least one orderId is required'
      );
    });

    it('throws BadRequestException when an order is not PENDING', async () => {
      const confirmedOrder = createMockOrder({
        id: 'order-1',
        customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        notes: '[GuestToken: guest_abc123]',
      });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return confirmedOrder;
        if (id === 'order-2') return mockOrder2;
        return null;
      });

      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, 'guest_abc123')).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, 'guest_abc123')).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order order-1 is not in PENDING status'
      );
    });

    it('throws BadRequestException when orders have different customer emails', async () => {
      const otherEmailOrder = createMockOrder({
        id: 'order-2',
        customerEmail: 'other@example.com',
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        notes: '[GuestToken: guest_abc123]',
      });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return mockOrder1;
        if (id === 'order-2') return otherEmailOrder;
        return null;
      });

      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, 'guest_abc123')).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, 'guest_abc123')).rejects.toThrow(
        'All orders in a batch must belong to the same customer'
      );
    });

    it('throws BadRequestException when orders have different guest tokens', async () => {
      const differentTokenOrder = createMockOrder({
        id: 'order-2',
        customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        notes: '[GuestToken: guest_different]',
      });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return mockOrder1;
        if (id === 'order-2') return differentTokenOrder;
        return null;
      });

      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, 'guest_abc123')).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, 'guest_abc123')).rejects.toThrow(
        'All orders in a batch must share the same guest checkout token'
      );
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException when guest token is provided but does not match', async () => {
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, 'wrong_token')).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, 'wrong_token')).rejects.toThrow(
        'Invalid guest token for batch shipping recalculation'
      );
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException when guest token is missing for guest checkout', async () => {
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Invalid guest token for batch shipping recalculation'
      );
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException when authenticated user email does not match order email', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'different@example.com' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const ordersNoGuest = [
        { ...mockOrder1, notes: 'Test order' },
        { ...mockOrder2, notes: 'Test order' },
      ];
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return ordersNoGuest[0];
        if (id === 'order-2') return ordersNoGuest[1];
        return null;
      });

      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], user, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], user, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Not allowed to recalculate shipping for this order batch'
      );
    });

    it('deduplicates orderIds and preserves order', async () => {
      await resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2', 'order-1'], undefined, 'guest_abc123');

      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
      expect(mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).toHaveBeenCalledWith([mockOrder1, mockOrder2]);
    });
