    it('throws ForbiddenException for anonymous user with no guestToken on orders without GuestToken but with customerEmail', async () => {
      const ordersNoGuest = [
        { ...mockOrder1, notes: 'Test order', customerEmail: 'a@x.com' },
        { ...mockOrder2, notes: 'Test order', customerEmail: 'a@x.com' },
      ];
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return ordersNoGuest[0];
        if (id === 'order-2') return ordersNoGuest[1];
        return null;
      });

      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], undefined, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Not allowed to recalculate shipping for this order batch'
      );
      expect(mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException when batch has GuestToken but logged-in caller provides no guestToken', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'customer@example.com' } as AuthenticatedUser;

      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], user, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], user, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Invalid guest token for batch shipping recalculation'
      );
      expect(mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('throws ForbiddenException when logged-in user has empty email and orders have empty customerEmail (no token)', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: '' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const ordersEmptyEmail = [
        { ...mockOrder1, notes: 'Test order', customerEmail: '' },
        { ...mockOrder2, notes: 'Test order', customerEmail: '' },
      ];
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return ordersEmptyEmail[0];
        if (id === 'order-2') return ordersEmptyEmail[1];
        return null;
      });

      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], user, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        ForbiddenException
      );
      await expect(resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], user, undefined)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Not allowed to recalculate shipping for this order batch'
      );
      expect(mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('succeeds for logged-in owner with case-insensitive email match (A@x.com vs a@x.com) and no token', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'A@x.com' } as AuthenticatedUser;
      const ordersNoGuest = [
        { ...mockOrder1, notes: 'Test order', customerEmail: 'a@x.com' },
        { ...mockOrder2, notes: 'Test order', customerEmail: 'a@x.com' },
      ];
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockImplementation(async (id: string) => {
        if (id === 'order-1') return ordersNoGuest[0];
        if (id === 'order-2') return ordersNoGuest[1];
        return null;
      });

      const result = await resolver.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(['order-1', 'order-2'], user, undefined);

      expect(mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(mockOrderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).toHaveBeenCalledWith(ordersNoGuest);
      expect(result).toEqual(recalculatedOrders);
    });
  });
});
===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\order.resolver.status-transitions.spec.ts =====
import { OrderResolver } from './order.resolver';
import {
  Order,
  OrderItem,
  OrderStatus,
  User,
  UserRole,
  toSellerOrder,
  SellerOrder,
  SellerInfo,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { ForbiddenException, BadRequestException, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';

describe('OrderResolver - Seller Status Transitions', () => {
  let resolver: OrderResolver;
  let mockOrderService: {
    findOne: jest.Mock;
    updateOrderStatus: jest.Mock;
  };
  let mockSellerService: {
    findOneByUserId: jest.Mock;
  };

  // Test data factories (re-declared locally per spec)
  const createMockSellerInfo = (overrides: Partial<SellerInfo> = {}): SellerInfo => ({
    id: 1,
    storeName: 'Test Store',
    phone: '09123456789',
    address: 'Seller Address',
    city: 'Tehran',
    state: 'Tehran',
    zipCode: '12345',
    country: 'Iran',
    businessName: 'Test Business',
    isActive: true,
    canManageCatalog: true,
    shippingFee: 10,
    user: {} as never,
    sellerProducts: [],
    ...overrides,
  });

  const createMockOrder = (overrides: Partial<Order> = {}): Order => ({
    id: 'order-1',
    orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
    customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
    customerPhone: '1234567890',
    customerName: 'John Doe',
    shippingAddress: '123 Main St',
    city: 'Tehran',
    state: 'Tehran',
    zipCode: '12345',
    country: 'Iran',
    status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
    subtotal: 100,
    shippingCost: 10,
    total: 110,
    sellerId: 1,
    sellerStoreName: 'Test Store',
    sellerPhone: '09123456789',
    sellerAddress: 'Seller Address',
    notes: 'Test order',
    trackingNumber: 'TRACK-123',
    stockReserved: false,
    createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    updatedAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    items: [
      {
        id: 'item-1',
        orderId: 'order-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productName: 'Product 1',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 100,
        sellerProductId: 'seller-prod-1',
      } as OrderItem,
    ],
    seller: createMockSellerInfo(),
    ...overrides,
  });

  const createMockSellerUser = (overrides: Partial<User> = {}): User =>
    createMockUser({
      id: 'seller-user-1',
      email: 'seller@example.com',
      username: 'seller',
      role: UserRole.SELLER,
      ...overrides,
    });

  const createMockAdminUser = (overrides: Partial<User> = {}): User =>
    createMockUser({
      id: 'admin-user-1',
      email: 'admin@example.com',
      username: 'admin',
      role: UserRole.ADMIN,
      ...overrides,
    });

  const createMockUser = (overrides: Partial<User> = {}): User => ({
    id: 'user-1',
    name: 'John',
    lastName: 'Doe',
    username: 'johndoe',
    email: 'john@example.com',
    password: 'hashed',
    role: UserRole.CUSTOMER,
    locale: 'en',
    ...overrides,
  });

  const createMockSellerOrder = (overrides: Partial<SellerOrder> = {}): SellerOrder => ({
    id: 'order-1',
    orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
    status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
    subtotal: 100,
    shippingCost: 10,
    total: 110,
    notes: 'Test order',
    trackingNumber: 'TRACK-123',
    createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    updatedAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    sellerId: 1,
    sellerStoreName: 'Test Store',
    sellerPhone: '09123456789',
    sellerAddress: 'Seller Address',
    items: [
      {
        id: 'item-1',
        orderId: 'order-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productName: 'Product 1',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 100,
        sellerProductId: 'seller-prod-1',
      } as OrderItem,
    ],
    ...overrides,
  });

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockOrderService = {
      findOne: jest.fn(),
      updateOrderStatus: jest.fn(),
    };
    mockSellerService = {
      findOneByUserId: jest.fn(),
    };

    resolver = new OrderResolver(
      mockOrderService as never,
      mockSellerService as never
    );
  });

  describe('updateOrderStatus - Seller allowed transitions', () => {
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    // Valid forward transitions: CONFIRMED->PROCESSING, PROCESSING->SHIPPED, SHIPPED->DELIVERED
    it.each([
      ['CONFIRMED -> PROCESSING', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, OrderStatus.PROCESSING],
      ['PROCESSING -> SHIPPED', OrderStatus.PROCESSING, OrderStatus.SHIPPED],
      ['SHIPPED -> DELIVERED', OrderStatus.SHIPPED, OrderStatus.DELIVERED],
    ])('allows owning SELLER to move %s', async (_, fromStatus, toStatus) => {
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: fromStatus });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: toStatus });
      // SellerOrder should not have customerEmail
      const updatedSellerOrder = toSellerOrder(updatedOrder);

      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      const result = await resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: toStatus });

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', toStatus);
      // Result should be redacted SellerOrder (no customerEmail)
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedSellerOrder);
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeUndefined();
    });
  });

  describe('updateOrderStatus - Seller rejected transitions', () => {
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    // Rejected transitions for owning SELLER
    it.each([
      ['PENDING -> CONFIRMED', OrderStatus.PENDING, OrderStatus.CONFIRMED],
      ['PENDING -> CANCELLED', OrderStatus.PENDING, OrderStatus.CANCELLED],
      ['CONFIRMED -> CANCELLED', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, OrderStatus.CANCELLED],
      ['CONFIRMED -> REFUNDED', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, OrderStatus.REFUNDED],
      ['SHIPPED -> REFUNDED', OrderStatus.SHIPPED, OrderStatus.REFUNDED],
      ['DELIVERED -> REFUNDED', OrderStatus.DELIVERED, OrderStatus.REFUNDED],
      ['CONFIRMED -> SHIPPED (skip)', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, OrderStatus.SHIPPED],
      ['PROCESSING -> CONFIRMED (backwards)', OrderStatus.PROCESSING, OrderStatus.CONFIRMED],
      ['CONFIRMED -> CONFIRMED (no-op)', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, OrderStatus.CONFIRMED],
      ['DELIVERED -> PROCESSING (backwards)', OrderStatus.DELIVERED, OrderStatus.PROCESSING],
    ])('rejects owning SELLER %s with BadRequestException and does not call service', async (_, fromStatus, toStatus) => {
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: fromStatus });

      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: toStatus })
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: toStatus })
      ).rejects.toThrow(`Sellers can only move orders through fulfilment steps (CONFIRMED->PROCESSING->SHIPPED->DELIVERED); ${fromStatus} -> ${toStatus} is not allowed`);

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });

  describe('updateOrderStatus - Non-owning seller rejected', () => {
    it('rejects non-owning SELLER on CONFIRMED->PROCESSING with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, sellerId: 1 });
      const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-seller-user' } as never });

      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);

      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'other-seller-user' });

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING })
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING })
      ).rejects.toThrow('You can only update status for your own seller orders');

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('other-seller-user');
      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });

  describe('updateOrderStatus - JWT shape { sub, userType: "seller" }', () => {
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 's-user' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    it('allows owning SELLER with { sub, userType: "seller" } CONFIRMED->PROCESSING', async () => {
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, sellerId: 1 });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING });
      const updatedSellerOrder = toSellerOrder(updatedOrder);

      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const user = { sub: 's-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
      const result = await resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING });

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s-user');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.PROCESSING);
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedSellerOrder);
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeUndefined();
    });
  });

  describe('updateOrderStatus - ADMIN unchanged', () => {
    const mockOrder = createMockOrder({ sellerId: 1 });
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    it.each([
      ['PENDING -> CONFIRMED', OrderStatus.PENDING, OrderStatus.CONFIRMED],
      ['CONFIRMED -> REFUNDED', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, OrderStatus.REFUNDED],
      ['PENDING -> CANCELLED', OrderStatus.PENDING, OrderStatus.CANCELLED],
      ['DELIVERED -> REFUNDED', OrderStatus.DELIVERED, OrderStatus.REFUNDED],
    ])('allows ADMIN %s (service called, full Order returned)', async (_, fromStatus, toStatus) => {
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: fromStatus });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: toStatus });

      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const result = await resolver.updateOrderStatus(user, { id: 'order-1', status: toStatus });

      expect(mockOrderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', toStatus);
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedOrder);
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeDefined(); // Full Order returned for ADMIN
    });
  });
});
===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\order.resolver.tracking.spec.ts =====
import { OrderResolver } from './order.resolver';
import {
  Order,
  OrderItem,
  OrderStatus,
  User,
  UserRole,
  toSellerOrder,
  SellerOrder,
  SellerInfo,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { OrdersConnection, OrderEdge, SellerOrdersConnection } from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';
import { ForbiddenException, BadRequestException, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';

describe('OrderResolver - updateOrderTracking', () => {
  let resolver: OrderResolver;
  let mockOrderService: {
    findAll: jest.Mock;
    findOne: jest.Mock;
    updateOrder: jest.Mock;
    updateOrderStatus: jest.Mock;
    deleteOrder: jest.Mock;
    cancelPendingOrder: jest.Mock;
    getOrdersBySeller: jest.Mock;
    getOrdersByCustomerEmail: jest.Mock;
    updateTrackingNumber: jest.Mock;
  };
  let mockSellerService: {
    findOneByUserId: jest.Mock;
  };

  // Test data factories (mirrored from order.resolver.spec.ts)
  const createMockSellerInfo = (overrides: Partial<SellerInfo> = {}): SellerInfo => ({
    id: 1,
    storeName: 'Test Store',
    phone: '09123456789',
    address: 'Seller Address',
    city: 'Tehran',
    state: 'Tehran',
    zipCode: '12345',
    country: 'Iran',
    businessName: 'Test Business',
    isActive: true,
    canManageCatalog: true,
    shippingFee: 10,
    user: {} as never,
    sellerProducts: [],
    ...overrides,
  });

  const createMockOrder = (overrides: Partial<Order> = {}): Order => ({
    id: 'order-1',
    orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
    customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
    customerPhone: '1234567890',
    customerName: 'John Doe',
    shippingAddress: '123 Main St',

    subtotal: 100,

    stockReserved: false,
    createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    updatedAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    items: [

        id: 'item-1',
        orderId: 'order-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productName: 'Product 1',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 100,
        sellerProductId: 'seller-prod-1',
      } as OrderItem,
    ],
    seller: createMockSellerInfo(),
    ...overrides,
  });

  const createMockUser = (overrides: Partial<User> = {}): User => ({
    id: 'user-1',
    name: 'John',
    lastName: 'Doe',
    username: 'johndoe',
    email: 'john@example.com',
    password: 'hashed',
    role: UserRole.CUSTOMER,
    locale: 'en',
    ...overrides,
  });

  const createMockSellerUser = (overrides: Partial<User> = {}): User =>
    createMockUser({
      id: 'seller-user-1',
      email: 'seller@example.com',
      username: 'seller',
      role: UserRole.SELLER,
      ...overrides,
    });

  const createMockAdminUser = (overrides: Partial<User> = {}): User =>
    createMockUser({
      id: 'admin-user-1',
      email: 'admin@example.com',
      username: 'admin',
      role: UserRole.ADMIN,
      ...overrides,
    });

  const createMockSellerOrder = (overrides: Partial<SellerOrder> = {}): SellerOrder => ({
    id: 'order-1',
    orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
    status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
    subtotal: 100,
    shippingCost: 10,
    total: 110,
    notes: 'Test order',
    trackingNumber: 'TRACK-123',
    createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    updatedAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
    sellerId: 1,
    sellerStoreName: 'Test Store',
    sellerPhone: '09123456789',
    sellerAddress: 'Seller Address',
    items: [
      {
        id: 'item-1',
        orderId: 'order-1',
        productId: 'prod-1',
        productName: 'Product 1',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 100,
        sellerProductId: 'seller-prod-1',
      } as OrderItem,
    ],
    ...overrides,
  });

  beforeEach(() => {
    mockOrderService = {
      findAll: jest.fn(),
      findOne: jest.fn(),
      updateOrder: jest.fn(),
      updateOrderStatus: jest.fn(),
      deleteOrder: jest.fn(),
      cancelPendingOrder: jest.fn(),
      getOrdersBySeller: jest.fn(),
      getOrdersByCustomerEmail: jest.fn(),
      updateTrackingNumber: jest.fn(),
    };
    mockSellerService = {
      findOneByUserId: jest.fn(),
    };

    resolver = new OrderResolver(
      mockOrderService as never,
      mockSellerService as never
    );
  });

  describe('updateOrderTracking mutation', () => {
    const mockSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 1, user: { id: 'seller-user-1' } as never });

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerInfo);
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockUser({ role: UserRole.CUSTOMER });
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow('Customers are not allowed to update tracking');
      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects SELLER not owning order with ForbiddenException and does not call updateTrackingNumber', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'other-seller-user' });
      const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-seller-user' } as never });
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, sellerId: 1 });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow('You can only update tracking for your own seller orders');
      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('other-seller-user');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects SELLER with no profile with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(null);
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, sellerId: 1 });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow('You can only update tracking for your own seller orders');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('allows SELLER owning a CONFIRMED order, calls updateTrackingNumber with trimmed value, returns redacted SellerOrder', async () => {
      const user = createMockSellerUser({ id: 'seller-user-1' });
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, sellerId: 1, trackingNumber: '' });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, sellerId: 1, trackingNumber: 'TRK123' });
      const updatedSellerOrder = toSellerOrder(updatedOrder);
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', '  TRK123  ');

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', 'TRK123');
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedSellerOrder);
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeUndefined();
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerPhone']).toBeUndefined();
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerName']).toBeUndefined();
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['shippingAddress']).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('allows ADMIN on SHIPPED order, returns full Order', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED, sellerId: 1, trackingNumber: '' });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED, sellerId: 1, trackingNumber: 'TRK123' });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123');

      expect(mockOrderService.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', 'TRK123');
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedOrder);
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('rejects PENDING order with BadRequestException and does not call updateTrackingNumber', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PENDING });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow('Tracking can only be set on confirmed, processing or shipped orders');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects DELIVERED order with BadRequestException and does not call updateTrackingNumber', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.DELIVERED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow('Tracking can only be set on confirmed, processing or shipped orders');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects CANCELLED order with BadRequestException and does not call updateTrackingNumber', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects REFUNDED order with BadRequestException and does not call updateTrackingNumber', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.REFUNDED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects empty tracking number with BadRequestException', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', '')
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', '')
      ).rejects.toThrow('Tracking number must be 1-100 characters');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects whitespace-only tracking number with BadRequestException', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(

      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', '   ')
      ).rejects.toThrow('Tracking number must be 1-100 characters');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects 101-character tracking number with BadRequestException', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      const longTracking = 'A'.repeat(101);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', longTracking)
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', longTracking)
      ).rejects.toThrow('Tracking number must be 1-100 characters');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('accepts 100-character tracking number', async () => {
      const user = createMockAdminUser();
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, trackingNumber: 'A'.repeat(100) });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'A'.repeat(100));

      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', 'A'.repeat(100));
      expect(result.trackingNumber).toBe('A'.repeat(100));
    });

    it('allows SELLER with JWT shape { sub, userType: "seller" } owning order', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'seller-user-1', userType: 'seller' } as never;
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING, sellerId: 1 });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING, sellerId: 1, trackingNumber: 'TRK123' });
      const updatedSellerOrder = toSellerOrder(updatedOrder);
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', '  TRK123  ');

      expect(mockSellerService.findOneByUserId).toHaveBeenCalledWith('seller-user-1');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', 'TRK123');
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedSellerOrder);
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('rejects UNKNOWN role with ForbiddenException', async () => {
      const user = { id: 'user-1', userType: 'INVALID' } as never;
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow('Customers are not allowed to update tracking');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects SELLER with JWT shape { sub, userType: "seller" } not owning order', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'other-s-user', userType: 'seller' } as never;
      const otherSellerInfo = createMockSellerInfo({ id: 999, user: { id: 'other-s-user' } as never });
      mockSellerService.findOneByUserId.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerInfo);
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, sellerId: 1 });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow('You can only update tracking for your own seller orders');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects CUSTOMER with JWT shape { sub, userType: "customer" }', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'c-user', userType: 'customer', email: 'customer@example.com' } as never;
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);
      await expect(
        resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123')
      ).rejects.toThrow('Customers are not allowed to update tracking');
      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('allows ADMIN with JWT shape { sub, userType: "admin" } on CONFIRMED order', async () => {
      const user = { sub: 'a-user', userType: 'admin' } as never;
      const order = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED });
      const updatedOrder = createMockOrder({ status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, trackingNumber: 'TRK123' });
      mockOrderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await resolver.updateOrderTracking(user, 'order-1', 'TRK123');

      expect(mockOrderService.updateTrackingNumber).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', 'TRK123');
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedOrder);
      expect((result as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)['customerEmail']).toBeDefined();
    });
  });
});
===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\order.resolver.ts =====
import {
  Resolver,
  Query,
  Mutation,
  Args,
  ResolveField,
  Parent,
} from '@nestjs/graphql';
import {
  UseGuards,
  NotFoundException,
  ForbiddenException,
  BadRequestException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  Order,
  OrderItem,
  OrderStatus,
  User,
  toSellerOrder,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import {
  CreateOrderInput,
  OrdersConnection,
  SellerOrdersConnection,
  UpdateOrderInput,
  UpdateOrderStatusInput,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';
import {
  createOrderSchema,
  cursorSchema,
  idSchema,
  optionalIdSchema,
  paginationFirstSchema,
  trackingNumberSchema,
  updateOrderSchema,
  updateOrderStatusSchema,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/validation';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from './zod-validation.pipe';

const SELLER_STATUS_TRANSITIONS: Partial<Record<OrderStatus, OrderStatus>> = {
  [OrderStatus.CONFIRMED]: OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
  [OrderStatus.PROCESSING]: OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
  [OrderStatus.SHIPPED]: OrderStatus.DELIVERED,
};
import { OrderService, SellerService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { GraphqlAuthGuard } from '@kickzone-workspace/guards';
import {
  CurrentUser,
  type AuthenticatedUser,
  type OptionalAuthenticatedUser,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/decorators';

@Resolver(() => Order)
export class OrderResolver {
  constructor(
    private readonly orderService: OrderService,
    private readonly sellerService: SellerService
  ) {}

  /**
   * Extract user ID from various possible fields in the user object.
   */
  private getUserId(user: OptionalAuthenticatedUser): string {
    return String(user?.id ?? user?.sub ?? '');
  }

  /**
   * Check if the customer user owns the order by comparing emails.
   * Returns false if either email is empty/undefined to prevent empty-email matches.
   */
  private isCustomerOrderOwner(
    user: OptionalAuthenticatedUser,
    order: Order
  ): boolean {
    const userEmail = String(user?.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const orderEmail = String(order?.customerEmail ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    return userEmail.length > 0 && orderEmail.length > 0 && userEmail === orderEmail;
  }

  /**
   * Normalize user role to uppercase standard values.
   */
  private getUserRole(user: OptionalAuthenticatedUser): 'ADMIN' | 'SELLER' | 'CUSTOMER' | 'UNKNOWN' {
    const rawRole = user?.role ?? user?.userType;
    if (!rawRole) return 'UNKNOWN';
    const normalized = String(rawRole).toUpperCase();
    if (normalized === 'ADMIN') return 'ADMIN';
    if (normalized === 'SELLER') return 'SELLER';
    if (normalized === 'CUSTOMER') return 'CUSTOMER';
    return 'UNKNOWN';
  }

  /**
   * Assert that the caller is an admin.
   */
  private assertAdmin(user: OptionalAuthenticatedUser): void {
    if (this.getUserRole(user) !== 'ADMIN') {
      throw new ForbiddenException('Admin access required');
    }
  }

  @Query(() => OrdersConnection)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async orders(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args(
      'first',
      { type: () => Number, defaultValue: 10 },
      new ZodValidationPipe(paginationFirstSchema)
    )
    first: number,
    @Args(
      'after',
      { type: () => String, nullable: true },
      new ZodValidationPipe(cursorSchema)
    )
    after?: string,
    @Args(
      'sellerId',
      { type: () => String, nullable: true },
      new ZodValidationPipe(optionalIdSchema)
    )
    sellerId?: string,
    @Args('status', { type: () => OrderStatus, nullable: true })
    status?: OrderStatus
  ) {
    this.assertAdmin(user);
    return this.orderService.findAll(first, after, sellerId, status);
  }

  @Query(() => Order)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async order(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('id', { type: () => String }, new ZodValidationPipe(idSchema)) id: string
  ) {
    const order = await this.orderService.findOne(id);
    const role = this.getUserRole(user);

    switch (role) {
      case 'ADMIN':
        return order;

      case 'SELLER': {
        // Get seller info for this user to check ownership
        const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(this.getUserId(user));
        if (!seller || order.sellerId !== seller.id) {
          throw new ForbiddenException('You do not have permission to view this order');
        }
        return toSellerOrder(order);
      }

      case 'CUSTOMER': {
        // Customer ownership is determined by email
        if (!this.isCustomerOrderOwner(user, order)) {
          throw new ForbiddenException('You do not have permission to view this order');
        }
        return order;
      }

      default:
        throw new ForbiddenException('Access denied');
    }
  }

  @Query(() => [Order])
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async myOrders(
    @CurrentUser() user: User,
    @Args(
      'first',
      { type: () => Number, defaultValue: 10 },
      new ZodValidationPipe(paginationFirstSchema)
    )
    first: number,
    @Args(
      'after',
      { type: () => String, nullable: true },
      new ZodValidationPipe(cursorSchema)
    )
    after?: string
  ) {
    // For CUSTOMER: Get orders by customer email
    if (this.getUserRole(user) === 'CUSTOMER') {
      if (!user?.email || String(user.email).trim() === '') {
        return [];
      }
      const result = await this.orderService.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(
        user.email,
        first,
        after
      );
      return result.edges.map((edge) => edge.node);
    }

    // For SELLER: Use mySellerOrders query instead (returns SellerOrder without customer PII)
    throw new NotFoundException(
      'Sellers should use mySellerOrders query instead'
    );
  }

  /**
   * Seller orders query - returns SellerOrder type WITHOUT customer PII
   * Sellers should NOT see customer name, email, phone, or address
   */
  @Query(() => SellerOrdersConnection)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async mySellerOrders(
    @CurrentUser() user: User,
    @Args(
      'first',
      { type: () => Number, defaultValue: 10 },
      new ZodValidationPipe(paginationFirstSchema)
    )
    first: number,
    @Args(
      'after',
      { type: () => String, nullable: true },
      new ZodValidationPipe(cursorSchema)
    )
    after?: string,
    @Args('status', { type: () => OrderStatus, nullable: true })
    status?: OrderStatus
  ) {
    // Only sellers can access this
    if (this.getUserRole(user) !== 'SELLER') {
      throw new NotFoundException('Only sellers can access seller orders');
    }

    // Get seller info from user
    const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(this.getUserId(user));
    if (!seller) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Seller not found for this user');
    }

    // Get orders for this seller
    const result = await this.orderService.getOrdersBySeller(
      seller.id.toString(),
      first,
      after,
      status
    );

    // Convert to SellerOrder connection (removes customer PII)
    return {
      edges: result.edges.map((edge) => ({
        cursor: edge.cursor,
        node: toSellerOrder(edge.node),
      })),
      pageInfo: result.pageInfo,
      totalCount: result.totalCount,
    };
  }

  @Query(() => OrdersConnection)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async adminSellerOrders(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('sellerId', { type: () => String }, new ZodValidationPipe(idSchema))
    sellerId: string,
    @Args(
      'first',
      { type: () => Number, defaultValue: 10 },
      new ZodValidationPipe(paginationFirstSchema)
    )
    first: number,
    @Args(
      'after',
      { type: () => String, nullable: true },
      new ZodValidationPipe(cursorSchema)
    )
    after?: string,
    @Args('status', { type: () => OrderStatus, nullable: true })
    status?: OrderStatus
  ) {
    this.assertAdmin(user);
    return this.orderService.findAll(first, after, sellerId, status);
  }

  @Query(() => OrdersConnection)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async allSellerOrders(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args(
      'first',
      { type: () => Number, defaultValue: 10 },
      new ZodValidationPipe(paginationFirstSchema)
    )
    first: number,
    @Args(
      'after',
      { type: () => String, nullable: true },
      new ZodValidationPipe(cursorSchema)
    )
    after?: string,
    @Args('status', { type: () => OrderStatus, nullable: true })
    status?: OrderStatus
  ) {
    this.assertAdmin(user);
    return this.orderService.findAll(first, after, undefined, status);
  }

  @Mutation(() => Order)
  async createOrder(
    @Args('createOrderInput', new ZodValidationPipe(createOrderSchema))
    createOrderInput: CreateOrderInput
  ) {
    return this.orderService.createOrder(createOrderInput);
  }

  @Mutation(() => Order)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async updateOrder(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('id', { type: () => String }, new ZodValidationPipe(idSchema)) id: string,
    @Args('updateOrderInput', new ZodValidationPipe(updateOrderSchema))
    updateOrderInput: UpdateOrderInput
  ) {
    this.assertAdmin(user);

    const order = await this.orderService.findOne(id);

    // Forbid modifying items or financial totals after order creation
    // items is not part of UpdateOrderInput; guard against it arriving anyway
    const input: UpdateOrderInput & { items?: unknown } = updateOrderInput;
    if (
      input.items != null ||
      order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING
    ) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation'
      );
    }

    return this.orderService.updateOrder(id, updateOrderInput);
  }

  @Mutation(() => Order)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async updateOrderStatus(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('updateOrderStatusInput', new ZodValidationPipe(updateOrderStatusSchema))
    updateOrderStatusInput: UpdateOrderStatusInput
  ) {
    const { id, status } = updateOrderStatusInput;
    const order = await this.orderService.findOne(id);

    const role = this.getUserRole(user);

    switch (role) {
      case 'ADMIN':
        // Admin can update any order status
        break;

      case 'SELLER': {
        // Get seller info for this user to check ownership
        const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(this.getUserId(user));
        if (!seller || order.sellerId !== seller.id) {
          throw new ForbiddenException('You can only update status for your own seller orders');
        }
        // Sellers can only move orders through fulfilment steps
        if (SELLER_STATUS_TRANSITIONS[order.status] !== status) {
          throw new BadRequestException(
            `Sellers can only move orders through fulfilment steps (CONFIRMED->PROCESSING->SHIPPED->DELIVERED); ${order.status} -> ${status} is not allowed`
          );
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'CUSTOMER':
      default:
        throw new ForbiddenException('Customers are not allowed to update order status');
    }

    const updated = await this.orderService.updateOrderStatus(id, status);

    // If caller is SELLER, return redacted SellerOrder
    if (role === 'SELLER') {
      return toSellerOrder(updated);
    }

    return updated;
  }

  @Mutation(() => Boolean)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async cancelPendingOrder(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('orderId', { type: () => String }, new ZodValidationPipe(idSchema)) orderId: string
  ) {
    const order = await this.orderService.findOne(orderId);

    const role = this.getUserRole(user);

    // Ownership check
    let isOwner = false;
    if (role === 'ADMIN') {
      isOwner = true;
    } else if (role === 'CUSTOMER') {
      // Customer ownership by email (prevents empty-email matches)
      isOwner = this.isCustomerOrderOwner(user, order);
    } else if (role === 'SELLER') {
      // Seller ownership via SellerInfo
      const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(this.getUserId(user));
      isOwner = seller != null && order.sellerId === seller.id;
    }

    if (!isOwner) {
      throw new ForbiddenException('You do not have permission to cancel this order');
    }

    // Status check: only PENDING orders can be cancelled
    if (order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Only pending orders can be cancelled');
    }

    await this.orderService.cancelPendingOrder(orderId);
    return true;
  }

  @Mutation(() => Boolean)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async deleteOrder(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('id', { type: () => String }, new ZodValidationPipe(idSchema)) id: string
  ) {
    this.assertAdmin(user);

    const order = await this.orderService.findOne(id);

    // If order is in an active paid status, cancel it first to restore stock
    const activePaidStatuses = [
      OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
      OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
      OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
    ];

    if (activePaidStatuses.includes(order.status) || order.stockReserved === true) {
      await this.orderService.updateOrderStatus(id, OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
    }

    await this.orderService.deleteOrder(id);
    return true;
  }

  @Mutation(() => Order)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async updateOrderTracking(
    @CurrentUser() user: AuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('id', { type: () => String }, new ZodValidationPipe(idSchema)) id: string,
    @Args(
      'trackingNumber',
      { type: () => String },
      new ZodValidationPipe(trackingNumberSchema)
    )
    trackingNumber: string
  ) {
    const trimmed = String(trackingNumber ?? '').trim();
    if (!trimmed || trimmed.length > 100) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Tracking number must be 1-100 characters');
    }

    const order = await this.orderService.findOne(id);
    const role = this.getUserRole(user);

    switch (role) {
      case 'ADMIN':
        break;

      case 'SELLER': {
        const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(this.getUserId(user));
        if (!seller || order.sellerId !== seller.id) {
          throw new ForbiddenException('You can only update tracking for your own seller orders');
        }
        break;
      }

      case 'CUSTOMER':
      default:
        throw new ForbiddenException('Customers are not allowed to update tracking');
    }

    const allowedStatuses = [OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, OrderStatus.PROCESSING, OrderStatus.SHIPPED];
    if (!allowedStatuses.includes(order.status)) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Tracking can only be set on confirmed, processing or shipped orders');
    }

    const updated = await this.orderService.updateTrackingNumber(id, trimmed);
    return role === 'SELLER' ? toSellerOrder(updated) : updated;
  }

  @Mutation(() => [Order])
  async recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(
    @Args('orderIds', { type: () => [String] }) orderIds: string[],
    @CurrentUser() user?: OptionalAuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('guestToken', { type: () => String, nullable: true }) guestToken?: string
  ): Promise<Order[]> {
    const uniqueOrderIds = Array.from(
      new Set(
        (orderIds ?? []).filter(
          (id): id is string => typeof id === 'string' && id.trim().length > 0
        )
      )
    );
    if (uniqueOrderIds.length === 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('At least one orderId is required');
    }

    const orders = await Promise.all(
      uniqueOrderIds.map((id) => this.orderService.findOne(id))
    );

    const firstEmail = String(orders[0].customerEmail ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const extractToken = (notes?: string | null) => {
      const m = /\[GuestToken:\s*([^\]]+)\]/.exec(notes ?? '');
      return m ? m[1].trim() : null;
    };
    const firstToken = extractToken(orders[0].notes);

    for (const order of orders) {
      if (order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          `Order ${order.id} is not in PENDING status`
        );
      }
      if (String(order.customerEmail ?? '').trim().toLowerCase() !== firstEmail) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          'All orders in a batch must belong to the same customer'
        );
      }
      if (extractToken(order.notes) !== firstToken) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          'All orders in a batch must share the same guest checkout token'
        );
      }
      if (firstToken) {
        const provided = guestToken?.trim();
        if (!provided || provided !== firstToken) {
          throw new ForbiddenException('Invalid guest token for batch shipping recalculation');
        }
      } else {
        const userEmail = String(user?.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!userEmail || !firstEmail || firstEmail !== userEmail) {
          throw new ForbiddenException('Not allowed to recalculate shipping for this order batch');
        }
      }
    }

    return this.orderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(orders);
  }

  @ResolveField(() => [OrderItem])
  async items(@Parent() order: Order) {
    return order.items;
  }
}
===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\payment.resolver.integration.spec.ts =====
import { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { PaymentResolver } from './payment.resolver';
import { PaymentService, OrderService, CurrencyService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { OrderStatus } from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput,
  ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput,
} from './payment.resolver';

describe('PaymentResolver Integration Tests', () => {
  let resolver: PaymentResolver;
  let paymentService: jest.Mocked<PaymentService>;
  let orderService: jest.Mocked<OrderService>;
  let currencyService: jest.Mocked<CurrencyService>;

  const mockPaymentService = {
    requestPayment: jest.fn(),
    verifyPayment: jest.fn(),
    getGatewayUrl: jest.fn(),
  };

  const mockOrderService = {
    confirmOrderPayment: jest.fn(),
    cancelPendingOrder: jest.fn(),
    findOne: jest.fn(),
    setPaymentIntent: jest.fn(),
    findOrdersByPaymentAuthority: jest.fn(),
    updateOrderStatus: jest.fn(),
  };

  const mockCurrencyService = {
    getCurrentExchangeRate: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({ rate: 1000 }),
  };

  beforeEach(async () => {
    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({
      providers: [
        PaymentResolver,
        {
          provide: PaymentService,
          useValue: mockPaymentService,
        },
        {
          provide: OrderService,
          useValue: mockOrderService,
        },
        {
          provide: CurrencyService,
          useValue: mockCurrencyService,
        },
      ],
    }).compile();

    resolver = module.get<PaymentResolver>(PaymentResolver);
    paymentService = module.get(PaymentService);
    orderService = module.get(OrderService);
    currencyService = module.get(CurrencyService);
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    mockCurrencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate.mockResolvedValue({ rate: 1000 });
  });

  describe('Full Payment Flow Integration', () => {
    const orderId = 'order-123';
    const authority = 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000';
    const amount = 100000; // 100,000 Toman

    const paymentRequestInput: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput = {
      amount,
      callback_url: 'https://example.com/payment/callback',
      description: 'Test Order Payment',
      currency: 'IRT',
      mobile: '09123456789',
      email: 'test@example.com',
      order_id: orderId,
    };

    const mockOrder = {
      id: orderId,
      status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      total: 100, // USD, so 100 * 1000 = 100000 IRT amount
      paymentAuthority: authority,
      paymentAmount: amount,
      paymentCurrency: 'IRT',
      items: [
        {
          id: 'item-1',
          productId: 'product-1',
          sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
          quantity: 2,
        },
      ],
    };

    describe('Successful Payment Flow', () => {
      it('should complete full payment flow: request â†’ verify â†’ confirm order', async () => {
        orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder as never);
        orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([mockOrder] as never);
        orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

        // Step 1: Request payment
        paymentService.requestPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          data: {
            code: 100,
            message: 'Success',
            authority,
            fee_type: 'Merchant',
            fee: 0,
          },
          errors: [],
        } as never);

        const requestResult = await resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(paymentRequestInput);

        expect(paymentService.requestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
          amount: paymentRequestInput.amount,
          callback_url: paymentRequestInput.callback_url,
          description: paymentRequestInput.description,
          currency: 'IRT',
          metadata: {
            mobile: paymentRequestInput.mobile,
            email: paymentRequestInput.email,
            order_id: paymentRequestInput.order_id,
          },
        });
        expect(requestResult.code).toBe(100);
        expect(requestResult.authority).toBe(authority);

        // Step 2: Verify payment (successful)
        const verificationInput: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput = {
          amount,
          authority,
          order_id: orderId,
        };

        const confirmedOrder = {
          ...mockOrder,
          status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        };

        paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          data: {
            code: 100,
            message: 'Payment verified',
            ref_id: 123456789,
            card_pan: '1234-****-****-5678',
            fee_type: 'Merchant',
            fee: 0,
          },
          errors: [],
        } as never);

        orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder as never);
        orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);
        orderService.confirmOrderPayment.mockResolvedValue(
          confirmedOrder as never
        );

        const verifyResult = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(verificationInput);

        expect(paymentService.verifyPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
          amount: verificationInput.amount,
          authority: verificationInput.authority,
        });
        expect(verifyResult.code).toBe(100);
        expect(verifyResult.ref_id).toBe(123456789);

        // Verify order was confirmed (stock decreased)
        expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith(orderId);
        expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      });

      it('should handle payment verification code 101 (already verified)', async () => {
        const verificationInput: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput = {
          amount,
          authority,
          order_id: orderId,
        };

        paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          data: {
            code: 101,
            message: 'Payment already verified',
            ref_id: 123456789,
          },
          errors: [],
        } as never);

        orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder as never);
        orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([mockOrder] as never);
        orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);
        orderService.confirmOrderPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          ...mockOrder,
          status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        } as never);

        const verifyResult = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(verificationInput);

        expect(verifyResult.code).toBe(101);
        // Order should still be confirmed even with code 101
        expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith(orderId);
      });
    });

    describe('Failed Payment Flow', () => {
      it('should cancel order when payment verification fails', async () => {
        const verificationInput: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput = {
          amount,
          authority,
          order_id: orderId,
        };

        // Payment verification fails
        paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          data: {
            code: -11,
            message: 'Payment not found',
          },
          errors: [],
        } as never);

        orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder as never);
        orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([mockOrder] as never);
        orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);
        orderService.cancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue({
          ...mockOrder,
          status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        } as never);

        const verifyResult = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(verificationInput);

        expect(verifyResult.code).toBe(-11);
        // Order should be cancelled
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(orderId);
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
        // Order should NOT be confirmed
        expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });

      it('should cancel order when payment verification returns non-success code', async () => {
        const verificationInput: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput = {
          amount,
          authority,
          order_id: orderId,
        };

        // Payment verification fails with different error code
        paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          data: {
            code: -10,
            message: 'Invalid amount',
          },
          errors: [],
        } as never);

        orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder as never);
        orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([mockOrder] as never);
        orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);
        orderService.cancelPendingOrder.mockResolvedValue({
          ...mockOrder,
          status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        } as never);

        const verifyResult = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(verificationInput);

        expect(verifyResult.code).toBe(-10);
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(orderId);
        expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });
    });

    describe('Payment Flow Without Order ID', () => {
      it('should reject verification when order_id is not provided', async () => {
        const verificationInput: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput = {
          amount,
          authority,
          // order_id is undefined
        };

        paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          data: {
            code: 100,
            message: 'Payment verified',
            ref_id: 123456789,
          },
          errors: [],
        } as never);

        orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([] as never);

        await expect(
          resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(verificationInput)
        ).rejects.toThrow('No orders found for this payment authority');

        expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });
    });

    describe('Error Handling in Order Confirmation', () => {
      it('should return success: false with ref_id when order confirmation fails but payment was verified', async () => {
        const verificationInput: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput = {
          amount,
          authority,
          order_id: orderId,
        };

        paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          data: {
            code: 100,
            message: 'Payment verified',
            ref_id: 123456789,
          },
          errors: [],
        } as never);

        orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder as never);
        orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([mockOrder] as never);
        orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

        // Order confirmation fails
        orderService.confirmOrderPayment.mockRejectedValue(
          new Error('Order confirmation failed')
        );

        const verifyResult = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(verificationInput);

        // Payment was captured but order confirmation failed - returns code 100 but success: false
        expect(verifyResult.code).toBe(100);
        expect(verifyResult.ref_id).toBe(123456789);
        expect(verifyResult.success).toBe(false);
        expect(verifyResult.message).toContain('Order confirmation failed');
        // Order confirmation was attempted
        expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith(orderId);
      });

      it('should still return failure if order cancellation fails but payment failed', async () => {
        const verificationInput: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput = {
          amount,
          authority,
          order_id: orderId,
        };

        // Payment verification fails
        paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
          data: {
            code: -11,
            message: 'Payment not found',
          },
          errors: [],
        } as never);

        orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder as never);
        orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([mockOrder] as never);
        orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

        // Order cancellation fails
        orderService.cancelPendingOrder.mockRejectedValue(
          new Error('Order cancellation failed')
        );

        const verifyResult = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(verificationInput);

        // Payment verification should still return failure
        expect(verifyResult.code).toBe(-11);
        // Order cancellation was attempted
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith(orderId);
      });
    });

    describe('Gateway URL Generation', () => {
      it('should return correct gateway URL for given authority', async () => {
        const authority = 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000';
        const expectedUrl = `https://sandbox.zarinpal.com/pg/StartPay/${authority}`;

        paymentService.getGatewayUrl.mockReturnValue(expectedUrl);

        const result = await resolver.getZarinPalGatewayUrl(
          { id: 'user-1' } as never,
          authority
        );

        expect(paymentService.getGatewayUrl).toHaveBeenCalledWith(authority);
        expect(result.url).toBe(expectedUrl);
      });
    });
  });

  describe('Payment Request Integration', () => {
    const createPendingOrder = (
      orderId: string,
      total: number,
      paymentCurrency: 'IRT' | 'IRR' = 'IRT'
    ) => ({
      id: orderId,
      status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      total: total / 1000, // USD, so total * 1000 = paymentAmount (IRT)
      paymentAuthority: 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000',
      paymentAmount: total,
      paymentCurrency,
      items: [],
    });

    it('should handle payment request with all optional fields', async () => {
      const input: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput = {
        amount: 50000,
        callback_url: 'https://example.com/callback',
        description: 'Order #123',
        currency: 'IRT',
        mobile: '09123456789',
        email: 'customer@example.com',
        order_id: 'order-123',
      };

      paymentService.requestPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Success',
          authority: 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000',
          fee_type: 'Merchant',
          fee: 0,
        },
        errors: [],
      } as never);

      orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue({
        ...createPendingOrder(input.order_id!, input.amount),
      } as never);
      orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

      const result = await resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(input);

      expect(paymentService.requestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        amount: input.amount,
        callback_url: input.callback_url,
        description: input.description,
        currency: 'IRT',
        metadata: {
          mobile: input.mobile,
          email: input.email,
          order_id: input.order_id,
        },
      });
      expect(result.code).toBe(100);
      expect(result.authority).toBeDefined();
    });

    it('should handle payment request with minimal required fields', async () => {
      const input: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput = {
        amount: 50000,
        callback_url: 'https://example.com/callback',
        description: 'Order #123',
        order_id: 'order-456',
      };

      paymentService.requestPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Success',
          authority: 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000',
        },
      } as never);

      orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue({
        ...createPendingOrder(input.order_id!, input.amount),
      } as never);
      orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

      const result = await resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(input);

      expect(paymentService.requestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        amount: input.amount,
        callback_url: input.callback_url,
        description: input.description,
        currency: 'IRT', // Default currency
        metadata: {
          mobile: undefined,
          email: undefined,
          order_id: input.order_id,
        },
      });
      expect(result.code).toBe(100);
    });

    it('should use IRR currency when specified', async () => {
      const input: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput = {
        amount: 5000000,
        callback_url: 'https://example.com/callback',
        description: 'Order #123',
        currency: 'IRR',
        order_id: 'order-789',
      };

      paymentService.requestPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Success',
          authority: 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000',
        },
      } as never);

      orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue({
        ...createPendingOrder(input.order_id!, input.amount, 'IRR'),
      } as never);
      orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

      await resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(input);

      expect(paymentService.requestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          currency: 'IRR',
        })
      );
    });
  });

  describe('Multi-Seller Batch Payment Flow (requestZarinPalPayment â†’ verifyZarinPalPayment)', () => {
    const authority = 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000';

    const createBatchOrder = (
      orderId: string,
      totalUsd: number,
      customerEmail: string,
      status: OrderStatus = OrderStatus.PENDING,
      items: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [
        {
          id: `item-${orderId}`,
          productId: `product-${orderId}`,
          sellerProductId: `seller-product-${orderId}`,
          quantity: 1,
          sellerProduct: { id: `seller-product-${orderId}`, stockQuantity: 10 },
        },
      ]
    ) => ({
      id: orderId,
      status,
      total: totalUsd, // USD
      paymentAuthority: authority,
      paymentAmount: totalUsd * 1000, // IRT
      paymentCurrency: 'IRT',
      customerEmail,
      items,
      orderNumber: `ORD-${orderId}`,
    });

    const basePaymentInput: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput = {
      amount: 100000, // 100 USD * 1000 = 100000 IRT
      callback_url: 'https://example.com/payment/callback',
      description: 'Batch Payment for Multiple Orders',
      currency: 'IRT',
      mobile: '09123456789',
      email: 'buyer@example.com',
      orderIds: ['order-1', 'order-2'],
    };

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockCurrencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate.mockResolvedValue({ rate: 1000 });
    });

    it('should request and verify batch payment for orders with same customer email (case-insensitive)', async () => {
      const order1 = createBatchOrder('order-1', 60, 'Buyer@Example.com');
      const order2 = createBatchOrder('order-2', 40, 'buyer@example.com');

      // Step 1: Request batch payment
      orderService.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order1 as never)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order2 as never);
      orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

      paymentService.requestPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Success',
          authority,
          fee_type: 'Merchant',
          fee: 0,
        },
        errors: [],
      } as never);

      const requestResult = await resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(basePaymentInput);

      expect(requestResult.code).toBe(100);
      expect(requestResult.authority).toBe(authority);

      // setPaymentIntent should be called for BOTH orders with expectedBatchAmount
      expect(orderService.setPaymentIntent).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
      expect(orderService.setPaymentIntent).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          orderId: 'order-1',
          authority,
          amount: 100000, // (60 + 40) * 1000 = 100000 IRT
        })
      );
      expect(orderService.setPaymentIntent).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          orderId: 'order-2',
          authority,
          amount: 100000,
        })
      );

      // Step 2: Verify batch payment (successful)
      const verificationInput: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput = {
        amount: 100000,
        authority,
        // order_id not provided - should find by authority
      };

      paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Payment verified',
          ref_id: 999888,
          card_pan: '1234-****-****-5678',
          fee_type: 'Merchant',
          fee: 0,
        },
        errors: [],
      } as never);

      orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([order1, order2] as never);
      orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);
      orderService.confirmOrderPayment
        .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ...order1, status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED } as never)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ...order2, status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED } as never);

      const verifyResult = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(verificationInput);

      // verifyPayment is called with firstOrder.paymentAmount (order-1's individual amount: 60 * 1000 = 60000)
      expect(paymentService.verifyPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        amount: 60000,
        authority,
      });

      // Both orders confirmed in deterministic id ASC order
      expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
      expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, 'order-1');
      expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, 'order-2');

      expect(verifyResult.code).toBe(100);
      expect(verifyResult.ref_id).toBe(999888);
      expect(verifyResult.success).toBe(true);
      expect(verifyResult.orderIds).toEqual(['order-1', 'order-2']);
      expect(verifyResult.orderId).toBe('order-1');
      expect(verifyResult.orderNumber).toContain('order-1');
      expect(verifyResult.orderNumber).toContain('order-2');
    });

    it('should reject batch payment when orderIds belong to different customer emails', async () => {
      const order1 = createBatchOrder('order-1', 60, 'buyer1@example.com');
      const order2 = createBatchOrder('order-2', 40, 'buyer2@example.com');

      orderService.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order1 as never)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order2 as never);

      await expect(resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(basePaymentInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'All orders in a batch must belong to the same customer'
      );

      expect(orderService.setPaymentIntent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(paymentService.requestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject batch payment when one order is missing', async () => {
      const order1 = createBatchOrder('order-1', 60, 'buyer@example.com');
      // order-2 is missing (returns null)

      orderService.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order1 as never)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(null as never);

      await expect(resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(basePaymentInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order(s) not found: order-2'
      );

      expect(orderService.setPaymentIntent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(paymentService.requestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject batch payment when one order is not PENDING', async () => {
      const order1 = createBatchOrder('order-1', 60, 'buyer@example.com');
      const order2 = createBatchOrder('order-2', 40, 'buyer@example.com', OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);

      orderService.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order1 as never)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order2 as never);

      await expect(resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(basePaymentInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        "Order(s) order-2 are not in PENDING status and cannot be paid"
      );

      expect(orderService.setPaymentIntent).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(paymentService.requestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject batch payment when client amount deviates beyond tolerance', async () => {
      const order1 = createBatchOrder('order-1', 60, 'buyer@example.com');
      const order2 = createBatchOrder('order-2', 40, 'buyer@example.com');

      orderService.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order1 as never)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order2 as never);
      orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

      // Expected: (60 + 40) * 1000 = 100000 IRT
      // Tolerance: max(10, 2 * 2) = 10 Tomans
      // Client sends 100050 (diff = 50 > 10) - should reject
      const badInput: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput = {
        ...basePaymentInput,
        amount: 100050,
      };

      await expect(resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(badInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Payment amount does not match batch order total'
      );

      expect(paymentService.requestPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should accept batch payment when client amount is within tolerance', async () => {
      const order1 = createBatchOrder('order-1', 60, 'buyer@example.com');
      const order2 = createBatchOrder('order-2', 40, 'buyer@example.com');

      orderService.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order1 as never)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(order2 as never);
      orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

      // Expected: 100000, Tolerance: 10
      // Client sends 100005 (diff = 5 <= 10) - should accept
      const goodInput: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput = {
        ...basePaymentInput,
        amount: 100005,
      };

      paymentService.requestPayment.mockResolvedValue({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Success',
          authority,
          fee_type: 'Merchant',
          fee: 0,
        },
        errors: [],
      } as never);

      const requestResult = await resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(goodInput);

      expect(requestResult.code).toBe(100);
      expect(paymentService.requestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          amount: 100000, // Server uses server-computed amount
        })
      );
    });
  });

  describe('FX Rate Fail-Closed Integration', () => {
    const singleOrderInput: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput = {
      amount: 100000,
      callback_url: 'https://example.com/callback',
      description: 'Single Order Payment',
      currency: 'IRT',
      order_id: 'order-single',
    };

    const createPendingOrder = (orderId: string, totalUsd: number) => ({
      id: orderId,
      status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      total: totalUsd,
      paymentAuthority: 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000000',
      paymentAmount: totalUsd * 1000,
      paymentCurrency: 'IRT',
      customerEmail: 'buyer@example.com',
      items: [],
    });

    beforeEach(() => {
      orderService.findOne.mockResolvedValue(
        createPendingOrder(singleOrderInput.order_id!, 100) as never
      );
      orderService.setPaymentIntent.mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);
    });

    it('should reject requestZarinPalPayment when getCurrentExchangeRate returns rate: 0', async () => {
      mockCurrencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate.mockResolvedValue({ rate: 0 });

      await expect(resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(singleOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Exchange rate is not configured or invalid. Cannot process ZarinPal payment.'
      );
    });

    it('should reject requestZarinPalPayment when getCurrentExchangeRate returns rate: 1', async () => {
      mockCurrencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate.mockResolvedValue({ rate: 1 });

      await expect(resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(singleOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Exchange rate is not configured or invalid. Cannot process ZarinPal payment.'
      );
    });

    it('should reject requestZarinPalPayment when getCurrentExchangeRate returns rate: -500', async () => {
      mockCurrencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate.mockResolvedValue({ rate: -500 });

      await expect(resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(singleOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Exchange rate is not configured or invalid. Cannot process ZarinPal payment.'
      );
    });

    it('should reject requestZarinPalPayment when getCurrentExchangeRate returns rate: NaN', async () => {
      mockCurrencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate.mockResolvedValue({ rate: NaN });

      await expect(resolver.requestZarinPalPayment(singleOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Exchange rate is not configured or invalid. Cannot process ZarinPal payment.'
      );
    });

    it('should reject requestZarinPalPayment when getCurrentExchangeRate returns null', async () => {
      mockCurrencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate.mockResolvedValue(null as never);
