          paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
            data: { code: 100, message: 'Success', ref_id: 222333 },
            errors: [],
          } as never);

          const input = {
            amount: 6000000,
            authority,
            order_id: 'order-1',
          } as never;

          await expect(resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(input)).rejects.toThrow(
            'Insufficient stock for product sp-1: required 4, available 3'
          );
          expect(paymentService.verifyPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
          expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        });

        it('should rollback confirmed siblings when later order fails confirmOrderPayment (3-order batch, id ASC sort)', async () => {
          // Input orders: order-3, order-1, order-2 (unsorted)
          // After sort: order-1, order-2, order-3
          // order-1 confirms, order-2 confirms, order-3 throws
          const order1 = createMockOrderWithItems({
            id: 'order-1',
            orderNumber: 'ORD-001',
            paymentAmount: 150000,
            items: [{ quantity: 1, sellerProduct: { id: 'sp-x', stockQuantity: 10 } }],
          });
          const order2 = createMockOrderWithItems({
            id: 'order-2',
            orderNumber: 'ORD-002',
            paymentAmount: 150000,
            items: [{ quantity: 1, sellerProduct: { id: 'sp-x', stockQuantity: 10 } }],
          });
          const order3 = createMockOrderWithItems({
            id: 'order-3',
            orderNumber: 'ORD-003',
            paymentAmount: 150000,
            items: [{ quantity: 1, sellerProduct: { id: 'sp-3', stockQuantity: 10 } }],
          });

          // Return in unsorted order to test id ASC sorting
          orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([order3, order1, order2] as never);

          paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
            data: { code: 100, message: 'Verified', ref_id: 777888 },
            errors: [],
          } as never);

          // order-1 succeeds, order-2 succeeds, order-3 throws
          orderService.confirmOrderPayment
            .mockResolvedValueOnce(undefined as never) // order-1
            .mockResolvedValueOnce(undefined as never) // order-2
            .mockRejectedValueOnce(new BadRequestException('Insufficient stock for product sp-3 during lock'));

          // Mock updateOrderStatus for rollback verification
          orderService.updateOrderStatus = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(undefined as never);

          const input = {
            amount: 150000,
            authority,
            order_id: 'order-1',
          } as never;

          const result = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(input);

          // Confirm calls should be in id ASC order: order-1, order-2, order-3
          expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, 'order-1');
          expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, 'order-2');
          expect(orderService.confirmOrderPayment).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(3, 'order-3');

          // Rollback should be called for BOTH order-1 and order-2
          expect(orderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
          expect(orderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(1, 'order-1', OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
          expect(orderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenNthCalledWith(2, 'order-2', OrderStatus.CANCELLED);

          // Must return success: false with ref_id
          expect(result.success).toBe(false);
          expect(result.code).toBe(100);
          expect(result.ref_id).toBe(777888);
          expect(result.message).toContain('777888');
        });

        it('should survive rollback failure (updateOrderStatus rejects) and still return success:false with ref_id', async () => {
          const order1 = createMockOrderWithItems({
            id: 'order-1',
            orderNumber: 'ORD-001',
            paymentAmount: 100000,
            items: [{ quantity: 1, sellerProduct: { id: 'sp-1', stockQuantity: 10 } }],
          });
          const order2 = createMockOrderWithItems({
            id: 'order-2',
            orderNumber: 'ORD-002',
            paymentAmount: 100000,
            items: [{ quantity: 1, sellerProduct: { id: 'sp-2', stockQuantity: 10 } }],
          });

          orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([order1, order2] as never);

          paymentService.verifyPayment.mockResolvedValue({
            data: { code: 100, message: 'Verified', ref_id: 444555 },
            errors: [],
          } as never);

          // order-1 confirms, order-2 throws
          orderService.confirmOrderPayment
            .mockResolvedValueOnce(undefined as never) // order-1
            .mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('Confirmation DB failure'));

          // updateOrderStatus for rollback ALSO rejects (DB connection lost)
          orderService.updateOrderStatus = jest.fn().mockRejectedValue(new Error('Rollback DB connection lost'));

          const input = {
            amount: 100000,
            authority,
            order_id: 'order-1',
          } as never;

          const result = await resolver.verifyZarinPalPayment(input);

          // Should NOT throw unhandled error
          // Should have attempted rollback for order-1
          expect(orderService.updateOrderStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1', OrderStatus.CANCELLED);

          // Must return success: false with ref_id and original error message
          expect(result.success).toBe(false);
          expect(result.code).toBe(100);
          expect(result.ref_id).toBe(444555);
          expect(result.message).toContain('Confirmation DB failure');
        });
      });
    });

    describe('cancelPendingZarinPalOrder - Multi-Seller Batch', () => {
      it('should cancel all PENDING sibling orders sharing the same authority', async () => {
        const order1 = createMockOrder({ id: 'order-1', paymentAuthority: authority });
        const order2 = createMockOrder({ id: 'order-2', paymentAuthority: authority });
        const order3 = createMockOrder({ id: 'order-3', status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED, paymentAuthority: authority });

        orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority.mockResolvedValue([order1, order2, order3] as never);
        orderService.cancelPendingOrder
          .mockResolvedValueOnce(createMockOrder({ id: 'order-1', status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED }) as never)
          .mockResolvedValueOnce(createMockOrder({ id: 'order-2', status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED }) as never);

        const result = await resolver.cancelPendingZarinPalOrder(undefined, authority);

        expect(orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority).toHaveBeenCalledWith(authority);
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-1');
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-2');
        // Should NOT cancel order-3 (already CANCELLED)
        expect(orderService.cancelPendingOrder).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith('order-3');
        expect(result).toBe(true);
      });
    });
  });
});

describe('PaymentResolver - Batch Shipping Recomputation & Amount Guard', () => {
  function setupResolver() {
    const ordersDb: Record<string, Partial<Order>> = {
      'ord-cod': {
        id: 'ord-cod',
        orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
        customerEmail: 'buyer@example.com',
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        sellerId: 1,
        subtotal: 100,
        shippingCost: 15, // stale/poisoned value before recalculation
        total: 115,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
        shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
      },
      'ord-online': {
        id: 'ord-online',
        orderNumber: 'ORD-2',
        customerEmail: 'buyer@example.com',
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        sellerId: 2,
        subtotal: 50,
        shippingCost: 0, // client tried to send 0
        total: 50,
        shippingMethod: 'express', // 45% of $10 = $4.50
        shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
      },
    };

    const paymentService = {
      requestPayment: jest.fn(async () => ({
        data: {
          code: 100,
          message: 'Success',
          authority: 'A00000000000000000000000000000000000',
        },
        errors: [],
      })),
    };

    const orderService = {
      findOne: jest.fn(async (id: string) => ordersDb[id] ?? null),
      recalculateBatchOrdersShipping: jest.fn(async (orders: Partial<Order>[]) => {
        for (const o of orders) {
          if (o.id === 'ord-cod') {
            o.shippingCost = 0;
            o.total = 100;
          } else if (o.id === 'ord-online') {
            o.shippingCost = 4.5;
            o.total = 54.5;
          }
        }
        return orders as Order[];
      }),
      setPaymentIntent: jest.fn(async () => ({})),
    };

    const currencyService = {
      getCurrentExchangeRate: jest.fn(async () => ({ rate: 60000 })),
    };

    const resolver = new PaymentResolver(
      paymentService as unknown as PaymentService,
      orderService as unknown as OrderService,
      currencyService as unknown as CurrencyService,
    );

    return { resolver, paymentService, orderService };
  }

  it('recomputes batch totals (COD = 0, Online Express = 4.50) and requests exact server Toman amount', async () => {
    const { resolver, paymentService, orderService } = setupResolver();

    // Server total = $100 (COD) + $54.50 (Online) = $154.50 * 60,000 = 9,270,000 Tomans
    const expectedTomans = Math.round(154.5 * 60000);

    const res = await resolver.requestZarinPalPayment({
      amount: expectedTomans,
      callback_url: 'https://saintbala.com/payment-callback',
      description: 'Batch Order Payment',
      currency: 'IRT',
      orderIds: ['ord-cod', 'ord-online'],
    });

    expect(orderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(paymentService.requestPayment).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({
        amount: 9270000,
      }),
    );
    expect(res.authority).toBeDefined();
  });

  it('rejects payment request when client amount omits PAY_ONLINE shipping cost', async () => {
    const { resolver } = setupResolver();

    // Client tries to pay only item subtotals ($150 * 60,000 = 9,000,000) without the $4.50 online shipping
    const tamperedClientAmount = Math.round(150 * 60000);

    await expect(
      resolver.requestZarinPalPayment({
        amount: tamperedClientAmount,
        callback_url: 'https://saintbala.com/payment-callback',
        description: 'Tampered Payment',
        currency: 'IRT',
        orderIds: ['ord-cod', 'ord-online'],
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });
});

===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\payment.resolver.ts =====
import { Resolver, Mutation, Args } from '@nestjs/graphql';
import { UseGuards, BadRequestException, ForbiddenException, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { OrderStatus, type OrderItem } from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';

/** OrderItem with the sellerProduct relation loaded (not declared on the entity class). */
type OrderItemWithSellerProduct = OrderItem & {
  sellerProduct?: { id?: string; stockQuantity?: number } | null;
};
import { PaymentService, OrderService, CurrencyService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { GraphqlAuthGuard } from '@kickzone-workspace/guards';
import { CurrentUser, type OptionalAuthenticatedUser } from '@kickzone-workspace/decorators';
import { ObjectType, Field, Int, InputType } from '@nestjs/graphql';

@ObjectType()
export class ZarinPalPaymentResponse {
  @Field(() => Int)
  code: number;

  @Field()
  message: string;

  @Field()
  authority: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  fee_type?: string;

  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })
  fee?: number;
}

@ObjectType()
export class ZarinPalVerificationResponse {
  @Field(() => Int)
  code: number;

  @Field()
  message: string;

  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })
  ref_id?: number;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  card_pan?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  card_hash?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  fee_type?: string;

  @Field(() => Int, { nullable: true })
  fee?: number;

  @Field(() => [String], { nullable: true })
  orderIds?: string[];

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  orderId?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  orderNumber?: string;

  @Field(() => Boolean, { nullable: true })
  success?: boolean;
}

@InputType()
export class ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput {
  @Field(() => Int)
  amount: number;

  @Field()
  callback_url: string;

  @Field()
  description: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  currency?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  mobile?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  email?: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  order_id?: string;

  @Field(() => [String], { nullable: true })
  orderIds?: string[];
}

@InputType()
export class ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput {
  @Field(() => Int)
  amount: number;

  @Field()
  authority: string;

  @Field({ nullable: true })
  order_id?: string; // Optional: if provided, order status will be updated
}

@ObjectType()
export class GatewayUrlResponse {
  @Field()
  url: string;
}

@Resolver()
export class PaymentResolver {
  constructor(
    private readonly paymentService: PaymentService,
    private readonly orderService: OrderService,
    private readonly currencyService: CurrencyService
  ) {}

  private readonly inFlightVerifications = new Map<
    string,
    Promise<ZarinPalVerificationResponse>
  >();

  private extractGuestToken(notes?: string | null): string | null {
    const match = /\[GuestToken:\s*([^\]]+)\]/.exec(notes ?? '');
    return match ? match[1].trim() : null;
  }

  @Mutation(() => ZarinPalPaymentResponse)
  async requestZarinPalPayment(
    @Args('input') input: ZarinPalPaymentRequestInput
  ) {
    // Normalize the list of order IDs from both orderId (singular) and orderIds (array)
    const targetOrderIds = Array.from(
      new Set([
        ...(input.orderIds ?? []),
        ...(input.order_id ? [input.order_id] : []),
      ].filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string' && id.trim().length > 0))
    );

    if (targetOrderIds.length === 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('At least one orderId is required');
    }

    // Validate callback_url early - before any database lookups
    let parsedCallbackUrl: URL;
    try {
      parsedCallbackUrl = new URL(input.callback_url);
    } catch {
      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid callback_url');
    }
    if (
      parsedCallbackUrl.protocol !== 'http:' &&
      parsedCallbackUrl.protocol !== 'https:'
    ) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid callback_url protocol');
    }

    const isSingleOrder = targetOrderIds.length === 1 && !input.orderIds;

    // Load all orders in targetOrderIds
    const orders = await Promise.all(
      targetOrderIds.map((id) => this.orderService.findOne(id))
    );

    // Verify all orders exist
    const missingOrders = targetOrderIds.filter(
      (_, index) => !orders[index]
    );
    if (missingOrders.length > 0) {
      if (isSingleOrder) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Order not found');
      }
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `Order(s) not found: ${missingOrders.join(', ')}`
      );
    }

    // Verify all orders are PENDING
    const nonPendingOrders = orders.filter(
      (order) => order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING
    );
    if (nonPendingOrders.length > 0) {
      if (isSingleOrder) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          `Order ${targetOrderIds[0]} is not in PENDING status and cannot be paid`
        );
      }
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `Order(s) ${nonPendingOrders
          .map((o) => o.id)
          .join(', ')} are not in PENDING status and cannot be paid`
      );
    }

    // Verify all orders belong to the same checkout customer/guest (by email)
    // Only for multi-order batches
    if (!isSingleOrder) {
      const firstOrder = orders[0];
      const firstCustomerEmail = String(firstOrder.customerEmail ?? '')
        .toLowerCase();
      const firstGuestToken = this.extractGuestToken(firstOrder.notes);

      for (const order of orders) {
        const customerEmail = String(order.customerEmail ?? '').toLowerCase();
        if (customerEmail !== firstCustomerEmail) {
          throw new BadRequestException(
            'All orders in a batch must belong to the same customer'
          );
        }
        const orderGuestToken = this.extractGuestToken(order.notes);
        if (firstGuestToken !== orderGuestToken) {
          throw new BadRequestException(
            'All orders in a batch must share the same guest checkout token'
          );
        }
      }
    }

    // Recompute server-authoritative shipping across the entire batch before summing totals
    if (typeof this.orderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping === 'function') {
      await this.orderService.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(orders);
    }

    // Sum the USD totals across all orders in the batch
    const batchTotalUsd = orders.reduce(
      (sum, o) => sum + Number(o.total),
      0
    );

    // For single order, also validate the individual order total
    if (isSingleOrder) {
      const orderTotalUsd = Number(orders[0].total);
      if (!Number.isFinite(orderTotalUsd) || orderTotalUsd <= 0) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Order total is invalid or missing');
      }
    }

    // Fetch current USD -> IRR exchange rate (IRR represents Toman for ZarinPal)
    const rateRecord = await this.currencyService.getCurrentExchangeRate(
      'USD',
      'IRR'
    );
    const fxRate = Number(rateRecord?.rate ?? rateRecord);

    // Fail-closed FX guard: reject if rate is missing, not finite, or <= 1
    if (!Number.isFinite(fxRate) || fxRate <= 1) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'Exchange rate is not configured or invalid. Cannot process ZarinPal payment.'
      );
    }

    // Compute server-authoritative amount in Tomans
    const expectedBatchAmount = Math.round(batchTotalUsd * fxRate);

    // Client tampering check: if client sends amount, it must match expectedAmount within rounding tolerance
    if (input.amount != null) {
      const clientAmount = Number(input.amount);
      const tolerance = isSingleOrder
        ? Math.max(1, Math.round(expectedBatchAmount * 0.001)) // 0.1% or at least 1 Toman for single order
        : Math.max(
            10,
            targetOrderIds.length * 2
          ); // At least 10 Tomans or 2 per order for batch
      if (Math.abs(clientAmount - expectedBatchAmount) > tolerance) {
        if (isSingleOrder) {
          throw new BadRequestException('Payment amount does not match order total');
        }
        throw new BadRequestException(
          'Payment amount does not match batch order total'
        );
      }
    }

    // Always use server-computed expectedAmount for ZarinPal request
    const response = await this.paymentService.requestPayment({
      amount: expectedBatchAmount,
      callback_url: input.callback_url,
      description: input.description,
      currency: (input.currency as 'IRR' | 'IRT') || 'IRT',
      metadata: {
        mobile: input.mobile,
        email: input.email,
        order_id: targetOrderIds[0], // Primary order for metadata
      },
    });

    // Bind authority and server-computed expected amount to EVERY order in the batch
    for (const order of orders) {
      await this.orderService.setPaymentIntent({
        orderId: order.id,
        authority: response.data.authority,
        amount: expectedBatchAmount,
        currency: input.currency || 'IRT',
      });
    }

    return {
      code: response.data.code,
      message: response.data.message,
      authority: response.data.authority,
      fee_type: response.data.fee_type,
      fee: response.data.fee,
    };
  }

  @Mutation(() => ZarinPalVerificationResponse)
  async verifyZarinPalPayment(
    @Args('input') input: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput
  ) {
    const existingInFlight = this.inFlightVerifications.get(input.authority);
    if (existingInFlight) {
      return existingInFlight;
    }
    const verificationPromise = this.executeVerifyZarinPalPayment(input).finally(
      () => {
        this.inFlightVerifications.delete(input.authority);
      }
    );
    this.inFlightVerifications.set(input.authority, verificationPromise);
    return verificationPromise;
  }

  private async executeVerifyZarinPalPayment(
    input: ZarinPalVerificationRequestInput
  ): Promise<ZarinPalVerificationResponse> {
    const orders = this.orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority
      ? await this.orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority(input.authority)
      : input.order_id
        ? [await this.orderService.findOne(input.order_id)].filter(
            (o): o is NonNullable<typeof o> => Boolean(o)
          )
        : [];

    if (orders.length === 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'No orders found for this payment authority'
      );
    }

    // Verify all orders have the matching authority
    for (const order of orders) {
      if (order.paymentAuthority !== input.authority) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          'Payment authority does not match the order payment intent'
        );
      }
    }

    // Reject before calling the gateway if any order bound to this authority is already CANCELLED or REFUNDED
    const cancelledOrRefunded = orders.filter(
      (o) =>
        o.status === OrderStatus.CANCELLED || o.status === OrderStatus.REFUNDED
    );
    if (cancelledOrRefunded.length > 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `Cannot verify payment for cancelled or refunded order(s): ${cancelledOrRefunded
          .map((o) => o.id)
          .join(', ')}`
      );
    }

    // Pre-verify: ensure every PENDING order has sufficient stock on all items
    // This prevents one sibling order from confirming if another is out of stock
    const pendingOrders = orders.filter((o) => o.status === OrderStatus.PENDING);
    const stockRequirements = new Map<string, number>(); // sellerProductId -> requiredQty
    for (const order of pendingOrders) {
      for (const item of order.items ?? []) {
        const sp = (item as OrderItemWithSellerProduct).sellerProduct;
        if (sp?.id != null && typeof sp.stockQuantity === 'number') {
          const current = stockRequirements.get(sp.id) ?? 0;
          stockRequirements.set(sp.id, current + (item.quantity ?? 0));
        }
      }
    }
    for (const [sellerProductId, requiredQty] of stockRequirements.entries()) {
      // Find the sellerProduct to check current stock
      const sampleItem = pendingOrders
        .flatMap((o): OrderItemWithSellerProduct[] => o.items ?? [])
        .find((i) => i.sellerProduct?.id === sellerProductId);
      const available = sampleItem?.sellerProduct?.stockQuantity ?? 0;
      if (available < requiredQty) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          `Insufficient stock for product ${sellerProductId}: required ${requiredQty}, available ${available}`
        );
      }
    }

    // Use the payment amount from the first order (they all share the same batch amount)
    const firstOrder = orders[0];
    if (
      firstOrder.paymentAmount === null ||
      firstOrder.paymentAmount === undefined
    ) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'Payment amount not recorded for this order. Please initiate payment again.'
      );
    }

    const amountToVerify = firstOrder.paymentAmount;

    const response = await this.paymentService.verifyPayment({
      amount: amountToVerify,
      authority: input.authority,
    });

    // If payment verification was successful (code 100 or 101)
    if (response.data.code === 100 || response.data.code === 101) {
      // Handle code 101 idempotently: if every order for this authority is already in a paid status
      // and has stock reserved, return success without re-decrementing stock.
      const paidStatuses = new Set<OrderStatus>([
        OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
        OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
        OrderStatus.DELIVERED,
      ]);
      const allOrdersConfirmedAndStockReserved = orders.every(
        (o) => paidStatuses.has(o.status) && o.stockReserved === true
      );

      if (response.data.code === 101 && allOrdersConfirmedAndStockReserved) {
        // Idempotent success: already verified, stock already reserved
        return {
          code: response.data.code,
          message: response.data.message,
          ref_id: response.data.ref_id,
          card_pan: response.data.card_pan,
          card_hash: response.data.card_hash,
          fee_type: response.data.fee_type,
          fee: response.data.fee,
          orderIds: orders.map((o) => o.id),
          orderId: orders[0]?.id,
          orderNumber: orders.map((o) => o.orderNumber).join(', '),
          success: true,
        };
      }

      // Confirm ALL PENDING orders in deterministic order (id ASC)
      const pendingOrders = orders
        .filter((o) => o.status === OrderStatus.PENDING)
        .sort((a, b) => a.id.localeCompare(b.id));

      const confirmedOrderIds: string[] = [];
      for (const order of pendingOrders) {
        try {
          // Persist authority/amount before confirming to prevent tampering/replay
          await this.orderService.setPaymentIntent({
            orderId: order.id,
            authority: input.authority,
            amount: amountToVerify,
            currency: order.paymentCurrency || 'IRT',
          });

          await this.orderService.confirmOrderPayment(order.id);
          confirmedOrderIds.push(order.id);
        } catch (confirmError) {
          console.error(
            `Failed to finalize order ${order.id} after successful payment (authority: ${input.authority}, refId: ${response.data.ref_id}):`,
            confirmError
          );
          // Rollback any orders confirmed earlier in this batch
          for (const confirmedId of confirmedOrderIds) {
            try {
              if (this.orderService.updateOrderStatus) {
                await this.orderService.updateOrderStatus(confirmedId, OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
              }
            } catch (rollbackError) {
              console.error(
                `Rollback failed for order ${confirmedId}:`,
                rollbackError
              );
            }
          }
          // CRITICAL FIX: Return success: false when confirmOrderPayment throws.
          // The payment was captured by gateway (refId exists) but order confirmation/stock reservation failed.
          // Frontend must NOT clear cart and must show error with refId for support/refund.
          const errorMessage = confirmError instanceof Error
            ? `Payment was captured by gateway (Ref: ${response.data.ref_id}), but order confirmation failed: ${confirmError.message}`
            : `Payment was captured by gateway (Ref: ${response.data.ref_id}), but stock reservation failed.`;
          return {
            code: response.data.code,
            message: errorMessage,
            ref_id: response.data.ref_id,
            card_pan: response.data.card_pan,
            card_hash: response.data.card_hash,
            fee_type: response.data.fee_type,
            fee: response.data.fee,
            orderIds: orders.map((o) => o.id),
            orderId: orders[0]?.id,
            orderNumber: orders.map((o) => o.orderNumber).join(', '),
            success: false,
          };
        }
      }
    }

    // If payment verification failed (non-100/101), cancel ALL PENDING orders
    if (response.data.code !== 100 && response.data.code !== 101) {
      const pendingOrders = orders.filter(
        (o) => o.status === OrderStatus.PENDING
      );
      for (const order of pendingOrders) {
        try {
          await this.orderService.cancelPendingOrder(order.id);
        } catch (error) {
          // Log error but don't fail - payment verification response is what matters
          console.error(
            `Failed to cancel order ${order.id} after payment failure:`,
            error
          );
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      code: response.data.code,
      message: response.data.message,
      ref_id: response.data.ref_id,
      card_pan: response.data.card_pan,
      card_hash: response.data.card_hash,
      fee_type: response.data.fee_type,
      fee: response.data.fee,
      orderIds: orders.map((o) => o.id),
      orderId: orders[0]?.id,
      orderNumber: orders.map((o) => o.orderNumber).join(', '),
      success: response.data.code === 100 || response.data.code === 101,
    };
  }

  @Mutation(() => GatewayUrlResponse)
  @UseGuards(GraphqlAuthGuard)
  async getZarinPalGatewayUrl(
    @CurrentUser() _user: { id: string },
    @Args('authority') authority: string
  ) {
    return {
      url: this.paymentService.getGatewayUrl(authority),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Cancel a pending order when the buyer aborts on ZarinPal (Status=NOK).
   * Per ZarinPal docs, verify must not be called when Status is NOK.
   *
   * Two authorization paths:
   * 1. With non-empty `authority`: any caller (guest or authenticated) may cancel
   *    all PENDING orders bound to that authority. This preserves the existing
   *    guest-compatible behavior where the authority token proves possession.
   * 2. Without `authority` (empty/undefined): caller MUST provide `orderId` and be
   *    authorized as the order owner. Authorization succeeds if:
   *    - `user?.email` is non-empty and equals `order.customerEmail` (authenticated customer), OR
   *    - `guestToken` (trimmed, length >= 16) is found in `order.notes` as the literal
   *      substring `[GuestToken: <token>]` (guest who placed the order).
   *
   * @param orderId - Order ID (required when authority is not provided)
   * @param authority - ZarinPal authority token; if non-empty, authorizes via authority match
   * @param user - Current authenticated user (optional, from @CurrentUser decorator)
   * @param guestToken - Guest token for unauthenticated callers (optional)
   * @returns true if cancellation succeeded (idempotent for already-cancelled orders)
   * @throws BadRequestException if orderId missing without authority, or order not PENDING/already paid
   * @throws ForbiddenException if caller is not the order owner (without authority)
   */
  @Mutation(() => Boolean)
  async cancelPendingZarinPalOrder(
    @Args('orderId', { type: () => String, nullable: true }) orderId?: string,
    @Args('authority', { type: () => String, nullable: true }) authority?: string,
    @CurrentUser() user?: OptionalAuthenticatedUser,
    @Args('guestToken', { type: () => String, nullable: true }) guestToken?: string
  ) {
    // Path 1: Non-empty authority provided -> keep EXACT existing logic
    if (authority && authority.trim() !== '') {
      // Look up orders matching the authority
      const ordersByAuthority = await this.orderService.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority(authority);

      // If orderId was also passed, verify it matches the authority
      let matchedOrders = ordersByAuthority;
      if (orderId) {
        const orderById = await this.orderService.findOne(orderId);
        if (orderById.paymentAuthority !== authority) {
          throw new BadRequestException('Order does not match the provided payment authority');
        }
        // Ensure the orderById is in the matched list (in case findOrdersByPaymentAuthority missed it)
        if (!matchedOrders.some((o) => o.id === orderId)) {
          matchedOrders = [orderById, ...matchedOrders];
        }
      }

      if (matchedOrders.length === 0) {
        throw new BadRequestException('No matching order found for payment authority');
      }

      // Cancel each matched order that is PENDING
      for (const order of matchedOrders) {
        if (order.status === OrderStatus.CANCELLED) {
          // Idempotent: already cancelled, treat as success
          continue;
        }
        if (order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING) {
          throw new BadRequestException(
            `Cannot cancel order ${order.id} with status ${order.status}. Only PENDING orders can be cancelled.`
          );
        }
        await this.orderService.cancelPendingOrder(order.id);
      }

      return true;
    }

    // Path 2: No authority (or empty) -> require orderId and ownership authorization
    if (!orderId || orderId.trim() === '') {
      throw new BadRequestException('Order ID is required when payment authority is not provided');
    }

    let order;
    try {
      order = await this.orderService.findOne(orderId);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof NotFoundException) {
        throw new ForbiddenException('Not allowed to cancel this order');
      }
      throw error;
    }

    // Authorization check
    const userEmail = user?.email?.trim();
    const providedGuestToken = guestToken?.trim();

    // Validate guestToken format: no ']' or newlines to prevent injection
    if (providedGuestToken && /[\]\r\n]/.test(providedGuestToken)) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid guest token format');
    }

    const isOwnerByEmail = Boolean(userEmail && userEmail.length > 0 && order.customerEmail?.toLowerCase() === userEmail.toLowerCase());

    const isOwnerByGuestToken = Boolean(
      providedGuestToken &&
      providedGuestToken.length >= 16 &&
      (order.notes ?? '').includes(`[GuestToken: ${providedGuestToken}]`)
    );

    if (!isOwnerByEmail && !isOwnerByGuestToken) {
      throw new ForbiddenException('Not allowed to cancel this order');
    }

    // Status checks
    if (order.status === OrderStatus.CANCELLED) {
      // Idempotent: already cancelled, treat as success
      return true;
    }

    if (order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING || order.paymentVerifiedAt) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `Cannot cancel order ${order.id} with status ${order.status}. Only unpaid PENDING orders can be cancelled.`
      );
    }

    await this.orderService.cancelPendingOrder(order.id);
    return true;
  }
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\seller-info-validation.spec.ts =====
import { BadRequestException, PipeTransform } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ROUTE_ARGS_METADATA } from '@nestjs/common/constants';
import { SellerResolver } from './seller-info.resolver';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from './zod-validation.pipe';

type ParamMeta = { index: number; pipes?: PipeTransform[] };

function zodPipe(method: string, argIndex: number): PipeTransform {
  const raw = (Reflect.getMetadata(ROUTE_ARGS_METADATA, SellerResolver, method) ?? {}) as Record<
    string,
    ParamMeta
  >;
  const param = Object.values(raw).find((p) => p.index === argIndex);
  const pipe = param?.pipes?.find((p) => p instanceof ZodValidationPipe);
  if (!pipe) throw new Error(`no ZodValidationPipe on ${method} arg ${argIndex}`);
  return pipe;
}

const run = (method: string, index: number, value: unknown) =>
  zodPipe(method, index).transform(value, { type: 'body' });

describe('SellerResolver argument validation', () => {
  const product = { productInfoId: 3, price: 150, costPrice: 100, discount: 10, stockQuantity: 2 };

  it('validates seller create/update inputs', () => {
    expect(() =>
      run('createSeller', 0, {
        name: 'A', lastName: 'B', username: 'ab', email: 'a@b.co', password: 'x', storeName: 'S',
      })
    ).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run('createSeller', 0, { name: 'A' })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
    expect(() => run('updateSeller', 0, { id: 1, email: 'bad' })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it.each([
    ['createSellerProduct', 1],
    ['createMySellerProduct', 1],
  ])('%s validates the product input', (method, index) => {
    expect(() => run(method, index, product)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run(method, index, { ...product, discount: 150 })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });

  it.each([
    ['updateSellerProduct', 1],
    ['updateMySellerProduct', 1],
  ])('%s validates the product update', (method, index) => {
    expect(() => run(method, index, { id: 'sp1', price: 10 })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => run(method, index, { id: 'sp1', stockQuantity: -1 })).toThrow(BadRequestException);
  });
});

import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { ExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/common';
import { RolesGuard } from '@kickzone-workspace/guards';
import { SellerResolver } from './seller-info.resolver';
import { SellerService, ProductService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { ShippingPaymentMode, UserRole } from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';

jest.mock('@nestjs/graphql', () => {
  const actual = jest.requireActual('@nestjs/graphql');
  return {
    ...actual,
    GqlExecutionContext: {
      create: jest.fn((ctx: ExecutionContext) => ({
        getContext: () => ctx.switchToHttp().getRequest(),
      })),
    },
  };
});

describe('SellerResolver - Admin-Only shippingPaymentMode Guard & Mutation', () => {
  const reflector = new Reflector();
  const guard = new RolesGuard(reflector);

  function mockGqlContext(user?: { id: string; role: UserRole }) {
    return {
      getHandler: () => SellerResolver.prototype.updateSeller,
      switchToHttp: () => ({
        getRequest: () => ({ req: { user } }),
      }),
    } as unknown as ExecutionContext;
  }

  it('allows only ADMIN role to invoke updateSeller (which toggles shippingPaymentMode)', () => {
    expect(
      guard.canActivate(mockGqlContext({ id: '1', role: UserRole.ADMIN })),
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(
      guard.canActivate(mockGqlContext({ id: '2', role: UserRole.SELLER })),
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(
      guard.canActivate(mockGqlContext({ id: '3', role: UserRole.CUSTOMER })),
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(guard.canActivate(mockGqlContext(undefined))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('forwards shippingPaymentMode update to SellerService.update', async () => {
    const sellerService = {
      update: jest.fn(async (input) => ({
        id: input.id,
        storeName: 'Selected Seller',
        shippingFee: 12,
        shippingPaymentMode: input.shippingPaymentMode,
      })),
    };
    const resolver = new SellerResolver(
      sellerService as unknown as SellerService,
      {} as unknown as ProductService
    );

    const updated = await resolver.updateSeller({
      id: 7,
      shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
    });

    expect(sellerService.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      id: 7,
      shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
    });
    expect(updated?.shippingPaymentMode).toBe(ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE);
  });
});

import {
  ProductConnection,
  SellerConnection,
  SellerInfo,
  SellerProduct,
  User,
  UserRole,
  ShippingPaymentMode,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { RolesGuard } from '@kickzone-workspace/guards';
import {
  CreateSellerInput,
  CreateSellerProductInput,
  ProductFilterInput,
  SellerFilterInput,
  UpdateSellerInput,
  UpdateSellerProductInput,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';
import { ProductService, SellerService } from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { createConnection } from '@kickzone-workspace/utils';
import {
  createSellerProductSchema,
  createSellerSchema,
  cursorSchema,
  paginationFirstSchema,
  productFilterSchema,
  sellerFilterSchema,
  updateSellerProductSchema,
  updateSellerSchema,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/validation';
import { ZodValidationPipe } from './zod-validation.pipe';
import { SetMetadata, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  Args,
  Context,
  Int,
  Mutation,
  Parent,
  Query,
  ResolveField,
  Resolver,
} from '@nestjs/graphql';

@Resolver(() => SellerInfo)
export class SellerResolver {
  constructor(
    private readonly sellerService: SellerService,
    private readonly productService: ProductService
  ) {}

  @Mutation(() => SellerInfo)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  createSeller(
    @Args('createSellerInput', new ZodValidationPipe(createSellerSchema))
    createSellerInput: CreateSellerInput
  ) {
    return this.sellerService.create(createSellerInput);
  }

  @Query(() => SellerConnection)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN, UserRole.CUSTOMER])
  async sellers(
    @Context() context: { req: { user?: User } },
    @Args('filter', { nullable: true }, new ZodValidationPipe(sellerFilterSchema))
    filter?: SellerFilterInput,
    @Args(
      'first',
      { type: () => Int, defaultValue: 100 },
      new ZodValidationPipe(paginationFirstSchema)
    )
    first = 100,
    @Args(
      'after',
      { type: () => String, nullable: true },
      new ZodValidationPipe(cursorSchema)
    )
    after?: string
  ) {
    // For customers, only allow querying by IDs
    const user = context.req.user;
    if (user?.role === UserRole.CUSTOMER) {
      if (!filter?.ids || filter.ids.length === 0) {
        // Customers can only query by IDs
        return {
          edges: [],
          pageInfo: {
            endCursor: null,
            hasNextPage: false,
          },
          totalCount: 0,
        };
      }
    }

    const [sellers, totalCount] = await this.sellerService.findAll({
      ...filter,
      first,
      after,
    });
    // Build edges and pageInfo for SellerConnection
    const edges = sellers.map((seller) => ({
      node: seller,
      cursor: seller.id.toString(),
    }));
    const pageInfo = {
      endCursor: edges.length > 0 ? edges[edges.length - 1].cursor : null,
      hasNextPage: filter?.first ? sellers.length === filter.first : false,
    };
    return {
      edges,
      pageInfo,
      totalCount,
    };
  }

  @Query(() => SellerInfo, { nullable: true })
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  findOne(@Args('id', { type: () => Int }) id: number) {
    return this.sellerService.findOne(id);
  }

  @Query(() => SellerInfo, { nullable: true })
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.SELLER])
  async mySellerInfo(@Context() context: { req: { user: User } }) {
    const user = context.req.user;
    if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    if (user.role !== UserRole.SELLER) throw new Error('Not a seller');
    return this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(user.id);
  }

  @Mutation(() => SellerInfo)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  updateSeller(
    @Args('updateSellerInput', new ZodValidationPipe(updateSellerSchema))
    updateSellerInput: UpdateSellerInput
  ) {
    return this.sellerService.update(updateSellerInput);
  }

  @Mutation(() => SellerInfo)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  toggleSellerActive(@Args('id', { type: () => Int }) id: number) {
    return this.sellerService.toggleActive(id);
  }

  @Mutation(() => SellerProduct)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  createSellerProduct(
    @Args('sellerId', { type: () => Int }) sellerId: number,
    @Args('input', new ZodValidationPipe(createSellerProductSchema))
    input: CreateSellerProductInput
  ) {
    return this.sellerService.createSellerProduct(sellerId, input, true);
  }

  @Mutation(() => SellerProduct)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.ADMIN])
  updateSellerProduct(
    @Args('sellerId', { type: () => Int }) sellerId: number,
    @Args('input', new ZodValidationPipe(updateSellerProductSchema))
    input: UpdateSellerProductInput
  ) {
    return this.sellerService.updateSellerProduct(sellerId, input, true);
  }

  // Seller-specific mutations for creating and updating their own products
  @Mutation(() => SellerProduct)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.SELLER])
  async createMySellerProduct(
    @Context() context: { req: { user: User } },
    @Args('input', new ZodValidationPipe(createSellerProductSchema))
    input: CreateSellerProductInput
  ) {
    const user = context.req.user;
    if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    if (user.role !== UserRole.SELLER) throw new Error('Not a seller');

    const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(user.id);
    if (!seller) throw new Error('Seller info not found');

    return this.sellerService.createSellerProduct(seller.id, input, false);
  }

  @Mutation(() => SellerProduct)
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.SELLER])
  async updateMySellerProduct(
    @Context() context: { req: { user: User } },
    @Args('input', new ZodValidationPipe(updateSellerProductSchema))
    input: UpdateSellerProductInput
  ) {
    const user = context.req.user;
    if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    if (user.role !== UserRole.SELLER) throw new Error('Not a seller');

    const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(user.id);
    if (!seller) throw new Error('Seller info not found');

    return this.sellerService.updateSellerProduct(seller.id, input, false);
  }

  // Relay-style sellerProducts for the current seller (for sellers themselves)
  @Query(() => ProductConnection)
  async sellerProductsForMe(
    @Context() context: { req: { user: User } },
    @Args('filter', { nullable: true }, new ZodValidationPipe(productFilterSchema))
    filter?: ProductFilterInput
  ) {
    const user = context.req.user;
    if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    if (user.role !== UserRole.SELLER) throw new Error('Not a seller');
    const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(user.id);
    if (!seller) throw new Error('Seller info not found');
    const [products, totalCount] = await this.productService.getSellerProducts(
      seller.id,
      filter
    );
    return createConnection(
      products,
      totalCount,
      filter?.first || 10,
      filter?.after
    );
  }

  // Get a specific seller product by ID for editing
  @Query(() => SellerProduct, { nullable: true })
  @UseGuards(RolesGuard)
  @SetMetadata('roles', [UserRole.SELLER])
  async mySellerProductById(
    @Context() context: { req: { user: User } },
    @Args('id', { type: () => String }) id: string
  ) {
    const user = context.req.user;
    if (!user) throw new Error('Not authenticated');
    if (user.role !== UserRole.SELLER) throw new Error('Not a seller');

    const seller = await this.sellerService.findOneByUserId(user.id);
    if (!seller) throw new Error('Seller info not found');

    return this.sellerService.findSellerProductById(id, seller.id);
  }

  @ResolveField(() => User)
  user(@Parent() seller: SellerInfo) {
    return seller.user;
  }

  // This is the only sellerProducts ResolveField for SellerInfo
  @ResolveField(() => [SellerProduct])
  sellerProducts(@Parent() seller: SellerInfo) {
    return seller.sellerProducts;
  }

  @ResolveField(() => Number)
  shippingFee(@Parent() seller: SellerInfo) {
    return seller.shippingFee;
  }

  @ResolveField(() => ShippingPaymentMode)
  shippingPaymentMode(@Parent() seller: SellerInfo): ShippingPaymentMode {
    return seller.shippingPaymentMode ?? ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY;
  }
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\resolvers\src\lib\shipping.resolver.ts =====
import {
  Resolver,
  Query,
  Args,
  Float,
  InputType,
  Field,
} from '@nestjs/graphql';
import {
  ShippingCalculationService,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/services';
import { ObjectType } from '@nestjs/graphql';

@InputType()
export class SellerShippingInfoInput {
  @Field(() => String)
  sellerId: string;

  @Field(() => Float)
  shippingFee: number;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  sellerName?: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  shippingPaymentMode?: string;
}

@ObjectType()
export class ShippingCalculationResult {
  @Field(() => Float)
  totalShippingCost: number;

  @Field(() => Boolean)
  freeShippingApplied: boolean;

  @Field(() => String)
  strategyUsed: string;

  @Field(() => [SellerShippingBreakdown], { nullable: true })
  shippingBreakdown?: SellerShippingBreakdown[];
}

@ObjectType()
export class SellerShippingBreakdown {
  @Field(() => String)
  sellerId: string;

  @Field(() => String, { nullable: true })
  sellerName?: string;

  @Field(() => Float)
  originalFee: number;

  @Field(() => Float)
  appliedFee: number;
}

@Resolver()
export class ShippingResolver {
  constructor(
    private readonly shippingCalculationService: ShippingCalculationService
  ) {}

  /**
   * Calculate shipping cost for a cart
   * Used by frontend to display accurate shipping costs
   * No auth required since it only uses provided data
   */
  @Query(() => ShippingCalculationResult)
  async calculateShippingCost(
    @Args('cartSubtotal', { type: () => Float }) cartSubtotal: number,
    @Args('sellerShippingFees', { type: () => [SellerShippingInfoInput] })
    sellerShippingFees: SellerShippingInfoInput[]
  ): Promise<ShippingCalculationResult> {
    const result = await this.shippingCalculationService.calculateShippingCost(
      cartSubtotal,
      sellerShippingFees.map((f) => ({
        sellerId: f.sellerId,
        shippingFee: f.shippingFee,
        sellerName: f.sellerName,
        shippingPaymentMode: f.shippingPaymentMode,
      }))
    );

    return {
      totalShippingCost: result.totalShippingCost,
      freeShippingApplied: result.freeShippingApplied,
      strategyUsed: result.strategyUsed,
      shippingBreakdown: result.shippingBreakdown.map((item) => ({
        sellerId: String(item.sellerId),
        sellerName: item.sellerName,
        originalFee: item.originalFee,
        appliedFee: item.appliedFee,
      })),
    };
  }
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\services\src\lib\currency.service.ts =====
import {
  Injectable,
  Logger,
  NotFoundException,
  BadRequestException,
  OnModuleInit,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository, LessThanOrEqual } from 'typeorm';
import {
  Currency,
  ExchangeRate,
  CurrencyPreference,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';

/** Core currencies seeded on startup so the admin currency screen works out of the box. */
export const BASIC_CURRENCIES: Array<{
  code: string;
  name: string;
  symbol: string;
  decimalPlaces: number;
  isBaseCurrency: boolean;
}> = [
  {
    code: 'USD',
    name: 'US Dollar',
    symbol: '$',
    decimalPlaces: 2,
    isBaseCurrency: true,
  },
  {
    code: 'IRR',
    // Code is IRR for legacy reasons; amounts/symbol are Toman (ZarinPal IRT).
    name: 'Iranian Toman',
    symbol: 'طھظˆظ…ط§ظ†',
    decimalPlaces: 0,
    isBaseCurrency: false,
  },
];

/** Locale â†’ currency defaults seeded on startup. */
export const BASIC_CURRENCY_PREFERENCES: Array<{
  locale: string;
  currencyCode: string;
}> = [
  { locale: 'fa', currencyCode: 'IRR' },
  { locale: 'en', currencyCode: 'USD' },
];

@Injectable()
export class CurrencyService implements OnModuleInit {
  private readonly logger = new Logger(CurrencyService.name);

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Currency)
    private currencyRepository: Repository<Currency>,
    @InjectRepository(ExchangeRate)
    private exchangeRateRepository: Repository<ExchangeRate>,
    @InjectRepository(CurrencyPreference)
    private currencyPreferenceRepository: Repository<CurrencyPreference>
  ) {}

  async onModuleInit() {
    await this.seedBasicCurrencies();
  }

  /**
   * Idempotently insert USD/IRR and default locale preferences.
   * Does not set an exchange rate â€” admins configure that in the UI.
   */
  async seedBasicCurrencies(): Promise<void> {
    let createdCurrencies = 0;

    for (const basic of BASIC_CURRENCIES) {
      let currency = await this.currencyRepository.findOne({
        where: { code: basic.code },
      });
      if (!currency) {
        currency = await this.currencyRepository.save(
          this.currencyRepository.create({
            ...basic,
            isActive: true,
          })
        );
        createdCurrencies += 1;
      }
    }

    let createdPreferences = 0;
    for (const basic of BASIC_CURRENCY_PREFERENCES) {
      const existing = await this.currencyPreferenceRepository.findOne({
        where: { locale: basic.locale },
      });
      if (existing) {
        continue;
      }

      const currency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(basic.currencyCode);
      if (!currency) {
        continue;
      }

      await this.currencyPreferenceRepository.save(
        this.currencyPreferenceRepository.create({
          locale: basic.locale,
          currencyId: currency.id,
          isActive: true,
        })
      );
      createdPreferences += 1;
    }

    if (createdCurrencies > 0 || createdPreferences > 0) {
      this.logger.log(
        `Seeded ${createdCurrencies} currency(ies) and ${createdPreferences} preference(s)`
      );
    }
  }

  // =============== Currency Management ===============

  async findAllCurrencies(): Promise<Currency[]> {
    return this.currencyRepository.find({
      order: { isBaseCurrency: 'DESC', code: 'ASC' },
    });
  }

  async findCurrencyById(id: number): Promise<Currency | null> {
    return this.currencyRepository.findOne({ where: { id } });
  }

  async findCurrencyByCode(code: string): Promise<Currency | null> {
    return this.currencyRepository.findOne({
      where: { code: code.toUpperCase() },
    });
  }

  async createCurrency(data: {
    code: string;
    name: string;
    symbol: string;
    decimalPlaces: number;
    isActive?: boolean;
    isBaseCurrency?: boolean;
  }): Promise<Currency> {
    // If setting as base currency, unset all others
    if (data.isBaseCurrency) {
      await this.currencyRepository.update(
        { isBaseCurrency: true },
        { isBaseCurrency: false }
      );
    }

    const currency = this.currencyRepository.create({
      ...data,
      code: data.code.toUpperCase(),
    });

    return this.currencyRepository.save(currency);
  }

  async updateCurrency(
    id: number,
    data: {
      code?: string;
      name?: string;
      symbol?: string;
      decimalPlaces?: number;
      isActive?: boolean;
      isBaseCurrency?: boolean;
    }
  ): Promise<Currency> {
    const currency = await this.findCurrencyById(id);
    if (!currency) {
      throw new NotFoundException(`Currency with ID ${id} not found`);
    }

    // If setting as base currency, unset all others
    if (data.isBaseCurrency && !currency.isBaseCurrency) {
      await this.currencyRepository.update(
        { isBaseCurrency: true },
        { isBaseCurrency: false }
      );
    }

    Object.assign(currency, {
      ...data,
      code: data.code ? data.code.toUpperCase() : currency.code,
    });

    return this.currencyRepository.save(currency);
  }

  async getBaseCurrency(): Promise<Currency> {
    const baseCurrency = await this.currencyRepository.findOne({
      where: { isBaseCurrency: true, isActive: true },
    });

    if (!baseCurrency) {
      throw new NotFoundException('No base currency configured');
    }

    return baseCurrency;
  }

  // =============== Exchange Rate Management ===============

  async setExchangeRate(data: {
    fromCurrencyCode: string;
    toCurrencyCode: string;
    rate: number;
    effectiveDate?: Date;
    notes?: string;
    createdById?: string;
  }): Promise<ExchangeRate> {
    const fromCurrency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(data.fromCurrencyCode);
    const toCurrency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(data.toCurrencyCode);

    if (!fromCurrency) {
      throw new NotFoundException(
        `Currency ${data.fromCurrencyCode} not found`
      );
    }
    if (!toCurrency) {
      throw new NotFoundException(`Currency ${data.toCurrencyCode} not found`);
    }

    if (data.rate <= 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Exchange rate must be greater than 0');
    }

    const exchangeRate = this.exchangeRateRepository.create({
      fromCurrencyId: fromCurrency.id,
      toCurrencyId: toCurrency.id,
      rate: data.rate,
      effectiveDate: data.effectiveDate || new Date(),
      notes: data.notes,
      createdById: data.createdById,
    });

    const saved = await this.exchangeRateRepository.save(exchangeRate);

    // Reload with relations to satisfy GraphQL schema
    return this.exchangeRateRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: saved.id },
      relations: ['fromCurrency', 'toCurrency', 'createdBy'],
    }) as Promise<ExchangeRate>;
  }

  async getCurrentExchangeRate(
    fromCurrencyCode: string,
    toCurrencyCode: string
  ): Promise<ExchangeRate | null> {
    const fromCurrency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(fromCurrencyCode);
    const toCurrency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(toCurrencyCode);

    if (!fromCurrency || !toCurrency) {
      return null;
    }

    // If same currency, return rate of 1
    if (fromCurrency.id === toCurrency.id) {
      return this.exchangeRateRepository.create({
        fromCurrencyId: fromCurrency.id,
        toCurrencyId: toCurrency.id,
        rate: 1,
        effectiveDate: new Date(),
        id: 0,
      });
    }

    const exchangeRate = await this.exchangeRateRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        fromCurrencyId: fromCurrency.id,
        toCurrencyId: toCurrency.id,
        effectiveDate: LessThanOrEqual(new Date()),
      },
      order: { effectiveDate: 'DESC' },
      relations: ['fromCurrency', 'toCurrency', 'createdBy'],
    });

    return exchangeRate;
  }

  async getExchangeRateHistory(
    fromCurrencyCode: string,
    toCurrencyCode: string,
    limit = 10
  ): Promise<ExchangeRate[]> {
    const fromCurrency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(fromCurrencyCode);
    const toCurrency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(toCurrencyCode);

    if (!fromCurrency || !toCurrency) {
      return [];
    }

    return this.exchangeRateRepository.find({
      where: {
        fromCurrencyId: fromCurrency.id,
        toCurrencyId: toCurrency.id,
      },
      order: { effectiveDate: 'DESC' },
      take: limit,
      relations: ['fromCurrency', 'toCurrency', 'createdBy'],
    });
  }

  async convertPrice(
    amount: number,
    fromCurrencyCode: string,
    toCurrencyCode: string
  ): Promise<{ amount: number; rate: number }> {
    const exchangeRate = await this.getCurrentExchangeRate(
      fromCurrencyCode,
      toCurrencyCode
    );

    if (!exchangeRate) {
      throw new NotFoundException(
        `No exchange rate found from ${fromCurrencyCode} to ${toCurrencyCode}`
      );
    }

    const convertedAmount = amount * exchangeRate.rate;
    const toCurrency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(toCurrencyCode);

    // Round based on decimal places
    const rounded = toCurrency
      ? Number(convertedAmount.toFixed(toCurrency.decimalPlaces))
      : convertedAmount;

    return {
      amount: rounded,
      rate: exchangeRate.rate,
    };
  }

  // =============== Currency Preference Management ===============

  async findAllCurrencyPreferences(): Promise<CurrencyPreference[]> {
    return this.currencyPreferenceRepository.find({
      relations: ['currency'],
      order: { locale: 'ASC' },
    });
  }

  async findCurrencyPreferenceByLocale(
    locale: string
  ): Promise<CurrencyPreference | null> {
    return this.currencyPreferenceRepository.findOne({
      where: { locale },
      relations: ['currency'],
    });
  }

  async setCurrencyPreference(
    locale: string,
    currencyCode: string
  ): Promise<CurrencyPreference> {
    const currency = await this.findCurrencyByCode(currencyCode);
    if (!currency) {
      throw new NotFoundException(`Currency ${currencyCode} not found`);
    }

    let preference = await this.findCurrencyPreferenceByLocale(locale);

    if (preference) {
      preference.currencyId = currency.id;
      preference.currency = currency;
    } else {
      preference = this.currencyPreferenceRepository.create({
        locale,
        currencyId: currency.id,
        currency,
      });
    }

    return this.currencyPreferenceRepository.save(preference);
  }

  async deleteCurrencyPreference(id: number): Promise<boolean> {
    const result = await this.currencyPreferenceRepository.delete(id);
    return result.affected ? result.affected > 0 : false;
  }

  async getCurrencyForLocale(locale: string): Promise<Currency | null> {
    const preference = await this.findCurrencyPreferenceByLocale(locale);
    return preference ? preference.currency : null;
  }
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\services\src\lib\order.service.spec.ts =====
import { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { getRepositoryToken } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository, EntityManager, DataSource, FindOneOptions, FindOptionsWhere } from 'typeorm';

import { OrderService } from './order.service';
import {
  Order,
  OrderItem,
  OrderStatus,
  SellerProduct,
  SellerInfo,
  ShippingPaymentMode,

  PlatformSettings,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';

  ShippingCalculationService,

    };

    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({
      providers: [

          provide: getRepositoryToken(Order),

          provide: getRepositoryToken(OrderItem),

          provide: DataSource,
          useValue: dataSource,
        },
      ],
    }).compile();

    service = module.get<OrderService>(OrderService);
    orderRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(Order));
    orderItemRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(OrderItem));
    sellerProductRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(SellerProduct));
    sellerInfoRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(SellerInfo));

    orderRepository.createQueryBuilder = jest
      .fn()
      .mockReturnValue(mockQueryBuilder);

    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  describe('createOrder', () => {
    const createOrderInput: CreateOrderInput = {
      customerEmail: 'customer@example.com',
      customerPhone: '1234567890',
      customerName: 'John Doe',
      shippingAddress: '123 Main St',
      city: 'New York',
      state: 'NY',
      zipCode: '10001',
      country: 'USA',
      sellerId: 1,
      items: [
        {
          productId: 'product-1',
          sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
          productName: 'Test Product',
          quantity: 2,
          unitPrice: 50,
          totalPrice: 100,
        },
      ],
      shippingCost: 10,
    };

    const mockSeller: SellerInfo = {
      id: 1,
      storeName: 'Test Store',
      phone: '9876543210',
      address: 'Seller Address',
      shippingFee: 15,
    } as SellerInfo;

    const mockSellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
      id: 'seller-product-1',
      price: 50,
      discount: 0,
      productInfoId: 1,
      stockQuantity: 100,
      seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'],
    } as SellerProduct;

    const mockSavedOrder = {
      id: 'order-1',
      orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
      trackingNumber: 'KZ-20240101-ABC123',
      customerEmail: createOrderInput.customerEmail,
      customerPhone: createOrderInput.customerPhone,
      customerName: createOrderInput.customerName,
      shippingAddress: createOrderInput.shippingAddress,
      city: createOrderInput.city,
      state: createOrderInput.state,
      zipCode: createOrderInput.zipCode,
      country: createOrderInput.country,
      sellerId: createOrderInput.sellerId,
      status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      stockReserved: false,
      subtotal: 100,
      shippingCost: 10,
      total: 110,
      sellerStoreName: mockSeller.storeName,
      sellerPhone: mockSeller.phone,
      sellerAddress: mockSeller.address,
      items: undefined,
      seller: mockSeller,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    } as unknown as Order;

    const mockOrderItem: OrderItem = {
      id: 'item-1',
      orderId: 'order-1',
      productId: 'product-1',
      sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
      productName: 'Test Product',
      quantity: 2,
      unitPrice: 50,
      totalPrice: 100,
      originalPrice: 50,
      discountPercentage: 0,
      productInfoId: 1,
    } as OrderItem;

    it('should create an order successfully with stockReserved = false', async () => {
      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({ ...orderWithoutItems } as unknown as typeof orderWithoutItems);
      orderItemRepository.save.mockResolvedValue([mockOrderItem] as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(createOrderInput);

      expect(orderRepository.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.not.objectContaining({ items: expect.any(Array) }),
      );
      expect(sellerInfoRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: createOrderInput.sellerId },
      });
      expect(orderRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({ items: undefined }),
      );
      expect(orderItemRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(result).toBeDefined();
      expect(result.orderNumber).toBeDefined();
      expect(result.trackingNumber).toBeDefined();
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.PENDING);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
      expect(result.items).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException if seller does not exist', async () => {
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(service.createOrder(createOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        NotFoundException,
      );
      await expect(service.createOrder(createOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        `Seller with ID ${createOrderInput.sellerId} not found`,
      );
    });

    it('should calculate subtotal and total correctly', async () => {
      const inputWithTwoItems = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [
          { ...createOrderInput.items[0], sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1', totalPrice: 100 },
          { ...createOrderInput.items[0], productId: 'product-2', sellerProductId: 'seller-product-2', totalPrice: 50 },
        ],
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
      };
      // Catalog prices: product-1 = 50, product-2 = 25 (different from client prices)
      const sellerProduct1 = { ...mockSellerProduct, id: 'seller-product-1', price: 50, discount: 0, seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'] } as SellerProduct;
      const sellerProduct2 = { ...mockSellerProduct, id: 'seller-product-2', price: 25, discount: 0, seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'] } as SellerProduct;
      const mockOrderItem1 = { ...mockOrderItem, totalPrice: 100, id: 'item-1', unitPrice: 50 } as OrderItem;
      const mockOrderItem2 = { ...mockOrderItem, productId: 'product-2', totalPrice: 50, id: 'item-2', unitPrice: 25 } as OrderItem;
      const onlineSeller = { ...mockSeller, shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE, shippingFee: 10 };
      const orderWithoutItems = {
        ...mockSavedOrder,
        items: undefined,
        subtotal: 150, // 50*2 + 25*2 = 100 + 50 = 150
        total: 160, // 150 + 10 (vip = full fee)
        shippingCost: 10,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
        shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
      } as never;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(onlineSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct1)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct2);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(orderWithoutItems);
      orderItemRepository.save.mockResolvedValue([mockOrderItem1, mockOrderItem2] as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(inputWithTwoItems);

      expect(result.subtotal).toBe(150);
      expect(result.total).toBe(160);
      expect(result.items).toHaveLength(2);
    });

    it('should compute server shipping cost for PAY_ONLINE seller ignoring client shippingCost', async () => {
      const inputWithShipping = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        shippingCost: 20, // This should be ignored
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
      };
      const onlineSeller = { ...mockSeller, shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE, shippingFee: 10 };
      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(onlineSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...orderWithoutItems,
        shippingCost: 10, // Server computed: vip = 100% of 10
        total: 110,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
        shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
      });
      orderItemRepository.save.mockResolvedValue([mockOrderItem] as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(inputWithShipping);

      expect(result.shippingCost).toBe(10); // Server computed, not client's 20
      expect(result.total).toBe(110);
    });

    it('should compute zero shipping for PAY_ON_DELIVERY seller regardless of shippingFee', async () => {
      const inputWithoutShipping = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        shippingCost: undefined,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
      };
      const codSeller = { ...mockSeller, shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY, shippingFee: 15 };
      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(codSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...orderWithoutItems,
        shippingCost: 0,
        total: 100,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
        shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
      });
      orderItemRepository.save.mockResolvedValue([mockOrderItem] as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(inputWithoutShipping);

      expect(result.shippingCost).toBe(0);
      expect(result.total).toBe(100);
    });

    it('should preserve seller snapshot information', async () => {
      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(orderWithoutItems);
      orderItemRepository.save.mockResolvedValue([mockOrderItem] as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(createOrderInput);

      expect(result.sellerStoreName).toBe(mockSeller.storeName);
      expect(result.sellerPhone).toBe(mockSeller.phone);
      expect(result.sellerAddress).toBe(mockSeller.address);
    });

    it('should NOT decrease stock when creating order', async () => {
      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(orderWithoutItems);
      orderItemRepository.save.mockResolvedValue([] as never);

      await service.createOrder(createOrderInput);

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(mockSellerProduct.stockQuantity).toBe(100);
    });

    it('should preserve product pricing snapshot in order items', async () => {
      const sellerProductWithDiscount = {
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        price: 60,
        discount: 10,
        seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'],
      } as SellerProduct;
      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(
        sellerProductWithDiscount,
      );
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(orderWithoutItems);
      orderItemRepository.save.mockImplementation(((items: unknown[]) =>
        Promise.resolve(items)) as never);

      await service.createOrder(createOrderInput);

      expect(orderItemRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      const savedItems = orderItemRepository.save.mock.calls[0][0] as OrderItem[];
      expect(Array.isArray(savedItems)).toBe(true);
      expect(savedItems.length).toBe(1);
      expect(savedItems[0]).toHaveProperty('originalPrice', 60);
      expect(savedItems[0]).toHaveProperty('discountPercentage', 10);
      expect(savedItems[0]).toHaveProperty('orderId', mockSavedOrder.id);
    });

    it('should insert each order item only once (no cascade + manual save duplication)', async () => {
      const inputWithTwoItems: CreateOrderInput = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [
          {
            productId: 'product-1',
            sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
            productName: 'Test Product 1',
            quantity: 1,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 50,
          },
          {
            productId: 'product-2',
            sellerProductId: 'seller-product-2',
            productName: 'Test Product 2',
            quantity: 2,
            unitPrice: 30,
            totalPrice: 60,
          },
        ],
      };

      const sellerProduct2 = { ...mockSellerProduct, id: 'seller-product-2', seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'] } as SellerProduct;

      const orderWithoutItems = {
        ...mockSavedOrder,
        items: undefined,
        subtotal: 110,
        total: 120,
      } as unknown as Order;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(mockSellerProduct)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct2);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({ ...orderWithoutItems } as unknown as typeof orderWithoutItems);
      orderItemRepository.save.mockImplementation(((items: object[]) =>
        Promise.resolve(items.map((i: object, idx: number) => ({ ...i, id: `item-${idx + 1}` })))) as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(inputWithTwoItems);

      const savedOrderArg = orderRepository.save.mock.calls[0][0] as Order;
      expect(savedOrderArg.items).toBeUndefined();

      expect(orderItemRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

      expect(result.items).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(result.items[0].quantity).toBe(1);
      expect(result.items[1].quantity).toBe(2);
    });

    it('should override client prices with catalog prices (price tampering protection)', async () => {
      // Client sends unitPrice: 10, totalPrice: 20 but catalog price is 50
      const inputWithTamperedPrices = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [
          {
            ...createOrderInput.items[0],
            unitPrice: 10,
            totalPrice: 20,
          },
        ],
      };
      const sellerProductWithHigherPrice = {
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        price: 50,
        discount: 0,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProductWithHigherPrice);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...orderWithoutItems,
        subtotal: 100, // 50 * 2 = 100 (catalog price)
        total: 110,
      });
      orderItemRepository.save.mockImplementation(((items: unknown[]) =>
        Promise.resolve(items)) as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(inputWithTamperedPrices);

      expect(result.subtotal).toBe(100); // Catalog price, not client's 20
      expect(result.total).toBe(110);
      const savedItems = orderItemRepository.save.mock.calls[0][0] as OrderItem[];
      expect(savedItems[0].unitPrice).toBe(50); // Catalog unit price
      expect(savedItems[0].totalPrice).toBe(100); // Catalog total price
    });

    it('should apply discount when computing catalog price', async () => {
      const sellerProductWithDiscount = {
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        price: 100,
        discount: 20, // 20% off
        seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'],
      } as SellerProduct;

      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProductWithDiscount);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...orderWithoutItems,
        subtotal: 160, // 80 * 2 = 160 (after 20% discount)
        total: 170,
      });
      orderItemRepository.save.mockImplementation(((items: unknown[]) =>
        Promise.resolve(items)) as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(createOrderInput);

      expect(result.subtotal).toBe(160);
      expect(result.total).toBe(170);
      const savedItems = orderItemRepository.save.mock.calls[0][0] as OrderItem[];
      expect(savedItems[0].unitPrice).toBe(80); // 100 * 0.8 = 80
      expect(savedItems[0].totalPrice).toBe(160); // 80 * 2 = 160
      expect(savedItems[0].originalPrice).toBe(100);
      expect(savedItems[0].discountPercentage).toBe(20);
    });

    it('should reject cross-seller ownership (product belongs to different seller)', async () => {
      const sellerProductWrongSeller = {
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        seller: { id: 999 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'], // Different seller
      } as SellerProduct;

      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProductWrongSeller);

      await expect(service.createOrder(createOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(service.createOrder(createOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        /does not belong to seller 1/,
      );
    });

    it('should reject zero quantity', async () => {
      const inputZeroQty = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [{ ...createOrderInput.items[0], quantity: 0 }],
      };

      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);

      await expect(service.createOrder(inputZeroQty)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(service.createOrder(inputZeroQty)).rejects.toThrow(
        /Quantity must be a positive integer/,
      );
    });

    it('should reject negative quantity', async () => {
      const inputNegativeQty = {
        ...createOrderInput,
