        items: [{ ...createOrderInput.items[0], quantity: -1 }],
      };

      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);

      await expect(service.createOrder(inputNegativeQty)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(service.createOrder(inputNegativeQty)).rejects.toThrow(
        /Quantity must be a positive integer/,
      );
    });

    it('should reject non-integer quantity', async () => {
      const inputFloatQty = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [{ ...createOrderInput.items[0], quantity: 2.5 }],
      };

      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);

      await expect(service.createOrder(inputFloatQty)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(service.createOrder(inputFloatQty)).rejects.toThrow(
        /Quantity must be a positive integer/,
      );
    });

    it('should ignore client shippingCost (negative value is ignored, server computes based on seller mode)', async () => {
      const inputNegativeShipping = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        shippingCost: -5,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
      };
      const onlineSeller = { ...mockSeller, shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE, shippingFee: 10 };
      const orderWithoutItems = { ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder;
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(onlineSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...orderWithoutItems,
        shippingCost: 10,
        total: 110,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
        shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
      });
      orderItemRepository.save.mockResolvedValue([mockOrderItem] as never);

      const result = await service.createOrder(inputNegativeShipping);

      // Client shippingCost (-5) is ignored; server computes 10 for PAY_ONLINE + vip
      expect(result.shippingCost).toBe(10);
      expect(result.total).toBe(110);
    });

    it('should reject insufficient stock at create', async () => {
      const sellerProductLowStock = {
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 1, // Only 1 in stock
        seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'],
      } as SellerProduct;

      const inputExceedsStock = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [{ ...createOrderInput.items[0], quantity: 5 }],
      };

      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProductLowStock);

      await expect(service.createOrder(inputExceedsStock)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(service.createOrder(inputExceedsStock)).rejects.toThrow(
        /Insufficient stock for product seller-product-1. Available: 1, Requested: 5/,
      );
    });

    it('should reject missing sellerProductId', async () => {
      const inputMissingSellerProductId = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [{ ...createOrderInput.items[0], sellerProductId: undefined }],
      };

      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);

      await expect(service.createOrder(inputMissingSellerProductId)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(service.createOrder(inputMissingSellerProductId)).rejects.toThrow(
        /sellerProductId is required/,
      );
    });

    it('should reject non-existent sellerProduct', async () => {
      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(service.createOrder(createOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
      await expect(service.createOrder(createOrderInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        /SellerProduct with ID seller-product-1 not found/,
      );
    });

    it('should reject empty items array', async () => {
      const inputEmptyItems = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [],
      };

      await expect(service.createOrder(inputEmptyItems)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(service.createOrder(inputEmptyItems)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order must contain at least one item',
      );
    });

    it('should reject when aggregated quantity across items exceeds stock', async () => {
      // Two line items for the same sellerProductId, each quantity <= stock individually,
      // but combined quantity > stock
      const sellerProductLimitedStock = {
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 3, // Only 3 in stock
        seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'],
      } as SellerProduct;

      const inputTwoItemsSameProduct = {
        ...createOrderInput,
        items: [
          {
            ...createOrderInput.items[0],
            quantity: 2,
            sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
          },
          {
            ...createOrderInput.items[0],
            productId: 'product-1-variant',
            sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1', // Same sellerProductId
            quantity: 2, // Combined = 4 > 3
          },
        ],
      };

      orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ ...mockSavedOrder, items: undefined } as unknown as typeof mockSavedOrder);
      sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSeller);
      // findOne will be called twice per service call (once per item in Promise.all),
      // and the test calls the service twice (two expect statements), so 4 total calls
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProductLimitedStock);

      await expect(service.createOrder(inputTwoItemsSameProduct)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.createOrder(inputTwoItemsSameProduct)).rejects.toThrow(
        /Insufficient stock for product seller-product-1. Available: 3, Requested: 4/,
      );
    });

    // Heavy 6: Locked Tests 1-9
    describe('Heavy 6: createOrder catalog pricing, aggregated stock, and input validation', () => {
      const heavy6Seller: SellerInfo = {
        id: 1,
        storeName: 'Heavy 6 Store',
        phone: '1112223333',
        address: 'Heavy 6 Address',
        shippingFee: 15,
      } as SellerInfo;

      const baseItem = {
        productId: 'prod-1',
        sellerProductId: 'sp-1',
        productName: 'Heavy 6 Product',
        productImage: 'img.jpg',
        color: 'Red',
        size: '42',
        unitPrice: 100,
        totalPrice: 100,
      };

      const makeSellerProduct = (overrides: Partial<SellerProduct> = {}): SellerProduct => ({
        id: 'sp-1',
        price: 100,
        discount: 0,
        stockQuantity: 5,
        seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'],
        productInfoId: 1,
        product: undefined as never,
        productInfo: undefined as never,
        costPrice: undefined as never,
        ...overrides,
      } as SellerProduct);

      const baseInput = (overrides: Partial<CreateOrderInput> = {}): CreateOrderInput => ({
        customerEmail: 'c@e.com',
        customerPhone: '123',
        customerName: 'Customer',
        shippingAddress: 'Addr',
        city: 'City',
        state: 'ST',
        zipCode: '12345',
        country: 'US',
        sellerId: 1,
        items: [],
        ...overrides,
      });

      const setupSuccessMocks = (sellerProduct: SellerProduct, savedOrderOverrides: Partial<Order> = {}) => {
        const orderWithoutItems = {
          id: 'order-1',
          orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
          trackingNumber: 'KZ-123',
          customerEmail: 'c@e.com',
          customerPhone: '123',
          customerName: 'Customer',
          shippingAddress: 'Addr',
          city: 'City',
          state: 'ST',
          zipCode: '12345',
          country: 'US',
          sellerId: 1,
          status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
          stockReserved: false,
          subtotal: 0,
          shippingCost: 0,
          total: 0,
          sellerStoreName: heavy6Seller.storeName,
          sellerPhone: heavy6Seller.phone,
          sellerAddress: heavy6Seller.address,
          items: undefined,
          seller: heavy6Seller,
          createdAt: new Date(),
          updatedAt: new Date(),
          ...savedOrderOverrides,
        } as never;
        orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue(orderWithoutItems);
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(heavy6Seller);
        sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
        orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(orderWithoutItems);
        orderItemRepository.save.mockImplementation(((items: unknown) => Promise.resolve(Array.isArray(items) ? items.map((i: object, idx: number) => ({ ...i, id: `item-${idx + 1}` })) : items)) as never);
      };

      // Locked Test 1: items: [], null, undefined each reject with 'Order must contain at least one item'
      it('Locked Test 1: rejects empty items array, null, and undefined', async () => {
        const emptyInput = baseInput({ items: [] });
        await expect(service.createOrder(emptyInput)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(service.createOrder(emptyInput)).rejects.toThrow('Order must contain at least one item');

        const nullInput = baseInput({ items: null as never });
        await expect(service.createOrder(nullInput)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(service.createOrder(nullInput)).rejects.toThrow('Order must contain at least one item');

        const undefinedInput = baseInput({ items: undefined as never });
        await expect(service.createOrder(undefinedInput)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(service.createOrder(undefinedInput)).rejects.toThrow('Order must contain at least one item');
      });

      // Locked Test 2: 3 items on 'sp-1' (stock 5) qty 2,2,2 -> rejects 'Insufficient stock for product sp-1. Available: 5, Requested: 6'
      it('Locked Test 2: aggregated quantity across 3 items exceeds stock', async () => {
        const sellerProduct = makeSellerProduct({ stockQuantity: 5 });
        const input = baseInput({
          items: [
            { ...baseItem, quantity: 2, size: '42' },
            { ...baseItem, quantity: 2, size: '43' },
            { ...baseItem, quantity: 2, size: '44' },
          ],
        });
        orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ id: 'o', items: undefined } as never);
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(heavy6Seller);
        sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);

        await expect(service.createOrder(input)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(service.createOrder(input)).rejects.toThrow('Insufficient stock for product sp-1. Available: 5, Requested: 6');
        expect(orderRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
        expect(orderItemRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });

      // Locked Test 3: 2 items on 'sp-1' (stock 5) qty 2 (size 42) and 3 (size 43) -> resolves; order.save subtotal 500; orderItem.save 2 items unitPrice 100/totalPrice 200 and 100/300
      it('Locked Test 3: two items same sellerProductId different sizes within stock resolves with correct pricing', async () => {
        const sellerProduct = makeSellerProduct({ stockQuantity: 5, price: 100, discount: 0 });
        const input = baseInput({
          items: [
            { ...baseItem, quantity: 2, size: '42' },
            { ...baseItem, quantity: 3, size: '43' },
          ],
        });
        setupSuccessMocks(sellerProduct, { subtotal: 500, shippingCost: 15, total: 515 });

        const result = await service.createOrder(input);

        expect(orderRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ subtotal: 500 }));
        const savedItems = orderItemRepository.save.mock.calls[0][0] as OrderItem[];
        expect(savedItems).toHaveLength(2);
        expect(savedItems[0]).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ unitPrice: 100, totalPrice: 200 }));
        expect(savedItems[1]).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ unitPrice: 100, totalPrice: 300 }));
        expect(result.subtotal).toBe(500);
      });

      // Locked Test 4: sp-1 (stock 5) qty 1 and 1; sp-2 (stock 3) qty 2 and 2 -> rejects 'Insufficient stock for product sp-2. Available: 3, Requested: 4'
      it('Locked Test 4: aggregated quantity across multiple sellerProductIds exceeds stock on sp-2', async () => {
        const sellerProduct1 = makeSellerProduct({ id: 'sp-1', stockQuantity: 5 });
        const sellerProduct2 = makeSellerProduct({ id: 'sp-2', stockQuantity: 3 });
        const input = baseInput({
          items: [
            { ...baseItem, sellerProductId: 'sp-1', quantity: 1 },
            { ...baseItem, sellerProductId: 'sp-1', quantity: 1 },
            { ...baseItem, sellerProductId: 'sp-2', quantity: 2 },
            { ...baseItem, sellerProductId: 'sp-2', quantity: 2 },
          ],
        });
        orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ id: 'o', items: undefined } as never);
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(heavy6Seller);
        sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockImplementation(({ where }: FindOneOptions<SellerProduct>) => {
          const id = (where as FindOptionsWhere<SellerProduct> | undefined)?.id;
          if (id === 'sp-1') return Promise.resolve(sellerProduct1);
          if (id === 'sp-2') return Promise.resolve(sellerProduct2);
          return Promise.resolve(null);
        });

        await expect(service.createOrder(input)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(service.createOrder(input)).rejects.toThrow('Insufficient stock for product sp-2. Available: 3, Requested: 4');
        expect(orderRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      });

      // Locked Test 5: quantity 0, -3, 1.5, NaN each reject /Quantity must be a positive integer for product/; sellerProductId '' rejects /sellerProductId is required for product/
      it('Locked Test 5: invalid quantities and missing sellerProductId reject with correct messages', async () => {
        const sellerProduct = makeSellerProduct();
        orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ id: 'o', items: undefined } as never);
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(heavy6Seller);
        sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);

        const invalidQuantities = [0, -3, 1.5, NaN];
        for (const qty of invalidQuantities) {
          const input = baseInput({ items: [{ ...baseItem, quantity: qty }] });
          await expect(service.createOrder(input)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
          await expect(service.createOrder(input)).rejects.toThrow(/Quantity must be a positive integer for product/);
        }

        const missingSellerProductIdInput = baseInput({ items: [{ ...baseItem, sellerProductId: '', quantity: 1 }] });
        await expect(service.createOrder(missingSellerProductIdInput)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(service.createOrder(missingSellerProductIdInput)).rejects.toThrow(/sellerProductId is required for product/);
      });

      // Locked Test 6: sellerId 1 with seller.id '1' as any succeeds; seller.id '99' as any rejects 'Product sp-1 does not belong to seller 1'
      it('Locked Test 6: seller ownership check - string "1" succeeds, string "99" rejects', async () => {
        const sellerProductSeller1 = makeSellerProduct({ seller: { id: '1' } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'] });
        const input = baseInput({ items: [{ ...baseItem, quantity: 1 }] });
        setupSuccessMocks(sellerProductSeller1);

        await expect(service.createOrder(input)).resolves.toBeDefined();

        const sellerProductSeller99 = makeSellerProduct({ seller: { id: '99' } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'] });
        orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ id: 'o', items: undefined } as never);
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(heavy6Seller);
        sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProductSeller99);

        await expect(service.createOrder(input)).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
        await expect(service.createOrder(input)).rejects.toThrow('Product sp-1 does not belong to seller 1');
      });

      // Locked Test 7: tampered client fields (unitPrice 0.01, totalPrice 0.01, subtotal 0.01, total 0.01 as any), qty 2, price 200, discount 25, stock 10, seller.shippingFee 10, shippingCost undefined -> order.save objectContaining({ subtotal: 300, shippingCost: 10, total: 310 }); orderItem.save [objectContaining({ unitPrice: 150, totalPrice: 300, originalPrice: 200, discountPercentage: 25 })]
      it('Locked Test 7: tampered client prices are ignored; catalog pricing and shippingFee fallback used', async () => {
        const sellerProduct = makeSellerProduct({ price: 200, discount: 25, stockQuantity: 10 });
        const heavy6SellerWithFee = { ...heavy6Seller, shippingFee: 10, shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE };
        const input = baseInput({
          shippingCost: undefined,
          shippingMethod: 'vip',
          items: [{
            ...baseItem,
            quantity: 2,
            unitPrice: 0.01,
            totalPrice: 0.01,
          }],
        });
        setupSuccessMocks(sellerProduct, { subtotal: 300, shippingCost: 10, total: 310, shippingMethod: 'vip', shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE });
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(heavy6SellerWithFee);

        const result = await service.createOrder(input);

        expect(orderRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ subtotal: 300, shippingCost: 10, total: 310 }));
        const savedItems = orderItemRepository.save.mock.calls[0][0] as OrderItem[];
        expect(savedItems[0]).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({
          unitPrice: 150, // 200 * (1 - 25/100) = 150
          totalPrice: 300, // 150 * 2
          originalPrice: 200,
          discountPercentage: 25,
        }));
        expect(result.subtotal).toBe(300);
        expect(result.shippingCost).toBe(10);
        expect(result.total).toBe(310);
      });

      // Locked Test 8: price 99.99, discount 15, stock 10, qty 3, shippingCost 0 -> orderItem.save [objectContaining({ unitPrice: 84.99, totalPrice: 254.97 })]; order.save objectContaining({ subtotal: 254.97, shippingCost: 0, total: 254.97 })
      it('Locked Test 8: precision pricing with discount and zero shippingCost', async () => {
        const sellerProduct = makeSellerProduct({ price: 99.99, discount: 15, stockQuantity: 10 });
        const codSeller = { ...heavy6Seller, shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY };
        const input = baseInput({
          shippingCost: 0,
          shippingMethod: 'vip',
          items: [{ ...baseItem, quantity: 3 }],
        });
        setupSuccessMocks(sellerProduct, { subtotal: 254.97, shippingCost: 0, total: 254.97, shippingMethod: 'vip', shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY });

        const result = await service.createOrder(input);

        const savedItems = orderItemRepository.save.mock.calls[0][0] as OrderItem[];
        expect(savedItems[0]).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({
          unitPrice: 84.99, // 99.99 * 0.85 = 84.9915 -> 84.99
          totalPrice: 254.97, // 84.99 * 3 = 254.97
        }));
        expect(orderRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ subtotal: 254.97, shippingCost: 0, total: 254.97 }));
        expect(result.subtotal).toBe(254.97);
        expect(result.shippingCost).toBe(0);
        expect(result.total).toBe(254.97);
      });

      // Locked Test 9: seller.shippingFee 15, item subtotal 100: client shippingCost is ignored; server computes based on seller shippingPaymentMode
      it('Locked Test 9: client shippingCost ignored; server computes based on seller shippingPaymentMode', async () => {
        const sellerProduct = makeSellerProduct({ price: 100, discount: 0, stockQuantity: 10 });

        // (a) PAY_ON_DELIVERY seller: any client shippingCost -> shippingCost = 0
        const codSeller = { ...heavy6Seller, shippingFee: 15, shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY };
        const inputZero = baseInput({ shippingCost: 0, shippingMethod: 'vip', items: [{ ...baseItem, quantity: 1 }] });
        setupSuccessMocks(sellerProduct, { subtotal: 100, shippingCost: 0, total: 100, shippingMethod: 'vip', shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY });
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(codSeller);
        let result = await service.createOrder(inputZero);
        expect(result).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ subtotal: 100, shippingCost: 0, total: 100 }));

        // (b) PAY_ONLINE seller with vip: client shippingCost null -> server computes full fee (15)
        const onlineSeller = { ...heavy6Seller, shippingFee: 15, shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE };
        const inputNull = baseInput({ shippingCost: null as never, shippingMethod: 'vip', items: [{ ...baseItem, quantity: 1 }] });
        setupSuccessMocks(sellerProduct, { subtotal: 100, shippingCost: 15, total: 115, shippingMethod: 'vip', shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE });
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(onlineSeller);
        result = await service.createOrder(inputNull);
        expect(result).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ subtotal: 100, shippingCost: 15, total: 115 }));

        // (c) PAY_ONLINE seller: client shippingCost NaN -> ignored, server computes
        const inputNaN = baseInput({ shippingCost: NaN, shippingMethod: 'vip', items: [{ ...baseItem, quantity: 1 }] });
        orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ id: 'o', items: undefined } as never);
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(onlineSeller);
        sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
        result = await service.createOrder(inputNaN);
        expect(result).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ subtotal: 100, shippingCost: 15, total: 115 }));

        // (d) PAY_ONLINE seller: client shippingCost -5 -> ignored, server computes
        const inputNegative = baseInput({ shippingCost: -5, shippingMethod: 'vip', items: [{ ...baseItem, quantity: 1 }] });
        orderRepository.create.mockReturnValue({ id: 'o', items: undefined } as never);
        sellerInfoRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(onlineSeller);
        sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
        result = await service.createOrder(inputNegative);
        expect(result).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ subtotal: 100, shippingCost: 15, total: 115 }));
      });
    });
  });

  describe('findOne', () => {
    const orderId = 'order-1';
    const mockOrder: Order = {
      id: orderId,
      orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
      status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      stockReserved: false,
      items: [],
      seller: {} as SellerInfo,
      customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
      customerPhone: '1234567890',
      customerName: 'Test User',
      shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
      city: 'Test City',
      state: 'Test State',
      zipCode: '12345',
      country: 'Test Country',
      sellerId: 1,
      subtotal: 0,
      shippingCost: 0,
      total: 0,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    } as Order;

    it('should return an order by id', async () => {
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockOrder);

      const result = await service.findOne(orderId);

      expect(orderRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: orderId },
        relations: ['items', 'seller'],
      });
      expect(result).toEqual(mockOrder);
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException if order does not exist', async () => {
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(service.findOne(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
      await expect(service.findOne(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        `Order with ID ${orderId} not found`,
      );
    });
  });

  describe('findAll', () => {
    const mockOrders: Order[] = [
      {
        id: 'order-1',
        orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
        createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
      } as Order,
      {
        id: 'order-2',
        orderNumber: 'ORD-2',
        createdAt: new Date('2024-01-02'),
      } as Order,
    ];

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockQueryBuilder.getMany.mockResolvedValue(mockOrders);
      mockQueryBuilder.getCount.mockResolvedValue(2);
    });

    it('should return paginated orders', async () => {
      const result = await service.findAll(10);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.leftJoinAndSelect).toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.orderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt',
        'DESC',
      );
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.take).toHaveBeenCalledWith(11);
      expect(result.edges).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(result.totalCount).toBe(2);
    });

    it('should filter by sellerId', async () => {
      await service.findAll(10, undefined, '1');

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.sellerId = :sellerId',
        { sellerId: 1 },
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for invalid sellerId', async () => {
      await expect(service.findAll(10, undefined, 'invalid')).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.findAll(10, undefined, 'invalid')).rejects.toThrow(
        'Invalid sellerId',
      );
    });

    it('should filter by status', async () => {
      await service.findAll(10, undefined, undefined, OrderStatus.PENDING);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.status = :status',
        { status: OrderStatus.PENDING },
      );
    });

    it('should handle pagination cursor', async () => {
      const cursor = Buffer.from(new Date().toISOString()).toString('base64');
      await service.findAll(10, cursor);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should indicate hasNextPage when more items exist', async () => {
      const manyOrders = Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => ({
        id: `order-${i}`,
        createdAt: new Date(),
      })) as Order[];
      mockQueryBuilder.getMany.mockResolvedValue(manyOrders);

      const result = await service.findAll(10);

      expect(result.pageInfo.hasNextPage).toBe(true);
    });

    it('should apply id DESC tie-breaker ordering for deterministic pagination', async () => {
      await service.findAll(10);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.orderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt',
        'DESC',
      );
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.addOrderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.id',
        'DESC',
      );
    });

    it('should filter by both sellerId and status', async () => {
      await service.findAll(10, undefined, '1', OrderStatus.DELIVERED);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.sellerId = :sellerId',
        { sellerId: 1 },
      );
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.status = :status',
        { status: OrderStatus.DELIVERED },
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for invalid cursor', async () => {
      const invalidCursor = 'not-valid-base64-date';
      await expect(service.findAll(10, invalidCursor)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.findAll(10, invalidCursor)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Invalid cursor',
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for empty string cursor', async () => {
      await expect(service.findAll(10, '')).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(service.findAll(10, '')).rejects.toThrow('Invalid cursor');
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for whitespace-only cursor', async () => {
      await expect(service.findAll(10, '   ')).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.findAll(10, '   ')).rejects.toThrow('Invalid cursor');
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for base64-encoded non-date string', async () => {
      const nonDateCursor = Buffer.from('not-a-date').toString('base64');
      await expect(service.findAll(10, nonDateCursor)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.findAll(10, nonDateCursor)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Invalid cursor',
      );
    });

    it('should handle valid cursor and set hasPreviousPage to true', async () => {
      const validDate = new Date('2026-01-15T10:00:00.000Z');
      const validCursor = Buffer.from(validDate.toISOString()).toString('base64');
      await service.findAll(10, validCursor);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt < :cursor',
        { cursor: validDate },
      );
    });

    it('should return hasPreviousPage: false when after is undefined', async () => {
      const result = await service.findAll(10, undefined);

      expect(result.pageInfo.hasPreviousPage).toBe(false);
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt < :cursor',
        expect.any(Object),
      );
    });

    it('should return hasPreviousPage: false when after is null', async () => {
      const result = await service.findAll(10, null as never);

      expect(result.pageInfo.hasPreviousPage).toBe(false);
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt < :cursor',
        expect.any(Object),
      );
    });

    it('should return hasPreviousPage: false when after is empty string', async () => {
      await expect(service.findAll(10, '')).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
    });

    it('should apply orderBy createdAt DESC and addOrderBy id DESC', async () => {
      await service.findAll(10);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.orderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt',
        'DESC',
      );
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.addOrderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order.id', 'DESC');
    });
  });

  describe('updateOrder', () => {
    const orderId = 'order-1';
    const existingOrder: Order = {
      id: orderId,
      orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
      subtotal: 100,
      shippingCost: 10,
      total: 110,
      stockReserved: false,
      items: [],
      status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      seller: {} as SellerInfo,
      customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
      customerPhone: '1234567890',
      customerName: 'Test User',
      shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
      city: 'Test City',
      state: 'Test State',
      zipCode: '12345',
      country: 'Test Country',
      sellerId: 1,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
    } as Order;

    const updateInput: UpdateOrderInput = {
      customerName: 'Updated Name',
    };

    it('should update order successfully', async () => {
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...existingOrder,
        ...updateInput,
      } as Order);

      const result = await service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInput);

      expect(orderRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: orderId },
        relations: ['items', 'seller'],
      });
      expect(result).toEqual({ ...existingOrder, ...updateInput });
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException when items are provided', async () => {
      const updateInputWithItems: UpdateOrderInput = {
        items: [
          {
            productId: 'product-1',
            productName: 'Product 1',
            quantity: 3,
            unitPrice: 50,
            totalPrice: 150,
          },
        ],
      };

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);

      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInputWithItems)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInputWithItems)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation',
      );
      expect(orderRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException when subtotal is provided', async () => {
      const updateInputWithSubtotal = { ...updateInput, subtotal: 200 as unknown };

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);

      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInputWithSubtotal)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInputWithSubtotal)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation',
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException when total is provided', async () => {
      const updateInputWithTotal = { ...updateInput, total: 200 as unknown };

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);

      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInputWithTotal)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInputWithTotal)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation',
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException when shippingCost is provided', async () => {
      const updateInputWithShippingCost = { ...updateInput, shippingCost: 20 as unknown };

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);

      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInputWithShippingCost)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInputWithShippingCost)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation',
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException when order status is not PENDING', async () => {
      const confirmedOrder = { ...existingOrder, status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED };

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(confirmedOrder);

      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.updateOrder(orderId, updateInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation',
      );
      expect(orderRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });

  describe('updateOrderStatus', () => {
    const orderId = 'order-1';
    const mockOrderItems: OrderItem[] = [
      {
        id: 'item-1',
        productId: 'product-1',
        sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
        quantity: 2,
      } as OrderItem,
    ];

    it('should update order status successfully PENDING -> CONFIRMED with stock decrease', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
      );

      expect(sellerProductRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: 'seller-product-1' },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          stockQuantity: 8,
        }),
      );
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(true);
    });

    it('should restore stock when cancelling a CONFIRMED order (stockReserved = true)', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
      );

      expect(sellerProductRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: 'seller-product-1' },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          stockQuantity: 10,
        }),
      );
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should NOT restore stock when cancelling a PENDING order (stockReserved = false)', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
      );

      expect(sellerProductRepository.findOne).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should restore stock when refunding a CONFIRMED order (stockReserved = true)', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
      );

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.REFUNDED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should not restore stock when order was already cancelled', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await service.updateOrderStatus(orderId, OrderStatus.CANCELLED);

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for invalid transition CANCELLED -> DELIVERED', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        service.updateOrderStatus(orderId, OrderStatus.DELIVERED),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        service.updateOrderStatus(orderId, OrderStatus.DELIVERED),
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid status transition from CANCELLED to DELIVERED');
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for invalid transition REFUNDED -> CANCELLED', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        service.updateOrderStatus(orderId, OrderStatus.CANCELLED),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        service.updateOrderStatus(orderId, OrderStatus.CANCELLED),
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid status transition from REFUNDED to CANCELLED');
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for invalid transition PENDING -> PROCESSING', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(
        service.updateOrderStatus(orderId, OrderStatus.PROCESSING),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        service.updateOrderStatus(orderId, OrderStatus.PROCESSING),
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid status transition from PENDING to PROCESSING');
    });

    it('should allow PENDING -> CONFIRMED transition', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
    });

    it('should allow PENDING -> CANCELLED transition', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
    });

    it('should allow CONFIRMED -> PROCESSING transition', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.PROCESSING);
    });

    it('should allow CONFIRMED -> CANCELLED transition with stock restore', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should allow CONFIRMED -> REFUNDED transition with stock restore', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.REFUNDED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should allow PROCESSING -> SHIPPED transition', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.SHIPPED);
    });

    it('should allow PROCESSING -> REFUNDED transition with stock restore', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.REFUNDED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should allow SHIPPED -> DELIVERED transition', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.DELIVERED,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.DELIVERED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.DELIVERED);
    });

    it('should allow SHIPPED -> REFUNDED transition with stock restore', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.REFUNDED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should allow DELIVERED -> REFUNDED transition with stock restore', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.DELIVERED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
      );

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.REFUNDED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should return order idempotently if status unchanged', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      const result = await service.updateOrderStatus(
        orderId,
        OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(order);
      expect(orderRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });

  describe('deleteOrder', () => {
    const orderId = 'order-1';
    const mockOrderItems: OrderItem[] = [
      {
        id: 'item-1',
        productId: 'product-1',
        sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
        quantity: 2,
      } as OrderItem,
    ];

    it('should delete an order and restore stock if stockReserved = true', async () => {
      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 8,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(sellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.remove.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await service.deleteOrder(orderId);

      expect(orderRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: orderId },
        relations: ['items'],
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });
      expect(sellerProductRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: 'seller-product-1' },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          stockQuantity: 10,
        }),
      );
      expect(orderRepository.remove).toHaveBeenCalledWith(order);
    });

    it('should delete an order without restoring stock if stockReserved = false', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.remove.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await service.deleteOrder(orderId);

      expect(sellerProductRepository.findOne).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(orderRepository.remove).toHaveBeenCalledWith(order);
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException if order does not exist', async () => {
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(service.deleteOrder(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        NotFoundException,
      );
      await expect(service.deleteOrder(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        `Order with ID ${orderId} not found`,
      );
    });
  });

  describe('confirmOrderPayment', () => {
    const orderId = 'order-1';
    const mockOrderItems: OrderItem[] = [
      {
        id: 'item-1',
        productId: 'product-1',
        sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
        productName: 'Test Product',
        quantity: 2,
      } as OrderItem,
    ];

    const mockSellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
      id: 'seller-product-1',
      stockQuantity: 100,
    } as SellerProduct;

    it('should confirm payment and decrease stock for PENDING order', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 98,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
      });

      const result = await service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId);

      expect(sellerProductRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: 'seller-product-1' },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          stockQuantity: 98,
        }),
      );
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(true);
    });

    it('should return order without changes if already CONFIRMED and stockReserved', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      const result = await service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId);

      expect(result).toEqual(order);
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(orderRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException if order is not PENDING', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Cannot confirm payment for order in status CANCELLED',
      );
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException if seller product does not exist', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        NotFoundException,
      );
      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        'SellerProduct with ID seller-product-1 not found',
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException if insufficient stock', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: [
          {
            ...mockOrderItems[0],
            quantity: 150,
          },
        ],
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);

      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Insufficient stock',
      );
    });

    it('should use productId as fallback if sellerProductId is not provided', async () => {
      const orderItemsWithoutSellerProductId: OrderItem[] = [
        {
          id: 'item-1',
          productId: 'product-1',
          sellerProductId: undefined,
          productName: 'Test Product',
          quantity: 2,
        } as OrderItem,
      ];

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: orderItemsWithoutSellerProductId,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 98,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
      } as Order);

      await service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId);

      expect(sellerProductRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: 'product-1' },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });
    });

    it('should decrement stock only once per line item when confirmOrderPayment is called', async () => {
      const orderItems: OrderItem[] = [
        {
          id: 'item-1',
          productId: 'product-1',
          sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
          productName: 'Test Product',
          quantity: 3,
        } as OrderItem,
      ];

      const sellerProduct: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: orderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      // findOne is called twice: once for stock check, once for actual decrement
      sellerProductRepository.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct)  // stock check
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct as never);  // actual decrement
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...sellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 7,
      } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
      } as Order);

      const result = await service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId);

      expect(sellerProductRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({
          stockQuantity: 7,
        }),
      );
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for negative quantity', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: [
          {
            ...mockOrderItems[0],
            quantity: -3,
          },
        ],
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order item quantity must be a positive integer',
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for zero quantity', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: [
          {
            ...mockOrderItems[0],
            quantity: 0,
          },
        ],
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order item quantity must be a positive integer',
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for non-integer quantity', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: [
          {
            ...mockOrderItems[0],
            quantity: 2.5,
          },
        ],
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Order item quantity must be a positive integer',
      );
    });

    it('should fail atomically when multi-item stock check fails - no partial deduction', async () => {
      const orderItems: OrderItem[] = [
        {
          id: 'item-1',
          productId: 'product-1',
          sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
          productName: 'Product 1',
          quantity: 2,
        } as OrderItem,
        {
          id: 'item-2',
          productId: 'product-2',
          sellerProductId: 'seller-product-2',
          productName: 'Product 2',
          quantity: 3,
        } as OrderItem,
      ];

      const sellerProduct1: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-1',
        stockQuantity: 5,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const sellerProduct2: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-2',
        stockQuantity: 1,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: orderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      // 4 calls to findOne: 2 for stock check (sorted: seller-product-1, seller-product-2), 
      // 2 for actual decrement (same order)
      sellerProductRepository.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct1)  // check stock for product 1
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct2)  // check stock for product 2 (fails here)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct1)  // would be called for decrement product 1 (not reached)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProduct2); // would be called for decrement product 2 (not reached)

      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Insufficient stock for product seller-product-2',
      );

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should sort sellerProductIds to prevent deadlocks', async () => {
      const orderItems: OrderItem[] = [
        {
          id: 'item-1',
          productId: 'product-1',
          sellerProductId: 'seller-product-b',
          productName: 'Product B',
          quantity: 1,
        } as OrderItem,
        {
          id: 'item-2',
          productId: 'product-2',
          sellerProductId: 'seller-product-a',
          productName: 'Product A',
          quantity: 1,
        } as OrderItem,
      ];

      const sellerProductA: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-a',
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const sellerProductB: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'seller-product-b',
        stockQuantity: 10,
      } as SellerProduct;

      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: orderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      sellerProductRepository.findOne
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProductA)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce(sellerProductB);
      sellerProductRepository.save
        .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ...sellerProductA, stockQuantity: 9 } as SellerProduct)
        .mockResolvedValueOnce({ ...sellerProductB, stockQuantity: 9 } as SellerProduct);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
      } as Order);

      await service.confirmOrderPayment(orderId);

      const findOneCalls = sellerProductRepository.findOne.mock.calls;
      const firstCallWhere = findOneCalls[0][0].where as FindOptionsWhere<SellerProduct>;
      const secondCallWhere = findOneCalls[1][0].where as FindOptionsWhere<SellerProduct>;
      expect(firstCallWhere.id).toBe('seller-product-a');
      expect(secondCallWhere.id).toBe('seller-product-b');
    });
  });

  describe('cancelPendingOrder', () => {
    const orderId = 'order-1';
    const mockOrderItems: OrderItem[] = [
      {
        id: 'item-1',
        productId: 'product-1',
        sellerProductId: 'seller-product-1',
        quantity: 2,
      } as OrderItem,
    ];

    it('should cancel PENDING order without restoring stock (stockReserved = false)', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...order,
        status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
        stockReserved: false,
      });

      const result = await service.cancelPendingOrder(orderId);

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(false);
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException when trying to cancel CONFIRMED order', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
        stockReserved: true,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(order);

      await expect(service.cancelPendingOrder(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException,
      );
      await expect(service.cancelPendingOrder(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Only PENDING orders can be cancelled',
      );
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException if order does not exist', async () => {
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(service.cancelPendingOrder(orderId)).rejects.toThrow(
        NotFoundException,
      );
    });

    it('should pass pessimistic_write lock when running inside a DataSource transaction', async () => {
      const order: Order = {
        id: orderId,
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
        stockReserved: false,
        items: mockOrderItems,
      } as Order;

      // Simulate transaction by providing a manager
      const transactionOrderRepo = {
        findOne: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(order),
        save: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue({
          ...order,
          status: OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
          stockReserved: false,
        }),
      };
      const transactionSellerProductRepo = {
        findOne: jest.fn(),
        save: jest.fn(),
      };
      mockTransactionManager.getRepository.mockImplementation((entity: unknown) => {
        if (entity === Order) return transactionOrderRepo;
        if (entity === SellerProduct) return transactionSellerProductRepo;
        return mockOrderRepository;
      });

      const result = await service.cancelPendingOrder(orderId);

      expect(transactionOrderRepo.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: orderId },
        relations: ['items'],
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });
      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
    });
  });

  describe('getOrdersByCustomerEmail', () => {
    const customerEmail = 'customer@example.com';
    const mockOrders: Order[] = [
      {
        id: 'order-1',
        orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
        customerEmail,
        createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
      } as Order,
    ];

    beforeEach(() => {
      mockQueryBuilder.getMany.mockResolvedValue(mockOrders);
      orderRepository.count.mockResolvedValue(1);
    });

    it('should return orders filtered by customer email', async () => {
      const result = await service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.where).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.customerEmail = :customerEmail',
        { customerEmail },
      );
      expect(result.edges).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(result.totalCount).toBe(1);
    });

    it('should handle pagination with cursor', async () => {
      const cursor = Buffer.from(new Date().toISOString()).toString('base64');
      await service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, cursor);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should apply id DESC tie-breaker ordering for deterministic pagination', async () => {
      await service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.orderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt',
        'DESC',
      );
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.addOrderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.id',
        'DESC',
      );
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for invalid cursor', async () => {
      const invalidCursor = 'not-valid-base64-date';
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, invalidCursor),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, invalidCursor),
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid cursor');
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for empty cursor', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, ''),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, ''),
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid cursor');
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for whitespace-only cursor', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, '   '),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, '   '),
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid cursor');
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for invalid base64 cursor', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, 'not-a-valid-cursor'),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, 'not-a-valid-cursor'),
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid cursor');
    });

    it('should throw BadRequestException for base64-encoded non-date string', async () => {
      const nonDateCursor = Buffer.from('not-a-date').toString('base64');
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, nonDateCursor),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
      await expect(
        service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, nonDateCursor),
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid cursor');
    });

    it('should handle valid cursor and set hasPreviousPage to true', async () => {
      const validDate = new Date('2026-01-15T10:00:00.000Z');
      const validCursor = Buffer.from(validDate.toISOString()).toString('base64');
      await service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, validCursor);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt < :cursor',
        { cursor: validDate },
      );
    });

    it('should return hasPreviousPage: false when after is undefined', async () => {
      const result = await service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, undefined);

      expect(result.pageInfo.hasPreviousPage).toBe(false);
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt < :cursor',
        expect.any(Object),
      );
    });

    it('should return hasPreviousPage: false when after is null', async () => {
      const result = await service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10, null as never);

      expect(result.pageInfo.hasPreviousPage).toBe(false);
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.andWhere).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt < :cursor',
        expect.any(Object),
      );
    });

    it('should apply orderBy createdAt DESC and addOrderBy id DESC', async () => {
      await service.getOrdersByCustomerEmail(customerEmail, 10);

      expect(mockQueryBuilder.orderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'order.createdAt',
        'DESC',
      );
      expect(mockQueryBuilder.addOrderBy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('order.id', 'DESC');
    });
  });

  describe('getOrdersBySeller', () => {
    it('should call findAll with sellerId', async () => {
      const findAllSpy = jest.spyOn(service, 'findAll').mockResolvedValue({
        edges: [],
        pageInfo: {
          hasNextPage: false,
          hasPreviousPage: false,
          endCursor: undefined,
        },
        totalCount: 0,
      });

      await service.getOrdersBySeller('1', 10);

      expect(findAllSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(10, undefined, '1', undefined);

      findAllSpy.mockRestore();
    });

    it('should call findAll with sellerId and status filter', async () => {
      const findAllSpy = jest.spyOn(service, 'findAll').mockResolvedValue({
        edges: [],
        pageInfo: {
          hasNextPage: false,
          hasPreviousPage: false,
          endCursor: undefined,
        },
        totalCount: 0,
      });

      await service.getOrdersBySeller('1', 10, undefined, OrderStatus.DELIVERED);

      expect(findAllSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(10, undefined, '1', OrderStatus.DELIVERED);

      findAllSpy.mockRestore();
    });

    it('should call findAll with sellerId and undefined status (no filter)', async () => {
      const findAllSpy = jest.spyOn(service, 'findAll').mockResolvedValue({
        edges: [],
        pageInfo: {
          hasNextPage: false,
          hasPreviousPage: false,
          endCursor: undefined,
        },
        totalCount: 0,
      });

      await service.getOrdersBySeller('1', 10, undefined, undefined);

      expect(findAllSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(10, undefined, '1', undefined);

      findAllSpy.mockRestore();
    });
  });

  describe('getOrdersByStatus', () => {
    it('should call findAll with status', async () => {
      const findAllSpy = jest.spyOn(service, 'findAll').mockResolvedValue({
        edges: [],
        pageInfo: {
          hasNextPage: false,
          hasPreviousPage: false,
          endCursor: undefined,
        },
        totalCount: 0,
      });

      await service.getOrdersByStatus(OrderStatus.PENDING, 10);

      expect(findAllSpy).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        10,
        undefined,
        undefined,
        OrderStatus.PENDING,
      );

      findAllSpy.mockRestore();
    });
  });

  describe('findOrdersByPaymentAuthority', () => {
    const authority = 'test-authority-123';

    it('should return all orders matching a paymentAuthority', async () => {
      const mockOrders: Order[] = [
        {
          id: 'order-1',
          orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
          paymentAuthority: authority,
          items: [],
          seller: {} as SellerInfo,
          customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
          customerPhone: '1234567890',
          customerName: 'Test User',
          shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
          city: 'Test City',
          state: 'Test State',
          zipCode: '12345',
          country: 'Test Country',
          sellerId: 1,
          status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
          stockReserved: false,
          subtotal: 100,
          shippingCost: 10,
          total: 110,
          createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        } as Order,
        {
          id: 'order-2',
          orderNumber: 'ORD-2',
          paymentAuthority: authority,
          items: [],
          seller: {} as SellerInfo,
          customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
          customerPhone: '1234567890',
          customerName: 'Test User',
          shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
          city: 'Test City',
          state: 'Test State',
          zipCode: '12345',
          country: 'Test Country',
          sellerId: 2,
          status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
          stockReserved: false,
          subtotal: 200,
          shippingCost: 15,
          total: 215,
          createdAt: new Date('2024-01-02'),
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        } as Order,
      ];

      orderRepository.find.mockResolvedValue(mockOrders);

      const result = await service.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority(authority);

      expect(orderRepository.find).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { paymentAuthority: authority },
        relations: ['items', 'seller'],
        order: { id: 'ASC' },
      });
      expect(result).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(result[0].id).toBe('order-1');
      expect(result[1].id).toBe('order-2');
    });

    it('should return empty array for empty authority string', async () => {
      const result = await service.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority('');

      expect(result).toEqual([]);
      expect(orderRepository.find).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should return empty array when no orders match authority', async () => {
      orderRepository.find.mockResolvedValue([]);

      const result = await service.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority('non-existent');

      expect(result).toEqual([]);
      expect(orderRepository.find).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { paymentAuthority: 'non-existent' },
        relations: ['items', 'seller'],
        order: { id: 'ASC' },
      });
    });
