    // Locked Test: relations passed to orderRepository.find are EXACTLY ['items', 'seller']
    it('Locked: relations passed to find are EXACTLY [\'items\', \'seller\'] without unknown relations', async () => {
      const mockOrders: Order[] = [
        {
          id: 'order-1',
          orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
          paymentAuthority: authority,
          items: [
            {
              id: 'item-1',
              orderId: 'order-1',
              productId: 'product-1',
              sellerProductId: 'sp-1',
              productName: 'Test Product',
              quantity: 1,
              unitPrice: 100,
              totalPrice: 100,
            } as OrderItem,
          ],
          seller: {} as SellerInfo,
          customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
          customerPhone: '1234567890',
          customerName: 'Test User',
          shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
          city: 'Test City',
          state: 'Test State',
          zipCode: '12345',
          country: 'Test Country',
          sellerId: 1,
          status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
          stockReserved: false,
          subtotal: 100,
          shippingCost: 10,
          total: 110,
          createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        } as Order,
      ];

      orderRepository.find.mockResolvedValue(mockOrders);
      sellerProductRepository.findBy.mockResolvedValue([]);

      await service.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority(authority);

      const findCall = orderRepository.find.mock.calls[0][0] as {
        relations?: string[];
      };
      const relations = findCall.relations ?? [];
      // LOCKED: Order has only items/seller relations; unknown relations make TypeORM throw
      expect(relations).toEqual(['items', 'seller']);
      expect(relations).not.toContain('customer');
      expect(relations).not.toContain('items.sellerProduct');
    });

    it('hydrates sellerProduct on items when sellerProductId exists', async () => {
      const mockSellerProduct1: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'sp-1',
        price: 100,
        discount: 0,
        stockQuantity: 10,
        productInfoId: 1,
        seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'],
      } as SellerProduct;
      const mockSellerProduct2: SellerProduct = {
        id: 'sp-2',
        price: 200,
        discount: 10,
        stockQuantity: 5,
        productInfoId: 2,
        seller: { id: 1 } as unknown as SellerProduct['seller'],
      } as SellerProduct;

      const mockOrders: Order[] = [
        {
          id: 'order-1',
          orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
          paymentAuthority: authority,
          items: [
            {
              id: 'item-1',
              orderId: 'order-1',
              productId: 'product-1',
              sellerProductId: 'sp-1',
              productName: 'Product 1',
              quantity: 1,
              unitPrice: 100,
              totalPrice: 100,
            } as OrderItem,
            {
              id: 'item-2',
              orderId: 'order-1',
              productId: 'product-2',
              sellerProductId: 'sp-2',
              productName: 'Product 2',
              quantity: 2,
              unitPrice: 200,
              totalPrice: 400,
            } as OrderItem,
            {
              id: 'item-3',
              orderId: 'order-1',
              productId: 'product-3',
              sellerProductId: 'sp-unknown',
              productName: 'Product 3',
              quantity: 1,
              unitPrice: 50,
              totalPrice: 50,
            } as OrderItem,
          ],
          seller: {} as SellerInfo,
          customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
          customerPhone: '1234567890',
          customerName: 'Test User',
          shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
          city: 'Test City',
          state: 'Test State',
          zipCode: '12345',
          country: 'Test Country',
          sellerId: 1,
          status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
          stockReserved: false,
          subtotal: 550,
          shippingCost: 10,
          total: 560,
          createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        } as Order,
      ];

      orderRepository.find.mockResolvedValue(mockOrders);
      sellerProductRepository.findBy.mockResolvedValue([
        mockSellerProduct1,
        mockSellerProduct2,
      ]);

      const result = await service.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority(authority);

      // findBy called with unique ids
      expect(sellerProductRepository.findBy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      const findByArg = sellerProductRepository.findBy.mock.calls[0][0] as FindOptionsWhere<SellerProduct>;
      // TypeORM In operator creates a FindOperator, check its value
      const inOperator = findByArg.id as unknown as { _value?: string[] };
      expect(inOperator._value?.sort()).toEqual(['sp-1', 'sp-2', 'sp-unknown'].sort());

      // sellerProduct attached to items
      const order = result[0];
      expect((order.items[0] as OrderItem & { sellerProduct?: SellerProduct | null }).sellerProduct).toEqual(mockSellerProduct1);
      expect((order.items[1] as OrderItem & { sellerProduct?: SellerProduct | null }).sellerProduct).toEqual(mockSellerProduct2);
      // unknown id gets null
      expect((order.items[2] as OrderItem & { sellerProduct?: SellerProduct | null }).sellerProduct).toBeNull();
    });

    it('does NOT call findBy when no item has a sellerProductId', async () => {
      const mockOrders: Order[] = [
        {
          id: 'order-1',
          orderNumber: 'ORD-1',
          paymentAuthority: authority,
          items: [
            {
              id: 'item-1',
              orderId: 'order-1',
              productId: 'product-1',
              sellerProductId: '',
              productName: 'Product 1',
              quantity: 1,
              unitPrice: 100,
              totalPrice: 100,
            } as OrderItem,
            {
              id: 'item-2',
              orderId: 'order-1',
              productId: 'product-2',
              sellerProductId: undefined,
              productName: 'Product 2',
              quantity: 1,
              unitPrice: 200,
              totalPrice: 200,
            } as OrderItem,
          ],
          seller: {} as SellerInfo,
          customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
          customerPhone: '1234567890',
          customerName: 'Test User',
          shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
          city: 'Test City',
          state: 'Test State',
          zipCode: '12345',
          country: 'Test Country',
          sellerId: 1,
          status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
          stockReserved: false,
          subtotal: 300,
          shippingCost: 10,
          total: 310,
          createdAt: new Date('2024-01-01'),
          updatedAt: new Date(),
        } as Order,
      ];

      orderRepository.find.mockResolvedValue(mockOrders);

      const result = await service.findOrdersByPaymentAuthority(authority);

      expect(sellerProductRepository.findBy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(result).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(result[0].items).toHaveLength(2);
      // sellerProduct should be undefined/null since findBy wasn't called
      expect((result[0].items[0] as OrderItem & { sellerProduct?: SellerProduct | null }).sellerProduct).toBeUndefined();
      expect((result[0].items[1] as OrderItem & { sellerProduct?: SellerProduct | null }).sellerProduct).toBeUndefined();
    });
  });
});

describe('OrderService - Shipping Security & Payment Modes', () => {
  const baseOrderInput = {
    customerName: 'Ali Rezaei',
    customerEmail: 'ali@example.com',
    customerPhone: '+989121234567',
    shippingAddress: 'Valiasr St 123',
    city: 'Tehran',
    state: 'Tehran',
    zipCode: '1968633111',
    country: 'Iran',
    sellerId: 1,
    items: [
      {
        productId: '10',
        sellerProductId: '11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',
        productName: 'Nike Tech Fleece',
        quantity: 2,
        unitPrice: 50,
        totalPrice: 100,
      },
    ],
  };

  function createHarness(options?: {
    sellers?: Record<number, { id: number; storeName: string; shippingFee: number; shippingPaymentMode?: ShippingPaymentMode }>;
    platformSettings?: Partial<{
      freeShippingEnabled: boolean;
      freeShippingThreshold: number;
      shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy;
      additionalSellerDiscount: number;
      maxShippingCapEnabled: boolean;
      maxShippingCap: number;
    }>;
  }) {
    const sellersMap = options?.sellers ?? {
      1: { id: 1, storeName: 'Seller One', shippingFee: 10 },
    };

    const orderRepository = {
      create: jest.fn((dto) => ({ ...dto })),
      save: jest.fn(async (entity) => ({ id: entity.id || 'order-1', ...entity })),
      findOne: jest.fn(),
    };
    const orderItemRepository = {
      save: jest.fn(async (items) => items),
    };
    const sellerProductRepository = {
      findOne: jest.fn(async ({ where: { id } }) => ({
        id,
        price: 50,
        discount: 0,
        stockQuantity: 10,
        productInfoId: 100,
        seller: sellersMap[1],
      })),
    };
    const sellerInfoRepository = {
      findOne: jest.fn(async ({ where: { id } }) => sellersMap[id] ?? null),
    };
    const platformSettingsRepository = {
      findOne: jest.fn(async () => ({
        id: 1,
        freeShippingEnabled: false,
        freeShippingThreshold: 500,
        shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT,
        additionalSellerDiscount: 0.5,
        maxShippingCapEnabled: false,
        maxShippingCap: 50,
        ...options?.platformSettings,
      })),
    };

    const shippingCalcService = new ShippingCalculationService(
      platformSettingsRepository as unknown as jest.Mocked<Repository<PlatformSettings>>
    );

    const service = new OrderService(
      orderRepository as unknown as jest.Mocked<Repository<Order>>,
      orderItemRepository as unknown as jest.Mocked<Repository<OrderItem>>,
      sellerProductRepository as unknown as jest.Mocked<Repository<SellerProduct>>,
      sellerInfoRepository as unknown as jest.Mocked<Repository<SellerInfo>>,
      shippingCalcService,
      undefined
    );

    return { service, orderRepository, sellerProductRepository };
  }

  it('1. defaults seller shippingPaymentMode to PAY_ON_DELIVERY and forces shippingCost to 0', async () => {
    const { service } = createHarness({
      sellers: {
        1: { id: 1, storeName: 'Default Mode Seller', shippingFee: 15 },
      },
    });

    const order = await service.createOrder({
      ...baseOrderInput,
      shippingMethod: 'vip',
    });

    expect(order.shippingPaymentMode).toBe(ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY);
    expect(order.shippingCost).toBe(0);
    expect(order.subtotal).toBe(100);
    expect(order.total).toBe(100);
  });

  it('2. ignores any client shippingCost (0, positive, or huge) for a PAY_ON_DELIVERY seller and yields 0', async () => {
    const { service } = createHarness({
      sellers: {
        1: {
          id: 1,
          storeName: 'COD Seller',
          shippingFee: 20,
          shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
        },
      },
    });

    for (const clientFee of [0, 12.5, 999.99]) {
      const order = await service.createOrder({
        ...baseOrderInput,
        shippingCost: clientFee,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
      });
      expect(order.shippingCost).toBe(0);
      expect(order.total).toBe(100);
    }
  });

  it('3. computes server fee for PAY_ONLINE sellers per method (pickup = 0, express = 45%, vip = 100%) ignoring client 0', async () => {
    const { service } = createHarness({
      sellers: {
        1: {
          id: 1,
          storeName: 'Online Seller',
          shippingFee: 10,
          shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
        },
      },
    });

    const pickupOrder = await service.createOrder({
      ...baseOrderInput,
      shippingCost: 0,
      shippingMethod: 'pickup',
    });
    expect(pickupOrder.shippingCost).toBe(0);
    expect(pickupOrder.total).toBe(100);

    const expressOrder = await service.createOrder({
      ...baseOrderInput,
      shippingCost: 0,
      shippingMethod: 'express',
    });
    expect(expressOrder.shippingCost).toBe(4.5);
    expect(expressOrder.total).toBe(104.5);

    const vipOrder = await service.createOrder({
      ...baseOrderInput,
      shippingCost: 0,
      shippingMethod: 'vip',
    });
    expect(vipOrder.shippingCost).toBe(10);
    expect(vipOrder.total).toBe(110);
  });

  it('4. overrides a higher client shippingCost on a PAY_ONLINE seller with the exact server-computed fee', async () => {
    const { service } = createHarness({
      sellers: {
        1: {
          id: 1,
          storeName: 'Online Seller',
          shippingFee: 10,
          shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
        },
      },
    });

    const order = await service.createOrder({
      ...baseOrderInput,
      shippingCost: 500,
      shippingMethod: 'express',
    });

    expect(order.shippingCost).toBe(4.5);
    expect(order.total).toBe(104.5);
  });

  it('5. recalculates mixed cart batch properly: PAY_ON_DELIVERY gets 0 and does not consume first-seller tier for PAY_ONLINE sellers', async () => {
    const { service } = createHarness({
      sellers: {
        1: {
          id: 1,
          storeName: 'COD Seller 1',
          shippingFee: 25,
          shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
        },
        2: {
          id: 2,
          storeName: 'Online Seller 2',
          shippingFee: 10,
          shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
        },
        3: {
          id: 3,
          storeName: 'Online Seller 3',
          shippingFee: 8,
          shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
        },
      },
      platformSettings: {
        shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT,
        additionalSellerDiscount: 0.5, // 50% off for 2nd online seller
      },
    });

    const batchOrders: Partial<Order>[] = [
      {
        id: 'ord-cod-1',
        sellerId: 1,
        subtotal: 100,
        shippingCost: 25, // poisoned initial value
        total: 125,
        shippingMethod: 'vip',
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      },
      {
        id: 'ord-on-3',
        sellerId: 3,
        subtotal: 50,
        shippingCost: 0,
        total: 50,
        shippingMethod: 'express', // 45% of (8 * 0.5 = 4) => 1.80
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      },
      {
        id: 'ord-on-2',
        sellerId: 2,
        subtotal: 80,
        shippingCost: 0,
        total: 80,
        shippingMethod: 'vip', // 1st online seller (id 2 < id 3): full 10
        status: OrderStatus.PENDING,
      },
    ];

    const updated = await service.recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(batchOrders as unknown as Order[]);
    const byId = Object.fromEntries(updated.map((o) => [o.id, o]));

    expect(byId['ord-cod-1'].shippingCost).toBe(0);
    expect(byId['ord-cod-1'].total).toBe(100);

    expect(byId['ord-on-2'].shippingCost).toBe(10);
    expect(byId['ord-on-2'].total).toBe(90);

    expect(byId['ord-on-3'].shippingCost).toBe(1.8);
    expect(byId['ord-on-3'].total).toBe(51.8);
  });
});
===== FILE: .\libs\server\services\src\lib\order.service.tracking.spec.ts =====
import { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { getRepositoryToken } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';
import { NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { OrderService } from './order.service';
import {
  Order,
  OrderItem,
  OrderStatus,
  SellerProduct,
  SellerInfo,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import { CreateOrderInput, UpdateOrderInput } from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';

describe('OrderService - updateTrackingNumber', () => {
  let service: OrderService;
  let orderRepository: jest.Mocked<Repository<Order>>;
  let orderItemRepository: jest.Mocked<Repository<OrderItem>>;
  let sellerProductRepository: jest.Mocked<Repository<SellerProduct>>;
  let sellerInfoRepository: jest.Mocked<Repository<SellerInfo>>;
  let dataSource: { transaction: jest.Mock };
  let mockTransactionManager: {
    getRepository: jest.Mock;
    findOne: jest.Mock;
    save: jest.Mock;
    remove: jest.Mock;
  };

  const mockOrderRepository = {
    create: jest.fn(),
    save: jest.fn(),
    findOne: jest.fn(),
    remove: jest.fn(),
    createQueryBuilder: jest.fn(),
    count: jest.fn(),
    find: jest.fn(),
  };

  const mockOrderItemRepository = {
    save: jest.fn(),
    delete: jest.fn(),
  };

  const mockSellerProductRepository = {
    findOne: jest.fn(),
    save: jest.fn(),
  };

  const mockSellerInfoRepository = {
    findOne: jest.fn(),
  };

  const mockQueryBuilder = {
    leftJoinAndSelect: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),
    where: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),
    andWhere: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),
    orderBy: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),
    addOrderBy: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),
    take: jest.fn().mockReturnThis(),
    getMany: jest.fn(),
    getCount: jest.fn(),
  };

  beforeEach(async () => {
    mockTransactionManager = {
      getRepository: jest.fn(),
      findOne: jest.fn(),
      save: jest.fn(),
      remove: jest.fn(),
    };

    dataSource = {
      transaction: jest.fn((fn) => fn(mockTransactionManager)),
    };

    mockTransactionManager.getRepository.mockImplementation((entity: unknown) => {
      if (entity === Order) return orderRepository;
      if (entity === SellerProduct) return sellerProductRepository;
      return mockOrderRepository;
    });

    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({
      providers: [
        OrderService,
        {
          provide: getRepositoryToken(Order),
          useValue: mockOrderRepository,
        },
        {
          provide: getRepositoryToken(OrderItem),
          useValue: mockOrderItemRepository,
        },
        {
          provide: getRepositoryToken(SellerProduct),
          useValue: mockSellerProductRepository,
        },
        {
          provide: getRepositoryToken(SellerInfo),
          useValue: mockSellerInfoRepository,
        },
        {
          provide: dataSource.constructor,
          useValue: dataSource,
        },
      ],
    }).compile();

    service = module.get<OrderService>(OrderService);
    orderRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(Order));
    orderItemRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(OrderItem));
    sellerProductRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(SellerProduct));
    sellerInfoRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(SellerInfo));

    orderRepository.createQueryBuilder = jest
      .fn()
      .mockReturnValue(mockQueryBuilder);

    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  describe('updateTrackingNumber', () => {
    const orderId = 'order-1';
    const existingOrder: Order = {
      id: orderId,
      orderNumber: 'ORD-123',
      subtotal: 100,
      shippingCost: 10,
      total: 110,
      stockReserved: false,
      items: [],
      status: OrderStatus.CONFIRMED,
      seller: {} as SellerInfo,
      customerEmail: 'test@example.com',
      customerPhone: '1234567890',
      customerName: 'Test User',
      shippingAddress: '123 Test St',
      city: 'Test City',
      state: 'Test State',
      zipCode: '12345',
      country: 'Test Country',
      sellerId: 1,
      createdAt: new Date(),
      updatedAt: new Date(),
      trackingNumber: '',
    } as Order;

    it('should update tracking number and trim whitespace', async () => {
      const updatedOrder = { ...existingOrder, trackingNumber: 'TRK123' };
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await service.updateTrackingNumber(orderId, '  TRK123  ');

      expect(orderRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        where: { id: orderId },
        relations: ['items', 'seller'],
      });
      expect(orderRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({ trackingNumber: 'TRK123' })
      );
      expect(result.trackingNumber).toBe('TRK123');
    });

    it('should only change trackingNumber field (status unchanged)', async () => {
      const updatedOrder = { ...existingOrder, trackingNumber: 'TRK123' };
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await service.updateTrackingNumber(orderId, 'TRK123');

      expect(result.status).toBe(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
      expect(result.subtotal).toBe(100);
      expect(result.shippingCost).toBe(10);
      expect(result.total).toBe(110);
      expect(result.trackingNumber).toBe('TRK123');
    });

    it('should only change trackingNumber field (items unchanged)', async () => {
      const items: OrderItem[] = [
        {
          id: 'item-1',
          orderId: orderId,
          productId: 'prod-1',
          sellerProductId: 'sp-1',
          productName: 'Product 1',
          quantity: 2,
          unitPrice: 50,
          totalPrice: 100,
        } as OrderItem,
      ];
      const orderWithItems = { ...existingOrder, items };
      const updatedOrder = { ...orderWithItems, trackingNumber: 'TRK123', items };
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(orderWithItems);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await service.updateTrackingNumber(orderId, 'TRK123');

      expect(result.items).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(result.items[0].productId).toBe('prod-1');
      expect(result.items[0].quantity).toBe(2);
    });

    it('should trim tracking number before saving', async () => {
      const updatedOrder = { ...existingOrder, trackingNumber: 'TRK123' };
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      await service.updateTrackingNumber(orderId, '  TRK123  ');

      const savedOrder = orderRepository.save.mock.calls[0][0] as Order;
      expect(savedOrder.trackingNumber).toBe('TRK123');
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException if order does not exist', async () => {
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(service.updateTrackingNumber('non-existent', 'TRK123')).rejects.toThrow(
        NotFoundException,
      );
      await expect(service.updateTrackingNumber('non-existent', 'TRK123')).rejects.toThrow(
        `Order with ID non-existent not found`,
      );
      expect(orderRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should accept tracking number with whitespace (validation done in resolver)', async () => {
      // Service does not validate length/whitespace - that's done in resolver
      // Service just trims and saves
      const updatedOrder = { ...existingOrder, trackingNumber: 'TRK123' };
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await service.updateTrackingNumber(orderId, '  TRK123  ');

      expect(result.trackingNumber).toBe('TRK123');
    });

    it('should preserve all other order fields unchanged', async () => {
      const updatedOrder = { ...existingOrder, trackingNumber: 'NEW-TRK' };
      orderRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(existingOrder);
      orderRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(updatedOrder);

      const result = await service.updateTrackingNumber(orderId, 'NEW-TRK');

      // Verify all original fields are preserved
      expect(result.id).toBe(existingOrder.id);
      expect(result.orderNumber).toBe(existingOrder.orderNumber);
      expect(result.customerEmail).toBe(existingOrder.customerEmail);
      expect(result.customerPhone).toBe(existingOrder.customerPhone);
      expect(result.customerName).toBe(existingOrder.customerName);
      expect(result.shippingAddress).toBe(existingOrder.shippingAddress);
      expect(result.city).toBe(existingOrder.city);
      expect(result.state).toBe(existingOrder.state);
      expect(result.zipCode).toBe(existingOrder.zipCode);
      expect(result.country).toBe(existingOrder.country);
      expect(result.sellerId).toBe(existingOrder.sellerId);
      expect(result.subtotal).toBe(existingOrder.subtotal);
      expect(result.shippingCost).toBe(existingOrder.shippingCost);
      expect(result.total).toBe(existingOrder.total);
      expect(result.status).toBe(existingOrder.status);
      expect(result.stockReserved).toBe(existingOrder.stockReserved);
      expect(result.paymentAmount).toBe(existingOrder.paymentAmount);
      expect(result.paymentCurrency).toBe(existingOrder.paymentCurrency);
      expect(result.paymentAuthority).toBe(existingOrder.paymentAuthority);
      expect(result.sellerStoreName).toBe(existingOrder.sellerStoreName);
      expect(result.sellerPhone).toBe(existingOrder.sellerPhone);
      expect(result.sellerAddress).toBe(existingOrder.sellerAddress);
      expect(result.notes).toBe(existingOrder.notes);
      expect(result.createdAt).toEqual(existingOrder.createdAt);
      // updatedAt may be updated by the database
    });
  });
});
===== FILE: .\libs\server\services\src\lib\order.service.ts =====
import { Injectable, NotFoundException, BadRequestException, Optional, Inject } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository, DataSource, EntityManager, In } from 'typeorm';
import {
  Order,
  OrderItem,
  OrderStatus,
  SellerProduct,
  SellerInfo,
  ShippingPaymentMode,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import {
  CreateOrderInput,
  UpdateOrderInput,
  OrdersConnection,
  OrderEdge,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';
import {
  ShippingCalculationService,
  applyShippingMethodMultiplier,
} from './shipping-calculation.service';

@Injectable()
export class OrderService {
  private static readonly ALLOWED_STATUS_TRANSITIONS: Record<
    OrderStatus,
    OrderStatus[]
  > = {
    [OrderStatus.PENDING]: [OrderStatus.CONFIRMED, OrderStatus.CANCELLED],
    [OrderStatus.CONFIRMED]: [
      OrderStatus.PROCESSING,
      OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
      OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
      OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
    ],
    [OrderStatus.PROCESSING]: [
      OrderStatus.SHIPPED,
      OrderStatus.CANCELLED,
      OrderStatus.REFUNDED,
    ],
    [OrderStatus.SHIPPED]: [OrderStatus.DELIVERED, OrderStatus.REFUNDED],
    [OrderStatus.DELIVERED]: [OrderStatus.REFUNDED],
    [OrderStatus.CANCELLED]: [],
    [OrderStatus.REFUNDED]: [],
  };

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Order)
    private orderRepository: Repository<Order>,
    @InjectRepository(OrderItem)
    private orderItemRepository: Repository<OrderItem>,
    @InjectRepository(SellerProduct)
    private sellerProductRepository: Repository<SellerProduct>,
    @InjectRepository(SellerInfo)
    private sellerInfoRepository: Repository<SellerInfo>,
    @Optional()
    private readonly shippingCalculationService?: ShippingCalculationService,
    @Optional()
    @Inject(DataSource)
    private readonly dataSource?: DataSource,
  ) {}

  private async runInTransaction<T>(
    fn: (manager?: EntityManager) => Promise<T>,
  ): Promise<T> {
    if (this.dataSource?.transaction) {
      return this.dataSource.transaction(fn);
    }
    return fn(undefined);
  }

  async createOrder(createOrderInput: CreateOrderInput): Promise<Order> {
    const { items: inputItems, ...orderData } = createOrderInput;

    if (!Array.isArray(inputItems) || inputItems.length === 0) {
      throw new BadRequestException('Order must contain at least one item');
    }

    const order = this.orderRepository.create(orderData);

    order.orderNumber = this.generateOrderNumber();
    order.trackingNumber = this.generateTrackingNumber();

    const seller = await this.sellerInfoRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: createOrderInput.sellerId },
    });

    if (!seller) {
      throw new NotFoundException(
        `Seller with ID ${createOrderInput.sellerId} not found`,
      );
    }

    order.sellerStoreName = seller.storeName;
    order.sellerPhone = seller.phone || undefined;
    order.sellerAddress = seller.address || undefined;

    // Validate and compute prices from catalog
    const validatedItems = await Promise.all(
      inputItems.map(async (item) => {
        // Validate quantity
        if (!Number.isInteger(Number(item.quantity)) || Number(item.quantity) <= 0) {
          throw new BadRequestException(
            `Quantity must be a positive integer for product ${item.productId}`,
          );
        }

        if (!item.sellerProductId) {
          throw new BadRequestException(
            `sellerProductId is required for product ${item.productId}`,
          );
        }

        const sellerProduct = await this.sellerProductRepository.findOne({
          where: { id: item.sellerProductId },
          relations: ['seller', 'productInfo'],
        });

        if (!sellerProduct) {
          throw new NotFoundException(
            `SellerProduct with ID ${item.sellerProductId} not found`,
          );
        }

        // Ownership check: sellerProduct must belong to the order's seller
        if (Number(sellerProduct.seller.id) !== createOrderInput.sellerId) {
          throw new BadRequestException(
            `Product ${item.sellerProductId} does not belong to seller ${createOrderInput.sellerId}`,
          );
        }

        // Stock check
        if (sellerProduct.stockQuantity < item.quantity) {
          throw new BadRequestException(
            `Insufficient stock for product ${item.sellerProductId}. Available: ${sellerProduct.stockQuantity}, Requested: ${item.quantity}`,
          );
        }

        // Compute catalog price: price * (1 - discount/100)
        const catalogUnitPrice = Number(
          (sellerProduct.price * (1 - (sellerProduct.discount || 0) / 100)).toFixed(2),
        );
        const catalogTotalPrice = Number((catalogUnitPrice * item.quantity).toFixed(2));

        return {
          ...item,
          sellerProduct,
          catalogUnitPrice,
          catalogTotalPrice,
          originalPrice: sellerProduct.price,
          discountPercentage: sellerProduct.discount,
          productInfoId: sellerProduct.productInfoId || undefined,
        };
      }),
    );

    // Aggregate stock check: sum quantities per sellerProductId across all line items
    const requestedBySellerProduct = new Map<
      string,
      { totalQty: number; available: number }
    >();
    for (const item of validatedItems) {
      const sellerProductId = item.sellerProductId!; // validated as required earlier
      const current = requestedBySellerProduct.get(sellerProductId) ?? {
        totalQty: 0,
        available: item.sellerProduct.stockQuantity,
      };
      current.totalQty += Number(item.quantity);
      if (current.totalQty > current.available) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          `Insufficient stock for product ${sellerProductId}. Available: ${current.available}, Requested: ${current.totalQty}`,
        );
      }
      requestedBySellerProduct.set(sellerProductId, current);
    }

    // Compute server-side totals from catalog prices
    order.subtotal = Number(
      validatedItems.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.catalogTotalPrice, 0).toFixed(2),
    );

    // Validate and normalize shipping method
    const shippingMethod = this.normalizeShippingMethod(
      createOrderInput.shippingMethod,
    );

    // Read seller's shipping payment mode
    const paymentMode =
      seller.shippingPaymentMode ?? ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY;

    order.shippingMethod = shippingMethod;
    order.shippingPaymentMode = paymentMode;

    let shippingCost = 0;
    if (paymentMode === ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE) {
      let baseFee = Number(seller.shippingFee || 0);
      if (this.shippingCalculationService) {
        const calc = await this.shippingCalculationService.calculateShippingCost(
          order.subtotal,
          [
            {
              sellerId: seller.id,
              shippingFee: baseFee,
              sellerName: seller.storeName,
              shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
            },
          ],
        );
        baseFee = calc.totalShippingCost;
      }
      shippingCost = applyShippingMethodMultiplier(baseFee, shippingMethod);
    }

    order.shippingCost = shippingCost;
    order.total = Number((order.subtotal + order.shippingCost).toFixed(2));

    const savedOrder = await this.orderRepository.save(order);

    const orderItemsToCreate = validatedItems.map((item) => ({
      orderId: savedOrder.id,
      productId: item.productId,
      sellerProductId: item.sellerProductId,
      productName: item.productName,
      productImage: item.productImage,
      quantity: item.quantity,
      unitPrice: item.catalogUnitPrice,
      totalPrice: item.catalogTotalPrice,
      color: item.color,
      size: item.size,
      originalPrice: item.originalPrice,
      discountPercentage: item.discountPercentage,
      productInfoId: item.productInfoId,
    }));

    const savedItems = await this.orderItemRepository.save(orderItemsToCreate);

    savedOrder.items = savedItems;

    return savedOrder;
  }

  private normalizeShippingMethod(
    method?: string | null,
  ): 'vip' | 'express' | 'pickup' {
    if (method === undefined || method === null || method === '') {
      return 'vip';
    }
    const normalized = String(method).trim().toLowerCase();
    if (
      normalized === 'vip' ||
      normalized === 'express' ||
      normalized === 'pickup'
    ) {
      return normalized;
    }
    throw new BadRequestException(`Invalid shipping method: ${method}`);
  }

  async recalculateBatchOrdersShipping(orders: Order[]): Promise<Order[]> {
    if (!Array.isArray(orders) || orders.length === 0) {
      return [];
    }

    // Load SellerInfo for each order if not already attached
    const sellerMap = new Map<number, SellerInfo>();
    for (const order of orders) {
      if (order.seller) {
        sellerMap.set(Number(order.sellerId), order.seller);
      } else if (!sellerMap.has(Number(order.sellerId))) {
        const found = await this.sellerInfoRepository.findOne({
          where: { id: Number(order.sellerId) },
        });
        if (found) {
          sellerMap.set(Number(order.sellerId), found);
        }
      }
    }

    const onlineOrders: Order[] = [];
    const codOrders: Order[] = [];

    for (const order of orders) {
      const seller = sellerMap.get(Number(order.sellerId));
      const mode =
        seller?.shippingPaymentMode ??
        order.shippingPaymentMode ??
        ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY;

      if (mode === ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE) {
        onlineOrders.push(order);
      } else {
        codOrders.push(order);
      }
    }

    // 1. Force all PAY_ON_DELIVERY orders to 0 shipping
    for (const order of codOrders) {
      const subtotal = Number(Number(order.subtotal).toFixed(2));
      const changed =
        Number(order.shippingCost) !== 0 ||
        Number(order.total) !== subtotal ||
        order.shippingPaymentMode !== ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY;

      order.shippingPaymentMode = ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY;
      order.shippingCost = 0;
      order.total = subtotal;

      if (changed) {
        await this.orderRepository.save(order);
      }
    }

    // 2. Recompute online shipping across all PAY_ONLINE orders in deterministic sellerId ASC order
    if (onlineOrders.length > 0) {
      const sortedOnline = [...onlineOrders].sort((a, b) => {
        const diff = Number(a.sellerId) - Number(b.sellerId);
        return diff !== 0 ? diff : String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id));
      });

      const onlineSubtotal = sortedOnline.reduce(
        (sum, o) => sum + Number(o.subtotal),
        0,
      );

      const sellerFeesInput = sortedOnline.map((o) => {
        const seller = sellerMap.get(Number(o.sellerId));
        return {
          sellerId: Number(o.sellerId),
          shippingFee: Number(seller?.shippingFee ?? 0),
          sellerName: seller?.storeName ?? o.sellerStoreName,
          shippingPaymentMode: ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE,
        };
      });

      const breakdownBySellerId = new Map<string, number>();
      if (this.shippingCalculationService) {
        const calc =
          await this.shippingCalculationService.calculateShippingCost(
            onlineSubtotal,
            sellerFeesInput,
          );
        for (const item of calc.shippingBreakdown) {
          breakdownBySellerId.set(String(item.sellerId), item.appliedFee);
        }
      } else {
        for (const item of sellerFeesInput) {
          breakdownBySellerId.set(String(item.sellerId), item.shippingFee);
        }
      }

      for (const order of sortedOnline) {
        const appliedBaseFee =
          breakdownBySellerId.get(String(order.sellerId)) ?? 0;
        const method = this.normalizeShippingMethod(order.shippingMethod);
        const computedShipping = applyShippingMethodMultiplier(
          appliedBaseFee,
          method,
        );
        const computedTotal = Number(
          (Number(order.subtotal) + computedShipping).toFixed(2),
        );

        const changed =
          Number(order.shippingCost) !== computedShipping ||
          Number(order.total) !== computedTotal ||
          order.shippingPaymentMode !== ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE ||
          order.shippingMethod !== method;

        order.shippingMethod = method;
        order.shippingPaymentMode = ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ONLINE;
        order.shippingCost = computedShipping;
        order.total = computedTotal;

        if (changed) {
          await this.orderRepository.save(order);
        }
      }
    }

    return orders;
  }

  async findAll(
    first = 10,
    after?: string,
    sellerId?: string,
    status?: OrderStatus,
  ): Promise<OrdersConnection> {
    const queryBuilder = this.orderRepository
      .createQueryBuilder('order')
      .leftJoinAndSelect('order.items', 'items')
      .leftJoinAndSelect('order.seller', 'seller')
      .orderBy('order.createdAt', 'DESC')
      .addOrderBy('order.id', 'DESC');

    if (sellerId) {
      const sellerIdInt = parseInt(sellerId, 10);
      if (isNaN(sellerIdInt)) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Invalid sellerId');
      }
      queryBuilder.andWhere('order.sellerId = :sellerId', {
        sellerId: sellerIdInt,
      });
    }

    if (status) {
      queryBuilder.andWhere('order.status = :status', { status });
    }

    if (after !== undefined && after !== null) {
      const cursor = await this.decodeCursor(after);
      queryBuilder.andWhere('order.createdAt < :cursor', { cursor });
    }

    const orders = await queryBuilder.take(first + 1).getMany();

    const hasNextPage = orders.length > first;
    const edges: OrderEdge[] = orders.slice(0, first).map((order) => ({
      node: order,
      cursor: this.encodeCursor(order.createdAt),
    }));

    return {
      edges,
      pageInfo: {
        hasNextPage,
        hasPreviousPage: after !== undefined && after !== null && after !== '',
        endCursor: edges[edges.length - 1]?.cursor,
      },
      totalCount: await this.getTotalCount(sellerId, status),
    };
  }

  async findOne(id: string): Promise<Order> {
    const order = await this.orderRepository.findOne({
      where: { id },
      relations: ['items', 'seller'],
    });

    if (!order) {
      throw new NotFoundException(`Order with ID ${id} not found`);
    }

    return order;
  }

  async updateOrder(
    id: string,
    updateOrderInput: UpdateOrderInput,
  ): Promise<Order> {
    const order = await this.findOne(id);

    const inputWithItems = updateOrderInput as UpdateOrderInput & {
      items?: unknown;
      subtotal?: unknown;
      total?: unknown;
      shippingCost?: unknown;
    };

    if (
      inputWithItems.items != null ||
      inputWithItems.subtotal != null ||
      inputWithItems.total != null ||
      inputWithItems.shippingCost != null ||
      order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING
    ) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'Order items and financial totals cannot be rewritten after order creation',
      );
    }

    if (updateOrderInput.customerEmail !== undefined) {
      order.customerEmail = updateOrderInput.customerEmail;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.customerPhone !== undefined) {
      order.customerPhone = updateOrderInput.customerPhone;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.customerName !== undefined) {
      order.customerName = updateOrderInput.customerName;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.shippingAddress !== undefined) {
      order.shippingAddress = updateOrderInput.shippingAddress;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.city !== undefined) {
      order.city = updateOrderInput.city;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.state !== undefined) {
      order.state = updateOrderInput.state;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.zipCode !== undefined) {
      order.zipCode = updateOrderInput.zipCode;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.country !== undefined) {
      order.country = updateOrderInput.country;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.notes !== undefined) {
      order.notes = updateOrderInput.notes;
    }
    if (updateOrderInput.trackingNumber !== undefined) {
      order.trackingNumber = updateOrderInput.trackingNumber;
    }

    return this.orderRepository.save(order);
  }

  async updateOrderStatus(id: string, status: OrderStatus): Promise<Order> {
    return this.runInTransaction(async (manager) => {
      const orderRepo = manager
        ? manager.getRepository(Order)
        : this.orderRepository;
      const sellerProductRepo = manager
        ? manager.getRepository(SellerProduct)
        : this.sellerProductRepository;

      const order = await orderRepo.findOne({
        where: { id },
        relations: ['items'],
        lock: manager ? { mode: 'pessimistic_write' } : undefined,
      });

      if (!order) {
        throw new NotFoundException(`Order with ID ${id} not found`);
      }

      if (order.status === status) {
        return order;
      }

      const allowedTransitions =
        OrderService.ALLOWED_STATUS_TRANSITIONS[order.status] || [];
      if (!allowedTransitions.includes(status)) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          `Invalid status transition from ${order.status} to ${status}`,
        );
      }

      if (
        order.status === OrderStatus.PENDING &&
        status === OrderStatus.CONFIRMED &&
        !order.stockReserved
      ) {
        await this.decreaseStock(order, sellerProductRepo);
        order.stockReserved = true;
      }

      if (
        (status === OrderStatus.CANCELLED || status === OrderStatus.REFUNDED) &&
        order.stockReserved
      ) {
        await this.restoreStock(order, sellerProductRepo);
        order.stockReserved = false;
      }

      order.status = status;
      return orderRepo.save(order);
    });
  }

  async deleteOrder(id: string): Promise<void> {
    return this.runInTransaction(async (manager) => {
      const orderRepo = manager
        ? manager.getRepository(Order)
        : this.orderRepository;
      const sellerProductRepo = manager
        ? manager.getRepository(SellerProduct)
        : this.sellerProductRepository;

      const order = await orderRepo.findOne({
        where: { id },
        relations: ['items'],
        lock: manager ? { mode: 'pessimistic_write' } : undefined,
      });

      if (!order) {
        throw new NotFoundException(`Order with ID ${id} not found`);
      }

      if (order.stockReserved) {
        await this.restoreStock(order, sellerProductRepo);
      }

      await orderRepo.remove(order);
    });
  }

  async getOrdersBySeller(
    sellerId: string,
    first = 10,
    after?: string,
    status?: OrderStatus,
  ): Promise<OrdersConnection> {
    return this.findAll(first, after, sellerId, status);
  }

  async getOrdersByStatus(
    status: OrderStatus,
    first = 10,
    after?: string,
  ): Promise<OrdersConnection> {
    return this.findAll(first, after, undefined, status);
  }

  async getOrdersByCustomerEmail(
    customerEmail: string,
    first = 10,
    after?: string,
  ): Promise<OrdersConnection> {
    const queryBuilder = this.orderRepository
      .createQueryBuilder('order')
      .leftJoinAndSelect('order.items', 'items')
      .leftJoinAndSelect('order.seller', 'seller')
      .where('order.customerEmail = :customerEmail', { customerEmail })
      .orderBy('order.createdAt', 'DESC')
      .addOrderBy('order.id', 'DESC');

    if (after !== undefined && after !== null) {
      const cursor = await this.decodeCursor(after);
      queryBuilder.andWhere('order.createdAt < :cursor', { cursor });
    }

    const orders = await queryBuilder.take(first + 1).getMany();

    const hasNextPage = orders.length > first;
    const edges: OrderEdge[] = orders.slice(0, first).map((order) => ({
      node: order,
      cursor: this.encodeCursor(order.createdAt),
    }));

    return {
      edges,
      pageInfo: {
        hasNextPage,
        hasPreviousPage: after !== undefined && after !== null && after !== '',
        endCursor: edges[edges.length - 1]?.cursor,
      },
      totalCount: await this.orderRepository.count({
        where: { customerEmail },
      }),
    };
  }

  private generateOrderNumber(): string {
    const timestamp = Date.now().toString();
    const random = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8).toUpperCase();
    return `ORD-${timestamp}-${random}`;
  }

  private generateTrackingNumber(): string {
    const now = new Date();
    const year = now.getFullYear();
    const month = String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
    const day = String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
    const random = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8).toUpperCase();
    return `KZ-${year}${month}${day}-${random}`;
  }

  private encodeCursor(date: Date): string {
    return Buffer.from(date.toISOString()).toString('base64');
  }

  private async decodeCursor(cursor: string): Promise<Date> {
    if (typeof cursor !== 'string' || cursor.trim() === '') {
      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid cursor');
    }
    try {
      const decoded = Buffer.from(cursor, 'base64').toString();
      const date = new Date(decoded);
      if (!decoded || Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Invalid cursor');
      }
      return date;
    } catch {
      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid cursor');
    }
  }

  private async getTotalCount(
    sellerId?: string,
    status?: OrderStatus,
  ): Promise<number> {
    const queryBuilder = this.orderRepository.createQueryBuilder('order');

    if (sellerId) {
      const sellerIdInt = parseInt(sellerId, 10);
      if (!isNaN(sellerIdInt)) {
        queryBuilder.andWhere('order.sellerId = :sellerId', {
          sellerId: sellerIdInt,
        });
      }
    }

    if (status) {
      queryBuilder.andWhere('order.status = :status', { status });
    }

    return queryBuilder.getCount();
  }

  private async decreaseStock(
    order: Order,
    sellerProductRepo: Repository<SellerProduct>,
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (order.stockReserved) {
      return;
    }

    for (const item of order.items) {
      if (
        !Number.isInteger(Number(item.quantity)) ||
        Number(item.quantity) <= 0
      ) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          'Order item quantity must be a positive integer',
        );
      }
    }

    const qtyBySellerProduct = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const item of order.items) {
      const sellerProductId = item.sellerProductId || item.productId;
      const existing = qtyBySellerProduct.get(sellerProductId) || 0;
      qtyBySellerProduct.set(sellerProductId, existing + Number(item.quantity));
    }

    const sortedSellerProductIds = Array.from(qtyBySellerProduct.keys()).sort();

    for (const sellerProductId of sortedSellerProductIds) {
      const requiredQty = qtyBySellerProduct.get(sellerProductId)!;

      const sellerProduct = await sellerProductRepo.findOne({
        where: { id: sellerProductId },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });

      if (!sellerProduct) {
        throw new NotFoundException(
          `SellerProduct with ID ${sellerProductId} not found`,
        );
      }

      if (sellerProduct.stockQuantity < requiredQty) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          `Insufficient stock for product ${sellerProductId}. Available: ${sellerProduct.stockQuantity}, Requested: ${requiredQty}`,
        );
      }
    }

    for (const sellerProductId of sortedSellerProductIds) {
      const requiredQty = qtyBySellerProduct.get(sellerProductId)!;

      const sellerProduct = await sellerProductRepo.findOne({
        where: { id: sellerProductId },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });

      if (sellerProduct) {
        sellerProduct.stockQuantity -= requiredQty;
        await sellerProductRepo.save(sellerProduct);
      }
    }
  }

  async confirmOrderPayment(
    orderId: string,
    refId?: string,
  ): Promise<Order> {
    return this.runInTransaction(async (manager) => {
      const orderRepo = manager
        ? manager.getRepository(Order)
        : this.orderRepository;
      const sellerProductRepo = manager
        ? manager.getRepository(SellerProduct)
        : this.sellerProductRepository;

      const order = await orderRepo.findOne({
        where: { id: orderId },
        relations: ['items'],
        lock: manager ? { mode: 'pessimistic_write' } : undefined,
      });

      if (!order) {
        throw new NotFoundException(`Order with ID ${orderId} not found`);
      }

      if (order.status === OrderStatus.CONFIRMED && order.stockReserved) {
        return order;
      }

      if (order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          `Cannot confirm payment for order in status ${order.status}`,
        );
      }

      await this.decreaseStock(order, sellerProductRepo);
      order.stockReserved = true;
      order.status = OrderStatus.CONFIRMED;
      order.paymentVerifiedAt = new Date();

      return orderRepo.save(order);
    });
  }

  async setPaymentIntent(params: {
    orderId: string;
    authority: string;
    amount: number;
    currency?: string;
  }): Promise<Order> {
    const order = await this.findOne(params.orderId);

    if (order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `Cannot create payment intent for order with status ${order.status}`,
      );
    }

    order.paymentAuthority = params.authority;
    order.paymentAmount = params.amount;
    order.paymentCurrency = params.currency || 'IRT';

    return this.orderRepository.save(order);
  }

  async findOrdersByPaymentAuthority(authority: string): Promise<Order[]> {
    if (!authority) return [];
    const orders = await this.orderRepository.find({
      where: { paymentAuthority: authority },
      relations: ['items', 'seller'],
      order: { id: 'ASC' },
    });

    // Collect unique non-empty sellerProductId from all order items
    const sellerProductIds = new Set<string>();
    for (const order of orders) {
      for (const item of order.items || []) {
        if (item.sellerProductId) {
          sellerProductIds.add(item.sellerProductId);
        }
      }
    }

    if (sellerProductIds.size > 0) {
      const sellerProducts = await this.sellerProductRepository.findBy({
        id: In(Array.from(sellerProductIds)),
      });
      const sellerProductMap = new Map(sellerProducts.map((sp) => [sp.id, sp]));

      for (const order of orders) {
        for (const item of order.items || []) {
          (item as OrderItem & { sellerProduct?: SellerProduct | null }).sellerProduct =
            item.sellerProductId ? sellerProductMap.get(item.sellerProductId) ?? null : null;
        }
      }
    }

    return orders;
  }

  async cancelPendingOrder(orderId: string): Promise<Order> {
    return this.runInTransaction(async (manager) => {
      const orderRepo = manager
        ? manager.getRepository(Order)
        : this.orderRepository;
      const sellerProductRepo = manager
        ? manager.getRepository(SellerProduct)
        : this.sellerProductRepository;

      const order = await orderRepo.findOne({
        where: { id: orderId },
        relations: ['items'],
        lock: manager ? { mode: 'pessimistic_write' } : undefined,
      });

      if (!order) {
        throw new NotFoundException(`Order with ID ${orderId} not found`);
      }

      if (order.status !== OrderStatus.PENDING) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Only PENDING orders can be cancelled');
      }

      if (order.stockReserved) {
        await this.restoreStock(order, sellerProductRepo);
      }

      order.status = OrderStatus.CANCELLED;
      return orderRepo.save(order);
    });
  }

  async updateTrackingNumber(id: string, trackingNumber: string): Promise<Order> {
    const order = await this.findOne(id);
    order.trackingNumber = trackingNumber.trim();
    return this.orderRepository.save(order);
  }

  private async restoreStock(
    order: Order,
    sellerProductRepo: Repository<SellerProduct>,
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (!order.stockReserved) {
      return;
    }

    for (const item of order.items) {
      if (!Number.isInteger(Number(item.quantity)) || Number(item.quantity) <= 0) {
        throw new BadRequestException(
          'Order item quantity must be a positive integer',
        );
      }
    }

    const qtyBySellerProduct = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const item of order.items) {
      const sellerProductId = item.sellerProductId || item.productId;
      const existing = qtyBySellerProduct.get(sellerProductId) || 0;
      qtyBySellerProduct.set(sellerProductId, existing + Number(item.quantity));
    }

    const sortedSellerProductIds = Array.from(qtyBySellerProduct.keys()).sort();

    for (const sellerProductId of sortedSellerProductIds) {
      const requiredQty = qtyBySellerProduct.get(sellerProductId)!;

      const sellerProduct = await sellerProductRepo.findOne({
        where: { id: sellerProductId },
        lock: { mode: 'pessimistic_write' },
      });

      if (sellerProduct) {
        sellerProduct.stockQuantity += requiredQty;
        await sellerProductRepo.save(sellerProduct);
      }
    }
  }
}

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { PinoLogger } from 'nestjs-pino';

@Injectable()
export class PaymentLogger {
  constructor(private readonly logger: PinoLogger) {
    this.logger.setContext(PaymentLogger.name);
  }

  logPaymentInitiated(input: {
    orderId?: string;
    userId?: string;
    amount: number;
    currency: string;
    gateway: string;
  }) {
    this.logger.info(
      {
        event: 'payment_initiated',
        orderId: input.orderId,
        userId: input.userId,
        amount: input.amount,
        currency: input.currency,
        gateway: input.gateway,
      },
      'Payment initiated'
    );
  }

  logGatewayResponse(input: {
    orderId?: string;
    authority?: string;
    code: number;
    message: string;
    gateway: string;
  }) {
    this.logger.info(
      {
        event: 'payment_gateway_response',
        orderId: input.orderId,
        authority: input.authority,
        code: input.code,
        message: input.message,
        gateway: input.gateway,
      },
      'Payment gateway responded'
    );
  }

  logVerificationResult(input: {
    orderId?: string;
    authority: string;
    amount: number;
    code: number;
    message: string;
    refId?: number;
  }) {
    this.logger.info(
      {
        event: 'payment_verification_result',
        orderId: input.orderId,
        authority: input.authority,
        amount: input.amount,
        code: input.code,
        message: input.message,
        refId: input.refId,
      },
      'Payment verification finished'
    );
  }

  logOrderConfirmationFailed(orderId: string, error: unknown) {
    this.logger.error(
      {
        event: 'order_confirmation_failed',
        orderId,
        error:
          error instanceof Error
            ? { name: error.name, message: error.message }
            : error,
      },
      'Failed to confirm order after successful payment'
    );
  }

  logOrderCancellationFailed(orderId: string, error: unknown) {
    this.logger.error(
      {
        event: 'order_cancellation_failed',
        orderId,
        error:
          error instanceof Error
            ? { name: error.name, message: error.message }
            : error,
      },
      'Failed to cancel order after failed payment'
    );
  }
}

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';

export interface ZarinPalPaymentRequest {
  amount: number; // Amount in unit of currency (Kickzone uses IRT / Toman)
  callback_url: string;
  description: string;
  metadata?: {
    mobile?: string;
    email?: string;
    order_id?: string;
  };
  currency?: 'IRR' | 'IRT';
}

export interface ZarinPalPaymentResponse {
  data: {
    code: number;
    message: string;
    authority: string;
    fee_type?: string;
    fee?: number;
  };
  errors: Array<{
    code: number;
    message: string;
  }>;
}

export interface ZarinPalVerificationRequest {
  amount: number; // Must match request amount / currency unit
  authority: string;
}

export interface ZarinPalVerificationResponse {
  data: {
    code: number;
    message: string;
    ref_id?: number;
    card_pan?: string;
    card_hash?: string;
    fee_type?: string;
    fee?: number;
  };
  errors: Array<{
    code: number;
    message: string;
  }>;
}

/** Error body returned by the ZarinPal REST API on non-2xx responses. */
interface ZarinPalErrorBody {
  errors?: { message?: string; code?: number }[];
  message?: string;
  data?: { message?: string };
}

@Injectable()
export class PaymentService {
  private readonly merchantId: string;
  private readonly isSandbox: boolean;
  private readonly apiBaseUrl: string;

  constructor(private readonly configService: ConfigService) {
    // Use a random UUID for sandbox if not configured (sandbox accepts any UUID)
    // Generate a random UUID: xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx
    const generateRandomUUID = () => {
      return 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-yxxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/[xy]/g, (c) => {
        const r = (Math.random() * 16) | 0;
        const v = c === 'x' ? r : (r & 0x3) | 0x8;
        return v.toString(16);
      });
    };

    this.merchantId =
      this.configService.get<string>('ZARINPAL_MERCHANT_ID') ||
      generateRandomUUID();

    // Check sandbox mode - default to true in development
    const nodeEnv = this.configService.get<string>('NODE_ENV', 'development');
    const sandboxEnv = this.configService.get<string>('ZARINPAL_SANDBOX');

    // Default to sandbox in development, require explicit false for production
    this.isSandbox =
      sandboxEnv === undefined ? nodeEnv !== 'production' : sandboxEnv !== 'false';

    this.apiBaseUrl = this.isSandbox
      ? 'https://sandbox.zarinpal.com/pg/v4/payment'
      : 'https://payment.zarinpal.com/pg/v4/payment';

    // Warn if using default merchant ID
    if (this.merchantId === '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') {
      if (!this.isSandbox) {
        console.error(
          'â‌Œ ZarinPal PRODUCTION: Merchant ID not configured! ' +
            'Payment requests will fail. Set ZARINPAL_MERCHANT_ID environment variable.'
        );
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Request payment from ZarinPal
   */
  async requestPayment(
    request: ZarinPalPaymentRequest
  ): Promise<ZarinPalPaymentResponse> {
    const payload = {
      merchant_id: this.merchantId,
      amount: request.amount,
      callback_url: request.callback_url,
      description: request.description,
      currency: request.currency || 'IRT',
      metadata: request.metadata,
    };

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${this.apiBaseUrl}/request.json`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Accept: 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        // Clone the response so we can read it multiple times
        const responseClone = response.clone();

        // Try to get error details from response body
        let errorDetails = '';
        let fullErrorBody: ZarinPalErrorBody | null = null;
        let responseText = '';

        try {
          // First try to get as text to see what we're dealing with
          responseText = await responseClone.text();
          console.error(
            `[PaymentService] â‌Œ ZarinPal API raw error response:`,
            responseText.substring(0, 1000) // First 1000 chars
          );

          // Try to parse as JSON
          try {
            const parsedBody: ZarinPalErrorBody = JSON.parse(responseText);
            fullErrorBody = parsedBody;
            console.error(
              `[PaymentService] â‌Œ ZarinPal API error response body (JSON):`,
              JSON.stringify(fullErrorBody, null, 2)
            );

            if (parsedBody.errors && parsedBody.errors.length > 0) {
              errorDetails = parsedBody.errors
                .map(
                  (e: { message?: string; code?: number }) =>
                    e.message || `Code: ${e.code}`
                )
                .join(', ');
            } else if (parsedBody.message) {
              errorDetails = parsedBody.message;
            } else if (parsedBody.data && parsedBody.data.message) {
              errorDetails = parsedBody.data.message;
            } else {
              errorDetails = JSON.stringify(fullErrorBody);
            }
          } catch (jsonError) {
            // Not JSON, use text as error details
            errorDetails = responseText || response.statusText;
            console.error(
              `[PaymentService] â‌Œ ZarinPal API error (non-JSON response):`,
              responseText.substring(0, 500)
            );
          }
        } catch (textError) {
          // Can't even read as text
          errorDetails = response.statusText;
          console.error(
            `[PaymentService] â‌Œ Could not read response body:`,
            textError
          );
        }

        const errorMessage = errorDetails
          ? `ZarinPal API error (${response.status}): ${errorDetails}`
          : `ZarinPal API error: HTTP ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`;

        console.error(`[PaymentService] â‌Œ Payment request failed:`, {
          status: response.status,
          statusText: response.statusText,
          errorDetails,
          fullResponse: fullErrorBody,
          orderId: request.metadata?.order_id,
          amount: request.amount,
          currency: request.currency || 'IRT',
          merchantId: this.merchantId,
          isSandbox: this.isSandbox,
          apiUrl: this.apiBaseUrl,
          payload: {
            merchant_id: this.merchantId.substring(0, 8) + '...',
            amount: request.amount,
            currency: request.currency || 'IRT',
            callback_url: request.callback_url,
          },
        });

        throw new Error(errorMessage);
      }

      const data: ZarinPalPaymentResponse = await response.json();

      if (data.errors && data.errors.length > 0) {
        const errorMessages = data.errors.map((e) => e.message).join(', ');
        throw new Error(`ZarinPal API error: ${errorMessages}`);
      }

      return data;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof Error) {
        throw error;
      }
      throw new Error('Failed to request payment from ZarinPal');
    }
  }

  /**
   * Verify payment with ZarinPal
   */
  async verifyPayment(
    request: ZarinPalVerificationRequest
  ): Promise<ZarinPalVerificationResponse> {
    const payload = {
      merchant_id: this.merchantId,
      amount: request.amount,
      authority: request.authority,
    };

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${this.apiBaseUrl}/verify.json`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Accept: 'application/json',
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      });

      if (!response.ok) {
        // Try to get error details from response body
        let errorDetails = '';
        try {
          const errorBody = await response.json();
          if (errorBody.errors && errorBody.errors.length > 0) {
            errorDetails = errorBody.errors
              .map(
                (e: { message?: string; code?: number }) =>
                  e.message || `Code: ${e.code}`
              )
              .join(', ');
          }
        } catch {
          // If parsing fails, use status text
          errorDetails = response.statusText;
        }

        const errorMessage = errorDetails
          ? `ZarinPal API error (${response.status}): ${errorDetails}`
          : `ZarinPal API error: HTTP ${response.status} ${response.statusText}`;

        console.error(`[PaymentService] â‌Œ Payment verification failed:`, {
          status: response.status,
          statusText: response.statusText,
          errorDetails,
          authority: request.authority,
          amount: request.amount,
          merchantId: this.merchantId.substring(0, 8) + '...',
          isSandbox: this.isSandbox,
        });

        throw new Error(errorMessage);
      }

      const data: ZarinPalVerificationResponse = await response.json();

      if (data.errors && data.errors.length > 0) {
        const errorMessages = data.errors.map((e) => e.message).join(', ');
        throw new Error(`ZarinPal API error: ${errorMessages}`);
      }

      return data;
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof Error) {
        throw error;
      }
      throw new Error('Failed to verify payment with ZarinPal');
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get payment gateway URL
   */
  getGatewayUrl(authority: string): string {
    const gatewayBaseUrl = this.isSandbox
      ? 'https://sandbox.zarinpal.com/pg/StartPay'
      : 'https://payment.zarinpal.com/pg/StartPay';
    return `${gatewayBaseUrl}/${authority}`;
  }
}

import { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';

describe('SellerService - Price Validation', () => {
  let service: SellerService;

  let sellerProductRepository: jest.Mocked<Repository<SellerProduct>>;

    sellerProductRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(SellerProduct));

    });

    it('rejects price < 0 (-10) with BadRequestException and does not call repositories', async () => {
      const input = { ...validCreateInput, price: -10 };

      await expect(service.createSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(service.createSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Price must be a positive number'
      );

      expect(productInfoRepository.findOne).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('rejects stockQuantity < 0 (-1) with BadRequestException and does not call repositories', async () => {
      const input = { ...validCreateInput, stockQuantity: -1 };

      await expect(service.createSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(service.createSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(

      expect(productInfoRepository.findOne).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    it('rejects stockQuantity non-integer (1.5) with BadRequestException and does not call repositories', async () => {

    it('rejects costPrice < 0 (-1) with BadRequestException and does not call repositories', async () => {
      const input = { ...validCreateInput, costPrice: -1 };

      await expect(service.createSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(service.createSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Cost price must be zero or greater'
      );

      expect(productInfoRepository.findOne).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('accepts valid values (price 100, discount 0..100 inclusive, stock 0) and proceeds', async () => {
      const input = { ...validCreateInput, discount: 100, stockQuantity: 0 };
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);

      await expect(service.createSellerProduct(1, input)).resolves.toBeDefined();

      expect(productInfoRepository.findOne).toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });

  describe('updateSellerProduct - validation', () => {
    beforeEach(() => {
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(mockSellerProduct);
    });

    it('rejects discount > 100 (101) with BadRequestException and does not call save', async () => {
      const input = { ...validUpdateInput, discount: 101 };

      await expect(service.updateSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    it('rejects price < 0 (-1) with BadRequestException and does not call save', async () => {
      const input = { ...validUpdateInput, price: -1 };

      await expect(service.updateSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(
        BadRequestException
      );
      await expect(service.updateSellerProduct(1, input)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Price must be a positive number'
      );

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();

    it('accepts discount = 100 (boundary) and calls save', async () => {
      const input = { ...validUpdateInput, discount: 100 };
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        discount: 100,
