      await expect(service.updateSellerProduct(1, input)).resolves.toBeDefined();

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('accepts stockQuantity = 0 (boundary) and calls save', async () => {
      const input = { ...validUpdateInput, stockQuantity: 0 };
      sellerProductRepository.save.mockResolvedValue({
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        stockQuantity: 0,
        setProductInfoId: jest.fn(),
      });

      await expect(service.updateSellerProduct(1, input)).resolves.toBeDefined();

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('throws Unauthorized for product owned by another seller (ownership check unchanged)', async () => {
      const otherSellerProduct = {
        ...mockSellerProduct,
        seller: { ...mockSeller, id: 2 },
      } as unknown as SellerProduct;
      sellerProductRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValue(otherSellerProduct);

      await expect(service.updateSellerProduct(1, validUpdateInput)).rejects.toThrow(
        'Unauthorized'
      );

      expect(sellerProductRepository.save).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });
});
===== FILE: .\libs\server\services\src\lib\seller-info.service.ts =====
import { Injectable, BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';
import {
  SellerInfo,
  SellerProduct,
  ProductInfo,
  User,
  UserRole,
  ShippingPaymentMode,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';
import {
  CreateSellerInput,
  UpdateSellerInput,
  CreateSellerProductInput,
  UpdateSellerProductInput,
  SellerFilterInput,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/inputs';
import * as bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';
import { formatProductInfoColorNames } from './product-info-colors.util';

@Injectable()
export class SellerService {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(SellerInfo)
    private sellerRepository: Repository<SellerInfo>,
    @InjectRepository(SellerProduct)
    private sellerProductRepository: Repository<SellerProduct>,
    @InjectRepository(ProductInfo)
    private productInfoRepository: Repository<ProductInfo>,
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private userRepository: Repository<User>,
    private authService: AuthService
  ) {}

  private assertValidSellerProductNumbers(values: {
    price?: number;
    costPrice?: number | null;
    discount?: number;
    stockQuantity?: number;
  }): void {
    if (values.price !== undefined) {
      if (!Number.isFinite(values.price) || values.price <= 0) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Price must be a positive number');
      }
    }
    if (values.costPrice !== undefined && values.costPrice !== null) {
      if (!Number.isFinite(values.costPrice) || values.costPrice < 0) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Cost price must be zero or greater');
      }
    }
    if (values.discount !== undefined) {
      if (!Number.isFinite(values.discount) || values.discount < 0 || values.discount > 100) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Discount must be between 0 and 100');
      }
    }
    if (values.stockQuantity !== undefined) {
      if (!Number.isInteger(values.stockQuantity) || values.stockQuantity < 0) {
        throw new BadRequestException('Stock quantity must be a non-negative integer');
      }
    }
  }

  async create(createSellerInput: CreateSellerInput): Promise<SellerInfo> {
    // Check if email already exists
    const existingUser = await this.userRepository.findOne({
      where: { email: createSellerInput.email },
    });
    if (existingUser) {
      throw new Error('Email already exists');
    }

    // Create user with hashed password
    const hashedPassword = await bcrypt.hash(createSellerInput.password, 10);
    const user = this.userRepository.create({
      name: createSellerInput.name,
      lastName: createSellerInput.lastName,
      username: createSellerInput.username,
      email: createSellerInput.email,
      password: hashedPassword,
      role: UserRole.SELLER,
    });
    await this.userRepository.save(user);

    // Create seller info
    const newSeller = this.sellerRepository.create({
      storeName: createSellerInput.storeName,
      phone: createSellerInput.phone,
      address: createSellerInput.address,
      shippingFee: createSellerInput.shippingFee ?? 0,
      shippingPaymentMode:
        createSellerInput.shippingPaymentMode ??
        ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY,
      canManageCatalog: createSellerInput.canManageCatalog ?? false,
      user,
    });
    const savedSeller = await this.sellerRepository.save(newSeller);

    // Initiate password setup
    try {
      const baseUrl =
        process.env['SELLERS_FRONTEND_URL'] || 'https://127.0.0.1:4500';
      await this.authService.initiatePasswordSetup(user.email, baseUrl);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        `Failed to initiate password setup for ${user.email}:`,
        error
      );
    }

    return savedSeller;
  }

  async findAll(filter?: SellerFilterInput): Promise<[SellerInfo[], number]> {
    const query = this.sellerRepository
      .createQueryBuilder('seller')
      .leftJoinAndSelect('seller.user', 'user');

    if (filter?.searchTerm) {
      query.andWhere(
        '(LOWER(user.name) LIKE :searchTerm OR LOWER(seller.storeName) LIKE :searchTerm OR LOWER(user.email) LIKE :searchTerm)',
        { searchTerm: `%${filter.searchTerm.toLowerCase()}%` }
      );
    }

    if (filter?.isActive !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere('seller.isActive = :isActive', {
        isActive: filter.isActive,
      });
    }

    if (filter?.ids && filter.ids.length > 0) {
      query.andWhere('seller.id IN (:...ids)', { ids: filter.ids });
    }

    const totalCount = await query.getCount();

    if (filter?.first) {
      query.take(filter.first);
    }

    if (filter?.after) {
      query.skip(parseInt(filter.after, 10));
    }

    const sellers = await query.getMany();
    return [sellers, totalCount];
  }

  findOne(id: number): Promise<SellerInfo | null> {
    return this.sellerRepository.findOne({
      where: { id },
      relations: ['user'],
    });
  }

  async findOneByUserId(userId: string): Promise<SellerInfo | null> {
    return this.sellerRepository.findOne({
      where: { user: { id: userId } },
      relations: ['user', 'sellerProducts'],
    });
  }

  async update(
    updateSellerInput: UpdateSellerInput
  ): Promise<SellerInfo | null> {
    const seller = await this.findOne(updateSellerInput.id);
    if (!seller) {
      throw new Error('Seller not found');
    }

    // Update user info if provided
    if (
      updateSellerInput.name ||
      updateSellerInput.username ||
      updateSellerInput.email
    ) {
      const user = seller.user;
      if (updateSellerInput.name) user.name = updateSellerInput.name;
      if (updateSellerInput.username)
        user.username = updateSellerInput.username;
      if (updateSellerInput.email) user.email = updateSellerInput.email;
      await this.userRepository.save(user);
    }

    // Update seller info
    if (updateSellerInput.storeName)
      seller.storeName = updateSellerInput.storeName;
    if (updateSellerInput.phone) seller.phone = updateSellerInput.phone;
    if (updateSellerInput.address) seller.address = updateSellerInput.address;
    if (updateSellerInput.shippingFee !== undefined)
      seller.shippingFee = updateSellerInput.shippingFee;
    if (updateSellerInput.shippingPaymentMode !== undefined)
      seller.shippingPaymentMode = updateSellerInput.shippingPaymentMode;
    if (updateSellerInput.isActive !== undefined)
      seller.isActive = updateSellerInput.isActive;
    if (updateSellerInput.canManageCatalog !== undefined)
      seller.canManageCatalog = updateSellerInput.canManageCatalog;

    return this.sellerRepository.save(seller);
  }

  async toggleActive(id: number): Promise<SellerInfo | null> {
    const seller = await this.findOne(id);
    if (!seller) {
      throw new Error('Seller not found');
    }

    seller.isActive = !seller.isActive;
    return this.sellerRepository.save(seller);
  }

  async createSellerProduct(
    sellerId: number,
    input: CreateSellerProductInput,
    allowRecommended = false
  ): Promise<SellerProduct> {
    this.assertValidSellerProductNumbers({
      price: input.price,
      costPrice: input.costPrice,
      discount: input.discount,
      stockQuantity: input.stockQuantity,
    });

    // Load productInfo with product relation
    const productInfo = await this.productInfoRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: input.productInfoId },
      relations: ['product', 'colorLinks', 'colorLinks.color'],
    });
    if (!productInfo) {
      throw new Error('Product variant not found');
    }
    if (!productInfo.product) {
      throw new Error('Product not found for this variant');
    }

    // Load seller
    const seller = await this.sellerRepository.findOneBy({ id: sellerId });
    if (!seller) {
      throw new Error('Seller not found');
    }

    // Check if seller already has a SellerProduct for this ProductInfo (unique constraint)
    const existingSellerProduct = await this.sellerProductRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        seller: { id: sellerId },
        productInfoId: productInfo.id,
      },
    });

    if (existingSellerProduct) {
      throw new Error(
        `You already have a listing for this product variant (${
          productInfo.size
        } - ${
          formatProductInfoColorNames(productInfo.colorLinks) || 'No color'
        }). Please update the existing listing instead.`
      );
    }

    // Create new SellerProduct
    const sellerProduct = this.sellerProductRepository.create({
      seller,
      product: productInfo.product,
      productInfo,
      productInfoId: productInfo.id, // Always use the actual productInfo.id
      price: input.price,
      costPrice: input.costPrice,
      discount: input.discount,
      stockQuantity: input.stockQuantity,
      recommended: allowRecommended ? input.recommended ?? false : false,
      boxLess: input.boxLess ?? false,
    });

    console.log(
      `[SellerService] Creating sellerProduct: sellerId=${sellerId}, productId=${productInfo.product.id}, productInfoId=${productInfo.id}`
    );

    try {
      const saved = await this.sellerProductRepository.save(sellerProduct);
      console.log(
        `[SellerService] âœ“ SellerProduct ${saved.id} created with productInfoId=${saved.productInfoId}`
      );
      return saved;
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      // Handle unique constraint violation
      const errorMessage =
        error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
      const errorCode = (error as { code?: string })?.code;
      if (errorCode === '23505' || errorMessage.includes('unique')) {
        throw new Error(
          `You already have a listing for this product variant. Please update the existing listing instead.`
        );
      }
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async updateSellerProduct(
    sellerId: number,
    input: UpdateSellerProductInput,
    allowRecommended = false
  ): Promise<SellerProduct> {
    const sellerProduct = await this.sellerProductRepository.findOne({
      where: { id: input.id },
      relations: ['seller', 'product', 'productInfo'],
    });
    if (!sellerProduct) throw new Error('Seller product not found');
    if (sellerProduct.seller.id !== sellerId) throw new Error('Unauthorized');

    this.assertValidSellerProductNumbers({
      price: input.price,
      costPrice: input.costPrice,
      discount: input.discount,
      stockQuantity: input.stockQuantity,
    });

    // If productInfoId is being updated, load the productInfo relation
    if (
      input.productInfoId !== undefined &&
      input.productInfoId !== sellerProduct.productInfoId
    ) {
      const productInfo = await this.productInfoRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: input.productInfoId },
        relations: ['product'],
      });
      if (!productInfo) throw new Error('Product variant not found');
      if (!productInfo.product)
        throw new Error('Product not found for this variant');

      console.log(
        `[SellerService] Updating sellerProduct ${sellerProduct.id}: productInfoId ${sellerProduct.productInfoId} -> ${input.productInfoId}`
      );

      // Update both the relation and the ID
      sellerProduct.productInfo = productInfo;
      sellerProduct.productInfoId = productInfo.id; // Use actual productInfo.id to ensure consistency
      sellerProduct.product = productInfo.product; // Ensure product is also updated
    }

    // Update other fields
    if (input.price !== undefined) sellerProduct.price = input.price;
    if (input.costPrice !== undefined)
      sellerProduct.costPrice = input.costPrice;
    if (input.discount !== undefined) sellerProduct.discount = input.discount;
    if (input.stockQuantity !== undefined)
      sellerProduct.stockQuantity = input.stockQuantity;
    if (allowRecommended && input.recommended !== undefined) {
      sellerProduct.recommended = input.recommended;
    }
    if (input.boxLess !== undefined) {
      sellerProduct.boxLess = input.boxLess;
    }

    return this.sellerProductRepository.save(sellerProduct);
  }

  async findSellerProductById(
    sellerProductId: string,
    sellerId: number
  ): Promise<SellerProduct | null> {
    return this.sellerProductRepository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: sellerProductId,
        seller: { id: sellerId },
      },
      relations: [
        'product',
        'productInfo',
        'productInfo.colorLinks',
        'productInfo.colorLinks.color',
        'product.productInfos',
        'product.categories',
      ],
    });
  }
}

===== FILE: .\libs\server\services\src\lib\shipping-calculation.service.spec.ts =====
import { Test, TestingModule } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';
import { getRepositoryToken } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { ShippingCalculationService, applyShippingMethodMultiplier } from './shipping-calculation.service';
import { PlatformSettings, ShippingStrategy, ShippingPaymentMode } from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';

describe('ShippingCalculationService', () => {
  let service: ShippingCalculationService;
  let mockSettingsRepository: jest.Mocked<Repository<PlatformSettings>>;

  const mockSettings: PlatformSettings = {
    id: 1,
    freeShippingEnabled: true,
    freeShippingThreshold: 100,
    shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT,
    additionalSellerDiscount: 0.5,
    additionalSellerFixedFee: 2.5,
    maxShippingCapEnabled: true,
    maxShippingCap: 15,
    createdAt: new Date(),
    updatedAt: new Date(),
  } as PlatformSettings;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    const module: TestingModule = await Test.createTestingModule({
      providers: [
        ShippingCalculationService,
        {
          provide: getRepositoryToken(PlatformSettings),
          useValue: {
            findOne: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(mockSettings),
            create: jest.fn().mockReturnValue(mockSettings),
            save: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(mockSettings),
          },
        },
      ],
    }).compile();

    service = module.get<ShippingCalculationService>(ShippingCalculationService);
    mockSettingsRepository = module.get(getRepositoryToken(PlatformSettings));
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
  });

  describe('Free Shipping', () => {
    it('should return totalShippingCost: 0 and appliedFee: 0 for all sellers when free shipping threshold is met', async () => {
      const sellerFees = [
        { sellerId: '1', shippingFee: 5.0, sellerName: 'Seller 1' },
        { sellerId: '2', shippingFee: 3.0, sellerName: 'Seller 2' },
      ];

      const result = await service.calculateShippingCost(150, sellerFees);

      expect(result.totalShippingCost).toBe(0);
      expect(result.freeShippingApplied).toBe(true);
      expect(result.shippingBreakdown).toHaveLength(2);
      result.shippingBreakdown.forEach((item) => {
        expect(item.appliedFee).toBe(0);
        expect(item.originalFee).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);
      });
    });

    it('should return originalFee as number even when shippingFee is 0', async () => {
      const sellerFees = [
        { sellerId: '1', shippingFee: 0, sellerName: 'Free Ship Seller' },
      ];

      const result = await service.calculateShippingCost(150, sellerFees);

      expect(result.totalShippingCost).toBe(0);
      expect(result.shippingBreakdown[0].originalFee).toBe(0);
      expect(result.shippingBreakdown[0].appliedFee).toBe(0);
    });

        { sellerId: '2', shippingFee: 10, sellerName: 'Paid Ship Seller' },

      expect(result.shippingBreakdown.find((s) => s.sellerId === '1')?.appliedFee).toBe(0);
      expect(result.shippingBreakdown.find((s) => s.sellerId === '2')?.appliedFee).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    });

    it('should return 0 for seller with shippingFee: 0 (VIP customer with TIERED_DISCOUNT)', async () => {
      mockSettingsRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValueOnce({
        ...mockSettings,
        freeShippingEnabled: false,
        shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT,
        additionalSellerDiscount: 0.5,

        { sellerId: '1', shippingFee: 0, sellerName: 'First Seller Free' },
        { sellerId: '2', shippingFee: 10, sellerName: 'Second Seller' },

      // First seller (index 0) pays full fee = 0

      expect(result.shippingBreakdown[1].appliedFee).toBe(5);

      mockSettingsRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValueOnce({
        ...mockSettings,
        freeShippingEnabled: false,
        shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.FULL_SUM,
      });

      const sellerFees = [

      // First seller: 10 (full)

        maxShippingCapEnabled: false,

      expect(result.totalShippingCost).toBe(10);
      expect(result.shippingBreakdown[0].appliedFee).toBe(10);
      expect(result.shippingBreakdown[0].originalFee).toBe(10);
    });

    it('should be case-insensitive for PAY_ON_DELIVERY', async () => {
      mockSettingsRepository.findOne.mockResolvedValueOnce({
        ...mockSettings,
        freeShippingEnabled: false,
        shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.FULL_SUM,
        maxShippingCapEnabled: false,
      });

      const sellerFees = [
        { sellerId: '1', shippingFee: 10, sellerName: 'Lowercase COD', shippingPaymentMode: 'pay_on_delivery' },
        { sellerId: '2', shippingFee: 10, sellerName: 'Mixed Case COD', shippingPaymentMode: 'Pay_On_Delivery' },
        { sellerId: '3', shippingFee: 10, sellerName: 'Online Seller', shippingPaymentMode: 'pay_online' },
      ];

      const result = await service.calculateShippingCost(50, sellerFees);

      expect(result.shippingBreakdown.find((s) => s.sellerId === '1')?.appliedFee).toBe(0);
      expect(result.shippingBreakdown.find((s) => s.sellerId === '2')?.appliedFee).toBe(0);
      expect(result.shippingBreakdown.find((s) => s.sellerId === '3')?.appliedFee).toBe(10);
      expect(result.totalShippingCost).toBe(10);
    });
  });

  describe('applyShippingMethodMultiplier', () => {
    it('should return 0 for pickup', () => {
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'pickup')).toBe(0);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'PICKUP')).toBe(0);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'Pickup')).toBe(0);
    });

    it('should return 45% of base fee rounded to 2 decimals for express', () => {
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'express')).toBe(4.5);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'EXPRESS')).toBe(4.5);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'Express')).toBe(4.5);
      // Test rounding: 10 * 0.45 = 4.5 (already 2 decimals)
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(11.11, 'express')).toBe(5.0); // 11.11 * 0.45 = 4.9995 -> 5.00
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(7.77, 'express')).toBe(3.5); // 7.77 * 0.45 = 3.4965 -> 3.50
    });

    it('should return full base fee rounded to 2 decimals for vip (default)', () => {
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'vip')).toBe(10);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'VIP')).toBe(10);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, 'Vip')).toBe(10);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10)).toBe(10); // undefined defaults to vip
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10, null)).toBe(10); // null defaults to vip
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(10.123, 'vip')).toBe(10.12); // rounded
    });

    it('should handle zero and negative base fees', () => {
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(0, 'express')).toBe(0);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(-5, 'express')).toBe(0); // safeBase treats negative as 0
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(0, 'vip')).toBe(0);
      expect(applyShippingMethodMultiplier(0, 'pickup')).toBe(0);
    });
  });
});
===== FILE: .\libs\server\services\src\lib\shipping-calculation.service.ts =====
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';
import {
  PlatformSettings,
  ShippingStrategy,
  ShippingPaymentMode,
} from '@kickzone-workspace/entities';

export interface SellerShippingInfo {
  sellerId: string | number;
  shippingFee: number;
  sellerName?: string;
  shippingPaymentMode?: ShippingPaymentMode | string;
}

export interface ShippingCalculationResult {
  totalShippingCost: number;
  shippingBreakdown: Array<{
    sellerId: string | number;
    sellerName?: string;
    originalFee: number;
    appliedFee: number;
  }>;
  freeShippingApplied: boolean;
  strategyUsed: ShippingStrategy;
}

export function applyShippingMethodMultiplier(
  baseFee: number,
  shippingMethod?: string | null
): number {
  const normalized = String(shippingMethod || 'vip').toLowerCase();
  const safeBase = Number.isFinite(Number(baseFee)) && Number(baseFee) > 0 ? Number(baseFee) : 0;
  if (normalized === 'pickup') {
    return 0;
  }
  if (normalized === 'express') {
    return Math.round(safeBase * 0.45 * 100) / 100;
  }
  return Math.round(safeBase * 100) / 100;
}

@Injectable()
export class ShippingCalculationService {
  private defaultSettings: Partial<PlatformSettings> = {
    freeShippingThreshold: 500.0, // Temporarily increased to test shipping fees
    freeShippingEnabled: false, // Temporarily disabled to test shipping fees
    shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT,
    additionalSellerDiscount: 0.5, // 50% discount
    additionalSellerFixedFee: undefined,
    maxShippingCap: 15.0,
    maxShippingCapEnabled: true,
  };

  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(PlatformSettings)
    private platformSettingsRepository: Repository<PlatformSettings>
  ) {}

  /**
   * Calculate shipping costs for multi-seller cart
   * Implements smart shipping strategies similar to Amazon/eBay
   * PAY_ON_DELIVERY sellers are excluded from online shipping calculation
   */
  async calculateShippingCost(
    cartSubtotal: number,
    sellerShippingFees: SellerShippingInfo[]
  ): Promise<ShippingCalculationResult> {
    // Get platform settings or use defaults
    const settings = await this.getPlatformSettings();

    // Separate PAY_ON_DELIVERY and PAY_ONLINE sellers
    // Only explicitly PAY_ON_DELIVERY (case-insensitive) are treated as COD; missing/undefined = PAY_ONLINE (legacy behavior)
    const onlineSellers = sellerShippingFees.filter(
      (s) =>
        s.shippingPaymentMode !== undefined &&
        s.shippingPaymentMode !== null &&
        String(s.shippingPaymentMode).toUpperCase() === ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY
          ? false
          : true
    );

    // Check for free shipping threshold (applies only to online sellers)
    if (
      settings.freeShippingEnabled &&
      settings.freeShippingThreshold &&
      cartSubtotal >= settings.freeShippingThreshold
    ) {
      return {
        totalShippingCost: 0,
        shippingBreakdown: sellerShippingFees.map((seller) => ({
          sellerId: seller.sellerId,
          sellerName: seller.sellerName,
          originalFee: Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0,
          appliedFee: 0,
        })),
        freeShippingApplied: true,
        strategyUsed: settings.shippingStrategy,
      };
    }

    // Calculate shipping based on strategy for ONLINE sellers only
    let onlineBreakdown: ShippingCalculationResult['shippingBreakdown'] = [];
    let totalShippingCost = 0;

    const computeStrategy = (
      sellers: SellerShippingInfo[]
    ): ShippingCalculationResult['shippingBreakdown'] => {
      switch (settings.shippingStrategy) {
        case ShippingStrategy.FULL_SUM:
          return sellers.map((seller) => ({
            sellerId: seller.sellerId,
            sellerName: seller.sellerName,
            originalFee: Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0,
            appliedFee: Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0,
          }));

        case ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT: {
          return sellers.map((seller, index) => {
            const originalFee = Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0;
            if (index === 0) {
              return {
                sellerId: seller.sellerId,
                sellerName: seller.sellerName,
                originalFee,
                appliedFee: originalFee,
              };
            } else {
              const discount = settings.additionalSellerDiscount || 0;
              const appliedFee = originalFee * (1 - discount);
              return {
                sellerId: seller.sellerId,
                sellerName: seller.sellerName,
                originalFee,
                appliedFee: Math.max(0, Math.round(appliedFee * 100) / 100),
              };
            }
          });
        }

        case ShippingStrategy.TIERED_FIXED: {
          const fixedFee = Number(settings.additionalSellerFixedFee) || 0;
          return sellers.map((seller, index) => {
            const originalFee = Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0;
            if (index === 0) {
              return {
                sellerId: seller.sellerId,
                sellerName: seller.sellerName,
                originalFee,
                appliedFee: originalFee,
              };
            } else {
              return {
                sellerId: seller.sellerId,
                sellerName: seller.sellerName,
                originalFee,
                appliedFee: Math.round(fixedFee * 100) / 100,
              };
            }
          });
        }

        case ShippingStrategy.HIGHEST_ONLY: {
          const fees = sellers.map((s) => Number(s.shippingFee) || 0);
          const highestFee = Math.max(...fees, 0);
          return sellers.map((seller, index) => {
            const originalFee = Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0;
            return {
              sellerId: seller.sellerId,
              sellerName: seller.sellerName,
              originalFee,
              appliedFee: index === 0 ? Math.round(highestFee * 100) / 100 : 0,
            };
          });
        }

        case ShippingStrategy.AVERAGE: {
          const fees = sellers.map((s) => Number(s.shippingFee) || 0);
          const averageFee =
            fees.reduce((sum, fee) => sum + fee, 0) / (fees.length || 1);
          const roundedAverage = Math.round(averageFee * 100) / 100;
          return sellers.map((seller, index) => {
            const originalFee = Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0;
            return {
              sellerId: seller.sellerId,
              sellerName: seller.sellerName,
              originalFee,
              appliedFee: index === 0 ? roundedAverage : 0,
            };
          });
        }

        default:
          return sellers.map((seller) => ({
            sellerId: seller.sellerId,
            sellerName: seller.sellerName,
            originalFee: Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0,
            appliedFee: Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0,
          }));
      }
    };

    if (onlineSellers.length > 0) {
      onlineBreakdown = computeStrategy(onlineSellers);
      totalShippingCost = onlineBreakdown.reduce(
        (sum, item) => sum + item.appliedFee,
        0
      );
    }

    // Apply maximum shipping cap if enabled (only to online sellers)
    if (
      settings.maxShippingCapEnabled &&
      settings.maxShippingCap &&
      totalShippingCost > settings.maxShippingCap
    ) {
      const capRatio = settings.maxShippingCap / totalShippingCost;
      onlineBreakdown = onlineBreakdown.map((item) => ({
        ...item,
        appliedFee: Math.round(item.appliedFee * capRatio * 100) / 100,
      }));
      totalShippingCost = Math.round(settings.maxShippingCap * 100) / 100;
    }

    // Merge COD sellers with 0 fees, preserving original order
    const breakdownMap = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const item of onlineBreakdown) {
      breakdownMap.set(String(item.sellerId), item.appliedFee);
    }

    const finalBreakdown = sellerShippingFees.map((seller) => {
      const isCod =
        seller.shippingPaymentMode !== undefined &&
        seller.shippingPaymentMode !== null &&
        String(seller.shippingPaymentMode).toUpperCase() === ShippingPaymentMode.PAY_ON_DELIVERY;
      const originalFee = Number(seller.shippingFee) || 0;
      const appliedFee = isCod ? 0 : breakdownMap.get(String(seller.sellerId)) ?? 0;
      return {
        sellerId: seller.sellerId,
        sellerName: seller.sellerName,
        originalFee: isCod ? 0 : Math.round(originalFee * 100) / 100,
        appliedFee: Math.round(appliedFee * 100) / 100,
      };
    });

    return {
      totalShippingCost: Math.round(totalShippingCost * 100) / 100,
      shippingBreakdown: finalBreakdown,
      freeShippingApplied: false,
      strategyUsed: settings.shippingStrategy,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get platform settings or return defaults
   */
  private async getPlatformSettings(): Promise<PlatformSettings> {
    try {
      // Try to get settings (assuming single row with id = 1)
      const settings = await this.platformSettingsRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: 1 },
      });
      if (settings) {
        return settings;
      }
      // Return defaults with required fields
      return {
        id: 1,
        shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT,
        freeShippingEnabled: true,
        maxShippingCapEnabled: true,
        ...this.defaultSettings,
      } as PlatformSettings;
    } catch (_error) {
      // If table doesn't exist or error, return defaults
      return {
        id: 1,
        shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT,
        freeShippingEnabled: true,
        maxShippingCapEnabled: true,
        ...this.defaultSettings,
      } as PlatformSettings;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get or create platform settings (for admin use)
   */
  async getOrCreateSettings(): Promise<PlatformSettings> {
    try {
      let settings = await this.platformSettingsRepository.findOne({
        where: { id: 1 },
      });

      if (!settings) {
        // Create default settings
        settings = this.platformSettingsRepository.create({
          ...this.defaultSettings,
          id: 1,
        } as PlatformSettings);
        settings = await this.platformSettingsRepository.save(settings);
      }

      return settings;
    } catch (_error) {
      // If error, return defaults
      return {
        id: 1,
        shippingStrategy: ShippingStrategy.TIERED_DISCOUNT,
        freeShippingEnabled: true,
        maxShippingCapEnabled: true,
        ...this.defaultSettings,
      } as PlatformSettings;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Update platform settings (admin only)
   */
  async updateSettings(
    updates: Partial<PlatformSettings>
  ): Promise<PlatformSettings> {
    const settings = await this.getOrCreateSettings();
    Object.assign(settings, updates);
    return this.platformSettingsRepository.save(settings);
  }
}
